[No.1] BirdSet: A Large-Scale Dataset for Audio Classification in Avian Bioacoustics
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: classif
标题: BirdSet：一种用于鸟类生物声学音频分类的大规模数据集
类别: datasets and benchmarks
作者: Lukas Rauch;Raphael Schwinger;Moritz Wirth;René Heinrich;Denis Huseljic;Marek Herde;Jonas Lange;Stefan Kahl;Bernhard Sick;Sven Tomforde;Christoph Scholz;
机构: University of Kassel;Christian-Albrechts-Universität;Fraunhofer Institute for Energy Economics and Energy Technology;;Cornell University;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28986
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.10380
英摘: Deep learning (DL) has greatly advanced audio classification, yet the field is limited by the scarcity of large-scale benchmark datasets that have propelled progress in other domains. While AudioSet is a pivotal step to bridge this gap as a universal-domain dataset, its restricted accessibility and limited range of evaluation use cases challenge its role as the sole resource. Therefore, we introduce BirdSet, a large-scale benchmark dataset for audio classification focusing on avian bioacoustics. BirdSet surpasses AudioSet with over 6,800 recording hours ($\uparrow\!17\%$) from nearly 10,000 classes ($\uparrow\!18\times$) for training and more than 400 hours ($\uparrow\!7\times$) across eight strongly labeled evaluation datasets. It serves as a versatile resource for use cases such as multi-label classification, covariate shift or self-supervised learning. We benchmark six well-known DL models in multi-label classification across three distinct training scenarios and outline further evaluation use cases in audio classification. We host our dataset on Hugging Face for easy accessibility and offer an extensive codebase to reproduce our results.
中摘: 深度学习（DL）在音频分类领域取得了巨大进展，但该领域受限于缺乏大规模基准数据集，而这些数据集在其他领域推动了进步。尽管AudioSet是一个重要的步骤，作为通用领域的数据集来缩小这一差距，但它受限的可访问性和有限的评估用例范围限制了其作为唯一资源的角色。因此，我们引入了BirdSet，这是一个专注于鸟类生物声学的大规模基准数据集。与AudioSet相比，BirdSet提供了超过6,800小时的录音数据（增长了17%），来自近10,000个类别（增长了18倍），用于训练，并且还提供了超过400小时的数据（增长了7倍）覆盖八个强标签评估数据集。它为多标签分类、协变量偏移或半监督学习等应用场景提供了一个多功能的资源。我们在多标签分类场景下对三种不同的训练情况进行六种知名DL模型进行了基准测试，并概述了音频分类中的进一步评估用例。我们将数据集托管在Hugging Face上以方便访问，并提供了详细的代码库以重现我们的结果。

[No.3] Not All LLM-Generated Data Are Equal: Rethinking Data Weighting in Text Classification
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: classif
标题: 并非所有由LLM生成的数据都等同：重新思考文本分类中的数据权重分配
类别: other topics in machine learning (i.e., none of the above)
作者: Hsun-Yu Kuo;Yin-Hsiang Liao;Yu-Chieh Chao;Wei-Yun Ma;Pu-Jen Cheng;
机构: EPFL;National Taiwan University;Academia Sinica;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28366
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.21526
英摘: Synthetic data augmentation via large language models (LLMs) allows researchers to leverage additional training data, thus enhancing the performance of downstream tasks, especially when real-world data is scarce. However, the generated data can deviate from the real-world data, and this misalignment can bring deficient outcomes while applying the trained model to applications. Therefore, we proposed efficient weighted-loss approaches to align synthetic data with real-world distribution by emphasizing high-quality and diversified data generated by LLMs with using merely a little real-world data. We empirically assessed the effectiveness of our method on multiple text classification tasks, and the results showed leveraging our approaches on a BERT-level model robustly outperformed standard cross-entropy and other data weighting approaches, providing potential solutions to effectively leveraging synthetic data from any suitable data generator for model training.
中摘: 通过大型语言模型（LLM）合成数据增强的方法使研究人员能够利用额外的训练数据，从而提高下游任务的表现，尤其是在真实世界数据稀缺的情况下。然而，生成的数据可能会偏离真实世界的数据，这种不匹配在将训练好的模型应用于实际应用时可能导致不足的结果。因此，我们提出了高效的加权损失方法，通过强调LLM生成的高质量和多样化数据来与少量的真实世界数据分布对齐。我们在多个文本分类任务上实证评估了我们方法的有效性，并且结果显示，在BERT级别的模型上应用我们的方法稳健地优于标准交叉熵和其他数据加权方法，提供了有效利用任何合适的数据生成器生成的合成数据进行模型训练的潜在解决方案。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.4] Enhancing Pre-trained Representation Classifiability can Boost its Interpretability
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: classif
标题: 增强预训练表示的分类能力可以提升其可解释性
类别: interpretability and explainable AI
作者: Shufan Shen;Zhaobo Qi;Junshu Sun;Qingming Huang;Qi Tian;Shuhui Wang;
机构: Chinese Academy of Sciences;Harbin Institute of Technology;University of Chinese Academy of Sciences;Huawei;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30262
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.24105
英摘: The visual representation of a pre-trained model prioritizes the classifiability on downstream tasks, while the widespread applications for pre-trained visual models have posed new requirements for representation interpretability. However, it remains unclear whether the pre-trained representations can achieve high interpretability and classifiability simultaneously. To answer this question, we quantify the representation interpretability by leveraging its correlation with the ratio of interpretable semantics within the representations. Given the pre-trained representations, only the interpretable semantics can be captured by interpretations, whereas the uninterpretable part leads to information loss. Based on this fact, we propose the Inherent Interpretability Score (IIS) that evaluates the information loss, measures the ratio of interpretable semantics, and quantifies the representation interpretability. In the evaluation of the representation interpretability with different classifiability, we surprisingly discover that the interpretability and classifiability are positively correlated, i.e., representations with higher classifiability provide more interpretable semantics that can be captured in the interpretations. This observation further supports two benefits to the pre-trained representations. First, the classifiability of representations can be further improved by fine-tuning with interpretability maximization. Second, with the classifiability improvement for the representations, we obtain predictions based on their interpretations with less accuracy degradation. The discovered positive correlation and corresponding applications show that practitioners can unify the improvements in interpretability and classifiability for pre-trained vision models. Codes are available at https://github.com/ssfgunner/IIS.
中摘: 预训练模型的视觉表示优先考虑下游任务的可分类性，而预训练视觉模型的广泛应用则提出了对表示解释性的新要求。然而，目前尚不清楚预训练表示能否同时实现高解释性和高可分类性。为了回答这个问题，我们通过利用表示中的可解释语义比例与解释性相关性来量化表示解释性。给定预训练表示后，只有可解释的语义可以通过解释捕获，而不可解释的部分会导致信息丢失。基于这一事实，我们提出了一种固有解释性评分（IIS），用于评估信息丢失、测量可解释语义的比例，并量化表示解释性。在不同可分类性下评估表示解释性时，我们惊讶地发现解释性和可分类性呈正相关，即具有更高可分类性的表示能够提供更多的可解释语义，这些语义可以在解释中被捕获。这一观察进一步支持了预训练表示的两个好处。首先，可以通过最大化解释性进行微调来进一步提高表示的可分类性。其次，在表示可分类性提高的情况下，我们可以根据它们的解释获得预测，且准确度下降较小。我们发现的正相关关系及其应用表明，从业者可以统一提升预训练视觉模型的解释性和可分类性。相关代码可在https://github.com/ssfgunner/IIS获取。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.5] Credal Wrapper of Model Averaging for Uncertainty Estimation in Classification
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: classif
标题: 基于信念模型平均的分类不确定性估计包层
类别: probabilistic methods (Bayesian methods, variational inference, sampling, UQ, etc.)
作者: Kaizheng Wang;Fabio Cuzzolin;Keivan Shariatmadar;David Moens;Hans Hallez;
机构: Katholieke Universiteit Leuven;Oxford Brookes University;;KU Leuven;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/29022
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.15047
英摘: This paper presents an innovative approach, called credal wrapper, to formulating a credal set representation of model averaging for Bayesian neural networks (BNNs) and deep ensembles (DEs), capable of improving uncertainty estimation in classification tasks. Given a finite collection of single predictive distributions derived from BNNs or DEs, the proposed credal wrapper approach extracts an upper and a lower probability bound per class, acknowledging the epistemic uncertainty due to the availability of a limited amount of distributions. Such probability intervals over classes can be mapped on a convex set of probabilities (a credal set) from which, in turn, a unique prediction can be obtained using a transformation called intersection probability transformation. In this article, we conduct extensive experiments on several out-of-distribution (OOD) detection benchmarks, encompassing various dataset pairs (CIFAR10/100 vs SVHN/Tiny-ImageNet, CIFAR10 vs CIFAR10-C, CIFAR100 vs CIFAR100-C and ImageNet vs ImageNet-O) and using different network architectures (such as VGG16, ResNet-18/50, EfficientNet B2, and ViT Base). Compared to the BNN and DE baselines, the proposed credal wrapper method exhibits superior performance in uncertainty estimation and achieves a lower expected calibration error on corrupted data.
中摘: 本文提出了一种名为“可信区间包装器”的创新方法，用于为贝叶斯神经网络（BNN）和深度ensemble（DE）建模平均形成可信区间表示，从而提高分类任务中的不确定性估计。给定来自BNN或DE的有限单个预测分布集合，所提出的可信区间包装器方法提取每个类别的上界和下界概率，承认由于可用分布数量有限而导致的知识性不确定性。这样的类别概率区间可以映射到一个概率凸集（即可信区间），从中可以使用一种称为交概率变换的转换来获得唯一的预测。在本文中，我们在多个离群值检测基准上进行了广泛的实验，涵盖各种数据集对（CIFAR10/100与SVHN/Tiny-ImageNet、CIFAR10与CIFAR10-C、CIFAR100与CIFAR100-C以及ImageNet与ImageNet-O），并使用不同的网络架构（如VGG16、ResNet-18/50、EfficientNet B2和ViT Base）。与BNN和DE基线相比，所提出的可信区间包装器方法在不确定性估计方面表现出更优性能，并在受污染数据上的预期校准误差较低。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.6] LR0.FM: Low-Res Benchmark and Improving robustness for Zero-Shot Classification in Foundation Models
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: classif
标题: LR0.FM：低分辨率基准与提高基础模型零样本分类的鲁棒性
类别: applications to computer vision, audio, language, and other modalities
作者: Priyank Pathak;Shyam Marjit;Shruti Vyas;Yogesh S Rawat;
机构: University of Central Florida;Indian Institute of Science;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30609
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.03950
英摘: Visual-language foundation Models (FMs) exhibit remarkable zero-shot generalization across diverse tasks, largely attributed to extensive pre-training on largescale datasets. However, their robustness on low-resolution/pixelated (LR) images, a common challenge in real-world scenarios, remains underexplored. We introduce LR0.FM, a comprehensive benchmark evaluating the impact of low resolution on the zero-shot classification performance of 10 FM(s) across 66 backbones and 15 datasets. We propose a novel metric, Weighted Aggregated Robustness, to address the limitations of existing metrics and better evaluate model performance across resolutions and datasets. Our key findings show that: (i) model size positively correlates with robustness to resolution degradation, (ii) pre-training dataset quality is more important than its size, and (iii) fine-tuned and higher resolution models are less robust against LR. Our analysis further reveals that the model makes semantically reasonable predictions at LR, and the lack of fine-grained details in input adversely impacts the model's initial layers more than the deeper layers. We use these insights and introduce a simple strategy, LR-TK0, to enhance the robustness of models without compromising their pre-trained weights. We demonstrate the effectiveness of LR-TK0 for robustness against low-resolution across several datasets and its generalization capability across backbones and other approaches. Code is available at https://github.com/shyammarjit/LR0.FM
中摘: 视觉语言基础模型（FMs）在多种任务上表现出显著的零样本泛化能力，这主要归因于其在大规模数据集上的广泛预训练。然而，在低分辨率/像素化（LR）图像上的鲁棒性，这是现实世界场景中的一个常见挑战，仍然未得到充分探索。我们引入了LR0.FM，这是一个全面的基准测试，评估了10种FM在66种骨干网络和15个数据集上的零样本分类性能受到低分辨率的影响。我们提出了一种新的度量标准——加权聚合鲁棒性，以解决现有度量标准的局限性，并更好地评估模型在不同分辨率和数据集上的性能。我们的关键发现表明：(i) 模型规模与对分辨率退化鲁棒性的正相关关系；(ii) 预训练数据集的质量比其规模更重要；(iii) 微调和高分辨率模型对低分辨率的鲁棒性较差。进一步的分析揭示，模型在低分辨率下能够做出语义合理的预测，但输入中缺乏细粒度细节会更不利地影响模型的初始层而非深层层。我们利用这些见解并引入了一个简单的策略——LR-TK0，以增强模型的鲁棒性而不牺牲其预训练权重。我们展示了LR-TK0在多个数据集上提高低分辨率鲁棒性的有效性，并证明了其跨不同骨干网络和其他方法的良好泛化能力。代码可在https://github.com/shyammarjit/LR0.FM获取。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.7] Strategic Classification With Externalities
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: classif
标题: 外部性下的战略分类
类别: alignment, fairness, safety, privacy, and societal considerations
作者: Safwan Hossain;Evi Micha;Yiling Chen;Ariel D. Procaccia;
机构: Harvard University;University of Southern California;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28377
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.08032
英摘: We propose a new variant of the strategic classification problem: a principal reveals a classifier, and $n$ agents report their (possibly manipulated) features to be classified. Motivated by real-world applications, our model crucially allows the manipulation of one agent to affect another; that is, it explicitly captures inter-agent externalities. The principal-agent interactions are formally modeled as a Stackelberg game, with the resulting agent manipulation dynamics captured as a simultaneous game. We show that under certain assumptions, the pure Nash Equilibrium of this agent manipulation game is unique and can be efficiently computed. Leveraging this result, PAC learning guarantees are established for the learner: informally, we show that it is possible to learn classifiers that minimize loss on the distribution, even when a random number of agents are manipulating their way to a pure Nash Equilibrium. We also comment on the optimization of such classifiers through gradient-based approaches. This work sets the theoretical foundations for a more realistic analysis of classifiers that are robust against multiple strategic actors interacting in a common environment.
中摘: 我们提出了一种战略分类问题的新变体：一个委托人公布了一个分类器，而有$n$个代理会报告他们的（可能被操纵的）特征来进行分类。我们的模型受到实际应用的启发，关键之处在于一个代理的操纵会影响另一个代理；也就是说，它明确地捕捉到了代理之间的外部性。委托人与代理人的互动被正式建模为一个Stackelberg博弈，代理人的操纵动态则被捕捉为一个同时博弈。在某些假设下，我们证明了这种代理操纵博弈的纯纳什均衡是唯一的，并且可以高效地计算出来。利用这一结果，我们建立了学习者的PAC学习保证：简而言之，即使有随机数量的代理人在通过纯纳什均衡进行操纵时，也有可能学习出能够最小化分布损失的分类器。我们还讨论了通过梯度基方法优化此类分类器的方法。这项工作为更现实地分析能够在多个战略行为者共同环境中保持鲁棒性的分类器奠定了理论基础。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.8] Fully-inductive Node Classification on Arbitrary Graphs
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: classif
标题: 任意图的完全归纳节点分类
类别: learning on graphs and other geometries & topologies
作者: Jianan Zhao;Zhaocheng Zhu;Mikhail Galkin;Hesham Mostafa;Michael M. Bronstein;Jian Tang;
机构: Université de Montréal;NVIDIA;Google;Intel;University of Oxford;HEC Montreal;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/31202
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.20445
英摘: One fundamental challenge in graph machine learning is generalizing to new graphs. Many existing methods following the inductive setup can generalize to test graphs with new structures, but assuming the feature and label spaces remain the same as the training ones. This paper introduces a fully-inductive setup, where models should perform inference on arbitrary test graphs with new structures, feature and label spaces. We propose GraphAny as the first attempt at this challenging setup. GraphAny models inference on a new graph as an analytical solution to a LinearGNN, which can be naturally applied to graphs with any feature and label spaces. To further build a stronger model with learning capacity, we fuse multiple LinearGNN predictions with learned inductive attention scores. Specifically, the attention module is carefully parameterized as a function of the entropy-normalized distance features between pairs of LinearGNN predictions to ensure generalization to new graphs. Empirically, GraphAny trained on a single Wisconsin dataset with only 120 labeled nodes can generalize to 30 new graphs with an average accuracy of 67.26%, surpassing not only all inductive baselines, but also strong transductive methods trained separately on each of the 30 test graphs.
中摘: 图机器学习中的一个基本挑战是如何将模型推广到新的图上。许多现有的方法遵循归纳设置，可以在具有新结构的测试图上进行推广，但前提是假设特征空间和标签空间与训练集保持一致。本文引入了一种完全归纳的设置，要求模型能够在任意具有新结构的测试图上进行推理，并且特征空间和标签空间也可以不同。我们提出了GraphAny作为这一具有挑战性的设置下的首个尝试。GraphAny通过将新图上的推理视为线性图神经网络（LinearGNN）的解析解来实现，这种解析解可以自然地应用于任何特征和标签空间的图。为了进一步构建具有更强学习能力的模型，我们将多个线性图神经网络预测结果融合，并学习到归纳注意力分数。具体来说，注意力模块被精心参数化为线性图神经网络预测之间归一化熵距离特征的函数，以确保在新图上的泛化能力。实验结果显示，仅在Wisconsin数据集中训练并使用了120个标记节点的GraphAny，可以推广到30个新图，并且平均准确率为67.26%，不仅超越了所有归纳基线，还超过了分别在30个测试图上独立训练的强大归纳方法。

[No.9] Sharper Guarantees for Learning Neural Network Classifiers with Gradient Methods
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: classif
标题: 基于梯度方法学习神经网络分类器的更严格的保证
类别: learning theory
作者: Hossein Taheri;Christos Thrampoulidis;Arya Mazumdar;
机构: University of California, San Diego;University of British Columbia;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28778
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10024
英摘: In this paper, we study the data-dependent convergence and generalization behavior of gradient methods for neural networks with smooth activation. Our first result is a novel bound on the excess risk of deep networks trained by the logistic loss, via an alogirthmic stability analysis. Compared to previous works, our results improve upon the shortcomings of the well-established Rademacher complexity-based bounds. Importantly, the bounds we derive in this paper are tighter, hold even for neural networks of small width, do not scale unfavorably with width, are algorithm-dependent, and consequently capture the role of initialization on the sample complexity of gradient descent for deep nets. Specialized to noiseless data separable with margin $γ$ by neural tangent kernel (NTK) features of a network of width $Ω(\text{poly}(\log(n)))$, we show the test-error rate to be $e^{O(L)}/{γ^2 n}$, where $n$ is the training set size and $L$ denotes the number of hidden layers. This is an improvement in the test loss bound compared to previous works while maintaining the poly-logarithmic width conditions. We further investigate excess risk bounds for deep nets trained with noisy data, establishing that under a polynomial condition on the network width, gradient descent can achieve the optimal excess risk. Finally, we show that a large step-size significantly improves upon the NTK regime's results in classifying the XOR distribution. In particular, we show for a one-hidden-layer neural network of constant width $m$ with quadratic activation and standard Gaussian initialization that mini-batch SGD with linear sample complexity and with a large step-size $η=m$ reaches the perfect test accuracy after only $\ceil{\log(d)}$ iterations, where $d$ is the data dimension.
中摘: 在本文中，我们研究了具有平滑激活函数的神经网络通过逻辑损失训练时梯度方法的数据依赖收敛性和泛化行为。我们的第一个结果是通过对深网络进行算法稳定性分析，获得了一个新颖的关于逻辑损失训练的深网络过拟合风险上界的表达式。与先前的工作相比，我们的结果改进了Rademacher复杂性基础的上界所存在的不足之处。重要的是，我们在本文中推导出的上界更紧，即使对于宽度较小的神经网络也适用，不会随着宽度的增加而不利地扩展，并且是算法相关的，因此能够捕捉初始化在网络深度学习样本复杂性中的作用。当数据无噪声并且可以通过网络宽度为Ω(poly(log(n)))的神经网络的神经核特征（NTK特征）以间隔γ分离时，我们展示了测试错误率可以达到e^(O(L))/γ^2 n，其中n是训练集大小，L表示隐藏层的数量。这比先前的工作在保持多项对数宽度条件下提高了测试损失上界的估计。我们进一步探讨了具有噪声数据的深网络的过拟合风险上界，证明在满足多项式条件的网络宽度下，梯度下降可以实现最优的过拟合风险。最后，我们展示了大步长在分类XOR分布时显著改善了神经核（NTK）范式下的结果。具体来说，对于一个宽度恒定为m的一隐藏层神经网络，其激活函数为二次函数且初始化为标准高斯分布，我们证明使用线性样本复杂性的mini-batch SGD并在大步长η=m的情况下，仅需ceil(log(d))次迭代即可达到完美的测试准确率，其中d是数据维度。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.10] Synthio: Augmenting Small-Scale Audio Classification Datasets with Synthetic Data
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: classif
标题: Synthio：通过合成数据增强小型音频分类数据集
类别: applications to computer vision, audio, language, and other modalities
作者: Sreyan Ghosh;Sonal Kumar;Zhifeng Kong;Rafael Valle;Bryan Catanzaro;Dinesh Manocha;
机构: University of Maryland;NVIDIA;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/29111
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.02056
英摘: We present Synthio, a novel approach for augmenting small-scale audio classification datasets with synthetic data. Our goal is to improve audio classification accuracy with limited labeled data. Traditional data augmentation techniques, which apply artificial transformations (e.g., adding random noise or masking segments), struggle to create data that captures the true diversity present in real-world audios. To address this shortcoming, we propose to augment the dataset with synthetic audio generated from text-to-audio (T2A) diffusion models. However, synthesizing effective augmentations is challenging because not only should the generated data be acoustically consistent with the underlying small-scale dataset, but they should also have sufficient compositional diversity. To overcome the first challenge, we align the generations of the T2A model with the small-scale dataset using preference optimization. This ensures that the acoustic characteristics of the generated data remain consistent with the small-scale dataset. To address the second challenge, we propose a novel caption generation technique that leverages the reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models to (1) generate diverse and meaningful audio captions and (2) iteratively refine their quality. The generated captions are then used to prompt the aligned T2A model. We extensively evaluate Synthio on ten datasets and four simulated limited-data settings. Results indicate our method consistently outperforms all baselines by 0.1%-39% using a T2A model trained only on weakly-captioned AudioSet.
中摘: 我们提出了Synthio，这是一种新颖的方法，用于通过合成数据增强小型音频分类数据集。我们的目标是利用有限的标注数据提高音频分类准确性。传统的数据增强技术（例如，添加随机噪声或掩盖音频片段）难以创建能够捕捉真实世界音频中真正多样性的数据。为了解决这一不足，我们提议使用文本转音频（T2A）扩散模型生成的合成音频来增强数据集。然而，生成有效的增强数据具有挑战性，因为不仅生成的数据应与基础的小规模数据集在声学上保持一致，它们还应该具备足够的组合多样性。为了克服第一个挑战，我们通过偏好优化将T2A模型的生成与小规模数据集对齐，这确保了生成数据的声学特征与小规模数据集保持一致。为了解决第二个挑战，我们提出了一种新颖的图caption生成技术，该技术利用大型语言模型的推理能力来（1）生成多样且有意义的音频图caption，并（2）逐步提升其质量。生成的caption随后被用来提示对齐的T2A模型。我们在十个数据集和四种模拟的有限数据设置上进行了广泛评估。结果显示，我们的方法在所有基线方法上均表现出一致的优越性，范围从0.1%到39%，仅使用了在弱标注AudioSet上训练的T2A模型。

[No.11] Generative Classifiers Avoid Shortcut Solutions
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: classif
标题: 生成式分类器避免捷径解决方案
类别: alignment, fairness, safety, privacy, and societal considerations
作者: Alexander Cong Li;Ananya Kumar;Deepak Pathak;
机构: Carnegie Mellon University;OpenAI;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28371
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.25034
英摘: Discriminative approaches to classification often learn shortcuts that hold in-distribution but fail even under minor distribution shift. This failure mode stems from an overreliance on features that are spuriously correlated with the label. We show that generative classifiers, which use class-conditional generative models, can avoid this issue by modeling all features, both core and spurious, instead of mainly spurious ones. These generative classifiers are simple to train, avoiding the need for specialized augmentations, strong regularization, extra hyperparameters, or knowledge of the specific spurious correlations to avoid. We find that diffusion-based and autoregressive generative classifiers achieve state-of-the-art performance on five standard image and text distribution shift benchmarks and reduce the impact of spurious correlations in realistic applications, such as medical or satellite datasets. Finally, we carefully analyze a Gaussian toy setting to understand the inductive biases of generative classifiers, as well as the data properties that determine when generative classifiers outperform discriminative ones.
中摘: 分类中的辨别方法往往会在分布内学习一些捷径，但在轻微分布变化下就会失效。这种失败模式源于对与标签偶然相关的特征的过度依赖。我们展示了生成式分类器可以通过使用条件生成模型来建模所有特征（核心和偶然），而不是主要建模偶然特征，从而避免这个问题。生成式分类器易于训练，无需特殊的增强、强烈的正则化、额外的超参数或了解具体的偶然相关性。我们发现，基于扩散和自回归的生成式分类器在五个标准的图像和文本分布变化基准测试中达到了最先进的性能，并且在现实应用中（如医疗或卫星数据集）减少了偶然相关性的影响。最后，我们仔细分析了一个高斯玩具设置，以理解生成式分类器的归纳偏置，以及哪些数据特性决定了生成式分类器何时优于辨别式分类器。

[No.12] QPM: Discrete Optimization for Globally Interpretable Image Classification
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: classif
标题: QPM：离散优化在全局可解释图像分类中的应用
类别: interpretability and explainable AI
作者: Thomas Norrenbrock;Timo Kaiser;Sovan Biswas;Ramesh Manuvinakurike;Bodo Rosenhahn;
机构: University of Hanover;Intel;Institut für Informationsverarbeitung;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30260
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.20130
英摘: Understanding the classifications of deep neural networks, e.g. used in safety-critical situations, is becoming increasingly important. While recent models can locally explain a single decision, to provide a faithful global explanation about an accurate model's general behavior is a more challenging open task. Towards that goal, we introduce the Quadratic Programming Enhanced Model (QPM), which learns globally interpretable class representations. QPM represents every class with a binary assignment of very few, typically 5, features, that are also assigned to other classes, ensuring easily comparable contrastive class representations. This compact binary assignment is found using discrete optimization based on predefined similarity measures and interpretability constraints. The resulting optimal assignment is used to fine-tune the diverse features, so that each of them becomes the shared general concept between the assigned classes. Extensive evaluations show that QPM delivers unprecedented global interpretability across small and large-scale datasets while setting the state of the art for the accuracy of interpretable models.
中摘: 理解深度神经网络的分类，尤其是在安全关键情况下使用的网络，变得越来越重要。虽然最近的模型可以在局部解释单个决策，但提供一个关于准确模型整体行为的忠实全局解释仍然是一个更具挑战性的开放任务。为了实现这一目标，我们引入了增强模型的二次规划（Quadratic Programming Enhanced Model，简称QPM），它学习全局可解释的类别表示。QPM使用二进制分配方式为每个类别选择极少数特征，通常只有5个特征，并且这些特征也会被分配给其他类别，确保不同类别之间的对比性特征表示易于比较。这种紧凑的二进制分配通过基于预定义相似度度量和可解释性约束的离散优化来找到。最终得到的最佳分配用于微调多样化的特征，使得每个特征成为所分配类别之间的共享通用概念。广泛的评估表明，QPM在小型和大型数据集上提供了前所未有的全局可解释性，同时在可解释模型的准确性方面达到了最先进的水平。

[No.14] Towards Understanding Why Label Smoothing Degrades Selective Classification and How to Fix It
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: classif
标题: 理解标签平滑为何会削弱选择性分类以及如何修复它
类别: other topics in machine learning (i.e., none of the above)
作者: Guoxuan Xia;Olivier Laurent;Gianni Franchi;Christos-Savvas Bouganis;
机构: Imperial College London;Université Paris-Saclay;École Nationale Supérieure de Techniques Avancées;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30864
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.14715
英摘: Label smoothing (LS) is a popular regularisation method for training neural networks as it is effective in improving test accuracy and is simple to implement. ``Hard'' one-hot labels are ``smoothed'' by uniformly distributing probability mass to other classes, reducing overfitting. Prior work has suggested that in some cases LS can degrade selective classification (SC) -- where the aim is to reject misclassifications using a model's uncertainty. In this work, we first demonstrate empirically across an extended range of large-scale tasks and architectures that LS consistently degrades SC. We then address a gap in existing knowledge, providing an explanation for this behaviour by analysing logit-level gradients: LS degrades the uncertainty rank ordering of correct vs incorrect predictions by suppressing the max logit more when a prediction is likely to be correct, and less when it is likely to be wrong. This elucidates previously reported experimental results where strong classifiers underperform in SC. We then demonstrate the empirical effectiveness of post-hoc logit normalisation for recovering lost SC performance caused by LS. Furthermore, linking back to our gradient analysis, we again provide an explanation for why such normalisation is effective.
中摘: 标签平滑（LS）是一种流行的大规模任务和架构中训练神经网络的正则化方法，它有效提高了测试准确性且易于实现。通过均匀地将概率质量分配给其他类，标签平滑可以减少“硬”的一热标签（即完全确定的标签）带来的过拟合。以往的研究表明，在某些情况下，LS可能会降低选择性分类（SC）的效果——即使用模型的不确定性来拒绝错误分类的能力。在本研究中，我们首先通过广泛的实验数据展示了LS在多种大型任务和架构中一致地降低了SC性能。然后，我们填补了现有知识的空白，通过对logit级梯度的分析提供了这种行为的解释：当预测正确时，LS会更大幅度地抑制最大logit值；而当预测错误时，则较少抑制。这一分析揭示了先前报道的实验结果，即强大的分类器在SC任务中表现不佳的原因。接着，我们展示了后处理logit归一化在恢复由LS导致的SC性能损失方面的有效性。最后，基于我们的梯度分析，再次解释了为何这样的归一化能够有效提高SC性能。

[No.16] OCCAM: Towards Cost-Efficient and Accuracy-Aware Classification Inference
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: classif
标题: OCCAM：面向成本效率和精度感知的分类推理
类别: optimization
作者: Dujian Ding;Bicheng Xu;Laks V. S. Lakshmanan;
机构: University of British Columbia;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30510
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.04508
英摘: Classification tasks play a fundamental role in various applications, spanning domains such as healthcare, natural language processing and computer vision. With the growing popularity and capacity of machine learning models, people can easily access trained classifiers as a service online or offline. However, model use comes with a cost and classifiers of higher capacity (such as large foundation models) usually incur higher inference costs. To harness the respective strengths of different classifiers, we propose a principled approach, OCCAM, to compute the best classifier assignment strategy over classification queries (termed as the optimal model portfolio) so that the aggregated accuracy is maximized, under user-specified cost budgets. Our approach uses an unbiased and low-variance accuracy estimator and effectively computes the optimal solution by solving an integer linear programming problem. On a variety of real-world datasets, OCCAM achieves 40% cost reduction with little to no accuracy drop.
中摘: 分类任务在各种应用中发挥着基础性作用，涵盖医疗保健、自然语言处理和计算机视觉等领域。随着机器学习模型 popularity 和容量的增长，人们可以轻松地在线或离线获取训练好的分类器作为服务。然而，使用模型会带来成本，并且容量更大的分类器（如大型基础模型）通常会产生更高的推理成本。为了充分发挥不同分类器的优势，我们提出了一种名为 OCCAM 的原则性方法，用于根据分类查询（称为最优模型组合）计算最佳分类器分配策略，从而在用户指定的成本预算下最大化聚合准确率。我们的方法使用无偏且低方差的准确率估计器，并通过求解整数线性规划问题有效地计算出最优解。在多种真实世界数据集上，OCCAM 实现了高达 40% 的成本减少，同时几乎没有任何准确率下降。

[No.20] Learning the Optimal Stopping for Early Classification within Finite Horizons via Sequential Probability Ratio Test
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: classif
标题: 通过序贯概率比检验在有限时间窗内学习早期分类的最佳停止时间
类别: learning on time series and dynamical systems
作者: Akinori F. Ebihara;Taiki Miyagawa;Kazuyuki Sakurai;Hitoshi Imaoka;
机构: NEC Corporation;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/29607
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.18059
英摘: Time-sensitive machine learning benefits from Sequential Probability Ratio Test (SPRT), which provides an optimal stopping time for early classification of time series. However, in finite horizon scenarios, where input lengths are finite, determining the optimal stopping rule becomes computationally intensive due to the need for backward induction, limiting practical applicability. We thus introduce FIRMBOUND, an SPRT-based framework that efficiently estimates the solution to backward induction from training data, bridging the gap between optimal stopping theory and real-world deployment. It employs density ratio estimation and convex function learning to provide statistically consistent estimators for sufficient statistic and conditional expectation, both essential for solving backward induction; consequently, FIRMBOUND minimizes Bayes risk to reach optimality. Additionally, we present a faster alternative using Gaussian process regression, which significantly reduces training time while retaining low deployment overhead, albeit with potential compromise in statistical consistency. Experiments across independent and identically distributed (i.i.d.), non-i.i.d., binary, multiclass, synthetic, and real-world datasets show that FIRMBOUND achieves optimalities in the sense of Bayes risk and speed-accuracy tradeoff. Furthermore, it advances the tradeoff boundary toward optimality when possible and reduces decision-time variance, ensuring reliable decision-making. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/Akinori-F-Ebihara/FIRMBOUND
中摘: 时间敏感的机器学习可以从序贯概率比检验（SPRT）中受益，SPRT为时间序列的早期分类提供了最优停止时间。然而，在输入长度有限的有限时段场景中，确定最优停止规则变得计算密集，这是因为需要进行反向归纳，这限制了其实用性。因此，我们引入了FIRMBOUND框架，这是一种基于SPRT的方法，能够高效地从训练数据中估计反向归纳的解，从而弥合了最优停止理论与实际部署之间的差距。FIRMBOUND采用密度比率估计和凸函数学习来提供统计上一致的充分统计量和条件期望的估计器，这两个估计器对于解决反向归纳问题至关重要；因此，FIRMBOUND能够最小化贝叶斯风险以达到最优性。此外，我们还提出了一种更快的替代方案，使用高斯过程回归，这种方法显著减少了训练时间，并且保留了较低的部署开销，尽管可能会在统计一致性方面有所妥协。实验表明，FIRMBOUND在独立同分布（i.i.d.）、非独立同分布（non-i.i.d.）、二分类、多分类、合成数据集和真实世界数据集上都能实现贝叶斯风险和速度-准确性的最优性。进一步而言，它能够在可能的情况下推动贸易-off边界向最优性靠近，并降低决策时间的方差，确保可靠的决策制定。代码已公开发布在https://github.com/Akinori-F-Ebihara/FIRMBOUND
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.21] U-Nets as Belief Propagation: Efficient Classification, Denoising, and Diffusion in Generative Hierarchical Models
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: classif
标题: U-Net作为一种信念传播：在生成性分层模型中高效分类、去噪和扩散
类别: learning theory
作者: Song Mei;
机构: University of California, Berkeley;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28085
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.18444
英摘: U-Nets are among the most widely used architectures in computer vision, renowned for their exceptional performance in applications such as image segmentation, denoising, and diffusion modeling. However, a theoretical explanation of the U-Net architecture design has not yet been fully established.
  This paper introduces a novel interpretation of the U-Net architecture by studying certain generative hierarchical models, which are tree-structured graphical models extensively utilized in both language and image domains. With their encoder-decoder structure, long skip connections, and pooling and up-sampling layers, we demonstrate how U-Nets can naturally implement the belief propagation denoising algorithm in such generative hierarchical models, thereby efficiently approximating the denoising functions. This leads to an efficient sample complexity bound for learning the denoising function using U-Nets within these models. Additionally, we discuss the broader implications of these findings for diffusion models in generative hierarchical models. We also demonstrate that the conventional architecture of convolutional neural networks (ConvNets) is ideally suited for classification tasks within these models. This offers a unified view of the roles of ConvNets and U-Nets, highlighting the versatility of generative hierarchical models in modeling complex data distributions across language and image domains.
中摘: U-网络是计算机视觉领域中最广泛使用的架构之一，以其在图像分割、去噪和扩散建模等应用中的卓越性能而闻名。然而，U-网络架构设计的理论解释尚未完全建立。

本文通过研究某些生成分层模型，对U-网络架构进行了新的解释，这些生成分层模型是语言和图像领域中广泛使用的树状结构图形模型。借助其编码器-解码器结构、长跳连接以及下采样和上采样层，我们展示了U-网络如何自然地在这些生成分层模型中实现信念传播去噪算法，从而高效地逼近去噪函数。这导致了在这些模型中使用U-网络学习去噪函数的有效样本复杂度边界。此外，我们还讨论了这些发现对于生成分层模型中的扩散模型更广泛的含义。我们还证明了传统的卷积神经网络（ConvNets）架构在这些模型内的分类任务中最为合适。这提供了一种统一的观点，即ConvNets和U-网络的角色，并突显了生成分层模型在语言和图像领域中建模复杂数据分布的灵活性。

[No.22] Intermediate Layer Classifiers for OOD generalization
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: classif
标题: 面向OOD泛化的中间层分类器
类别: transfer learning, meta learning, and lifelong learning
作者: Arnas Uselis;Seong Joon Oh;
机构: ;Eberhard Karls University of Tübingen;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30537
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.05461
英摘: Deep classifiers are known to be sensitive to data distribution shifts, primarily due to their reliance on spurious correlations in training data. It has been suggested that these classifiers can still find useful features in the network's last layer that hold up under such shifts. In this work, we question the use of last-layer representations for out-of-distribution (OOD) generalisation and explore the utility of intermediate layers. To this end, we introduce \textit{Intermediate Layer Classifiers} (ILCs). We discover that intermediate layer representations frequently offer substantially better generalisation than those from the penultimate layer. In many cases, zero-shot OOD generalisation using earlier-layer representations approaches the few-shot performance of retraining on penultimate layer representations. This is confirmed across multiple datasets, architectures, and types of distribution shifts. Our analysis suggests that intermediate layers are less sensitive to distribution shifts compared to the penultimate layer. These findings highlight the importance of understanding how information is distributed across network layers and its role in OOD generalisation, while also pointing to the limits of penultimate layer representation utility. Code is available at https://github.com/oshapio/intermediate-layer-generalization
中摘: 深层分类器被认为对数据分布的变化非常敏感，主要是因为它们在训练数据中依赖于虚假的相关性。有研究表明，这些分类器仍然可以在网络的最后一层找到一些有用的特征，即使在这样的变化下也能保持有效性。在这项工作中，我们质疑最后一层表示在领域外（OOD）泛化中的使用，并探索中间层表示的实用性。为此，我们引入了“中间层分类器”（ILCs）。我们发现中间层表示经常提供比倒数第二层表示更好的泛化能力。在许多情况下，使用较早层的表示进行零样本OOD泛化可以接近重新训练倒数第二层表示的少量样本性能。这一发现已在多个数据集、架构和类型的数据分布变化中得到验证。我们的分析表明，中间层相对于倒数第二层来说对数据分布变化的敏感性较低。这些发现强调了理解网络各层中信息分布及其在OOD泛化中的作用的重要性，同时也指出了倒数第二层表示实用性的局限性。代码可在https://github.com/oshapio/intermediate-layer-generalization获取。

[No.23] Universal Image Restoration Pre-training via Degradation Classification
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: classif
标题: 通过降级分类进行通用图像恢复预训练
类别: unsupervised, self-supervised, semi-supervised, and supervised representation learning
作者: JiaKui Hu;Lujia Jin;Zhengjian Yao;Yanye Lu;
机构: Peking University;China Mobile;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/29746
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.15510
英摘: This paper proposes the Degradation Classification Pre-Training (DCPT), which enables models to learn how to classify the degradation type of input images for universal image restoration pre-training. Unlike the existing self-supervised pre-training methods, DCPT utilizes the degradation type of the input image as an extremely weak supervision, which can be effortlessly obtained, even intrinsic in all image restoration datasets. DCPT comprises two primary stages. Initially, image features are extracted from the encoder. Subsequently, a lightweight decoder, such as ResNet18, is leveraged to classify the degradation type of the input image solely based on the features extracted in the first stage, without utilizing the input image. The encoder is pre-trained with a straightforward yet potent DCPT, which is used to address universal image restoration and achieve outstanding performance. Following DCPT, both convolutional neural networks (CNNs) and transformers demonstrate performance improvements, with gains of up to 2.55 dB in the 10D all-in-one restoration task and 6.53 dB in the mixed degradation scenarios. Moreover, previous self-supervised pretraining methods, such as masked image modeling, discard the decoder after pre-training, while our DCPT utilizes the pre-trained parameters more effectively. This superiority arises from the degradation classifier acquired during DCPT, which facilitates transfer learning between models of identical architecture trained on diverse degradation types. Source code and models are available at https://github.com/MILab-PKU/dcpt.
中摘: 本文提出了退化分类预训练（DCPT），使模型能够学习如何对输入图像的退化类型进行分类，从而实现通用图像恢复预训练。与现有的自监督预训练方法不同，DCPT利用输入图像的退化类型作为极其弱的监督信号，这种信号甚至可以在所有图像恢复数据集中自然获得。DCPT包括两个主要阶段。首先，从编码器提取图像特征。然后，使用轻量级解码器（如ResNet18）仅基于第一阶段提取的特征来分类输入图像的退化类型，而不使用输入图像本身。DCPT通过一个简单而强大的预训练过程对编码器进行预训练，该过程用于解决通用图像恢复问题并取得卓越性能。在完成DCPT后，卷积神经网络（CNNs）和变压器均显示出性能提升，在10D全能恢复任务中，提升幅度可达2.55 dB，在混合退化场景中，提升幅度可达6.53 dB。此外，与之前的自监督预训练方法（如掩码图像建模）不同，我们的DCPT会在预训练后更有效地利用解码器参数。这一优势源于DCPT过程中获得的退化分类器，它有助于相同架构但在不同退化类型上训练的模型之间的迁移学习。源代码和模型可在https://github.com/MILab-PKU/dcpt获取。

[No.27] BEEM: Boosting Performance of Early Exit DNNs using Multi-Exit Classifiers as Experts
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: classif
标题: BEEM：使用多出口分类器作为专家提升早期退出DNN性能
类别: other topics in machine learning (i.e., none of the above)
作者: Divya Jyoti Bajpai;Manjesh Kumar Hanawal;
机构: Indian Institute of Technology, Bombay;Indian Institute of Technology Bombay;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30371
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.00745
英摘: Early Exit (EE) techniques have emerged as a means to reduce inference latency in Deep Neural Networks (DNNs). The latency improvement and accuracy in these techniques crucially depend on the criteria used to make exit decisions. We propose a new decision criterion where exit classifiers are treated as experts BEEM and aggregate their confidence scores. The confidence scores are aggregated only if neighbouring experts are consistent in prediction as the samples pass through them, thus capturing their ensemble effect. A sample exits when the aggregated confidence value exceeds a threshold. The threshold is set using the error rates of the intermediate exits aiming to surpass the performance of conventional DNN inference. Experimental results on the COCO dataset for Image captioning and GLUE datasets for various language tasks demonstrate that our method enhances the performance of state-of-the-art EE methods, achieving improvements in speed-up by a factor 1.5x to 2.1x. When compared to the final layer, its accuracy is comparable in harder Image Captioning and improves in the easier language tasks. The source code for this work is publicly available at https://github.com/Div290/BEEM1/tree/main
中摘: 早期退出（Early Exit, EE）技术已经作为一种减少深度神经网络（DNNs）推理延迟的方法而出现。这些技术中的延迟改进和准确性关键取决于用于做出退出决策的标准。我们提出了一种新的决策标准，其中退出分类器被视为专家BEEM，并整合它们的信心分数。只有当相邻的专家在预测上保持一致时，信心分数才会被整合，从而捕捉到它们的ensemble效果。样本在聚合的信心值超过阈值时退出。该阈值通过中间退出的错误率来设置，目标是超越传统DNN推理的性能。在Image Captioning数据集上的COCO实验结果以及各种语言任务上的GLUE数据集实验表明，我们的方法提升了最先进的EE方法的性能，实现了1.5倍至2.1倍的速度提升。与最后一层相比，在更困难的图像字幕任务上其准确性相当，而在更简单的语言任务上则有所提高。这项工作的源代码可以在https://github.com/Div290/BEEM1/tree/main 公开获取。

[No.28] Convergence and Implicit Bias of Gradient Descent on Continual Linear Classification
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: classif
标题: 连续线性分类中梯度下降的收敛性和隐式偏置
类别: transfer learning, meta learning, and lifelong learning
作者: Hyunji Jung;Hanseul Cho;Chulhee Yun;
机构: Pohang University of Science and Technology;Google;Korea Advanced Institute of Science and Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30455
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.12712
英摘: We study continual learning on multiple linear classification tasks by sequentially running gradient descent (GD) for a fixed budget of iterations per task. When all tasks are jointly linearly separable and are presented in a cyclic/random order, we show the directional convergence of the trained linear classifier to the joint (offline) max-margin solution. This is surprising because GD training on a single task is implicitly biased towards the individual max-margin solution for the task, and the direction of the joint max-margin solution can be largely different from these individual solutions. Additionally, when tasks are given in a cyclic order, we present a non-asymptotic analysis on cycle-averaged forgetting, revealing that (1) alignment between tasks is indeed closely tied to catastrophic forgetting and backward knowledge transfer and (2) the amount of forgetting vanishes to zero as the cycle repeats. Lastly, we analyze the case where the tasks are no longer jointly separable and show that the model trained in a cyclic order converges to the unique minimum of the joint loss function.
中摘: 我们通过为每个任务固定预算的迭代顺序运行梯度下降（GD）来研究在多个线性分类任务上的持续学习。当所有任务联合线性可分且以循环/随机顺序呈现时，我们展示了训练的线性分类器的方向收敛于联合（离线）最大边缘解。这令人惊讶，因为单个任务上的GD训练会隐式地偏向于该任务的个体最大边缘解，而联合最大边缘解的方向可能与这些个体解相差很大。此外，当任务以循环顺序给出时，我们对循环平均遗忘进行了非渐近分析，揭示了以下两点：（1）任务之间的对齐确实与灾难性遗忘和反向知识转移密切相关；（2）随着循环的重复，遗忘的程度会消失至零。最后，我们分析了任务不再联合可分的情况，并展示了按循环顺序训练的模型会收敛到联合损失函数的唯一最小值。

[No.30] MLPs Learn In-Context on Regression and Classification Tasks
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: classif
标题: MLPs 在回归和分类任务中的增量学习
类别: transfer learning, meta learning, and lifelong learning
作者: William Lingxiao Tong;Cengiz Pehlevan;
机构: ;Harvard University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/29924
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.15618
英摘: In-context learning (ICL), the remarkable ability to solve a task from only input exemplars, is often assumed to be a unique hallmark of Transformer models. By examining commonly employed synthetic ICL tasks, we demonstrate that multi-layer perceptrons (MLPs) can also learn in-context. Moreover, MLPs, and the closely related MLP-Mixer models, learn in-context comparably with Transformers under the same compute budget in this setting. We further show that MLPs outperform Transformers on a series of classical tasks from psychology designed to test relational reasoning, which are closely related to in-context classification. These results underscore a need for studying in-context learning beyond attention-based architectures, while also challenging prior arguments against MLPs' ability to solve relational tasks. Altogether, our results highlight the unexpected competence of MLPs in a synthetic setting, and support the growing interest in all-MLP alternatives to Transformer architectures. It remains unclear how MLPs perform against Transformers at scale on real-world tasks, and where a performance gap may originate. We encourage further exploration of these architectures in more complex settings to better understand the potential comparative advantage of attention-based schemes.
中摘: 在上下文中的学习（ICL），即仅从输入示例中解决任务的能力，通常被认为是Transformer模型的独特标志。通过检查常用的人工合成ICL任务，我们发现多层感知机（MLPs）也能进行在上下文中的学习。此外，在相同的计算预算下，MLPs和相关的MLP-Mixer模型在这一设置中能够与Transformer模型相媲美地进行在上下文中的学习。我们进一步展示了，在心理学设计的经典任务中，这些任务旨在测试关系推理能力，而这些任务与在上下文分类密切相关时，MLPs的表现优于Transformer模型。这些结果强调了在注意力架构之外研究在上下文学习的重要性，同时也反驳了之前关于MLPs无法解决关系任务的说法。总之，我们的结果突显了MLPs在人工合成环境中出乎意料的能力，并支持了对Transformer架构进行全面MLP替代方案的兴趣。目前尚不清楚MLPs在大规模的真实世界任务中如何与Transformer模型竞争，以及性能差距可能源自何处。我们鼓励进一步探索这些架构在更复杂环境中的表现，以更好地理解基于注意力方案的潜在比较优势。

[No.31] Distribution-Specific Agnostic Conditional Classification With Halfspaces
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: classif
标题: 基于半空间的分布特定无差别的条件分类
类别: learning theory
作者: Jizhou Huang;Brendan Juba;
机构: Washington University in St. Louis;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30049
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.00172
英摘: We study ``selective'' or ``conditional'' classification problems under an agnostic setting. Classification tasks commonly focus on modeling the relationship between features and categories that captures the vast majority of data. In contrast to common machine learning frameworks, conditional classification intends to model such relationships only on a subset of the data defined by some selection rule. Most work on conditional classification either solves the problem in a realizable setting or does not guarantee the error is bounded compared to an optimal solution. In this work, we consider selective/conditional classification by sparse linear classifiers for subsets defined by halfspaces, and give both positive as well as negative results for Gaussian feature distributions. On the positive side, we present the first PAC-learning algorithm for homogeneous halfspace selectors with error guarantee $\bigO*{\sqrt{\mathrm{opt}}}$, where $\mathrm{opt}$ is the smallest conditional classification error over the given class of classifiers and homogeneous halfspaces. On the negative side, we find that, under cryptographic assumptions, approximating the conditional classification loss within a small additive error is computationally hard even under Gaussian distribution. We prove that approximating conditional classification is at least as hard as approximating agnostic classification in both additive and multiplicative form.
中摘: 我们在假设性的设定下研究“选择性”或“条件性”的分类问题。常见的分类任务通常关注建模特征与类别之间的关系，以捕捉大部分数据。与常见的机器学习框架不同，条件性分类旨在仅在由某些选择规则定义的数据子集上建模这种关系。大多数关于条件性分类的工作要么是在可实现的设定下解决这个问题，要么无法保证错误相对于最优解是被限制的。在这项工作中，我们考虑了由半空间定义的子集上的稀疏线性分类器的选择性/条件性分类，并分别对高斯特征分布给出了正向和负向的结果。在正向方面，我们提出了第一个PAC学习算法，用于同质半空间选择器，其误差保证为$\bigO*{\sqrt{\mathrm{opt}}}$，其中$\mathrm{opt}$是最小的给定分类器类和同质半空间下的条件性分类误差。在负向方面，我们发现，在某些密码学假设下，即使在高斯分布下，近似条件性分类损失在一个小的加性误差范围内都是计算上困难的。我们证明了，近似条件性分类至少与在加性和乘性形式下近似假设性分类一样困难。

[No.33] ConcreTizer: Model Inversion Attack via Occupancy Classification and Dispersion Control for 3D Point Cloud Restoration
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: classif
标题: ConcreTizer：基于 occupancy 分类和分散控制的 3D 点云恢复模型反转攻击
类别: alignment, fairness, safety, privacy, and societal considerations
作者: Youngseok Kim;Sunwook Hwang;Hyung-Sin Kim;Saewoong Bahk;
机构: Seoul National University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/32098
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.06986
英摘: The growing use of 3D point cloud data in autonomous vehicles (AVs) has raised serious privacy concerns, particularly due to the sensitive information that can be extracted from 3D data. While model inversion attacks have been widely studied in the context of 2D data, their application to 3D point clouds remains largely unexplored. To fill this gap, we present the first in-depth study of model inversion attacks aimed at restoring 3D point cloud scenes. Our analysis reveals the unique challenges, the inherent sparsity of 3D point clouds and the ambiguity between empty and non-empty voxels after voxelization, which are further exacerbated by the dispersion of non-empty voxels across feature extractor layers. To address these challenges, we introduce ConcreTizer, a simple yet effective model inversion attack designed specifically for voxel-based 3D point cloud data. ConcreTizer incorporates Voxel Occupancy Classification to distinguish between empty and non-empty voxels and Dispersion-Controlled Supervision to mitigate non-empty voxel dispersion. Extensive experiments on widely used 3D feature extractors and benchmark datasets, such as KITTI and Waymo, demonstrate that ConcreTizer concretely restores the original 3D point cloud scene from disrupted 3D feature data. Our findings highlight both the vulnerability of 3D data to inversion attacks and the urgent need for robust defense strategies.
中摘: 自动驾驶车辆（AVs）中3D点云数据的日益使用引发了严重的隐私担忧，尤其是因为可以从3D数据中提取出敏感信息。尽管在2D数据的背景下已经广泛研究了模型反转攻击，但其在3D点云中的应用仍然鲜有探索。为填补这一空白，我们首次对旨在恢复3D点云场景的模型反转攻击进行了深入研究。我们的分析揭示了3D点云所面临的独特挑战，包括3D点云固有的稀疏性和体素化后空体素和非空体素之间的模糊性，这些挑战进一步加剧了特征提取层中非空体素的分散性。为了应对这些挑战，我们引入了ConcreTizer，这是一种简单而有效的针对基于体素的3D点云数据设计的模型反转攻击方法。ConcreTizer结合了体素占有分类，以区分空体素和非空体素，并采用了分散控制监督来减轻非空体素的分散性。在广泛使用的3D特征提取器和基准数据集（如KITTI和Waymo）上的大量实验表明，ConcreTizer能够具体地从破坏后的3D特征数据中恢复原始的3D点云场景。我们的发现不仅突显了3D数据对抗反转攻击的脆弱性，还强调了迫切需要开发稳健的防御策略。

[No.34] Predicate Hierarchies Improve Few-Shot State Classification
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: classif
标题: 谓词层次结构提高少样本状态分类
类别: applications to computer vision, audio, language, and other modalities
作者: Emily Jin;Joy Hsu;Jiajun Wu;

类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28492
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.12481
英摘: State classification of objects and their relations is core to many long-horizon tasks, particularly in robot planning and manipulation. However, the combinatorial explosion of possible object-predicate combinations, coupled with the need to adapt to novel real-world environments, makes it a desideratum for state classification models to generalize to novel queries with few examples. To this end, we propose PHIER, which leverages predicate hierarchies to generalize effectively in few-shot scenarios. PHIER uses an object-centric scene encoder, self-supervised losses that infer semantic relations between predicates, and a hyperbolic distance metric that captures hierarchical structure; it learns a structured latent space of image-predicate pairs that guides reasoning over state classification queries. We evaluate PHIER in the CALVIN and BEHAVIOR robotic environments and show that PHIER significantly outperforms existing methods in few-shot, out-of-distribution state classification, and demonstrates strong zero- and few-shot generalization from simulated to real-world tasks. Our results demonstrate that leveraging predicate hierarchies improves performance on state classification tasks with limited data.
中摘: 物体及其关系的状态分类是许多长期任务的核心，特别是在机器人规划和操作方面。然而，可能的物体谓词组合的组合爆炸以及需要适应新的真实环境，使得状态分类模型能够在少量示例的情况下有效泛化到新颖查询成为必要。为此，我们提出了PHIER，它利用谓词层次结构在少样本场景中有效泛化。PHIER采用以对象为中心的场景编码器、自我监督损失来推断谓词之间的语义关系，并使用双曲距离度量来捕捉层次结构；它学习一个结构化的图像-谓词对的潜在空间，该空间引导对状态分类查询的推理。我们在CALVIN和BEHAVIOR机器人环境中评估了PHIER，并展示了PHIER在少样本、分布外状态分类方面显著优于现有方法，并且从模拟到真实世界的任务中表现出强大的零样本和少样本泛化能力。我们的结果表明，利用谓词层次结构能够提高在有限数据情况下进行状态分类任务的性能。

[No.35] Mining your own secrets: Diffusion Classifier Scores for Continual Personalization of Text-to-Image Diffusion Models
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: classif
标题: 挖掘自己的秘密：用于持续个性化文本到图像扩散模型的分类器评分
类别: generative models
作者: Saurav Jha;Shiqi Yang;Masato Ishii;Mengjie Zhao;christian simon;Muhammad Jehanzeb Mirza;Dong Gong;Lina Yao;Shusuke Takahashi;Yuki Mitsufuji;
机构: University of New South Wales;SoftBank Group;Sony Group Corporation;Sony Corporation;Massachusetts Institute of Technology;CSIRO;Tokyo Institute of Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28758
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.00700
英摘: Personalized text-to-image diffusion models have grown popular for their ability to efficiently acquire a new concept from user-defined text descriptions and a few images. However, in the real world, a user may wish to personalize a model on multiple concepts but one at a time, with no access to the data from previous concepts due to storage/privacy concerns. When faced with this continual learning (CL) setup, most personalization methods fail to find a balance between acquiring new concepts and retaining previous ones -- a challenge that continual personalization (CP) aims to solve. Inspired by the successful CL methods that rely on class-specific information for regularization, we resort to the inherent class-conditioned density estimates, also known as diffusion classifier (DC) scores, for continual personalization of text-to-image diffusion models. Namely, we propose using DC scores for regularizing the parameter-space and function-space of text-to-image diffusion models, to achieve continual personalization. Using several diverse evaluation setups, datasets, and metrics, we show that our proposed regularization-based CP methods outperform the state-of-the-art C-LoRA, and other baselines. Finally, by operating in the replay-free CL setup and on low-rank adapters, our method incurs zero storage and parameter overhead, respectively, over the state-of-the-art. Our project page: https://srvcodes.github.io/continual_personalization/
中摘: 个性化文本到图像扩散模型因其能够高效地从用户定义的文本描述和少量图像中获取新概念而变得流行。然而，在现实世界中，用户可能希望一次仅个性化一个模型于多个概念，但由于存储/隐私问题，他们无法访问之前概念的数据。面对这种连续学习（CL）的设置，大多数个性化方法在获取新概念和保留旧概念之间未能找到平衡——这是连续个性化（CP）要解决的挑战。受依赖于类特定信息进行正则化的成功连续学习方法的启发，我们采用内生的类条件密度估计，即扩散分类器（DC）分数，来实现文本到图像扩散模型的连续个性化。具体而言，我们建议使用DC分数来正则化文本到图像扩散模型的参数空间和函数空间，以实现连续个性化。通过多种多样化的评估设置、数据集和度量标准，我们展示了我们提出的基于正则化的连续个性化方法优于当前最先进的C-LoRA和其他基线方法。最后，我们的方法在无回放连续学习设置和低秩适配器上运行，分别不产生存储和参数开销，优于当前最先进的方法。我们的项目页面：https://srvcodes.github.io/continual_personalization/

[No.36] No Training, No Problem: Rethinking Classifier-Free Guidance for Diffusion Models
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: classif
标题: 无训练，无问题：重新思考扩散模型中的分类器免费引导
类别: generative models
作者: Seyedmorteza Sadat;Manuel Kansy;Otmar Hilliges;Romann M. Weber;
机构: ;ETH Zurich;Disney Research;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/29126
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.02687
英摘: Classifier-free guidance (CFG) has become the standard method for enhancing the quality of conditional diffusion models. However, employing CFG requires either training an unconditional model alongside the main diffusion model or modifying the training procedure by periodically inserting a null condition. There is also no clear extension of CFG to unconditional models. In this paper, we revisit the core principles of CFG and introduce a new method, independent condition guidance (ICG), which provides the benefits of CFG without the need for any special training procedures. Our approach streamlines the training process of conditional diffusion models and can also be applied during inference on any pre-trained conditional model. Additionally, by leveraging the time-step information encoded in all diffusion networks, we propose an extension of CFG, called time-step guidance (TSG), which can be applied to any diffusion model, including unconditional ones. Our guidance techniques are easy to implement and have the same sampling cost as CFG. Through extensive experiments, we demonstrate that ICG matches the performance of standard CFG across various conditional diffusion models. Moreover, we show that TSG improves generation quality in a manner similar to CFG, without relying on any conditional information.
中摘: 分类器无关引导（CFG）已成为增强条件扩散模型质量的标准方法。然而，使用CFG需要在主要扩散模型旁边训练一个无条件模型，或者通过定期插入空条件来修改训练程序。此外，CFG也没有明确的方法应用于无条件模型。在本文中，我们重新审视了CFG的核心原则，并引入了一种新的方法——独立条件引导（ICG），该方法无需任何特殊训练程序即可提供CFG的好处。我们的方法简化了条件扩散模型的训练过程，并且也可以在任何预训练的条件模型上进行推理时应用。另外，通过利用所有扩散网络中编码的时间步信息，我们提出了一种CFG的扩展方法——时间步引导（TSG），它可以应用于任何扩散模型，包括无条件模型。我们的引导技术易于实现，并且与CFG具有相同的采样成本。通过广泛的实验，我们证明了ICG在各种条件扩散模型中的性能与标准CFG相当。此外，我们展示了TSG可以以类似于CFG的方式提高生成质量，而无需依赖任何条件信息。

[No.37] Bonsai: Gradient-free Graph Condensation for Node Classification
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: classif
标题:  bonsai: 无梯度图凝练算法在节点分类中的应用
类别: learning on graphs and other geometries & topologies
作者: Mridul Gupta;Samyak Jain;Vansh Ramani;HARIPRASAD KODAMANA;Sayan Ranu;
机构: Indian Institute of Technology Delhi;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30924
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.17579
英摘: Graph condensation has emerged as a promising avenue to enable scalable training of GNNs by compressing the training dataset while preserving essential graph characteristics. Our study uncovers significant shortcomings in current graph condensation techniques. First, the majority of the algorithms paradoxically require training on the full dataset to perform condensation. Second, due to their gradient-emulating approach, these methods require fresh condensation for any change in hyperparameters or GNN architecture, limiting their flexibility and reusability. Finally, they fail to achieve substantial size reduction due to synthesizing fully-connected, edge-weighted graphs. To address these challenges, we present Bonsai, a novel graph condensation method empowered by the observation that \textit{computation trees} form the fundamental processing units of message-passing GNNs. Bonsai condenses datasets by encoding a careful selection of \textit{exemplar} trees that maximize the representation of all computation trees in the training set. This unique approach imparts Bonsai as the first linear-time, model-agnostic graph condensation algorithm for node classification that outperforms existing baselines across $7$ real-world datasets on accuracy, while being $22$ times faster on average. Bonsai is grounded in rigorous mathematical guarantees on the adopted approximation strategies making it robust to GNN architectures, datasets, and parameters.
中摘: 图凝聚已 emerges 作为通过压缩训练数据集以同时保留关键图特性来实现大规模训练 GNN 的有前途的方法。我们的研究揭示了当前图凝聚技术的重大不足。首先，大多数算法反常识地需要在完整数据集上进行训练才能执行凝聚。其次，由于它们采用梯度模拟的方法，这些方法要求每次更改超参数或 GNN 架构时都需要重新进行凝聚，这限制了它们的灵活性和可重用性。最后，由于生成完全连接且带权重的图，它们未能实现显著的规模缩减。为了解决这些挑战，我们提出了 Bonsai，一种新颖的图凝聚方法，该方法基于一个观察：计算树构成了消息传递 GNN 的基本处理单元。Bonsai 通过编码训练集中所有计算树的最大代表性的典范树来压缩数据集。这一独特的方法赋予 Bonsai 成为首个线性时间、模型无关且在准确率方面优于现有基线算法的节点分类图凝聚算法的能力，同时其平均速度比现有方法快 22 倍。Bonsai 的设计基于所采用近似策略的严格数学保证，使其对 GNN 架构、数据集和参数具有鲁棒性。

[No.38] Transformer Learns Optimal Variable Selection in Group-Sparse Classification
来源: ICLR 2025

英摘: Transformers have demonstrated remarkable success across various applications. However, the success of transformers have not been understood in theory. In this work, we give a case study of how transformers can be trained to learn a classic statistical model with "group sparsity", where the input variables form multiple groups, and the label only depends on the variables from one of the groups. We theoretically demonstrate that, a one-layer transformer trained by gradient descent can correctly leverage the attention mechanism to select variables, disregarding irrelevant ones and focusing on those beneficial for classification. We also demonstrate that a well-pretrained one-layer transformer can be adapted to new downstream tasks to achieve good prediction accuracy with a limited number of samples. Our study sheds light on how transformers effectively learn structured data.
中摘: 变压器在各种应用中已经展现了非凡的成功，然而其成功的原因尚未从理论上得到充分理解。在这项工作中，我们提供了一个案例研究，说明变压器如何被训练以学习具有“组稀疏性”的经典统计模型，其中输入变量分为多个组，而标签仅依赖于其中一个组的变量。我们理论证明了一层变压器通过梯度下降训练后可以正确利用注意力机制来选择对分类有益的变量，忽略无关的变量。此外，我们也证明了一层变压器经过良好预训练后可以适应新的下游任务，在少量样本的情况下实现良好的预测精度。我们的研究揭示了变压器如何有效地学习结构化数据。

[No.39] CLIPure: Purification in Latent Space via CLIP for Adversarially Robust Zero-Shot Classification
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: classif
标题: CLIPure：通过CLIP在潜在空间进行净化以实现对抗性鲁棒的零样本分类
类别: alignment, fairness, safety, privacy, and societal considerations
作者: Mingkun Zhang;Keping Bi;Wei Chen;Jiafeng Guo;Xueqi Cheng;
机构: Chinese Academy of Sciences;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/29543
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.18176
英摘: In this paper, we aim to build an adversarially robust zero-shot image classifier. We ground our work on CLIP, a vision-language pre-trained encoder model that can perform zero-shot classification by matching an image with text prompts ``a photo of a <class-name>.''. Purification is the path we choose since it does not require adversarial training on specific attack types and thus can cope with any foreseen attacks. We then formulate purification risk as the KL divergence between the joint distributions of the purification process of denoising the adversarial samples and the attack process of adding perturbations to benign samples, through bidirectional Stochastic Differential Equations (SDEs). The final derived results inspire us to explore purification in the multi-modal latent space of CLIP. We propose two variants for our CLIPure approach: CLIPure-Diff which models the likelihood of images' latent vectors with the DiffusionPrior module in DaLLE-2 (modeling the generation process of CLIP's latent vectors), and CLIPure-Cos which models the likelihood with the cosine similarity between the embeddings of an image and ``a photo of a.''. As far as we know, CLIPure is the first purification method in multi-modal latent space and CLIPure-Cos is the first purification method that is not based on generative models, which substantially improves defense efficiency. We conducted extensive experiments on CIFAR-10, ImageNet, and 13 datasets that previous CLIP-based defense methods used for evaluating zero-shot classification robustness. Results show that CLIPure boosts the SOTA robustness by a large margin, e.g., from 71.7% to 91.1% on CIFAR10, from 59.6% to 72.6% on ImageNet, and 108% relative improvements of average robustness on the 13 datasets over previous SOTA. The code is available at https://github.com/TMLResearchGroup-CAS/CLIPure.
中摘: 本文旨在构建一个对抗鲁棒的零样本图像分类器。我们基于CLIP，这是一种可以利用“一张<类名>的照片”这类文本提示将图像与之匹配来进行零样本分类的预训练视觉-语言编码模型。我们选择了净化路径，因为它不需要针对特定攻击类型进行对抗训练，因此可以应对任何预期的攻击。然后，我们通过双向随机微分方程（SDE）将净化风险定义为去噪对抗样本和向良性样本添加扰动的攻击过程之间的联合分布的KL散度。最终得出的结果启发我们探索CLIP多模态潜空间中的净化方法。我们为CLIPure方法提出了两种变体：CLIPure-Diff，它使用DaLLE-2中的DiffusionPrior模块来建模图像潜向量的概率分布（模拟CLIP潜向量生成过程），以及CLIPure-Cos，它使用图像嵌入与其“一张<类名>的照片”之间的余弦相似性来建模概率分布。据我们所知，CLIPure是第一个在多模态潜空间中使用的净化方法，而CLIPure-Cos则是第一个不基于生成模型的净化方法，这显著提高了防御效率。我们在CIFAR-10、ImageNet以及之前用于评估零样本分类鲁棒性的13个数据集上进行了广泛的实验。结果显示，CLIPure大幅提升了现有的最佳表现，例如，在CIFAR10上的提升从71.7%到91.1%，在ImageNet上的提升从59.6%到72.6%，并且在13个数据集上的平均鲁棒性相对改进达到了108%。相关代码可在https://github.com/TMLResearchGroup-CAS/CLIPure获取。

[No.41] Trained Transformer Classifiers Generalize and Exhibit Benign Overfitting In-Context
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: classif
标题: 训练好的变换器分类器在上下文内表现出泛化能力和良性过拟合现象
类别: learning theory
作者: Spencer Frei;Gal Vardi;
机构: Google;Weizmann Institute of Science;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28608
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.01774
英摘: Transformers have the capacity to act as supervised learning algorithms: by properly encoding a set of labeled training ("in-context") examples and an unlabeled test example into an input sequence of vectors of the same dimension, the forward pass of the transformer can produce predictions for that unlabeled test example. A line of recent work has shown that when linear transformers are pre-trained on random instances for linear regression tasks, these trained transformers make predictions using an algorithm similar to that of ordinary least squares. In this work, we investigate the behavior of linear transformers trained on random linear classification tasks. Via an analysis of the implicit regularization of gradient descent, we characterize how many pre-training tasks and in-context examples are needed for the trained transformer to generalize well at test-time. We further show that in some settings, these trained transformers can exhibit "benign overfitting in-context": when in-context examples are corrupted by label flipping noise, the transformer memorizes all of its in-context examples (including those with noisy labels) yet still generalizes near-optimally for clean test examples.
中摘: 变压器有能力作为监督学习算法工作：通过将一组标记的训练（“上下文”）示例和一个未标记的测试示例正确定码为具有相同维度的向量输入序列，变压器前向传播可以生成对该未标记测试示例的预测。最近的一项研究显示，当线性变压器在随机实例上预训练进行线性回归任务时，这些预训练好的变压器会使用类似于普通最小二乘法的算法来做出预测。在这项工作中，我们研究了在随机线性分类任务上预训练的线性变压器的行为。通过对梯度下降的隐式正则化进行分析，我们刻画了为了使预训练好的变压器在测试时能够很好地泛化，需要多少预训练任务和上下文示例。我们进一步表明，在某些设置下，这些预训练好的变压器可以表现出“良性过拟合上下文”：当上下文示例受到标签翻转噪声的影响时，变压器会记住所有其上下文示例（包括那些带有噪声标签的示例），但在干净的测试示例上仍能近似最优地泛化。

[No.43] Glauber Generative Model: Discrete Diffusion Models via Binary Classification
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: classif
标题: 格拉布伯生成模型：通过二元分类的离散扩散模型
类别: generative models
作者: Harshit Varma;Dheeraj Mysore Nagaraj;Karthikeyan Shanmugam;
机构: Inception Labs;Google;DeepMind;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30199
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.17035
英摘: We introduce the Glauber Generative Model (GGM), a new class of discrete diffusion models, to obtain new samples from a distribution given samples from a discrete space. GGM deploys a discrete Markov chain called the heat bath dynamics (or the Glauber dynamics) to denoise a sequence of noisy tokens to a sample from a joint distribution of discrete tokens. Our novel conceptual framework provides an exact reduction of the task of learning the denoising Markov chain to solving a class of binary classification tasks. More specifically, the model learns to classify a given token in a noisy sequence as signal or noise. In contrast, prior works on discrete diffusion models either solve regression problems to learn importance ratios, or minimize loss functions given by variational approximations. We apply GGM to language modeling and image generation, where images are discretized using image tokenizers like VQGANs. We show that it outperforms existing discrete diffusion models in language generation, and demonstrates strong performance for image generation without using dataset-specific image tokenizers. We also show that our model is capable of performing well in zero-shot control settings like text and image infilling.
中摘: 我们介绍了Glauber生成模型（GGM），这是一种新的离散扩散模型类别，用于从给定离散空间中的样本中获取新的样本。GGM 使用一种称为热浴动力学（或Glauber动力学）的离散马尔可夫链来去噪序列中的噪声标记，使其成为离散标记联合分布的一个样本。我们的新颖概念框架将学习去噪马尔可夫链的任务精确地减少为解决一类二元分类任务。具体来说，该模型学会将给定序列中的一个噪声标记分类为信号或噪声。相比之下，先前的离散扩散模型要么通过解决回归问题来学习重要性比率，要么根据变分近似给出的损失函数进行最小化。我们将GGM应用于语言建模和图像生成，在图像生成方面，使用如VQGAN等图像标记器将图像离散化。我们展示了它在语言生成方面优于现有的离散扩散模型，并且在无需使用特定数据集的图像标记器的情况下，表现出强大的图像生成性能。此外，我们还展示了该模型能够在文本和图像填充等零样本控制设置中表现出色。

[No.46] Boosting Methods for Interval-censored Data with Regression and Classification
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: classif
标题: 区间删失数据的提升方法：回归与分类
类别: other topics in machine learning (i.e., none of the above)
作者: Yuan Bian;Grace Yi;Wenqing He;
机构: Columbia University;University of Western Ontario;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30432
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.17973
英摘: Boosting has garnered significant interest across both machine learning and statistical communities. Traditional boosting algorithms, designed for fully observed random samples, often struggle with real-world problems, particularly with interval-censored data. This type of data is common in survival analysis and time-to-event studies where exact event times are unobserved but fall within known intervals. Effective handling of such data is crucial in fields like medical research, reliability engineering, and social sciences. In this work, we introduce novel nonparametric boosting methods for regression and classification tasks with interval-censored data. Our approaches leverage censoring unbiased transformations to adjust loss functions and impute transformed responses while maintaining model accuracy. Implemented via functional gradient descent, these methods ensure scalability and adaptability. We rigorously establish their theoretical properties, including optimality and mean squared error trade-offs. Our proposed methods not only offer a robust framework for enhancing predictive accuracy in domains where interval-censored data are common but also complement existing work, expanding the applicability of existing boosting techniques. Empirical studies demonstrate robust performance across various finite-sample scenarios, highlighting the practical utility of our approaches.
中摘:  boosting 在机器学习和统计学领域都引起了广泛关注。传统的 boosting 算法设计用于完全观察到的随机样本，但在处理现实世界问题时，尤其是区间截尾数据时，往往会遇到困难。这种类型的数据在生存分析和时间事件研究中很常见，其中具体事件时间未被观察到，但落在已知区间内。有效处理此类数据对于医学研究、可靠性工程和社会科学等领域至关重要。在这项工作中，我们引入了新颖的非参数 boosting 方法，用于回归和分类任务中的区间截尾数据。我们的方法利用截尾数据无偏转换来调整损失函数，并在保持模型准确性的前提下填补转换后的响应值。通过函数梯度下降实现，这些方法确保了可扩展性和适应性。我们严格地建立了它们的理论性质，包括最优性和均方误差权衡。我们提出的方法不仅为常见区间截尾数据的应用领域提供了增强预测准确性的稳健框架，还补充了现有工作，扩大了现有 boosting 技术的应用范围。实证研究表明，我们的方法在各种有限样本场景中表现出稳健性能，突显了其实际应用价值。

[No.47] Provably Safeguarding a Classifier from OOD and Adversarial Samples
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: classif
标题: 证明性地保护分类器免受OOD和 adversarial样本的影响
类别: alignment, fairness, safety, privacy, and societal considerations
作者: Nicolas Atienza;Johanne Cohen;Christophe Labreuche;Michele Sebag;
机构: Université Paris-Saclay;CNRS-LISN;;CNRS;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28554
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.10202
英摘: This paper introduces a novel method, Sample-efficient Probabilistic Detection using Extreme Value Theory (SPADE), which transforms a classifier into an abstaining classifier, offering provable protection against out-of-distribution and adversarial samples. The approach is based on a Generalized Extreme Value (GEV) model of the training distribution in the classifier's latent space, enabling the formal characterization of OOD samples. Interestingly, under mild assumptions, the GEV model also allows for formally characterizing adversarial samples. The abstaining classifier, which rejects samples based on their assessment by the GEV model, provably avoids OOD and adversarial samples. The empirical validation of the approach, conducted on various neural architectures (ResNet, VGG, and Vision Transformer) and medium and large-sized datasets (CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and ImageNet), demonstrates its frugality, stability, and efficiency compared to the state of the art.
中摘: 本文介绍了一种新颖的方法——基于极值理论的样本高效概率检测（SPADE），该方法将分类器转换为弃权分类器，提供了一种可证明地抵御分布外和对抗样本的保护机制。该方法基于分类器潜在空间中的训练分布的广义极值（GEV）模型，使分布外样本能够形式化地进行表征。有趣的是，在温和的假设下，GEV模型还允许形式化地表征对抗样本。基于GEV模型评估而拒绝样本的弃权分类器，能够可证明地避免分布外和对抗样本。在各种神经网络架构（ResNet、VGG和视觉变换器）以及中大型数据集（CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和ImageNet）上的实证验证表明，与现有技术相比，该方法具有节俭性、稳定性和效率。

[No.48] Bayesian WeakS-to-Strong from Text Classification to Generation
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: classif
标题: 贝叶斯弱监督到强监督：从文本分类到生成
类别: alignment, fairness, safety, privacy, and societal considerations
作者: Ziyun Cui;Ziyang Zhang;Guangzhi Sun;Wen Wu;Chao Zhang;
机构: Tsinghua University;University of Cambridge;Shanghai Artificial Intelligence Laboratory;University College London;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28306
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.03199
英摘: Advances in large language models raise the question of how alignment techniques will adapt as models become increasingly complex and humans will only be able to supervise them weakly. Weak-to-Strong mimics such a scenario where weak model supervision attempts to harness the full capabilities of a much stronger model. This work extends Weak-to-Strong to WeakS-to-Strong by exploring an ensemble of weak models which simulate the variability in human opinions. Confidence scores are estimated using a Bayesian approach to guide the WeakS-to-Strong generalization. Furthermore, we extend the application of WeakS-to-Strong from text classification tasks to text generation tasks where more advanced strategies are investigated for supervision. Moreover, direct preference optimization is applied to advance the student model's preference learning, beyond the basic learning framework of teacher forcing. Results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach for the reliability of a strong student model, showing potential for superalignment.
中摘: 大型语言模型的进步引发了如何随着模型变得越来越复杂，人类只能进行弱监督的情况下调整对齐技术的问题。Weak-to-Strong 类似于这样一种场景，即弱模型监督试图利用一个强大得多的模型的全部能力。本研究将 Weak-to-Strong 扩展为 WeakS-to-Strong，通过探索一系列模拟人类意见多样性的弱模型集合来进行。信心分数通过贝叶斯方法估计来指导 WeakS-to-Strong 的泛化。此外，我们还将 WeakS-to-Strong 的应用从文本分类任务扩展到了文本生成任务，在这些任务中，我们调查了更先进的监督策略。同时，直接偏好优化被应用于推进学生模型的偏好学习，超越了基本的教师强迫学习框架。实验结果表明，所提出的方法能够提高强学生模型的可靠性，并展示了超对齐的潜力。

[No.49] Visually Consistent Hierarchical Image Classification
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: classif
标题: 视觉一致的层次化图像分类
类别: unsupervised, self-supervised, semi-supervised, and supervised representation learning
作者: Seulki Park;Youren Zhang;Stella X. Yu;Sara Beery;Jonathan Huang;
机构: University of Michigan;;Massachusetts Institute of Technology;Google;University of California, Berkeley;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30832
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.11608
英摘: Hierarchical classification predicts labels across multiple levels of a taxonomy, e.g., from coarse-level 'Bird' to mid-level 'Hummingbird' to fine-level 'Green hermit', allowing flexible recognition under varying visual conditions. It is commonly framed as multiple single-level tasks, but each level may rely on different visual cues: Distinguishing 'Bird' from 'Plant' relies on global features like feathers or leaves, while separating 'Anna's hummingbird' from 'Green hermit' requires local details such as head coloration. Prior methods improve accuracy using external semantic supervision, but such statistical learning criteria fail to ensure consistent visual grounding at test time, resulting in incorrect hierarchical classification. We propose, for the first time, to enforce internal visual consistency by aligning fine-to-coarse predictions through intra-image segmentation. Our method outperforms zero-shot CLIP and state-of-the-art baselines on hierarchical classification benchmarks, achieving both higher accuracy and more consistent predictions. It also improves internal image segmentation without requiring pixel-level annotations.
中摘: 层次分类能够在分类学的不同层级上预测标签，例如从粗级别的“鸟”到中级别的“蜂鸟”再到细级别的“绿蜂鸟”，从而在不同视觉条件下实现灵活的识别。它通常被构架为多个单一级别的任务，但每个级别可能依赖于不同的视觉线索：区分“鸟”和“植物”需要利用全局特征如羽毛或叶子，而将“安娜蜂鸟”与“绿蜂鸟”区分开则需要关注局部细节如头部的颜色。以往的方法通过外部语义监督来提高准确性，但在测试时这些统计学习标准无法确保视觉基础的一致性，导致层次分类出现错误。我们首次提出通过图像内部分割对细到粗的预测进行对齐，以强制执行内部视觉一致性。我们的方法在层次分类基准测试中优于零样本CLIP和最先进的基线模型，实现了更高的准确性和更一致的预测。此外，该方法还无需像素级标注就能改进内部图像分割。

[No.50] CFG++: Manifold-constrained Classifier Free Guidance for Diffusion Models
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: classif
标题: CFG++：流形约束的分类器免费指导扩散模型
类别: generative models
作者: Hyungjin Chung;Jeongsol Kim;Geon Yeong Park;Hyelin Nam;Jong Chul Ye;
机构: Korea Advanced Institute of Science and Technology;University of Michigan;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30421
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.08070
英摘: Classifier-free guidance (CFG) is a fundamental tool in modern diffusion models for text-guided generation. Although effective, CFG has notable drawbacks. For instance, DDIM with CFG lacks invertibility, complicating image editing; furthermore, high guidance scales, essential for high-quality outputs, frequently result in issues like mode collapse. Contrary to the widespread belief that these are inherent limitations of diffusion models, this paper reveals that the problems actually stem from the off-manifold phenomenon associated with CFG, rather than the diffusion models themselves. More specifically, inspired by the recent advancements of diffusion model-based inverse problem solvers (DIS), we reformulate text-guidance as an inverse problem with a text-conditioned score matching loss and develop CFG++, a novel approach that tackles the off-manifold challenges inherent in traditional CFG. CFG++ features a surprisingly simple fix to CFG, yet it offers significant improvements, including better sample quality for text-to-image generation, invertibility, smaller guidance scales, reduced mode collapse, etc. Furthermore, CFG++ enables seamless interpolation between unconditional and conditional sampling at lower guidance scales, consistently outperforming traditional CFG at all scales. Moreover, CFG++ can be easily integrated into high-order diffusion solvers and naturally extends to distilled diffusion models. Experimental results confirm that our method significantly enhances performance in text-to-image generation, DDIM inversion, editing, and solving inverse problems, suggesting a wide-ranging impact and potential applications in various fields that utilize text guidance. Project Page: https://cfgpp-diffusion.github.io/.
中摘: 无分类指导（Classifier-Free Guidance, CFG）是现代文本引导生成扩散模型中的基本工具。尽管效果显著，但CFG也存在明显的缺点。例如，使用CFG的DDIM缺乏可逆性，这使得图像编辑变得复杂；此外，为了获得高质量的输出，需要较高的指导比例，而这种高指导比例往往会导致模式崩溃等问题。与普遍认为这些是扩散模型固有的局限性不同，本文揭示了这些问题实际上源于与CFG相关的离流现象，而不是扩散模型本身。具体来说，受到基于扩散模型的逆问题求解器（Inverse Problem Solvers based on Diffusion Models, DIS）近期进展的启发，我们将文本指导重新表述为一个条件文本下的逆问题，并开发了一种名为CFG++的新方法，以解决传统CFG固有的离流挑战。CFG++对CFG进行了一个出人意料简单的修正，但它提供了显著的改进，包括更好的文本到图像生成样本质量、可逆性、较小的指导比例、减少模式崩溃等。此外，CFG++能够在较低的指导比例下无缝地在无条件和有条件采样之间进行插值，在所有比例下都优于传统的CFG。另外，CFG++可以轻松集成到高阶扩散求解器中，并自然扩展到精简扩散模型。实验结果证实，我们的方法在文本到图像生成、DDIM逆过程、编辑和逆问题求解方面显著提升了性能，表明其广泛的影响和在各种利用文本指导的领域的潜在应用。项目页面：https://cfgpp-diffusion.github.io/。

[No.51] The Breakdown of Gaussian Universality in Classification of High-dimensional Linear Factor Mixtures
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: classif
标题: 高维线性因子混合分类中的高斯普遍性分解
类别: learning theory
作者: Xiaoyi MAI;Zhenyu Liao;
机构: Université de Toulouse-le-Mirail;Huazhong University of Science and Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/29450
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.05609
英摘: The assumption of Gaussian or Gaussian mixture data has been extensively exploited in a long series of precise performance analyses of machine learning (ML) methods, on large datasets having comparably numerous samples and features. To relax this restrictive assumption, subsequent efforts have been devoted to establish "Gaussian equivalent principles" by studying scenarios of Gaussian universality where the asymptotic performance of ML methods on non-Gaussian data remains unchanged when replaced with Gaussian data having the same mean and covariance. Beyond the realm of Gaussian universality, there are few exact results on how the data distribution affects the learning performance.
  In this article, we provide a precise high-dimensional characterization of empirical risk minimization, for classification under a general mixture data setting of linear factor models that extends Gaussian mixtures. The Gaussian universality is shown to break down under this setting, in the sense that the asymptotic learning performance depends on the data distribution beyond the class means and covariances. To clarify the limitations of Gaussian universality in the classification of mixture data and to understand the impact of its breakdown, we specify conditions for Gaussian universality and discuss their implications for the choice of loss function.
中摘: 在机器学习（ML）方法的大规模数据集上进行精确性能分析时，假设数据服从高斯分布或高斯混合分布被广泛采用。为了放松这一限制性假设，后续的研究致力于通过研究高斯普适性场景来建立“高斯等价原理”，即当用具有相同均值和协方差的高斯数据替换非高斯数据时，ML方法的渐近性能保持不变。然而，在高斯普适性之外的数据分布领域，关于数据分布如何影响学习性能的确切结果非常有限。

在这篇文章中，我们为线性因子模型的一般混合数据设置下的经验风险最小化提供了精确的高维表征，该设置扩展了高斯混合分布。在这一设置下，高斯普适性被证明会失效，也就是说，渐近学习性能不仅依赖于类别的均值和协方差，还依赖于数据的具体分布。为了阐明高斯普适性在混合数据分类中的局限性，并理解其失效的影响，我们指定了高斯普适性的条件，并讨论了这些条件对损失函数选择的影响。
