[No.52] CPSample: Classifier Protected Sampling for Guarding Training Data During Diffusion
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: classif
标题: CPSample：分类保护采样，用于扩散过程中保护训练数据
类别: generative models
作者: Joshua Kazdan;Hao Sun;Jiaqi Han;Felix Petersen;Frederick Vu;Stefano Ermon;
机构: Stanford University;University of California, Los Angeles;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30007
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.07025
英摘: Diffusion models have a tendency to exactly replicate their training data, especially when trained on small datasets. Most prior work has sought to mitigate this problem by imposing differential privacy constraints or masking parts of the training data, resulting in a notable substantial decrease in image quality. We present CPSample, a method that modifies the sampling process to prevent training data replication while preserving image quality. CPSample utilizes a classifier that is trained to overfit on random binary labels attached to the training data. CPSample then uses classifier guidance to steer the generation process away from the set of points that can be classified with high certainty, a set that includes the training data. CPSample achieves FID scores of 4.97 and 2.97 on CIFAR-10 and CelebA-64, respectively, without producing exact replicates of the training data. Unlike prior methods intended to guard the training images, CPSample only requires training a classifier rather than retraining a diffusion model, which is computationally cheaper. Moreover, our technique provides diffusion models with greater robustness against membership inference attacks, wherein an adversary attempts to discern which images were in the model's training dataset. We show that CPSample behaves like a built-in rejection sampler, and we demonstrate its capabilities to prevent mode collapse in Stable Diffusion.
中摘: 扩散模型倾向于精确复制其训练数据，尤其是在使用小数据集进行训练时。大多数先前的工作试图通过施加差分隐私约束或遮掩训练数据的一部分来减轻这一问题，但导致图像质量显著下降。我们提出了CPSample方法，该方法修改了采样过程以防止复制训练数据同时保持图像质量。CPSample利用了一个被训练在随机二元标签上过度拟合的分类器，并将分类器指导应用于引导生成过程远离那些可以用高确定性分类出的点，这些点包括训练数据本身。在CIFAR-10和CelebA-64上，CPSample分别实现了4.97和2.97的FID分数，而不会生成训练数据的精确副本。与旨在保护训练图像的方法不同，CPSample只需要训练一个分类器，而不是重新训练扩散模型，这在计算成本上更为经济。此外，我们的技术为扩散模型提供了更强的抗对抗推断攻击的能力，在这些攻击中，对手试图辨别哪些图像属于模型的训练数据集。我们展示了CPSample像内置的拒绝采样器一样行为，并证明了它能够防止Stable Diffusion中的模式崩溃。

[No.53] The mechanistic basis of data dependence and abrupt learning in an in-context classification task
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: classif
标题: 基于机制的数据依赖性和上下文分类任务中的突然学习基础
类别: general machine learning (i.e., none of the above)
作者: Gautam Reddy;
机构: 
类型: Oral
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2024/poster/18368
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2312.03002
英摘: Transformer models exhibit in-context learning: the ability to accurately predict the response to a novel query based on illustrative examples in the input sequence. In-context learning contrasts with traditional in-weights learning of query-output relationships. What aspects of the training data distribution and architecture favor in-context vs in-weights learning? Recent work has shown that specific distributional properties inherent in language, such as burstiness, large dictionaries and skewed rank-frequency distributions, control the trade-off or simultaneous appearance of these two forms of learning. We first show that these results are recapitulated in a minimal attention-only network trained on a simplified dataset. In-context learning (ICL) is driven by the abrupt emergence of an induction head, which subsequently competes with in-weights learning. By identifying progress measures that precede in-context learning and targeted experiments, we construct a two-parameter model of an induction head which emulates the full data distributional dependencies displayed by the attention-based network. A phenomenological model of induction head formation traces its abrupt emergence to the sequential learning of three nested logits enabled by an intrinsic curriculum. We propose that the sharp transitions in attention-based networks arise due to a specific chain of multi-layer operations necessary to achieve ICL, which is implemented by nested nonlinearities sequentially learned during training.
中摘: 变压器模型表现出上下文学习能力：能够基于输入序列中的示例实例准确预测新型查询的响应。上下文学习与传统的基于权重的学习方式（即查询-输出关系的学习）形成对比。哪些训练数据分布特性和架构方面更有利于上下文学习还是基于权重的学习？近期研究表明，语言固有的特定分布特性，如突发性、庞大的词汇量和偏斜的词频分布，控制着这两种学习形式之间的权衡或同时出现。我们首先展示了这些结果在简化数据集上训练的最小注意力网络中得到了重现。上下文学习（ICL）是由诱导头的突然出现驱动的，随后与基于权重的学习竞争。通过识别预示上下文学习的进步指标并进行针对性实验，我们构建了一个两参数模型来模拟基于注意力网络所展示的完整数据分布依赖性。诱导头形成的现象学模型将其突然出现追溯到通过内在课程顺序学习的三个嵌套逻辑层。我们提出，在基于注意力的网络中，尖锐的过渡源于实现ICL所需的特定多层操作链，这些操作在训练过程中通过顺序学习嵌套的非线性特性来实现。

[No.54] Inherently Interpretable Time Series Classification via Multiple Instance Learning
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: classif
标题: 固有可解释的时间序列分类方法通过多重实例学习
类别: unsupervised, self-supervised, semi-supervised, and supervised representation learning
作者: Joseph Early;Gavin Cheung;Kurt Cutajar;Hanting Xie;Jas Kandola;Niall Twomey;
机构: Helsing;Amazon;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2024/poster/17442
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.10049
英摘: Conventional Time Series Classification (TSC) methods are often black boxes that obscure inherent interpretation of their decision-making processes. In this work, we leverage Multiple Instance Learning (MIL) to overcome this issue, and propose a new framework called MILLET: Multiple Instance Learning for Locally Explainable Time series classification. We apply MILLET to existing deep learning TSC models and show how they become inherently interpretable without compromising (and in some cases, even improving) predictive performance. We evaluate MILLET on 85 UCR TSC datasets and also present a novel synthetic dataset that is specially designed to facilitate interpretability evaluation. On these datasets, we show MILLET produces sparse explanations quickly that are of higher quality than other well-known interpretability methods. To the best of our knowledge, our work with MILLET, which is available on GitHub (https://github.com/JAEarly/MILTimeSeriesClassification), is the first to develop general MIL methods for TSC and apply them to an extensive variety of domains
中摘: 传统的时序分类（TSC）方法往往是黑盒模型，掩盖了其决策过程内在的解释性。在本研究中，我们利用多重实例学习（MIL）来克服这一问题，并提出了一种新的框架，称为MILLETT：基于局部可解释性的多重实例学习时序分类。我们将MILLETT应用于现有的深度学习时序分类模型，并展示了这些模型如何在不牺牲（甚至在某些情况下还能提升）预测性能的前提下变得具有内在的解释性。我们在85个UCR时序分类数据集上评估了MILLETT，并且还提供了一个新颖的合成数据集，专门设计用于促进解释性评估。在这些数据集上，我们证明了MILLETT能够快速生成高质量的稀疏解释。据我们所知，我们的MILLETT工作（可在GitHub上获取：https://github.com/JAEarly/MILTimeSeriesClassification）是首次开发适用于时序分类的一般多重实例学习方法，并将其应用到广泛的领域中。

[No.55] Intriguing Properties of Generative Classifiers
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: classif
标题: 生成分类器的有趣性质
类别: generative models
作者: Priyank Jaini;Kevin Clark;Robert Geirhos;

类型: Spotlight
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2024/poster/17684
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2309.16779
英摘: What is the best paradigm to recognize objects -- discriminative inference (fast but potentially prone to shortcut learning) or using a generative model (slow but potentially more robust)? We build on recent advances in generative modeling that turn text-to-image models into classifiers. This allows us to study their behavior and to compare them against discriminative models and human psychophysical data. We report four intriguing emergent properties of generative classifiers: they show a record-breaking human-like shape bias (99% for Imagen), near human-level out-of-distribution accuracy, state-of-the-art alignment with human classification errors, and they understand certain perceptual illusions. Our results indicate that while the current dominant paradigm for modeling human object recognition is discriminative inference, zero-shot generative models approximate human object recognition data surprisingly well.
中摘: 识别物体的最佳范式是判别推断（快速但可能容易出现捷径学习）还是使用生成模型（缓慢但可能更稳健）？我们基于最近在生成建模方面的进展，将文本到图像的模型转化为分类器。这使我们能够研究它们的行为，并将其与判别模型和人类心理物理学数据进行比较。我们报告了四种有趣的新兴特性：生成分类器显示出创纪录的人类般的形状偏好（Imagen为99%），接近人类水平的分布外准确性，最先进的与人类分类错误对齐程度，以及理解某些知觉错觉的能力。我们的结果显示，尽管当前主导的人类物体识别建模范式是判别推断，但零样本生成模型惊人地逼近了人类物体识别的数据。

[No.56] Post-hoc bias scoring is optimal for fair classification
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: classif
标题: 事后偏差评分是实现公平分类的最佳方法
类别: societal considerations including fairness, safety, privacy
作者: Wenlong Chen;Yegor Klochkov;Yang Liu;
机构: RIKEN;ByteDance;University of California, Santa Cruz;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2024/poster/19069
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.05725
英摘: We consider a binary classification problem under group fairness constraints, which can be one of Demographic Parity (DP), Equalized Opportunity (EOp), or Equalized Odds (EO). We propose an explicit characterization of Bayes optimal classifier under the fairness constraints, which turns out to be a simple modification rule of the unconstrained classifier. Namely, we introduce a novel instance-level measure of bias, which we call bias score, and the modification rule is a simple linear rule on top of the finite amount of bias scores.Based on this characterization, we develop a post-hoc approach that allows us to adapt to fairness constraints while maintaining high accuracy. In the case of DP and EOp constraints, the modification rule is thresholding a single bias score, while in the case of EO constraints we are required to fit a linear modification rule with 2 parameters. The method can also be applied for composite group-fairness criteria, such as ones involving several sensitive attributes.
中摘: 我们在组公平性约束下考虑一个二分类问题，并提出了一种在公平性约束下的贝叶斯最优分类器的显式表征，这实际上是未受约束分类器的一个简单修改规则。具体而言，我们引入了一个新颖的实例级偏见度量，称为偏见分值，并且修改规则是在有限数量的偏见分值基础上的一个简单线性规则。基于这一表征，我们开发了一种后验方法，允许我们在满足公平性约束的同时保持高精度。对于人口平等（Demographic Parity, DP）和等效机会（Equalized Opportunity, EOp）约束，修改规则是阈值化单一偏见分值，而对于等价概率（Equalized Odds, EO）约束，则需要拟合一个包含两个参数的线性修改规则。该方法也可以应用于复合组公平性标准，例如涉及多个敏感属性的标准。

[No.57] Consistent algorithms for multi-label classification with macro-at-$k$ metrics
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: classif
标题: 多标签分类中的一致性算法与宏平均@$k$度量
类别: general machine learning (i.e., none of the above)
作者: Erik Schultheis;Wojciech Kotlowski;Marek Wydmuch;Rohit Babbar;Strom Borman;Krzysztof Dembczynski;

链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2024/poster/18450
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.16594
英摘: We consider the optimization of complex performance metrics in multi-label classification under the population utility framework. We mainly focus on metrics linearly decomposable into a sum of binary classification utilities applied separately to each label with an additional requirement of exactly $k$ labels predicted for each instance. These "macro-at-$k$" metrics possess desired properties for extreme classification problems with long tail labels. Unfortunately, the at-$k$ constraint couples the otherwise independent binary classification tasks, leading to a much more challenging optimization problem than standard macro-averages. We provide a statistical framework to study this problem, prove the existence and the form of the optimal classifier, and propose a statistically consistent and practical learning algorithm based on the Frank-Wolfe method. Interestingly, our main results concern even more general metrics being non-linear functions of label-wise confusion matrices. Empirical results provide evidence for the competitive performance of the proposed approach.
中摘: 我们在人口效用框架下考虑在多标签分类中优化复杂的性能度量。我们主要关注可以线性分解为每个标签独立应用的二元分类效用之和，并且还有一个额外的要求，即每个实例恰好预测出$k$个标签。“宏-$k$”度量具有在长尾标签的极端分类问题中所需的特性。不幸的是，宏-$k$约束将原本独立的二元分类任务耦合在一起，导致比标准宏平均值更困难的优化问题。我们提供了一个统计框架来研究这个问题，证明了最优分类器的存在性和形式，并提出了一种基于Frank-Wolfe方法的统计一致且实用的学习算法。有趣的是，我们的主要结果甚至涉及更一般的度量，这些度量是非线性函数，基于标签混淆矩阵。实验结果提供了所提方法具有竞争力性能的证据。

[No.61] One For All: Towards Training One Graph Model For All Classification Tasks
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: classif
标题: 一切皆可：面向所有分类任务训练一个图模型
类别: learning on graphs and other geometries & topologies
作者: Hao Liu;Jiarui Feng;Lecheng Kong;Ningyue Liang;Dacheng Tao;Yixin Chen;Muhan Zhang;
机构: Washington University in St. Louis;Amazon;Peking University;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2024/poster/19474
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.00149
英摘: Designing a single model to address multiple tasks has been a long-standing objective in artificial intelligence. Recently, large language models have demonstrated exceptional capability in solving different tasks within the language domain. However, a unified model for various graph tasks remains underexplored, primarily due to the challenges unique to the graph learning domain. First, graph data from different areas carry distinct attributes and follow different distributions. Such discrepancy makes it hard to represent graphs in a single representation space. Second, tasks on graphs diversify into node, link, and graph tasks, requiring distinct embedding strategies. Finally, an appropriate graph prompting paradigm for in-context learning is unclear. We propose \textbf{One for All (OFA)}, the first general framework that can use a single graph model to address the above challenges. Specifically, OFA proposes text-attributed graphs to unify different graph data by describing nodes and edges with natural language and uses language models to encode the diverse and possibly cross-domain text attributes to feature vectors in the same embedding space. Furthermore, OFA introduces the concept of nodes-of-interest to standardize different tasks with a single task representation. For in-context learning on graphs, OFA introduces a novel graph prompting paradigm that appends prompting substructures to the input graph, which enables it to address varied tasks without fine-tuning. We train the OFA model using graph data from multiple domains (including citation networks, molecular graphs, knowledge graphs, etc.) simultaneously and evaluate its ability in supervised, few-shot, and zero-shot learning scenarios. OFA performs well across different tasks, making it the first general-purpose across-domains classification model on graphs.
中摘: 在人工智能领域，设计一个单一模型来应对多种任务一直是长期目标。最近，大型语言模型在解决语言领域的不同任务方面展现了出色的能力。然而，对于各种图任务的统一模型仍然鲜有探索，主要是因为图学习领域独有的挑战。首先，来自不同领域的图数据具有独特的属性和不同的分布。这种差异使得在一个单一的表示空间中表示图变得困难。其次，图上的任务多样化为节点、边和图任务，需要不同的嵌入策略。最后，合适的图提示范式以实现上下文学习尚不明确。我们提出了一种名为**One for All (OFA)** 的通用框架，该框架可以使用单一图模型来应对上述挑战。具体而言，OFA 提出了文本描述的图，通过自然语言描述节点和边，将多样且可能跨域的文本属性编码到相同的嵌入空间中的特征向量中。此外，OFA 引入了兴趣节点的概念，以标准化不同的任务为单一的任务表示。对于图的上下文学习，OFA 引入了一个新颖的图提示范式，该范式在输入图中附加提示子结构，使其能够在无需微调的情况下应对各种任务。我们同时使用来自多个领域（包括引文网络、分子图、知识图等）的图数据训练 OFA 模型，并评估其在监督学习、少样本学习和零样本学习场景下的能力。OFA 在不同任务上表现良好，使其成为第一个适用于跨域图分类的一般目的模型。

[No.62] Classification with Conceptual Safeguards
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: classif
标题: 概念保障下的分类
类别: societal considerations including fairness, safety, privacy
作者: Hailey Joren;Charles Thomas Marx;Berk Ustun;
机构: Stanford University;Harvard University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2024/poster/17625
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.04342
英摘: We propose a new approach to promote safety in classification tasks with established concepts. Our approach -- called a conceptual safeguard -- acts as a verification layer for models that predict a target outcome by first predicting the presence of intermediate concepts. Given this architecture, a safeguard ensures that a model meets a minimal level of accuracy by abstaining from uncertain predictions. In contrast to a standard selective classifier, a safeguard provides an avenue to improve coverage by allowing a human to confirm the presence of uncertain concepts on instances on which it abstains. We develop methods to build safeguards that maximize coverage without compromising safety, namely techniques to propagate the uncertainty in concept predictions and to flag salient concepts for human review. We benchmark our approach on a collection of real-world and synthetic datasets, showing that it can improve performance and coverage in deep learning tasks.
中摘: 我们提出了一种新的方法，在分类任务中通过已有的概念促进安全性。我们的方法称为“概念保护层”，它作为模型验证的一层，在模型首先预测中间概念后再预测目标结果时发挥作用。鉴于这种架构，保护层确保模型在不确定预测时会放弃这些预测，从而达到最低的准确性标准。与标准的选择性分类器不同，保护层提供了一条提高覆盖范围的途径，允许人类在模型放弃预测的实例上确认不确定的概念。我们开发了方法来构建最大化覆盖范围而不牺牲安全性的保护层，包括传播概念预测中的不确定性以及标记重要概念供人类审查的技术。我们在一系列真实世界和合成数据集上对这种方法进行了基准测试，展示了它在深度学习任务中能够提高性能和覆盖范围。

[No.63] MetaCoCo: A New Few-Shot Classification Benchmark with Spurious Correlation
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: classif
标题: MetaCoCo：一个新的含有虚假相关性的少量样本分类基准测试
类别: datasets and benchmarks
作者: Min Zhang;Haoxuan Li;Fei Wu;Kun Kuang;
机构: Peking University;Zhejiang University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2024/poster/19133
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.19644
英摘: Out-of-distribution (OOD) problems in few-shot classification (FSC) occur when novel classes sampled from testing distributions differ from base classes drawn from training distributions, which considerably degrades the performance of deep learning models deployed in real-world applications. Recent studies suggest that the OOD problems in FSC mainly including: (a) cross-domain few-shot classification (CD-FSC) and (b) spurious-correlation few-shot classification (SC-FSC). Specifically, CD-FSC occurs when a classifier learns transferring knowledge from base classes drawn from seen training distributions but recognizes novel classes sampled from unseen testing distributions. In contrast, SC-FSC arises when a classifier relies on non-causal features (or contexts) that happen to be correlated with the labels (or concepts) in base classes but such relationships no longer hold during the model deployment. Despite CD-FSC has been extensively studied, SC-FSC remains understudied due to lack of the corresponding evaluation benchmarks. To this end, we present Meta Concept Context (MetaCoCo), a benchmark with spurious-correlation shifts collected from real-world scenarios. Moreover, to quantify the extent of spurious-correlation shifts of the presented MetaCoCo, we further propose a metric by using CLIP as a pre-trained vision-language model. Extensive experiments on the proposed benchmark are performed to evaluate the state-of-the-art methods in FSC, cross-domain shifts, and self-supervised learning. The experimental results show that the performance of the existing methods degrades significantly in the presence of spurious-correlation shifts. We open-source all codes of our benchmark and hope that the proposed MetaCoCo can facilitate future research on spurious-correlation shifts problems in FSC. The code is available at: https://github.com/remiMZ/MetaCoCo-ICLR24.
中摘: 在少量样本分类（FSC）中的分布外（OOD）问题发生在测试分布中采样的新类别与训练分布中抽取的基本类别不同，这显著降低了部署于实际应用中的深度学习模型的性能。近期的研究表明，FSC中的OOD问题主要包含以下两种类型：(a) 跨域少量样本分类（CD-FSC）和(b) 错误相关少量样本分类（SC-FSC）。具体而言，CD-FSC发生于分类器从已见过的训练分布中抽取的基本类别学习迁移知识，但识别来自未见过的测试分布中新类别的场景。相比之下，SC-FSC则出现在分类器依赖于非因果特征（或上下文），这些特征在基本类别中偶然与标签（或概念）相关联，但在模型部署时这种关系不再成立的情况。尽管CD-FSC已被广泛研究，但由于缺乏相应的评估基准，SC-FSC仍相对未被充分研究。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了Meta Concept Context（MetaCoCo），这是一个收集自真实场景的错误相关性转移基准。此外，为了量化所提出的MetaCoCo中的错误相关性转移程度，我们进一步提出了一种使用CLIP作为预训练的视觉语言模型的度量标准。我们在该提出的基准上进行了广泛的实验，以评估少量样本分类、跨域转移和自我监督学习领域的最新方法。实验结果表明，在存在错误相关性转移的情况下，现有方法的性能显著下降。我们开源了所有基准代码，并希望所提出的MetaCoCo能够促进未来少量样本分类中错误相关性转移问题的研究。代码可在以下链接获取：https://github.com/remiMZ/MetaCoCo-ICLR24。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

[No.65] Text-to-3D with Classifier Score Distillation
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: classif
标题: 基于分类器分数的知识提取到三维模型
类别: generative models
作者: Xin Yu;Yuan-Chen Guo;Yangguang Li;Ding Liang;Song-Hai Zhang;XIAOJUAN QI;
机构: University of Hong Kong;Shanghai AI Laboratory;Tsinghua University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2024/poster/17961
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.19415
英摘: Text-to-3D generation has made remarkable progress recently, particularly with methods based on Score Distillation Sampling (SDS) that leverages pre-trained 2D diffusion models. While the usage of classifier-free guidance is well acknowledged to be crucial for successful optimization, it is considered an auxiliary trick rather than the most essential component. In this paper, we re-evaluate the role of classifier-free guidance in score distillation and discover a surprising finding: the guidance alone is enough for effective text-to-3D generation tasks. We name this method Classifier Score Distillation (CSD), which can be interpreted as using an implicit classification model for generation. This new perspective reveals new insights for understanding existing techniques. We validate the effectiveness of CSD across a variety of text-to-3D tasks including shape generation, texture synthesis, and shape editing, achieving results superior to those of state-of-the-art methods. Our project page is https://xinyu-andy.github.io/Classifier-Score-Distillation
中摘: 最近，文本到3D生成取得了显著进展，特别是在基于Score Distillation Sampling (SDS) 方法上，这些方法利用了预训练的2D扩散模型。虽然无分类器引导在成功优化中的重要性得到了广泛认可，但它被认为是辅助技巧而非最关键的部分。在本文中，我们重新评估了无分类器引导在Score Distillation中的作用，并发现了一个令人惊讶的结果：仅引导就足以完成有效的文本到3D生成任务。我们称这种方法为Classifier Score Distillation (CSD)，可以将其解释为使用隐式分类模型进行生成的新方法。这一新的视角揭示了对现有技术理解的新见解。我们在形状生成、纹理合成和形状编辑等多种文本到3D任务中验证了CSD的有效性，其结果优于最先进的方法。我们的项目页面为 https://xinyu-andy.github.io/Classifier-Score-Distillation

[No.67] Label-free Node Classification on Graphs with Large Language Models (LLMs)
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: classif
标题: 基于大规模语言模型（LLM）的无标签节点分类图上
类别: learning on graphs and other geometries & topologies
作者: Zhikai Chen;Haitao Mao;Hongzhi Wen;Haoyu Han;Wei Jin;Haiyang Zhang;Hui Liu;Jiliang Tang;
机构: Michigan State University;Emory University;Amazon;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2024/poster/18104
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.04668
英摘: In recent years, there have been remarkable advancements in node classification achieved by Graph Neural Networks (GNNs). However, they necessitate abundant high-quality labels to ensure promising performance. In contrast, Large Language Models (LLMs) exhibit impressive zero-shot proficiency on text-attributed graphs. Yet, they face challenges in efficiently processing structural data and suffer from high inference costs. In light of these observations, this work introduces a label-free node classification on graphs with LLMs pipeline, LLM-GNN. It amalgamates the strengths of both GNNs and LLMs while mitigating their limitations. Specifically, LLMs are leveraged to annotate a small portion of nodes and then GNNs are trained on LLMs' annotations to make predictions for the remaining large portion of nodes. The implementation of LLM-GNN faces a unique challenge: how can we actively select nodes for LLMs to annotate and consequently enhance the GNN training? How can we leverage LLMs to obtain annotations of high quality, representativeness, and diversity, thereby enhancing GNN performance with less cost? To tackle this challenge, we develop an annotation quality heuristic and leverage the confidence scores derived from LLMs to advanced node selection. Comprehensive experimental results validate the effectiveness of LLM-GNN. In particular, LLM-GNN can achieve an accuracy of 74.9% on a vast-scale dataset \products with a cost less than 1 dollar.
中摘: 近年来，图神经网络（GNNs）在节点分类方面取得了显著进展。然而，它们需要大量的高质量标签以确保出色的性能。相比之下，大型语言模型（LLMs）在处理带有文本属性的图时表现出色，能够实现零样本学习。但它们在高效处理结构数据方面面临挑战，并且推理成本较高。鉴于这些观察结果，本研究提出了一种基于LLMs的无标签节点分类方法——LLM-GNN。该方法结合了GNNs和LLMs的优点，同时克服了它们各自的局限性。具体来说，LLMs用于标注一小部分节点，然后GNNs根据LLMs的标注进行训练，从而对剩余的大规模节点进行预测。LLM-GNN的实施面临着一个独特挑战：如何积极选择节点供LLMs标注，并相应地提高GNN的训练效果？如何利用LLMs获得高质量、代表性以及多样性的标注，从而以较低的成本提升GNN的性能？为应对这一挑战，我们开发了一种标注质量启发式方法，并利用从LLMs中得出的信心分数来优化节点选择。全面的实验结果验证了LLM-GNN的有效性。特别是，LLM-GNN可以在成本低于1美元的情况下，在大规模数据集\products上达到74.9%的准确率。

[No.68] Neural Field Classifiers via Target Encoding and Classification Loss
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: classif
标题: 通过目标编码和分类损失的神经场分类器
类别: representation learning for computer vision, audio, language, and other modalities
作者: Xindi Yang;Zeke Xie;Xiong Zhou;Boyu Liu;Buhua Liu;Yi Liu;Haoran Wang;YUNFENG CAI;Mingming Sun;
机构: Baidu;Harbin Institute of Technology;Beihang University;Beijing Jiao Tong University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2024/poster/19299
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.01058
英摘: Neural field methods have seen great progress in various long-standing tasks in computer vision and computer graphics, including novel view synthesis and geometry reconstruction. As existing neural field methods try to predict some coordinate-based continuous target values, such as RGB for Neural Radiance Field (NeRF), all of these methods are regression models and are optimized by some regression loss. However, are regression models really better than classification models for neural field methods? In this work, we try to visit this very fundamental but overlooked question for neural fields from a machine learning perspective. We successfully propose a novel Neural Field Classifier (NFC) framework which formulates existing neural field methods as classification tasks rather than regression tasks. The proposed NFC can easily transform arbitrary Neural Field Regressor (NFR) into its classification variant via employing a novel Target Encoding module and optimizing a classification loss. By encoding a continuous regression target into a high-dimensional discrete encoding, we naturally formulate a multi-label classification task. Extensive experiments demonstrate the impressive effectiveness of NFC at the nearly free extra computational costs. Moreover, NFC also shows robustness to sparse inputs, corrupted images, and dynamic scenes.
中摘: 神经场方法在计算机视觉和计算机图形学中的长期任务，如新颖视图合成和几何重建方面取得了显著进展。现有的神经场方法试图预测一些基于坐标的连续目标值，例如神经辐射场（NeRF）中的RGB值，所有这些方法都是回归模型，并通过某种回归损失进行优化。然而，对于神经场方法而言，回归模型真的比分类模型更好吗？在本工作中，我们从机器学习的角度出发，尝试回答这个被忽视的基本问题。我们成功提出了一种新的神经场分类器（NFC）框架，将现有的神经场方法重新表述为分类任务而非回归任务。所提出的NFC可以通过采用一种新型的目标编码模块并优化分类损失来轻松地将其任意的神经场回归器（NFR）转换为其分类变体。通过将连续的回归目标值编码为高维离散表示，我们自然地形成了一个多标签分类任务。广泛的实验表明，在几乎免费的额外计算成本下，NFC表现出色。此外，NFC还展示了对稀疏输入、损坏图像和动态场景的鲁棒性。

[No.69] Kalman Filter for Online Classification of Non-Stationary Data
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: classif
标题: 卡尔曼滤波器在非平稳数据在线分类中的应用
类别: transfer learning, meta learning, and lifelong learning
作者: Michalis Titsias;Alexandre Galashov;Amal Rannen-Triki;Razvan Pascanu;Yee Whye Teh;Jorg Bornschein;
机构: Google;Ecole Polytechnique;DeepMind;University of Oxford;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2024/poster/18380
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.08448
英摘: In Online Continual Learning (OCL) a learning system receives a stream of data and sequentially performs prediction and training steps. Important challenges in OCL are concerned with automatic adaptation to the particular non-stationary structure of the data, and with quantification of predictive uncertainty. Motivated by these challenges we introduce a probabilistic Bayesian online learning model by using a (possibly pretrained) neural representation and a state space model over the linear predictor weights. Non-stationarity over the linear predictor weights is modelled using a parameter drift transition density, parametrized by a coefficient that quantifies forgetting. Inference in the model is implemented with efficient Kalman filter recursions which track the posterior distribution over the linear weights, while online SGD updates over the transition dynamics coefficient allows to adapt to the non-stationarity seen in data. While the framework is developed assuming a linear Gaussian model, we also extend it to deal with classification problems and for fine-tuning the deep learning representation. In a set of experiments in multi-class classification using data sets such as CIFAR-100 and CLOC we demonstrate the predictive ability of the model and its flexibility to capture non-stationarity.
中摘: 在线持续学习（OCL）中，一个学习系统接收数据流，并依次执行预测和训练步骤。OCL 中的重要挑战在于自动适应特定的数据非平稳结构，以及量化预测不确定性。为应对这些挑战，我们引入了一个概率贝叶斯在线学习模型，该模型利用了一个可能预先训练的神经表示和一个基于线性预测器权重的状态空间模型。线性预测器权重的非平稳性通过参数漂移转换密度来建模，该密度由一个量化遗忘程度的系数参数化。在该模型中的推理通过高效的卡尔曼滤波递归实现，跟踪线性权重的后验分布，而在线SGD更新则允许根据数据中观察到的非平稳性进行调整。尽管该框架是在假设线性高斯模型的情况下开发的，我们还将其扩展以处理分类问题，并对深度学习表示进行微调。我们在使用CIFAR-100和CLOC等数据集进行多类分类实验中展示了该模型的预测能力和捕捉非平稳性的灵活性。

[No.70] Fair Classifiers that Abstain without Harm
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: classif
标题: 公正的分类器在不造成损害的情况下选择弃权
类别: societal considerations including fairness, safety, privacy
作者: Tongxin Yin;Jean-Francois Ton;Ruocheng Guo;Yuanshun Yao;Mingyan Liu;Yang Liu;
机构: University of Michigan;ByteDance;University of California, Santa Cruz;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2024/poster/17999
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.06205
英摘: In critical applications, it is vital for classifiers to defer decision-making to humans. We propose a post-hoc method that makes existing classifiers selectively abstain from predicting certain samples. Our abstaining classifier is incentivized to maintain the original accuracy for each sub-population (i.e. no harm) while achieving a set of group fairness definitions to a user specified degree. To this end, we design an Integer Programming (IP) procedure that assigns abstention decisions for each training sample to satisfy a set of constraints. To generalize the abstaining decisions to test samples, we then train a surrogate model to learn the abstaining decisions based on the IP solutions in an end-to-end manner. We analyze the feasibility of the IP procedure to determine the possible abstention rate for different levels of unfairness tolerance and accuracy constraint for achieving no harm. To the best of our knowledge, this work is the first to identify the theoretical relationships between the constraint parameters and the required abstention rate. Our theoretical results are important since a high abstention rate is often infeasible in practice due to a lack of human resources. Our framework outperforms existing methods in terms of fairness disparity without sacrificing accuracy at similar abstention rates.
中摘: 在关键应用中，分类器在决策时将某些样本交由人类做出是非常重要的。我们提出了一种后处理方法，使现有的分类器有选择地对某些样本放弃预测。我们的弃权分类器被激励保持每个子群体的原始准确率（即不造成伤害），同时在指定的程度上实现一组分组公平性定义。为此，我们设计了一个整数规划（IP）过程，为每个训练样本分配弃权决定，以满足一系列约束条件。为了将弃权决定推广到测试样本，我们随后基于IP解决方案，以端到端的方式训练一个代理模型来学习弃权决定。我们分析了IP过程的可行性，以确定不同不公平容忍度和准确率约束水平下可能的弃权率，从而实现不造成伤害的目标。据我们所知，这项工作是首次识别约束参数与所需弃权率之间的理论关系。由于缺乏人力资源，高弃权率在实践中往往是不可行的。我们的框架在相似的弃权率下，在不牺牲准确性的情况下优于现有方法，同时在公平性差异方面表现出色。

[No.71] Prediction Error-based Classification for Class-Incremental Learning
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: classif
标题: 基于预测误差的分类方法在类增量学习中的应用
类别: transfer learning, meta learning, and lifelong learning
作者: Michał Zając;Tinne Tuytelaars;Gido M van de Ven;
机构: FAR AI;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2024/poster/19144
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2305.18806
英摘: Class-incremental learning (CIL) is a particularly challenging variant of continual learning, where the goal is to learn to discriminate between all classes presented in an incremental fashion. Existing approaches often suffer from excessive forgetting and imbalance of the scores assigned to classes that have not been seen together during training. In this study, we introduce a novel approach, Prediction Error-based Classification (PEC), which differs from traditional discriminative and generative classification paradigms. PEC computes a class score by measuring the prediction error of a model trained to replicate the outputs of a frozen random neural network on data from that class. The method can be interpreted as approximating a classification rule based on Gaussian Process posterior variance. PEC offers several practical advantages, including sample efficiency, ease of tuning, and effectiveness even when data are presented one class at a time. Our empirical results show that PEC performs strongly in single-pass-through-data CIL, outperforming other rehearsal-free baselines in all cases and rehearsal-based methods with moderate replay buffer size in most cases across multiple benchmarks.
中摘: 类增量学习（CIL）是一种特别具有挑战性的持续学习变体，目标是在逐步呈现的方式下学会区分所有已展示的类别。现有方法往往在训练过程中因过度遗忘和未同时出现过的类别所赋予分数的不平衡而受到影响。在这项研究中，我们提出了一种新颖的方法——基于预测误差的分类（PEC），该方法与传统的判别式和生成式分类范式不同。PEC通过计算一个类别的分数来衡量模型在训练时用于复制冻结随机神经网络输出的数据对该类别的预测误差。该方法可以被解释为基于高斯过程后验方差近似分类规则。PEC提供了几个实际优势，包括样本效率、易于调整以及即使数据一次仅按类别呈现也能表现出色。我们的实证结果显示，在单次通过数据的CIL中，PEC表现强劲，在所有基准测试中均优于其他无回放基线方法，并且在大多数情况下，其性能也超过了使用中等大小重播缓冲区的基于回放的方法。

[No.72] A Data-Driven Measure of Relative Uncertainty for Misclassification Detection
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: classif
标题: 基于数据驱动的相对不确定性度量在分类错误检测中的应用
类别: societal considerations including fairness, safety, privacy
作者: Eduardo Dadalto Câmara Gomes;Marco Romanelli;Georg Pichler;Pablo Piantanida;
机构: Vienna University of Technology;Quebec AI Institute;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2024/poster/17676
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.01710
英摘: Misclassification detection is an important problem in machine learning, as it allows for the identification of instances where the model's predictions are unreliable. However, conventional uncertainty measures such as Shannon entropy do not provide an effective way to infer the real uncertainty associated with the model's predictions. In this paper, we introduce a novel data-driven measure of uncertainty relative to an observer for misclassification detection. By learning patterns in the distribution of soft-predictions, our uncertainty measure can identify misclassified samples based on the predicted class probabilities. Interestingly, according to the proposed measure, soft-predictions corresponding to misclassified instances can carry a large amount of uncertainty, even though they may have low Shannon entropy. We demonstrate empirical improvements over multiple image classification tasks, outperforming state-of-the-art misclassification detection methods.
中摘: 误分类检测是机器学习中的一个重要问题，因为它允许识别模型预测不可靠的实例。然而，传统的不确定性度量方法如香农熵，并不能有效地推断出模型预测的真实不确定性。在本文中，我们引入了一种新的数据驱动的不确定性度量方法，该方法相对于观察者用于误分类检测。通过学习软预测分布中的模式，我们的不确定性度量可以根据预测的类别概率来识别误分类样本。有趣的是，根据提出的度量方法，即使误分类实例的软预测可能具有较低的香农熵，它们也可能携带大量的不确定性。我们在多个图像分类任务上展示了实证改进，超过了最先进的误分类检测方法。

[No.73] Dual-Encoders for Extreme Multi-label Classification
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: classif
标题: 极端多标签分类的双编码器模型
类别: unsupervised, self-supervised, semi-supervised, and supervised representation learning
作者: Nilesh Gupta;Fnu Devvrit;Ankit Singh Rawat;Srinadh Bhojanapalli;Prateek Jain;Inderjit S Dhillon;
机构: University of Texas at Austin;Google;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2024/poster/18261
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.10636
英摘: Dual-encoder (DE) models are widely used in retrieval tasks, most commonly studied on open QA benchmarks that are often characterized by multi-class and limited training data. In contrast, their performance in multi-label and data-rich retrieval settings like extreme multi-label classification (XMC), remains under-explored. Current empirical evidence indicates that DE models fall significantly short on XMC benchmarks, where SOTA methods linearly scale the number of learnable parameters with the total number of classes (documents in the corpus) by employing per-class classification head. To this end, we first study and highlight that existing multi-label contrastive training losses are not appropriate for training DE models on XMC tasks. We propose decoupled softmax loss - a simple modification to the InfoNCE loss - that overcomes the limitations of existing contrastive losses. We further extend our loss design to a soft top-k operator-based loss which is tailored to optimize top-k prediction performance. When trained with our proposed loss functions, standard DE models alone can match or outperform SOTA methods by up to 2% at Precision@1 even on the largest XMC datasets while being 20x smaller in terms of the number of trainable parameters. This leads to more parameter-efficient and universally applicable solutions for retrieval tasks. Our code and models are publicly available at https://github.com/nilesh2797/dexml.
中摘: 双编码器（DE）模型在检索任务中广泛应用，最常在开放问答基准上进行研究，这些基准通常具有多类标签和有限的训练数据。相比之下，在多标签和数据丰富的检索设置（如极端多标签分类XMC）中，它们的表现尚未得到充分探索。当前的经验证据表明，在XMC基准测试中，DE模型在可学习参数数量上显著落后于SOTA方法，这些方法通过为每个类别使用独立分类头，使可学习参数的数量与语料库中的总类别数线性增加。为此，我们首先研究并指出现有的多标签对比训练损失并不适合用于训练DE模型完成XMC任务。我们提出了一种解耦softmax损失——这是对InfoNCE损失的一种简单修改，克服了现有对比损失的局限性。进一步地，我们将损失设计扩展到基于软top-k操作符的损失，这种损失专门针对优化top-k预测性能进行了定制。使用我们提出的损失函数训练标准DE模型时，即使在最大的XMC数据集中，它们也可以在Precision@1上达到或超过SOTA方法2%的表现，同时参数量仅为SOTA方法的20倍。这导致了更高效且普遍适用的检索任务解决方案。我们的代码和模型已公开发布在https://github.com/nilesh2797/dexml。

[No.75] Conformal Prediction via Regression-as-Classification
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: classif
标题: 回归转化为分类的同构预测
类别: probabilistic methods (Bayesian methods, variational inference, sampling, UQ, etc.)
作者: Etash Kumar Guha;Shlok Natarajan;Thomas Möllenhoff;Mohammad Emtiyaz Khan;Eugene Ndiaye;
机构: Salesforce;RIKEN;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2024/poster/17674
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.08168
英摘: Conformal prediction (CP) for regression can be challenging, especially when the output distribution is heteroscedastic, multimodal, or skewed. Some of the issues can be addressed by estimating a distribution over the output, but in reality, such approaches can be sensitive to estimation error and yield unstable intervals.~Here, we circumvent the challenges by converting regression to a classification problem and then use CP for classification to obtain CP sets for regression.~To preserve the ordering of the continuous-output space, we design a new loss function and make necessary modifications to the CP classification techniques.~Empirical results on many benchmarks shows that this simple approach gives surprisingly good results on many practical problems.
中摘: 回归的同形预测（CP）可能会面临挑战，尤其是在输出分布为异方差、多模态或偏斜的情况下。一些问题可以通过估计输出分布来解决，但在实际应用中，这样的方法可能会对估计误差敏感，并产生不稳定的区间。这里，我们通过将回归问题转化为分类问题，然后使用分类的同形预测技术来获得回归的同形预测集。为了保留连续输出空间的顺序性，我们设计了一个新的损失函数并对同形预测分类技术进行了必要的修改。在许多基准测试上的实验证明，这个简单的做法在许多实际问题上表现出了令人惊讶的好效果。

[No.77] Differentiable Euler Characteristic Transforms for Shape Classification
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: classif

类别: learning on graphs and other geometries & topologies
作者: Ernst Röell;Bastian Rieck;

英摘: The Euler Characteristic Transform (ECT) has proven to be a powerful representation, combining geometrical and topological characteristics of shapes and graphs. 中摘: 欧拉特征变换（ECT）已被证明是一种强大的表示方法，能够结合形状和图的几何学和拓扑学特性。然而，ECT此前无法学习特定任务的表示。我们解决了这一问题，并开发了一种新的计算层，使ECT能够在端到端的方式下进行学习。我们的方法——可微欧拉特征变换（DECT）——速度快且计算效率高，在图和点云分类任务中表现出与更复杂的模型相当的性能。此外，我们展示了这种看似简单的统计量与更复杂的拓扑深度学习层具有相同的拓扑表达能力。

[No.78] DRSM: De-Randomized Smoothing on Malware Classifier Providing Certified Robustness
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: classif
标题: DRSM：去随机化平滑在恶意软件分类器中的应用，提供可认证的鲁棒性
类别: general machine learning (i.e., none of the above)
作者: Shoumik Saha;Wenxiao Wang;Yigitcan Kaya;Soheil Feizi;Tudor Dumitras;
机构: University of Maryland;University of California, Santa Barbara;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2024/poster/17905
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2303.13372
英摘: Machine Learning (ML) models have been utilized for malware detection for over two decades. Consequently, this ignited an ongoing arms race between malware authors and antivirus systems, compelling researchers to propose defenses for malware-detection models against evasion attacks. However, most if not all existing defenses against evasion attacks suffer from sizable performance degradation and/or can defend against only specific attacks, which makes them less practical in real-world settings. In this work, we develop a certified defense, DRSM (De-Randomized Smoothed MalConv), by redesigning the de-randomized smoothing technique for the domain of malware detection. Specifically, we propose a window ablation scheme to provably limit the impact of adversarial bytes while maximally preserving local structures of the executables. After showing how DRSM is theoretically robust against attacks with contiguous adversarial bytes, we verify its performance and certified robustness experimentally, where we observe only marginal accuracy drops as the cost of robustness. To our knowledge, we are the first to offer certified robustness in the realm of static detection of malware executables. More surprisingly, through evaluating DRSM against 9 empirical attacks of different types, we observe that the proposed defense is empirically robust to some extent against a diverse set of attacks, some of which even fall out of the scope of its original threat model. In addition, we collected 15.5K recent benign raw executables from diverse sources, which will be made public as a dataset called PACE (Publicly Accessible Collection(s) of Executables) to alleviate the scarcity of publicly available benign datasets for studying malware detection and provide future research with more representative data of the time.
中摘: 机器学习（ML）模型在反病毒系统中被用于恶意软件检测已有二十多年的历史。这导致了恶意软件作者与反病毒系统之间持续的“军备竞赛”，促使研究人员提出针对逃避攻击的恶意软件检测模型的防御措施。然而，现有的大多数逃避攻击防御措施都会遭受显著的性能下降，或者只能防御特定类型的攻击，使得它们在实际应用中不够实用。在这项工作中，我们通过重新设计去随机化平滑技术来开发了一种认证防御方法DRSM（去随机化平滑MalConv）。具体来说，我们提出了一种窗口消减方案，以证明性地限制对抗字节的影响，同时最大限度地保留可执行文件的局部结构。在理论上证明DRSM对连续对抗字节攻击具有鲁棒性之后，我们通过实验验证了其性能和认证鲁棒性，发现为了提高鲁棒性，仅观察到了微小的准确率下降。据我们所知，这是我们首次在静态检测恶意软件可执行文件的领域提供认证鲁棒性。更令人惊讶的是，在评估DRSM对抗不同类型的9个经验攻击时，我们发现提出的防御措施在一定程度上对多种攻击具有经验上的鲁棒性，甚至包括一些超出了其原始威胁模型范围的攻击。此外，我们收集了来自多样来源的15500个近期良性原始可执行文件，并将这些数据集公开发布为名为PACE（公共可访问的可执行文件集合）的数据集，以缓解研究恶意软件检测时可用的良性数据集稀缺问题，并为未来的研究提供更具代表性的当前数据。

[No.81] Elucidating the design space of classifier-guided diffusion generation
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: classif
标题: 解析分类器引导扩散生成的设计空间
类别: generative models
作者: Jiajun Ma;Tianyang Hu;Wenjia Wang;Jiacheng Sun;
机构: Hong Kong University of Science and Technology;Huawei;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2024/poster/19302
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.11311
英摘: Guidance in conditional diffusion generation is of great importance for sample quality and controllability. However, existing guidance schemes are to be desired. On one hand, mainstream methods such as classifier guidance and classifier-free guidance both require extra training with labeled data, which is time-consuming and unable to adapt to new conditions. On the other hand, training-free methods such as universal guidance, though more flexible, have yet to demonstrate comparable performance. In this work, through a comprehensive investigation into the design space, we show that it is possible to achieve significant performance improvements over existing guidance schemes by leveraging off-the-shelf classifiers in a training-free fashion, enjoying the best of both worlds. Employing calibration as a general guideline, we propose several pre-conditioning techniques to better exploit pretrained off-the-shelf classifiers for guiding diffusion generation. Extensive experiments on ImageNet validate our proposed method, showing that state-of-the-art diffusion models (DDPM, EDM, DiT) can be further improved (up to 20%) using off-the-shelf classifiers with barely any extra computational cost. With the proliferation of publicly available pretrained classifiers, our proposed approach has great potential and can be readily scaled up to text-to-image generation tasks. The code is available at https://github.com/AlexMaOLS/EluCD/tree/main.
中摘: 条件扩散生成的指导对于样本质量和可控性非常重要。然而，现有的指导方案仍有改进空间。一方面，主流方法如分类器指导和无分类器指导都需要额外使用标记数据进行训练，这既耗时又难以适应新的条件。另一方面，无需训练的方法如通用指导虽然更具灵活性，但尚未展现出可比的性能。在本工作中，通过全面探索设计空间，我们展示了可以通过以训练免费的方式利用现成的分类器来显著提高现有指导方案的性能，从而兼得两者的优点。我们采用校准作为一般准则，提出了一些预处理技术，以更好地利用预训练的现成分类器来引导扩散生成。在ImageNet上的广泛实验验证了我们提出的这种方法，表明最先进的扩散模型（DDPM、EDM、DiT）可以仅通过微乎其微的额外计算成本就进一步提高性能（最多可达20%）。随着公共预训练分类器的普及，我们提出的方法具有巨大的潜力，并且可以很容易地扩展应用于文本到图像生成任务。相关代码可在https://github.com/AlexMaOLS/EluCD/tree/main获取。

[No.82] A Simple Interpretable Transformer for Fine-Grained Image Classification and Analysis
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: classif
标题: 一种简单的可解释Transformer模型用于细粒度图像分类与分析
类别: visualization or interpretation of learned representations
作者: DIPANJYOTI PAUL;Arpita Chowdhury;Xinqi Xiong;Feng-Ju Chang;David Edward Carlyn;Samuel Stevens;Kaiya L Provost;Anuj Karpatne;Bryan Carstens;Daniel Rubenstein;Charles Stewart;Tanya Berger-Wolf;Yu Su;Wei-Lun Chao;
机构: Ohio State University;Amazon;Adelphi University;Virginia Tech;Princeton University;Rensselaer Polytechnic Institute;Microsoft;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2024/poster/18324
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.04157
英摘: We present a novel usage of Transformers to make image classification interpretable. Unlike mainstream classifiers that wait until the last fully connected layer to incorporate class information to make predictions, we investigate a proactive approach, asking each class to search for itself in an image. We realize this idea via a Transformer encoder-decoder inspired by DEtection TRansformer (DETR). We learn "class-specific" queries (one for each class) as input to the decoder, enabling each class to localize its patterns in an image via cross-attention. We name our approach INterpretable TRansformer (INTR), which is fairly easy to implement and exhibits several compelling properties. We show that INTR intrinsically encourages each class to attend distinctively; the cross-attention weights thus provide a faithful interpretation of the prediction. Interestingly, via "multi-head" cross-attention, INTR could identify different "attributes" of a class, making it particularly suitable for fine-grained classification and analysis, which we demonstrate on eight datasets. Our code and pre-trained models are publicly accessible at the Imageomics Institute GitHub site: https://github.com/Imageomics/INTR.
中摘: 我们提出了一种新颖的Transformer使用方法，使其图像分类具有可解释性。与主流分类器在最后一层全连接层才将类别信息纳入预测不同，我们研究了一种积极的方法，让每个类别自己在图像中寻找其特征。我们通过借鉴DEtection TRansformer (DETR) 的Transformer编码器-解码器结构来实现这一想法。我们学习“类别特定”的查询（每个类别一个），将其作为解码器的输入，使每个类别能够通过交叉注意力机制在图像中定位其特征模式。我们将这种方法命名为INterpretable TRansformer (INTR)，它实现起来相对简单，并且具有几个令人信服的特性。我们展示了INTR固有地鼓励每个类别关注不同的特征；交叉注意力权重因此提供了对预测的忠实解释。有趣的是，通过“多头”交叉注意力，INTR能够识别出类别的不同“属性”，使其特别适用于细粒度分类和分析，我们在八个数据集上进行了演示。我们的代码和预训练模型可以在Imageomics Institute的GitHub站点上公开访问：https://github.com/Imageomics/INTR。

[No.83] Follow-Up Differential Descriptions: Language Models Resolve Ambiguities for Image Classification
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: classif
标题: 后续差异描述：语言模型解决图像分类中的歧义问题
类别: representation learning for computer vision, audio, language, and other modalities
作者: Reza Esfandiarpoor;Stephen Bach;
机构: Snorkel AI;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2024/poster/18158
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.07593
英摘: A promising approach for improving the performance of vision-language models like CLIP for image classification is to extend the class descriptions (i.e., prompts) with related attributes, e.g., using brown sparrow instead of sparrow. However, current zero-shot methods select a subset of attributes regardless of commonalities between the target classes, potentially providing no useful information that would have helped to distinguish between them. For instance, they may use color instead of bill shape to distinguish between sparrows and wrens, which are both brown. We propose Follow-up Differential Descriptions (FuDD), a zero-shot approach that tailors the class descriptions to each dataset and leads to additional attributes that better differentiate the target classes. FuDD first identifies the ambiguous classes for each image, and then uses a Large Language Model (LLM) to generate new class descriptions that differentiate between them. The new class descriptions resolve the initial ambiguity and help predict the correct label. In our experiments, FuDD consistently outperforms generic description ensembles and naive LLM-generated descriptions on 12 datasets. We show that differential descriptions are an effective tool to resolve class ambiguities, which otherwise significantly degrade the performance. We also show that high quality natural language class descriptions produced by FuDD result in comparable performance to few-shot adaptation methods.
中摘: 改进像CLIP这样的视觉-语言模型在图像分类中的性能的一种有前景的方法是扩展类描述（即提示），例如使用“棕色斑鸠”而不是“斑鸠”。然而，当前的零样本方法可能会根据目标类之间的相似性选择一部分属性，这可能导致提供的信息没有帮助区分这些类。例如，它们可能通过颜色而不是喙形来区分斑鸠和鹪鹩，尽管两者都是棕色的。我们提出了一种名为Follow-up Differential Descriptions（FuDD）的零样本方法，该方法针对每个数据集定制类描述，并产生更好的区分目标类的新属性。FuDD首先识别每张图像中的模糊类，然后利用大型语言模型（LLM）生成新的类描述，以区分这些模糊类。新的类描述解决了初始的模糊性，并有助于预测正确的标签。在我们的实验中，FuDD在12个数据集上始终优于通用描述集合和简单的LLM生成描述。我们展示了差分描述是一种有效的工具，用于解决类模糊性，否则会显著降低性能。我们还展示了由FuDD生成的高质量自然语言类描述可以达到与少量样本适应方法相当的性能。

[No.84] PerceptionCLIP: Visual Classification by Inferring and Conditioning on Contexts
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: classif
标题: 感知CLIP：通过推理和条件化背景进行视觉分类
类别: representation learning for computer vision, audio, language, and other modalities
作者: Bang An;Sicheng Zhu;Michael-Andrei Panaitescu-Liess;Chaithanya Kumar Mummadi;Furong Huang;
机构: University of Maryland;Bosch Center for Artificial Intelligence;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2024/poster/19552
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2308.01313
英摘: Vision-language models like CLIP are widely used in zero-shot image classification due to their ability to understand various visual concepts and natural language descriptions. However, how to fully leverage CLIP's unprecedented human-like understanding capabilities to achieve better performance is still an open question. This paper draws inspiration from the human visual perception process: when classifying an object, humans first infer contextual attributes (e.g., background and orientation) which help separate the foreground object from the background, and then classify the object based on this information. Inspired by it, we observe that providing CLIP with contextual attributes improves zero-shot image classification and mitigates reliance on spurious features. We also observe that CLIP itself can reasonably infer the attributes from an image. With these observations, we propose a training-free, two-step zero-shot classification method PerceptionCLIP. Given an image, it first infers contextual attributes (e.g., background) and then performs object classification conditioning on them. Our experiments show that PerceptionCLIP achieves better generalization, group robustness, and interoperability. Our code is available at https://github.com/umd-huang-lab/perceptionCLIP
中摘: 像CLIP这样的视觉-语言模型在零样本图像分类中得到了广泛应用，因为它们能够理解各种视觉概念和自然语言描述。然而，如何充分利用CLIP前所未有的类人理解能力以实现更好的性能仍然是一个开放的问题。本文从人类视觉感知过程汲取灵感：当对一个物体进行分类时，人类首先推断出上下文属性（例如背景和方向），这些属性有助于将前景物体与背景区分开来，然后基于这些信息对物体进行分类。受到这一过程的启发，我们观察到向CLIP提供上下文属性可以提高零样本图像分类性能，并减少对虚假特征的依赖。我们还观察到，CLIP本身可以从图像中合理地推断出这些属性。基于这些观察，我们提出了一种无需训练的两步零样本分类方法——PerceptionCLIP。给定一张图像，它首先推断出上下文属性（例如背景），然后根据这些属性进行物体分类。我们的实验表明，PerceptionCLIP在泛化能力、组鲁棒性和互操作性方面表现更好。我们的代码可在https://github.com/umd-huang-lab/perceptionCLIP获取。

[No.87] Mirage: Model-agnostic Graph Distillation for Graph Classification
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: classif
标题: 幻象：一种模型无关的图蒸馏方法用于图分类
类别: learning on graphs and other geometries & topologies
作者: Mridul Gupta;Sahil Manchanda;HARIPRASAD KODAMANA;Sayan Ranu;
机构: Indian Institute of Technology Delhi;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2024/poster/19373
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.09486
英摘: GNNs, like other deep learning models, are data and computation hungry. There is a pressing need to scale training of GNNs on large datasets to enable their usage on low-resource environments. Graph distillation is an effort in that direction with the aim to construct a smaller synthetic training set from the original training data without significantly compromising model performance. While initial efforts are promising, this work is motivated by two key observations: (1) Existing graph distillation algorithms themselves rely on training with the full dataset, which undermines the very premise of graph distillation. (2) The distillation process is specific to the target GNN architecture and hyper-parameters and thus not robust to changes in the modeling pipeline. We circumvent these limitations by designing a distillation algorithm called Mirage for graph classification. Mirage is built on the insight that a message-passing GNN decomposes the input graph into a multiset of computation trees. Furthermore, the frequency distribution of computation trees is often skewed in nature, enabling us to condense this data into a concise distilled summary. By compressing the computation data itself, as opposed to emulating gradient flows on the original training set-a prevalent approach to date-Mirage transforms into an unsupervised and architecture-agnostic distillation algorithm. Extensive benchmarking on real-world datasets underscores Mirage's superiority, showcasing enhanced generalization accuracy, data compression, and distillation efficiency when compared to state-of-the-art baselines.
中摘: GNNs，和其他深度学习模型一样，对数据和计算的需求很大。在大规模数据集上扩展GNNs的训练以使其能够在资源有限的环境中使用，这是一个迫切需要解决的问题。图蒸馏是朝着这个方向努力的一种方法，其目标是从原始训练数据中构建一个较小的合成训练集，而不显著牺牲模型性能。尽管早期的努力前景看好，但这项工作受到两个关键观察的启发：（1）现有的图蒸馏算法本身依赖于使用完整数据集进行训练，这实际上否定了图蒸馏的基本前提。（2）蒸馏过程与目标GNN架构和超参数密切相关，因此对建模管道的变化不够稳健。我们通过设计一种名为Mirage的图分类蒸馏算法来克服这些限制。Mirage基于这样一个洞察：消息传递GNN将输入图分解为多个计算树集合。此外，计算树的频率分布往往具有偏斜性，使我们可以将这些数据凝练成简洁的蒸馏总结。通过直接压缩计算数据本身，而不是像迄今为止常见的那样在原始训练集上模拟梯度流，Mirage转变为一种无监督且架构无关的蒸馏算法。在实际数据集上的广泛基准测试表明，与最先进的基线相比，Mirage在泛化准确性、数据压缩和蒸馏效率方面表现出优越性。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.88] Gen-Z: Generative Zero-Shot Text Classification with Contextualized Label Descriptions
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: classif
标题: Gen-Z：生成型零样本文本分类，带有上下文化标签描述
类别: representation learning for computer vision, audio, language, and other modalities
作者: Sachin Kumar;Chan Young Park;Yulia Tsvetkov;
机构: Allen Institute for Artificial Intelligence;Carnegie Mellon University;University of Washington;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2024/poster/17686
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.07115
英摘: Language model (LM) prompting--a popular paradigm for solving NLP tasks--has been shown to be susceptible to miscalibration and brittleness to slight prompt variations, caused by its discriminative prompting approach, i.e., predicting the label given the input. To address these issues, we propose Gen-Z--a generative prompting framework for zero-shot text classification. GEN-Z is generative, as it measures the LM likelihood of input text, conditioned on natural language descriptions of labels. The framework is multivariate, as label descriptions allow us to seamlessly integrate additional contextual information about the labels to improve task performance. On various standard classification benchmarks, with six open-source LM families, we show that zero-shot classification with simple contextualization of the data source of the evaluation set consistently outperforms both zero-shot and few-shot baselines while improving robustness to prompt variations. Further, our approach enables personalizing classification in a zero-shot manner by incorporating author, subject, or reader information in the label descriptions.
中摘: 语言模型（LM）提示——一种解决NLP任务的流行范式——已被证明对校准错误和轻微提示变化的脆弱性较为敏感，这主要是由于其区分性提示方法，即根据输入预测标签。为了解决这些问题，我们提出了一种名为Gen-Z的生成提示框架，用于零样本文本分类。Gen-Z是生成性的，因为它在给定自然语言标签描述的情况下测量输入文本的语言模型概率。该框架是多变量的，因为标签描述使我们能够无缝地整合关于标签的额外上下文信息，从而提高任务性能。在各种标准分类基准上，使用六种开源LM家族，我们展示了通过对评估集数据源进行简单的上下文化，零样本分类始终优于零样本和少量样本基线，并且提高了对提示变化的鲁棒性。此外，我们的方法通过将作者、主题或读者信息纳入标签描述，使零样本分类实现个性化。

[No.89] Hyper Evidential Deep Learning to Quantify Composite Classification Uncertainty
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: classif
标题: 基于高度证据深学习的复合分类不确定性量化
类别: general machine learning (i.e., none of the above)
作者: Changbin Li;Kangshuo Li;Yuzhe Ou;Lance M. Kaplan;Audun Jøsang;Jin-Hee Cho;DONG HYUN JEONG;Feng Chen;
机构: University of Texas at Dallas;US Army Research Laboratory;University of Oslo;Virginia Tech;University of the District of Columbia;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2024/poster/19273
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.10980
英摘: Deep neural networks (DNNs) have been shown to perform well on exclusive, multi-class classification tasks. However, when different classes have similar visual features, it becomes challenging for human annotators to differentiate them. This scenario necessitates the use of composite class labels. In this paper, we propose a novel framework called Hyper-Evidential Neural Network (HENN) that explicitly models predictive uncertainty due to composite class labels in training data in the context of the belief theory called Subjective Logic (SL). By placing a grouped Dirichlet distribution on the class probabilities, we treat predictions of a neural network as parameters of hyper-subjective opinions and learn the network that collects both single and composite evidence leading to these hyper-opinions by a deterministic DNN from data. We introduce a new uncertainty type called vagueness originally designed for hyper-opinions in SL to quantify composite classification uncertainty for DNNs. Our results demonstrate that HENN outperforms its state-of-the-art counterparts based on four image datasets. The code and datasets are available at: https://github.com/Hugo101/HyperEvidentialNN.
中摘: 深度神经网络（DNNs）在独占的多类分类任务中表现出色。然而，当不同类别的视觉特征相似时，人类注释者很难区分它们。在这种情况下，需要使用复合类别标签。本文提出了一种名为超证据神经网络（HENN）的新框架，在信念理论称为主观逻辑（SL）的背景下，明确地对训练数据中的复合类别标签导致的预测不确定性进行了建模。通过在类别概率上放置一个分组狄利克雷分布，我们将神经网络的预测视为超主观意见的参数，并通过确定性DNN从数据中学习能够收集单个和复合证据以形成这些超意见的网络。我们引入了一种新的不确定性类型——模糊性，最初设计用于SL中的超意见，以量化DNNs的复合分类不确定性。我们的结果显示，HENN在四个图像数据集上优于其基于最新技术的同类方法。相关代码和数据集可在以下链接获取：https://github.com/Hugo101/HyperEvidentialNN。

[No.93] Enhancing Instance-Level Image Classification with Set-Level Labels
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: classif
标题: 基于集合级标签增强实例级图像分类
类别: unsupervised, self-supervised, semi-supervised, and supervised representation learning
作者: Renyu Zhang;Aly A Khan;Yuxin Chen;Robert L. Grossman;
机构: University of Chicago;Toyota Technological Institute at Chicago;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2024/poster/19254
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.05659
英摘: Instance-level image classification tasks have traditionally relied on single-instance labels to train models, e.g., few-shot learning and transfer learning. However, set-level coarse-grained labels that capture relationships among instances can provide richer information in real-world scenarios. In this paper, we present a novel approach to enhance instance-level image classification by leveraging set-level labels. We provide a theoretical analysis of the proposed method, including recognition conditions for fast excess risk rate, shedding light on the theoretical foundations of our approach. We conducted experiments on two distinct categories of datasets: natural image datasets and histopathology image datasets. Our experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach, showcasing improved classification performance compared to traditional single-instance label-based methods. Notably, our algorithm achieves 13% improvement in classification accuracy compared to the strongest baseline on the histopathology image classification benchmarks. Importantly, our experimental findings align with the theoretical analysis, reinforcing the robustness and reliability of our proposed method. This work bridges the gap between instance-level and set-level image classification, offering a promising avenue for advancing the capabilities of image classification models with set-level coarse-grained labels.
中摘: 实例级别的图像分类任务 traditionally 一直依赖于单实例标签来训练模型，例如少量样本学习和迁移学习。然而，在现实世界场景中，集级别的粗粒度标签能够捕捉实例之间的关系，提供更为丰富的信息。在本文中，我们提出了一种新的方法，通过利用集级别标签来增强实例级别的图像分类。我们对所提出的方法进行了理论分析，包括快速超额风险率的识别条件，从而阐明了我们方法的理论基础。我们在两类不同的数据集上进行了实验：自然图像数据集和组织病理学图像数据集。我们的实验结果表明，该方法的有效性，展示了与传统基于单实例标签的方法相比改进的分类性能。值得注意的是，我们的算法在组织病理学图像分类基准测试中相较于最强的基线方法实现了13%的分类准确率提升。重要的是，我们的实验发现与理论分析一致，这进一步强化了我们所提出方法的稳健性和可靠性。这项工作填补了实例级别和集级别图像分类之间的空白，为使用集级别粗粒度标签提升图像分类模型的能力提供了有前景的发展途径。

[No.95] The optimality of kernel classifiers in Sobolev space
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: classif
标题: 核分类器在Sobolev空间中的最优性
类别: learning theory
作者: Jianfa Lai;zhifan Li;Dongming Huang;Qian Lin;
机构: Tsinghua University;Beijing Institute of Mathematical Sciences and Applications;National University of Singapore;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2024/poster/18930
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.01148
英摘: Kernel methods are widely used in machine learning, especially for classification problems. However, the theoretical analysis of kernel classification is still limited. This paper investigates the statistical performances of kernel classifiers. With some mild assumptions on the conditional probability $η(x)=\mathbb{P}(Y=1\mid X=x)$, we derive an upper bound on the classification excess risk of a kernel classifier using recent advances in the theory of kernel regression. We also obtain a minimax lower bound for Sobolev spaces, which shows the optimality of the proposed classifier. Our theoretical results can be extended to the generalization error of overparameterized neural network classifiers. To make our theoretical results more applicable in realistic settings, we also propose a simple method to estimate the interpolation smoothness of $2η(x)-1$ and apply the method to real datasets.
中摘: 核方法在机器学习中被广泛应用于分类问题，然而对于核分类器的理论分析仍然有限。本文探讨了核分类器的统计性能。通过在条件概率 $\eta(x) = \mathbb{P}(Y=1 \mid X=x)$ 方面提出一些温和的假设，我们利用核回归理论的最新进展推导出了核分类器分类超额风险的一个上界。此外，我们还得到了Sobolev空间中的最小最大下界，这表明所提出的分类器具有最优性。我们的理论结果可以扩展到过参数化神经网络分类器的一般化误差。为了使我们的理论结果更适用于实际场景，我们还提出了一种简单的方法来估计 $2\eta(x)-1$ 的插值光滑度，并将该方法应用到真实数据集上。

[No.97] MuSc: Zero-Shot Industrial Anomaly Classification and Segmentation with Mutual Scoring of the Unlabeled Images
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: classif
标题: MuSc：基于未标注图像相互评分的零样本工业异常分类与分割
类别: general machine learning (i.e., none of the above)
作者: Xurui Li;Ziming Huang;Feng Xue;Yu Zhou;
机构: Huazhong University of Science and Technology;University of Trento;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2024/poster/19269
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.16753
英摘: This paper studies zero-shot anomaly classification (AC) and segmentation (AS) in industrial vision. We reveal that the abundant normal and abnormal cues implicit in unlabeled test images can be exploited for anomaly determination, which is ignored by prior methods. Our key observation is that for the industrial product images, the normal image patches could find a relatively large number of similar patches in other unlabeled images, while the abnormal ones only have a few similar patches. We leverage such a discriminative characteristic to design a novel zero-shot AC/AS method by Mutual Scoring (MuSc) of the unlabeled images, which does not need any training or prompts. Specifically, we perform Local Neighborhood Aggregation with Multiple Degrees (LNAMD) to obtain the patch features that are capable of representing anomalies in varying sizes. Then we propose the Mutual Scoring Mechanism (MSM) to leverage the unlabeled test images to assign the anomaly score to each other. Furthermore, we present an optimization approach named Re-scoring with Constrained Image-level Neighborhood (RsCIN) for image-level anomaly classification to suppress the false positives caused by noises in normal images. The superior performance on the challenging MVTec AD and VisA datasets demonstrates the effectiveness of our approach. Compared with the state-of-the-art zero-shot approaches, MuSc achieves a $\textbf{21.1%}$ PRO absolute gain (from 72.7% to 93.8%) on MVTec AD, a $\textbf{19.4%}$ pixel-AP gain and a $\textbf{14.7%}$ pixel-AUROC gain on VisA. In addition, our zero-shot approach outperforms most of the few-shot approaches and is comparable to some one-class methods. Code is available at https://github.com/xrli-U/MuSc.
中摘: 这篇论文研究了工业视觉中的零样本异常分类（AC）和分割（AS）。我们揭示了未标记测试图像中隐含的丰富正常和异常线索可以被利用来进行异常确定，而这一点在先前的方法中被忽视了。我们的关键观察是，在工业产品图像中，正常图像块可以在其他未标记图像中找到相对较多的相似图像块，而异常图像块则只有少量的相似图像块。我们利用这种区分性特征设计了一种新的零样本AC/AS方法——互评分（MuSc）机制，该方法无需任何训练或提示。具体而言，我们采用了多种程度的局部邻域聚合（LNAMD）来获取能够表示不同大小异常的图像块特征。然后我们提出了互评分机制（MSM），利用未标记的测试图像相互评分以分配异常分数。此外，我们还提出了一种名为受约束图像级邻域重新评分（RsCIN）的优化方法，用于图像级异常分类，以抑制由正常图像噪声引起的假阳性。在具有挑战性的MVTec AD和VisA数据集上的优越性能证明了我们方法的有效性。与最先进的零样本方法相比，MuSc在MVTec AD数据集上实现了21.1%的PRO绝对增益（从72.7%提高到93.8%），在VisA数据集上实现了19.4%的像素AP增益和14.7%的像素AUROC增益。此外，我们的零样本方法优于大多数少样本方法，并且与一些单类方法相当。代码可在https://github.com/xrli-U/MuSc获取。

[No.98] Leveraging Uncertainty Estimates To Improve Classifier Performance
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: classif
标题: 利用不确定性估计提高分类器性能
类别: probabilistic methods (Bayesian methods, variational inference, sampling, UQ, etc.)
作者: Gundeep Arora;Srujana Merugu;Anoop Saladi;Rajeev Rastogi;
机构: Amazon;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2024/poster/17840
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.11723
英摘: Binary classification involves predicting the label of an instance based on whether the model score for the positive class exceeds a threshold chosen based on the application requirements (e.g., maximizing recall for a precision bound). However, model scores are often not aligned with the true positivity rate. This is especially true when the training involves a differential sampling across classes or there is distributional drift between train and test settings. In this paper, we provide theoretical analysis and empirical evidence of the dependence of model score estimation bias on both uncertainty and score itself. Further, we formulate the decision boundary selection in terms of both model score and uncertainty, prove that it is NP-hard, and present algorithms based on dynamic programming and isotonic regression. Evaluation of the proposed algorithms on three real-world datasets yield 25%-40% gain in recall at high precision bounds over the traditional approach of using model score alone, highlighting the benefits of leveraging uncertainty.
中摘: 二分类涉及基于正类模型评分是否超过某个阈值（该阈值根据应用需求选择，例如在精度限制下最大化召回率）来预测实例的标签。然而，模型评分往往与真正的阳性率不一致。特别是在训练过程中不同类别间存在差异性采样或训练和测试设置之间的分布漂移时，这种不一致性尤为明显。在本文中，我们提供了关于模型评分估计偏差依赖于不确定性和评分本身的理论分析和实证证据。此外，我们将决策边界的选取表述为评分和不确定性两者的关系，并证明这是一个NP难问题，随后提出了基于动态规划和等度回归的算法。在三个实际数据集上评估所提出的算法，在高精度限制下的召回率提高了25%-40%，突显了利用不确定性的好处。

[No.99] A Call to Reflect on Evaluation Practices for Failure Detection in Image Classification
来源: ICLR 2023
命中: classif
标题: 关于图像分类中故障检测评估实践的呼吁
类别: 
作者: Paul F Jaeger;Carsten Tim Lüth;Lukas Klein;Till J. Bungert;
机构: German Cancer Research Center;Deutsches Krebsforschungszentrum;
类型: Top-5%
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2023/poster/11579
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2211.15259
英摘: Reliable application of machine learning-based decision systems in the wild is one of the major challenges currently investigated by the field. A large portion of established approaches aims to detect erroneous predictions by means of assigning confidence scores. This confidence may be obtained by either quantifying the model's predictive uncertainty, learning explicit scoring functions, or assessing whether the input is in line with the training distribution. Curiously, while these approaches all state to address the same eventual goal of detecting failures of a classifier upon real-life application, they currently constitute largely separated research fields with individual evaluation protocols, which either exclude a substantial part of relevant methods or ignore large parts of relevant failure sources. In this work, we systematically reveal current pitfalls caused by these inconsistencies and derive requirements for a holistic and realistic evaluation of failure detection. To demonstrate the relevance of this unified perspective, we present a large-scale empirical study for the first time enabling benchmarking confidence scoring functions w.r.t all relevant methods and failure sources. The revelation of a simple softmax response baseline as the overall best performing method underlines the drastic shortcomings of current evaluation in the abundance of publicized research on confidence scoring. Code and trained models are at https://github.com/IML-DKFZ/fd-shifts.
中摘: 基于机器学习的决策系统在实际应用中的可靠部署是当前研究领域面临的主要挑战之一。许多已建立的方法旨在通过分配置信度分数来检测错误预测。这种置信度可以通过量化模型的预测不确定性、学习显式的评分函数，或者评估输入是否符合训练分布来获得。有趣的是，尽管这些方法都声称要解决同一最终目标——在实际生活应用中检测分类器的失败问题，但它们目前构成了各自独立的研究领域，拥有不同的评估协议，要么排除了大量相关方法，要么忽视了大量的相关失败源。在这项工作中，我们系统地揭示了由这些不一致所导致的现有问题，并推导出全面且现实的失败检测评估要求。为了证明这种统一视角的重要性，我们首次进行了大规模的实证研究，能够对置信度评分函数进行基准测试，涵盖所有相关方法和失败源。简单softmax响应基线作为整体表现最佳的方法的揭示，突显了当前置信度评分评估中存在的严重不足之处。相关代码和训练模型可在https://github.com/IML-DKFZ/fd-shifts获取。

[No.100] Simplicial Embeddings in Self-Supervised Learning and Downstream Classification
来源: ICLR 2023
命中: classif
标题: 简化形嵌入在自我监督学习和下游分类中的应用
类别: 
作者: Samuel Lavoie;Christos Tsirigotis;Max Schwarzer;Ankit Vani;Michael Noukhovitch;Kenji Kawaguchi;Aaron Courville;
机构: Quebec Artificial Intelligence Institute;University of Montreal;Mila;National University of Singapore;Université de Montréal;
类型: Top-25%
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2023/poster/12047
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2204.00616
英摘: Simplicial Embeddings (SEM) are representations learned through self-supervised learning (SSL), wherein a representation is projected into $L$ simplices of $V$ dimensions each using a softmax operation. This procedure conditions the representation onto a constrained space during pretraining and imparts an inductive bias for group sparsity. For downstream classification, we formally prove that the SEM representation leads to better generalization than an unnormalized representation. Furthermore, we empirically demonstrate that SSL methods trained with SEMs have improved generalization on natural image datasets such as CIFAR-100 and ImageNet. Finally, when used in a downstream classification task, we show that SEM features exhibit emergent semantic coherence where small groups of learned features are distinctly predictive of semantically-relevant classes.
中摘: 简单同态嵌入（Simplicial Embeddings, SEM）是通过自我监督学习（self-supervised learning, SSL）学习到的表示，在预训练过程中，表示被投影到每个由$V$个维度构成的$L$个单纯形上，使用了softmax操作。这一过程在预训练阶段将表示条件化在一个受限的空间内，并赋予其分组稀疏性的归纳偏置。对于下游分类任务，我们正式证明，SEM表示比未归一化的表示有更好的泛化能力。此外，我们通过实验展示了使用SEM进行训练的SSL方法在自然图像数据集如CIFAR-100和ImageNet上具有更好的泛化能力。最后，当我们把这些特征用于下游分类任务时，我们发现这些SEM特征表现出新兴的语义一致性，其中一小群学到的特征明显地预测与语义相关的类别。

[No.101] Visual Classification via Description from Large Language Models
来源: ICLR 2023
命中: classif
标题: 通过大型语言模型描述进行的视觉分类
类别: 
作者: Sachit Menon;Carl Vondrick;
机构: Meta;Columbia University;
类型: Top-5%
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2023/poster/11733
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2210.07183
英摘: Vision-language models (VLMs) such as CLIP have shown promising performance on a variety of recognition tasks using the standard zero-shot classification procedure -- computing similarity between the query image and the embedded words for each category. By only using the category name, they neglect to make use of the rich context of additional information that language affords. The procedure gives no intermediate understanding of why a category is chosen, and furthermore provides no mechanism for adjusting the criteria used towards this decision. We present an alternative framework for classification with VLMs, which we call classification by description. We ask VLMs to check for descriptive features rather than broad categories: to find a tiger, look for its stripes; its claws; and more. By basing decisions on these descriptors, we can provide additional cues that encourage using the features we want to be used. In the process, we can get a clear idea of what features the model uses to construct its decision; it gains some level of inherent explainability. We query large language models (e.g., GPT-3) for these descriptors to obtain them in a scalable way. Extensive experiments show our framework has numerous advantages past interpretability. We show improvements in accuracy on ImageNet across distribution shifts; demonstrate the ability to adapt VLMs to recognize concepts unseen during training; and illustrate how descriptors can be edited to effectively mitigate bias compared to the baseline.
中摘: 视觉语言模型（VLMs）如CLIP在使用标准零样本分类程序进行各种识别任务时展现出了令人鼓舞的性能——通过计算查询图像与每个类别的嵌入单词之间的相似性。仅通过使用类别名称，它们忽略了利用语言提供的丰富上下文信息的能力。该程序无法提供关于为何选择某个类别的中间理解，并且也无法为此决策调整标准的机制。我们提出了一种视觉语言模型分类的新框架，称为描述性分类。我们要求VLMs检查描述性的特征而非宽泛的类别：要找到老虎，就要寻找它的条纹；爪子；以及其他特征。通过基于这些描述符来做决策，我们可以提供额外的提示来鼓励使用我们想要使用的特征。在这个过程中，我们可以清楚地了解模型是根据哪些特征来做决定的；从而获得一定程度的固有解释性。我们向大型语言模型（例如GPT-3）查询这些描述符以实现可扩展获取。大量的实验表明，我们的框架不仅具有解释性方面的优势，还在ImageNet数据集的不同分布转移中提高了准确率；展示了VLMs适应未见过的概念的能力；并且说明了如何通过编辑描述符有效地减轻偏差问题。

[No.102] Human-Guided Fair Classification for Natural Language Processing
来源: ICLR 2023
命中: classif
标题: 人工引导的公平分类在自然语言处理中的应用
类别: 
作者: Florian E. Dorner;Momchil Peychev;Nikola Konstantinov;Naman Goel;Elliott Ash;Martin Vechev;
机构: ETH Zurich;University of Oxford;Swiss Federal Institute of Technology;
类型: Top-25%
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2023/poster/10687
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2212.10154
英摘: Text classifiers have promising applications in high-stake tasks such as resume screening and content moderation. These classifiers must be fair and avoid discriminatory decisions by being invariant to perturbations of sensitive attributes such as gender or ethnicity. However, there is a gap between human intuition about these perturbations and the formal similarity specifications capturing them. While existing research has started to address this gap, current methods are based on hardcoded word replacements, resulting in specifications with limited expressivity or ones that fail to fully align with human intuition (e.g., in cases of asymmetric counterfactuals). This work proposes novel methods for bridging this gap by discovering expressive and intuitive individual fairness specifications. We show how to leverage unsupervised style transfer and GPT-3's zero-shot capabilities to automatically generate expressive candidate pairs of semantically similar sentences that differ along sensitive attributes. We then validate the generated pairs via an extensive crowdsourcing study, which confirms that a lot of these pairs align with human intuition about fairness in the context of toxicity classification. Finally, we show how limited amounts of human feedback can be leveraged to learn a similarity specification that can be used to train downstream fairness-aware models.
中摘: 文本分类器在诸如简历筛选和内容审核等高风险任务中具有广阔的应用前景。这些分类器必须公平，避免在敏感属性（如性别或种族）发生扰动时做出歧视性决策。然而，人们对这些扰动的人类直觉与捕捉它们的形式相似性规范之间存在差距。尽管现有研究已经开始解决这一差距，但当前的方法主要基于硬编码的单词替换，导致规范表达能力有限，或者无法完全符合人类的直觉（例如，在不对称反事实情况下）。本项工作提出了新的方法来弥合这一差距，通过发现具有表现力和直观性的个体公平性规范。我们展示了如何利用无监督风格转换和GPT-3的零样本能力，自动生成具有表现力的语义相似句子候选对，这些句子在敏感属性上有所不同。然后，我们通过一项广泛的人众外包研究验证了生成的句子对，该研究确认了许多这些句子对在毒性分类背景下与公平性的人类直觉相一致。最后，我们展示了如何利用少量的人类反馈来学习一种相似性规范，这种规范可以用于训练下游的公平性意识模型。

[No.103] Masked Unsupervised Self-training for Label-free Image Classification
来源: ICLR 2023
命中: classif
标题: 无监督掩码自我训练在无标签图像分类中的应用
类别: 
作者: Junnan Li;Silvio Savarese;Steven Hoi;
机构: Stanford University;Singapore Management University;Salesforce;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2023/poster/11882
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2206.02967
英摘: State-of-the-art computer vision models are mostly trained with supervised learning using human-labeled images, which limits their scalability due to the expensive annotation cost. While self-supervised representation learning has achieved impressive progress, it still requires a second stage of finetuning on labeled data. On the other hand, models pre-trained with large-scale text-image supervision (e.g., CLIP) have enabled zero-shot transfer to downstream image classification tasks. However, the zero-shot performance of CLIP-like models are often insufficient for real-world adoption. In this paper, we aim to leverage the abundant unlabeled data from a target domain to improve the performance of a pre-trained zero-shot classifier, by unsupervised finetuning of the pre-trained model. We propose Masked Unsupervised Self-Training (MUST), a new unsupervised adaptation method which leverages two different and complementary sources of training signals: pseudo-labels and raw images. MUST jointly optimizes three objectives to learn both class-level global feature and pixel-level local feature and enforces a regularization between the two. We demonstrate the efficacy of MUST on a variety of downstream tasks, where it improves upon CLIP by a large margin. MUST also outperforms supervised few-shot adaptation methods. It achieves a top-1 accuracy of 77.7% on ImageNet using ViT-B, +9.4% higher than CLIP, and +6.2% higher than 16-shot CLIP adaptation. Our code is available at https://github.com/salesforce/MUST.
中摘: 最先进的计算机视觉模型大多通过监督学习使用人工标注的图像进行训练，这限制了它们的扩展性，因为标注成本高昂。虽然自监督表示学习已经取得了显著的进步，但它仍然需要在标记数据上进行第二阶段的微调。另一方面，使用大规模文本-图像监督预训练（例如CLIP）的模型能够实现下游图像分类任务的零样本迁移。然而，CLIP类模型的零样本性能往往不足以满足实际应用的需求。在本文中，我们旨在利用目标领域丰富的未标注数据来提升预训练零样本分类器的性能，通过预训练模型的无监督微调来实现。我们提出了Masked Unsupervised Self-Training (MUST)，这是一种新的无监督适应方法，它利用了两种不同且互补的训练信号来源：伪标签和原始图像。MUST联合优化三个目标，以学习类级别的全局特征和像素级别的局部特征，并在两者之间施加正则化。我们在多种下游任务上展示了MUST的有效性，其性能显著优于CLIP。此外，MUST还超越了监督下的少量样本适应方法。使用ViT-B时，MUST在ImageNet上的top-1精度达到了77.7%，比CLIP高9.4%，比16次少量样本CLIP适应高6.2%。我们的代码可在https://github.com/salesforce/MUST获取。

[No.104] Automated Data Augmentations for Graph Classification
来源: ICLR 2023
命中: classif
标题: 图分类的自动化数据增强方法
类别: 
作者: Youzhi Luo;Michael Curtis McThrow;Wing Yee Au;Tao Komikado;Kanji Uchino;Koji Maruhashi;Shuiwang Ji;
机构: Texas A&M University;Fujitsu Research of America;Fujitsu Research and Development Center;Fujitsu Limited;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2202.13248
英摘: Data augmentations are effective in improving the invariance of learning machines. We argue that the core challenge of data augmentations lies in designing data transformations that preserve labels. This is relatively straightforward for images, but much more challenging for graphs. In this work, we propose GraphAug, a novel automated data augmentation method aiming at computing label-invariant augmentations for graph classification. Instead of using uniform transformations as in existing studies, GraphAug uses an automated augmentation model to avoid compromising critical label-related information of the graph, thereby producing label-invariant augmentations at most times. To ensure label-invariance, we develop a training method based on reinforcement learning to maximize an estimated label-invariance probability. Experiments show that GraphAug outperforms previous graph augmentation methods on various graph classification tasks.
中摘: 数据增强在提高学习机器的不变性方面非常有效。我们认为数据增强的核心挑战在于设计能够保留标签的数据变换。对于图像来说，这种设计相对简单，但对于图形而言，则要困难得多。在本文中，我们提出了一种名为GraphAug的新颖自动数据增强方法，旨在为图形分类计算标签不变的数据增强。与现有研究中使用的统一变换不同，GraphAug使用一个自动增强模型来避免损害图形中的关键标签相关信息，从而大多数情况下生成标签不变的数据增强。为了确保标签不变性，我们基于强化学习开发了一种训练方法，以最大化估计的标签不变性概率。实验表明，GraphAug在各种图形分类任务上优于之前的图形增强方法。

[No.105] Personalized Federated Learning with Feature Alignment and Classifier Collaboration

英摘: Data heterogeneity is one of the most challenging issues in federated learning, which motivates a variety of approaches to learn personalized models for participating clients. One such approach in deep neural networks based tasks is employing a shared feature representation and learning a customized classifier head for each client. However, previous works do not utilize the global knowledge during local representation learning and also neglect the fine-grained collaboration between local classifier heads, which limit the model generalization ability. In this work, we conduct explicit local-global feature alignment by leveraging global semantic knowledge for learning a better representation. 中摘: 数据异质性是联邦学习中最具挑战性的问题之一，这促使了各种方法来为参与的客户端学习个性化模型。在基于深度神经网络的任务中，一种方法是使用共享特征表示，并为每个客户端学习一个定制化的分类器头部。然而，以往的工作在局部特征学习过程中并未利用全局知识，也忽视了局部分类器头部之间的细粒度协作，这限制了模型的泛化能力。在本文中，我们通过利用全局语义知识来进行显式的局部-全局特征对齐，以学习更好的特征表示。此外，我们量化了每个客户端的分类器组合收益，并将其作为组合权重的函数进行推导，从而得出一个优化问题来估计最优权重。最后，我们在包含各种异质数据场景的基准数据集上的广泛评估结果表明了我们提出的方法的有效性。代码可在https://github.com/JianXu95/FedPAC获取。
