[No.106] Is the Performance of My Deep Network Too Good to Be True? A Direct Approach to Estimating the Bayes Error in Binary Classification
来源: ICLR 2023
命中: classif
标题: 我的深度网络的表现是不是太好了？一种直接估计二分类问题贝叶斯错误率的方法
类别: 
作者: Takashi Ishida;Ikko Yamane;Nontawat Charoenphakdee;Gang Niu;Masashi Sugiyama;
机构: University of Tokyo;Ecole Nationale de la Statistique et de l'Analyse de l'information;Preferred Networks, Inc.;RIKEN;
类型: Top-5%
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2023/poster/10824
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2202.00395
英摘: There is a fundamental limitation in the prediction performance that a machine learning model can achieve due to the inevitable uncertainty of the prediction target. In classification problems, this can be characterized by the Bayes error, which is the best achievable error with any classifier. The Bayes error can be used as a criterion to evaluate classifiers with state-of-the-art performance and can be used to detect test set overfitting. We propose a simple and direct Bayes error estimator, where we just take the mean of the labels that show \emph{uncertainty} of the class assignments. Our flexible approach enables us to perform Bayes error estimation even for weakly supervised data. In contrast to others, our method is model-free and even instance-free. Moreover, it has no hyperparameters and gives a more accurate estimate of the Bayes error than several baselines empirically. Experiments using our method suggest that recently proposed deep networks such as the Vision Transformer may have reached, or is about to reach, the Bayes error for benchmark datasets. Finally, we discuss how we can study the inherent difficulty of the acceptance/rejection decision for scientific articles, by estimating the Bayes error of the ICLR papers from 2017 to 2023.
中摘: 由于预测目标不可避免的不确定性，机器学习模型在预测性能上存在基本限制。在分类问题中，这种限制可以通过贝叶斯误差来表征，即任何分类器所能达到的最佳可实现错误率。贝叶斯误差可以作为评估具有先进性能的分类器的标准，并可用于检测测试集过拟合。我们提出了一种简单直接的贝叶斯误差估计方法，只需计算显示“不确定性”的类别分配标签的平均值即可。我们的灵活方法使得即使对于弱监督数据，也能进行贝叶斯误差估计。与其他方法相比，我们的方法是无模型的，甚至是无实例的。此外，它没有超参数，并且在实验中比几个基线方法提供了更准确的贝叶斯误差估计。使用我们方法的实验表明，最近提出的深度网络，如视觉变换器（Vision Transformer），可能已经达到了或即将达到基准数据集上的贝叶斯误差。最后，我们讨论了如何通过估计从2017年到2023年的ICLR论文的贝叶斯误差来研究接受/拒绝决策的固有难度，以探讨科学文章评审的内在困难。

[No.107] Imbalanced Semi-supervised Learning with Bias Adaptive Classifier
来源: ICLR 2023
命中: classif
标题: 带有偏差自适应分类器的不平衡半监督学习
类别: 
作者: Renzhen Wang;Xixi Jia;Quanziang Wang;Yichen Wu;Deyu Meng;
机构: Xi'an Jiao Tong University;Xidian University;City University of Hong Kong;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2023/poster/11315
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2207.13856
英摘: Pseudo-labeling has proven to be a promising semi-supervised learning (SSL) paradigm. Existing pseudo-labeling methods commonly assume that the class distributions of training data are balanced. However, such an assumption is far from realistic scenarios and thus severely limits the performance of current pseudo-labeling methods under the context of class-imbalance. To alleviate this problem, we design a bias adaptive classifier that targets the imbalanced SSL setups. The core idea is to automatically assimilate the training bias caused by class imbalance via the bias adaptive classifier, which is composed of a novel bias attractor and the original linear classifier. The bias attractor is designed as a light-weight residual network and optimized through a bi-level learning framework. Such a learning strategy enables the bias adaptive classifier to fit imbalanced training data, while the linear classifier can provide unbiased label prediction for each class. We conduct extensive experiments under various imbalanced semi-supervised setups, and the results demonstrate that our method can be applied to different pseudo-labeling models and is superior to current state-of-the-art methods.
中摘: 伪标签方法已被证明是一种有前景的半监督学习（SSL）范式。现有的伪标签方法通常假设训练数据的类别分布是平衡的。然而，这种假设与现实场景相差甚远，因此严重限制了当前伪标签方法在类别不平衡情境下的性能。为了解决这一问题，我们设计了一种适应性偏差分类器，专门针对不平衡的半监督学习设置。核心思想是通过适应性偏差分类器自动吸收由类别不平衡引起的训练偏差，该分类器由一个新颖的偏差吸引子和原始线性分类器组成。偏差吸引子被设计为一个轻量级的残差网络，并通过双层学习框架进行优化。这种学习策略使适应性偏差分类器能够拟合不平衡的训练数据，而线性分类器则可以为每个类别提供无偏的标签预测。我们在各种不平衡的半监督设置下进行了广泛的实验，结果表明，我们的方法可以应用于不同的伪标签模型，并且优于当前最先进的方法。

[No.108] TabPFN: A Transformer That Solves Small Tabular Classification Problems in a Second
来源: ICLR 2023
命中: classif
标题: TabPFN：一个可在一秒钟内解决小型表格分类问题的变压器模型
类别: 
作者: Noah Hollmann;Samuel Müller;Katharina Eggensperger;Frank Hutter;
机构: Charite Universitätsmedizin Berlin;University of Freiburg;Eberhard Karls University of Tübingen;Albert-Ludwigs-Universität Freiburg;
类型: Top-25%
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2023/poster/12113
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2207.01848
英摘: We present TabPFN, a trained Transformer that can do supervised classification for small tabular datasets in less than a second, needs no hyperparameter tuning and is competitive with state-of-the-art classification methods. TabPFN performs in-context learning (ICL), it learns to make predictions using sequences of labeled examples (x, f(x)) given in the input, without requiring further parameter updates. TabPFN is fully entailed in the weights of our network, which accepts training and test samples as a set-valued input and yields predictions for the entire test set in a single forward pass. TabPFN is a Prior-Data Fitted Network (PFN) and is trained offline once, to approximate Bayesian inference on synthetic datasets drawn from our prior. This prior incorporates ideas from causal reasoning: It entails a large space of structural causal models with a preference for simple structures. On the 18 datasets in the OpenML-CC18 suite that contain up to 1 000 training data points, up to 100 purely numerical features without missing values, and up to 10 classes, we show that our method clearly outperforms boosted trees and performs on par with complex state-of-the-art AutoML systems with up to 230$\times$ speedup. This increases to a 5 700$\times$ speedup when using a GPU. We also validate these results on an additional 67 small numerical datasets from OpenML. We provide all our code, the trained TabPFN, an interactive browser demo and a Colab notebook at https://github.com/automl/TabPFN.
中摘: 我们介绍了TabPFN，这是一种经过训练的Transformer模型，能够在不到一秒钟的时间内对小型表格数据集进行监督分类，无需进行超参数调整，并且与最先进的分类方法具有竞争力。TabPFN 可以进行上下文学习（ICL），它通过输入中的标记示例序列（x, f(x)）来学习如何做出预测，而不需要进一步更新参数。我们的网络接受训练样本和测试样本作为集合输入，并在一次前向传播中为整个测试集生成预测。TabPFN 是一个先验数据拟合网络（PFN），并且仅在线下训练一次，以在来自我们先验的合成数据集上近似贝叶斯推断。这个先验包含了因果推理的想法：它偏好简单结构，涵盖了大量结构性因果模型。在OpenML-CC18套件中的18个数据集中，每个数据集包含最多1000个训练数据点、最多100个纯数值特征且无缺失值，以及最多10个类别，我们展示了该方法明显优于提升树，并且与复杂的先进自动化机器学习系统相比表现相当，速度提升可达230倍。使用GPU时，速度提升可高达5700倍。此外，我们在OpenML的额外67个小规模数值数据集中验证了这些结果。我们已在https://github.com/automl/TabPFN 提供了所有代码、训练好的TabPFN、交互式浏览器演示以及Colab笔记本。

[No.110] LPT: Long-tailed Prompt Tuning for Image Classification
来源: ICLR 2023
命中: classif
标题: 长尾提示调优在图像分类中的应用
类别: 
作者: Bowen Dong;Pan Zhou;Shuicheng YAN;Wangmeng Zuo;
机构: Harbin Institute of Technology;Sea Group;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2023/poster/12129
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2210.01033
英摘: For long-tailed classification, most works often pretrain a big model on a large-scale dataset, and then fine-tune the whole model for adapting to long-tailed data. Though promising, fine-tuning the whole pretrained model tends to suffer from high cost in computation and deployment of different models for different tasks, as well as weakened generalization ability for overfitting to certain features of long-tailed data. To alleviate these issues, we propose an effective Long-tailed Prompt Tuning method for long-tailed classification. LPT introduces several trainable prompts into a frozen pretrained model to adapt it to long-tailed data. For better effectiveness, we divide prompts into two groups: 1) a shared prompt for the whole long-tailed dataset to learn general features and to adapt a pretrained model into target domain; and 2) group-specific prompts to gather group-specific features for the samples which have similar features and also to empower the pretrained model with discrimination ability. Then we design a two-phase training paradigm to learn these prompts. In phase 1, we train the shared prompt via supervised prompt tuning to adapt a pretrained model to the desired long-tailed domain. In phase 2, we use the learnt shared prompt as query to select a small best matched set for a group of similar samples from the group-specific prompt set to dig the common features of these similar samples, then optimize these prompts with dual sampling strategy and asymmetric GCL loss. By only fine-tuning a few prompts while fixing the pretrained model, LPT can reduce training and deployment cost by storing a few prompts, and enjoys a strong generalization ability of the pretrained model. Experiments show that on various long-tailed benchmarks, with only ~1.1% extra parameters, LPT achieves comparable performance than previous whole model fine-tuning methods, and is more robust to domain-shift.
中摘: 对于长尾分类问题，大多数研究工作通常会在大规模数据集上预先训练一个大型模型，然后对整个模型进行微调以适应长尾数据。尽管这种方法前景看好，但对整个预训练模型进行微调往往会面临计算和部署不同任务的不同模型时成本高昂的问题，以及由于过度拟合长尾数据的某些特征而导致的一般化能力减弱。为了解决这些问题，我们提出了一种有效的长尾提示调优（LPT）方法，用于长尾分类。LPT将几个可训练的提示引入冻结的预训练模型，使其能够适应长尾数据。为了提高效果，我们将提示分为两组：1）一种适用于整个长尾数据集的共享提示，用于学习通用特征并使预训练模型适应目标领域；2）针对具有相似特征的样本的组别特定提示，用于收集这些样本的特定特征，并增强预训练模型的辨别能力。然后我们设计了两阶段训练范式来学习这些提示。在第一阶段，我们通过监督提示调优训练共享提示，以使预训练模型适应所需的长尾领域。在第二阶段，我们使用学到的共享提示作为查询，从组别特定提示集合中选择与一组相似样本最佳匹配的小型子集，挖掘这些相似样本的共同特征，然后使用双重采样策略和不对称GCL损失优化这些提示。通过仅微调几个提示而固定预训练模型，LPT可以降低训练和部署成本，同时保持预训练模型的强大一般化能力。实验表明，在各种长尾基准测试中，仅增加约1.1%的额外参数，LPT就能达到与之前整个模型微调方法相当的性能，并且对领域转移更具鲁棒性。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.111] FiT: Parameter Efficient Few-shot Transfer Learning for Personalized and Federated Image Classification
来源: ICLR 2023
命中: classif
标题: FiT：参数高效少量样本迁移学习在个性化和联邦图像分类中的应用
类别: 
作者: Aliaksandra Shysheya;John F Bronskill;Massimiliano Patacchiola;Sebastian Nowozin;Richard E Turner;
机构: University of Cambridge;Microsoft;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2023/poster/11913
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2206.08671
英摘: Modern deep learning systems are increasingly deployed in situations such as personalization and federated learning where it is necessary to support i) learning on small amounts of data, and ii) communication efficient distributed training protocols. In this work, we develop FiLM Transfer (FiT) which fulfills these requirements in the image classification setting by combining ideas from transfer learning (fixed pretrained backbones and fine-tuned FiLM adapter layers) and meta-learning (automatically configured Naive Bayes classifiers and episodic training) to yield parameter efficient models with superior classification accuracy at low-shot. The resulting parameter efficiency is key for enabling few-shot learning, inexpensive model updates for personalization, and communication efficient federated learning. We experiment with FiT on a wide range of downstream datasets and show that it achieves better classification accuracy than the leading Big Transfer (BiT) algorithm at low-shot and achieves state-of-the art accuracy on the challenging VTAB-1k benchmark, with fewer than 1% of the updateable parameters. Finally, we demonstrate the parameter efficiency and superior accuracy of FiT in distributed low-shot applications including model personalization and federated learning where model update size is an important performance metric.
中摘: 现代深度学习系统越来越多地应用于个性化和联邦学习等场景，在这些场景中需要支持在少量数据上进行学习以及高效的分布式训练协议。在这项工作中，我们开发了FiLM Transfer（FiT），通过结合迁移学习（固定预训练骨干网络和微调FiLM适配器层）和元学习（自动配置的朴素贝叶斯分类器和阶段性训练）的思想，在图像分类设置下实现了参数高效模型，并且在少样本情况下具有优越的分类准确性。这种参数效率对于实现少样本学习、低成本的个性化模型更新以及高效的联邦学习至关重要。我们在多种下游数据集上实验了FiT，并展示了它在少样本情况下的分类准确性优于领先的Big Transfer（BiT）算法，在具有挑战性的VTAB-1k基准测试中也达到了最先进的准确率，仅使用不到1%可更新参数。最后，我们在包括模型个性化和联邦学习在内的分布式少样本应用中证明了FiT的参数效率和优越准确性，其中模型更新大小是重要的性能指标。

[No.112] Humanly Certifying Superhuman Classifiers
来源: ICLR 2023
命中: classif
标题: 人工验证超人类分类器
类别: 
作者: Qiongkai Xu;Christian Walder;Chenchen Xu;
机构: University of Melbourne;Australian National University;
类型: Top-25%
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2023/poster/10696
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2109.07867
英摘: Estimating the performance of a machine learning system is a longstanding challenge in artificial intelligence research. Today, this challenge is especially relevant given the emergence of systems which appear to increasingly outperform human beings. In some cases, this "superhuman" performance is readily demonstrated; for example by defeating legendary human players in traditional two player games. On the other hand, it can be challenging to evaluate classification models that potentially surpass human performance. Indeed, human annotations are often treated as a ground truth, which implicitly assumes the superiority of the human over any models trained on human annotations. In reality, human annotators can make mistakes and be subjective. Evaluating the performance with respect to a genuine oracle may be more objective and reliable, even when querying the oracle is expensive or impossible. In this paper, we first raise the challenge of evaluating the performance of both humans and models with respect to an oracle which is unobserved. We develop a theory for estimating the accuracy compared to the oracle, using only imperfect human annotations for reference. Our analysis provides a simple recipe for detecting and certifying superhuman performance in this setting, which we believe will assist in understanding the stage of current research on classification. We validate the convergence of the bounds and the assumptions of our theory on carefully designed toy experiments with known oracles. Moreover, we demonstrate the utility of our theory by meta-analyzing large-scale natural language processing tasks, for which an oracle does not exist, and show that under our assumptions a number of models from recent years are with high probability superhuman.
中摘: 在人工智能研究中，估算机器学习系统的性能一直是一项长期挑战。今天，随着出现能够越来越超越人类表现的系统，这一挑战变得尤为重要。在某些情况下，“超人”级别的性能可以通过击败传统两人游戏中传奇的人类玩家来直观展示。然而，评估那些可能超越人类表现的分类模型却颇具挑战性。事实上，人类注解通常被视为黄金标准，这隐含地假设了人类优于基于人类注解训练的任何模型。然而，在现实情况下，人类注释员可能会出错并且具有主观性。相对于一个真实的“oracle”（真值）进行性能评估可能是更客观和可靠的，即使查询这个“oracle”昂贵或不可能。在这篇论文中，我们首先提出了一个挑战：如何在无法观察到“oracle”的情况下评估人类和模型的性能。我们发展了一种理论，利用不完美的人类注解作为参考来估计与“oracle”的准确性差异。我们的分析提供了一个简单的方法来检测和证明在这种情况下是否存在超人级别性能，并且我们认为这种方法将有助于理解当前分类任务研究的阶段。我们通过精心设计的玩具实验验证了边界和理论假设的收敛性，并且展示了该理论在大规模自然语言处理任务中的实用性。在这些任务中并不存在“oracle”，但根据我们的假设，近年来的一系列模型很可能已经达到了超人级别。

[No.113] Massively Scaling Heteroscedastic Classifiers
来源: ICLR 2023
命中: classif
标题: 大规模扩展异方差分类器
类别: 
作者: Mark Collier;Rodolphe Jenatton;Basil Mustafa;Neil Houlsby;Jesse Berent;Effrosyni Kokiopoulou;
机构: Google;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2023/poster/11622
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2301.12860
英摘: Heteroscedastic classifiers, which learn a multivariate Gaussian distribution over prediction logits, have been shown to perform well on image classification problems with hundreds to thousands of classes. However, compared to standard classifiers, they introduce extra parameters that scale linearly with the number of classes. This makes them infeasible to apply to larger-scale problems. In addition heteroscedastic classifiers introduce a critical temperature hyperparameter which must be tuned. We propose HET-XL, a heteroscedastic classifier whose parameter count when compared to a standard classifier scales independently of the number of classes. In our large-scale settings, we show that we can remove the need to tune the temperature hyperparameter, by directly learning it on the training data. On large image classification datasets with up to 4B images and 30k classes our method requires 14X fewer additional parameters, does not require tuning the temperature on a held-out set and performs consistently better than the baseline heteroscedastic classifier. HET-XL improves ImageNet 0-shot classification in a multimodal contrastive learning setup which can be viewed as a 3.5 billion class classification problem.
中摘: 异方差分类器通过学习预测逻辑上的多元高斯分布，在具有数百至数千类别的图像分类问题上表现出色。然而，与标准分类器相比，它们引入了额外的参数，这些参数的数量随着类别数量线性增长，这使得它们在处理大规模问题时不可行。此外，异方差分类器还引入了一个关键的温度超参数，需要进行调优。我们提出了HET-XL，这是一种相对于标准分类器而言，参数数量与其类别数量无关的异方差分类器。在我们的大规模设置中，我们展示了可以通过直接在训练数据上学习温度超参数来消除调温的需求。在具有多达40亿张图像和30000个类别的大型图像分类数据集上，我们的方法只需要基线异方差分类器的14倍少的额外参数，并且不需要在保留集上调整温度，同时在性能上也表现得更稳定。HET-XL在多模态对比学习设置下的ImageNet零样本分类中得到了改进，可以视为一个拥有35亿类别的分类问题。

[No.114] On the Perils of Cascading Robust Classifiers
来源: ICLR 2023
命中: classif
标题: 关于级联鲁棒分类器的风险
类别: 
作者: Ravi Mangal;Zifan Wang;Chi Zhang;Klas Leino;Corina Pasareanu;Matt Fredrikson;
机构: Carnegie Mellon University;Roam;NASA Ames Research Center;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2023/poster/11732
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2206.00278
英摘: Ensembling certifiably robust neural networks is a promising approach for improving the \emph{certified robust accuracy} of neural models. Black-box ensembles that assume only query-access to the constituent models (and their robustness certifiers) during prediction are particularly attractive due to their modular structure. Cascading ensembles are a popular instance of black-box ensembles that appear to improve certified robust accuracies in practice. However, we show that the robustness certifier used by a cascading ensemble is unsound. That is, when a cascading ensemble is certified as locally robust at an input $x$ (with respect to $ε$), there can be inputs $x'$ in the $ε$-ball centered at $x$, such that the cascade's prediction at $x'$ is different from $x$ and thus the ensemble is not locally robust. Our theoretical findings are accompanied by empirical results that further demonstrate this unsoundness. We present \emph{cascade attack} (CasA), an adversarial attack against cascading ensembles, and show that: (1) there exists an adversarial input for up to 88\% of the samples where the ensemble claims to be certifiably robust and accurate; and (2) the accuracy of a cascading ensemble under our attack is as low as 11\% when it claims to be certifiably robust and accurate on 97\% of the test set. Our work reveals a critical pitfall of cascading certifiably robust models by showing that the seemingly beneficial strategy of cascading can actually hurt the robustness of the resulting ensemble. Our code is available at \url{https://github.com/TristaChi/ensembleKW}.
中摘: 集成认证鲁棒神经网络是一种提高神经模型认证鲁棒准确性的有前途的方法。黑盒集成在预测时仅假设对构成模型及其鲁棒性认证器具有查询访问权限，因此特别具有吸引力，因为它们具有模块化结构。级联集成是黑盒集成的一种流行实例，在实践中似乎可以提高认证鲁棒准确性。然而，我们展示了级联集成所使用的鲁棒性认证器是不正确的。也就是说，当一个级联集成在输入 \(x\)（相对于 \(\varepsilon\)）处被认证为局部鲁棒时，可能存在位于 \(x\) 的 \(\varepsilon\) 球内的输入 \(x'\)，使得级联在 \(x'\) 处的预测与 \(x\) 处不同，从而该集成不是局部鲁棒的。我们的理论发现得到了进一步的实证结果的支持，这些结果进一步证明了这种不正确性。我们提出了针对级联集成的对抗攻击 \(\textit{cascade attack}\)（CasA），并展示了以下几点：(1) 在高达 88% 的样本中，存在对抗输入，尽管集成声称在这些样本上是认证鲁棒和准确的；(2) 当集成声称在测试集的 97% 样本上是认证鲁棒和准确时，我们的攻击下其准确率最低可降至 11%。我们的工作揭示了级联认证鲁棒模型的一个关键缺陷，即看似有益的级联策略实际上可能损害最终集成的鲁棒性。我们的代码可在 \url{https://github.com/TristaChi/ensembleKW} 获取。

[No.115] Neural-based classification rule learning for sequential data

命中: classif

英摘: Discovering interpretable patterns for classification of sequential data is of key importance for a variety of fields, ranging from genomics to fraud detection or more generally interpretable decision-making. 中摘: 发现可解释的模式以对序列数据进行分类在众多领域中至关重要，从基因组学到欺诈检测，再到更广泛的可解释决策制定。在本文中，我们提出了一种新颖的可微分完全可解释方法，用于发现局部和全局模式（即捕捉相对或绝对时间依赖性），并应用于基于规则的二元分类。该方法包括一个具有可解释神经滤波器的卷积二元神经网络以及一种基于动态强制稀疏性的训练策略。我们在合成数据集和一个开源的肽数据集上展示了该方法的有效性和实用性。关键在于，在学习规则的同时也学习了这些表达性强的模式。

[No.116] Schema Inference for Interpretable Image Classification
来源: ICLR 2023
命中: classif
标题: 可解释的图像分类的模式推理
类别: 
作者: Haofei Zhang;Mengqi Xue;Xiaokang Liu;Kaixuan Chen;Jie Song;Mingli Song;
机构: Zhejiang University;Hangzhou City University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2023/poster/11715
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2303.06635
英摘: In this paper, we study a novel inference paradigm, termed as schema inference, that learns to deductively infer the explainable predictions by rebuilding the prior deep neural network (DNN) forwarding scheme, guided by the prevalent philosophical cognitive concept of schema. We strive to reformulate the conventional model inference pipeline into a graph matching policy that associates the extracted visual concepts of an image with the pre-computed scene impression, by analogy with human reasoning mechanism via impression matching. To this end, we devise an elaborated architecture, termed as SchemaNet, as a dedicated instantiation of the proposed schema inference concept, that models both the visual semantics of input instances and the learned abstract imaginations of target categories as topological relational graphs. Meanwhile, to capture and leverage the compositional contributions of visual semantics in a global view, we also introduce a universal Feat2Graph scheme in SchemaNet to establish the relational graphs that contain abundant interaction information. Both the theoretical analysis and the experimental results on several benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed schema inference achieves encouraging performance and meanwhile yields a clear picture of the deductive process leading to the predictions. Our code is available at https://github.com/zhfeing/SchemaNet-PyTorch.
中摘: 在本文中，我们研究了一种新颖的推理范式，称为模式推理（schema inference），该范式通过重建先验深度神经网络（DNN）前向推理方案来学习演绎出可解释的预测，这一过程受到广泛流行的哲学认知概念——模式的指导。我们致力于将传统的模型推理管道重新构思为一个图匹配策略，通过印象匹配将图像中提取的视觉概念与预先计算的场景印象关联起来，类似于人类的推理机制。为此，我们设计了一个详细的架构，称为SchemaNet，作为所提出模式推理概念的专业实例，该架构将输入实例的视觉语义和目标类别的学习抽象想象建模为拓扑关系图。同时，为了从全局视角捕捉并利用视觉语义的组合贡献，我们在SchemaNet中引入了一个通用的Feat2Graph方案，以建立包含丰富交互信息的关系图。理论分析和几个基准上的实验结果都表明，所提出的模式推理达到了令人鼓舞的性能，并且清晰地展示了导致预测的演绎过程。我们的代码可在https://github.com/zhfeing/SchemaNet-PyTorch获取。

[No.117] The Devil is in the Wrongly-classified Samples: Towards Unified Open-set Recognition
来源: ICLR 2023
命中: classif
标题: 误分类样本中的魔鬼：迈向统一的开放集识别
类别: 
作者: Jun CEN;Di Luan;Shiwei Zhang;Yixuan Pei;Yingya Zhang;Deli Zhao;Shaojie Shen;Qifeng Chen;
机构: Hong Kong University of Science and Technology;Alibaba Group;Xi'an Jiao Tong University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2023/poster/12194
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2302.04002
英摘: Open-set Recognition (OSR) aims to identify test samples whose classes are not seen during the training process. Recently, Unified Open-set Recognition (UOSR) has been proposed to reject not only unknown samples but also known but wrongly classified samples, which tends to be more practical in real-world applications. The UOSR draws little attention since it is proposed, but we find sometimes it is even more practical than OSR in the real world applications, as evaluation results of known but wrongly classified samples are also wrong like unknown samples. In this paper, we deeply analyze the UOSR task under different training and evaluation settings to shed light on this promising research direction. For this purpose, we first evaluate the UOSR performance of several OSR methods and show a significant finding that the UOSR performance consistently surpasses the OSR performance by a large margin for the same method. We show that the reason lies in the known but wrongly classified samples, as their uncertainty distribution is extremely close to unknown samples rather than known and correctly classified samples. Second, we analyze how the two training settings of OSR (i.e., pre-training and outlier exposure) influence the UOSR. We find although they are both beneficial for distinguishing known and correctly classified samples from unknown samples, pre-training is also helpful for identifying known but wrongly classified samples while outlier exposure is not. In addition to different training settings, we also formulate a new evaluation setting for UOSR which is called few-shot UOSR, where only one or five samples per unknown class are available during evaluation to help identify unknown samples. We propose FS-KNNS for the few-shot UOSR to achieve state-of-the-art performance under all settings.
中摘: 开放集识别（OSR）旨在识别在训练过程中未见过类别的测试样本。最近，统一开放集识别（UOSR）被提出，不仅拒绝未知样本，还拒绝已知但错误分类的样本，这在实际应用中更具实用性。尽管自提出以来UOSR受到的关注较少，但我们发现有时它在实际应用中甚至比OSR更为实用，因为已知但错误分类的样本与未知样本的评估结果一样也是错误的。在本文中，我们从不同的训练和评估设置出发，深入分析UOSR任务，以揭示这一有前景的研究方向。为此，我们首先评估了几种OSR方法的UOSR性能，并展示了显著的发现：对于相同的算法，UOSR性能始终大幅超过OSR性能。我们表明原因在于已知但错误分类的样本，因为它们的不确定性分布极其接近未知样本而非已知且正确分类的样本。其次，我们分析了OSR两种训练设置（即预训练和异常值暴露）对UOSR的影响。我们发现，虽然这两种设置都有助于区分已知且正确分类的样本与未知样本，但预训练也有助于识别已知但错误分类的样本，而异常值暴露则没有帮助。除了不同的训练设置外，我们还为UOSR制定了一个新的评估设置，称为少量样本UOSR，在此设置下，仅在评估时每未知类别提供一或五个样本，以帮助识别未知样本。我们提出了FS-KNNS方法，以在所有设置下实现最先进的性能。

[No.118] NAGphormer: A Tokenized Graph Transformer for Node Classification in Large Graphs
来源: ICLR 2023
命中: classif
标题: NAGphormer：一种用于大型图中节点分类的标记化图变压器
类别: 
作者: Jinsong Chen;Kaiyuan Gao;Gaichao Li;Kun He;
机构: Huazhong University of Science and Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2023/poster/11258
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2206.04910
英摘: The graph Transformer emerges as a new architecture and has shown superior performance on various graph mining tasks. In this work, we observe that existing graph Transformers treat nodes as independent tokens and construct a single long sequence composed of all node tokens so as to train the Transformer model, causing it hard to scale to large graphs due to the quadratic complexity on the number of nodes for the self-attention computation. To this end, we propose a Neighborhood Aggregation Graph Transformer (NAGphormer) that treats each node as a sequence containing a series of tokens constructed by our proposed Hop2Token module. For each node, Hop2Token aggregates the neighborhood features from different hops into different representations and thereby produces a sequence of token vectors as one input. In this way, NAGphormer could be trained in a mini-batch manner and thus could scale to large graphs. Moreover, we mathematically show that as compared to a category of advanced Graph Neural Networks (GNNs), the decoupled Graph Convolutional Network, NAGphormer could learn more informative node representations from the multi-hop neighborhoods. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets from small to large are conducted to demonstrate that NAGphormer consistently outperforms existing graph Transformers and mainstream GNNs. Code is available at https://github.com/JHL-HUST/NAGphormer.
中摘: 图Transformer作为一种新的架构形式，在各种图挖掘任务上展现了卓越的性能。在本工作中，我们观察到现有的图Transformer将节点视为独立的标记，并构建了一个由所有节点标记组成的单一长序列以训练Transformer模型，但由于自我注意力计算的复杂度与节点数量呈二次关系，这导致其难以扩展到大规模图。为此，我们提出了一种邻域聚合图Transformer（NAGphormer），该方法将每个节点视为包含一系列由我们提出的Hop2Token模块构造的标记序列。对于每个节点，Hop2Token会从不同跳数中聚合邻域特征并生成不同的表示，从而产生一个标记向量序列作为输入。这样，NAGphormer可以以小批量的方式进行训练，因此能够扩展到大规模图。此外，我们从数学上证明了，与一类先进的图神经网络（GNNs）——解耦的图卷积网络相比，NAGphormer能够从多跳邻域中学习到更具有信息量的节点表示。我们在从小规模到大规模的基准数据集上进行了广泛的实验，结果显示NAGphormer始终优于现有的图Transformer和主流的GNNs。相关代码可在https://github.com/JHL-HUST/NAGphormer获取。

[No.119] Neural Collapse Inspired Feature-Classifier Alignment for Few-Shot Class-Incremental Learning
来源: ICLR 2023
命中: classif
标题: 神经坍缩启发的特征-分类器对齐方法用于少样本类增量学习
类别: 
作者: Yibo Yang;Haobo Yuan;Xiangtai Li;Zhouchen Lin;Philip Torr;Dacheng Tao;
机构: JD;Peking University;University of Oxford;
类型: Top-25%
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2023/poster/12186
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2302.03004
英摘: Few-shot class-incremental learning (FSCIL) has been a challenging problem as only a few training samples are accessible for each novel class in the new sessions. Finetuning the backbone or adjusting the classifier prototypes trained in the prior sessions would inevitably cause a misalignment between the feature and classifier of old classes, which explains the well-known catastrophic forgetting problem. In this paper, we deal with this misalignment dilemma in FSCIL inspired by the recently discovered phenomenon named neural collapse, which reveals that the last-layer features of the same class will collapse into a vertex, and the vertices of all classes are aligned with the classifier prototypes, which are formed as a simplex equiangular tight frame (ETF). It corresponds to an optimal geometric structure for classification due to the maximized Fisher Discriminant Ratio. We propose a neural collapse inspired framework for FSCIL. A group of classifier prototypes are pre-assigned as a simplex ETF for the whole label space, including the base session and all the incremental sessions. During training, the classifier prototypes are not learnable, and we adopt a novel loss function that drives the features into their corresponding prototypes. Theoretical analysis shows that our method holds the neural collapse optimality and does not break the feature-classifier alignment in an incremental fashion. Experiments on the miniImageNet, CUB-200, and CIFAR-100 datasets demonstrate that our proposed framework outperforms the state-of-the-art performances. Code address: https://github.com/NeuralCollapseApplications/FSCIL
中摘: 少样本类别增量学习（Few-shot Class-Incremental Learning, FSCIL）是一个具有挑战性的问题，因为在新的会话中，每个新型类别的可访问训练样本数量有限。调整前几次会话中训练的主干或分类器原型必然会引发旧类别特征与分类器之间的错位，这就是众所周知的灾难性遗忘问题的原因。在本文中，我们受到最近发现的现象——神经坍塌的启发，来解决FSCIL中的这一错位难题。神经坍塌现象揭示了同一类别最后一层特征将坍缩为一个顶点，并且所有类别的顶点都与形成的单纯形等角紧框架（ETF）中的分类器原型对齐。这种结构由于最大化了费歇尔判别比而被认为是分类的最佳几何结构。因此，我们提出了一种基于神经坍塌的FSCIL框架。整个标签空间，包括基础会话和所有增量会话，预先分配一组分类器原型作为单纯形ETF。在训练过程中，这些分类器原型不可学习，我们采用一种新颖的损失函数来驱动特征向其对应的原型靠拢。理论分析表明，我们的方法保持了神经坍塌的最优性，并且在增量方式下不会破坏特征与分类器的对齐。在miniImageNet、CUB-200和CIFAR-100数据集上的实验结果显示，我们提出的框架优于现有最佳性能。代码地址：https://github.com/NeuralCollapseApplications/FSCIL

[No.120] What Can we Learn From The Selective Prediction And Uncertainty Estimation Performance Of 523 Imagenet Classifiers?
来源: ICLR 2023
命中: classif
标题: 我们可以从523个ImageNet分类器的选择性预测和不确定性估计性能中学到什么？
类别: 
作者: Ido Galil;Mohammed Dabbah;Ran El-Yaniv;
机构: Technion-Israel Institute of Technology;Deci;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2023/poster/11783
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2302.11874
英摘: When deployed for risk-sensitive tasks, deep neural networks must include an uncertainty estimation mechanism. Here we examine the relationship between deep architectures and their respective training regimes, with their corresponding selective prediction and uncertainty estimation performance. We consider some of the most popular estimation performance metrics previously proposed including AUROC, ECE, AURC as well as coverage for selective accuracy constraint. We present a novel and comprehensive study of selective prediction and the uncertainty estimation performance of 523 existing pretrained deep ImageNet classifiers that are available in popular repositories. We identify numerous and previously unknown factors that affect uncertainty estimation and examine the relationships between the different metrics. We find that distillation-based training regimes consistently yield better uncertainty estimations than other training schemes such as vanilla training, pretraining on a larger dataset and adversarial training. Moreover, we find a subset of ViT models that outperform any other models in terms of uncertainty estimation performance. For example, we discovered an unprecedented 99% top-1 selective accuracy on ImageNet at 47% coverage (and 95% top-1 accuracy at 80%) for a ViT model, whereas a competing EfficientNet-V2-XL cannot obtain these accuracy constraints at any level of coverage. Our companion paper, also published in ICLR 2023 (A framework for benchmarking class-out-of-distribution detection and its application to ImageNet), examines the performance of these classifiers in a class-out-of-distribution setting.
中摘: 在部署于风险敏感任务时，深度神经网络必须包含不确定性估计机制。我们探讨了深度架构与其相应的训练制度之间的关系，以及它们在选择性预测和不确定性估计性能方面的表现。我们考虑了一些之前提出的最流行的估计性能指标，包括AUROC、ECE、AURC，以及针对选择性准确度约束的覆盖率。我们对523个现有的预训练ImageNet分类器进行了新颖而全面的研究，这些分类器可以在流行的数据仓库中找到。我们识别出许多以前未知的影响不确定性估计的因素，并研究了不同指标之间的关系。我们发现，基于蒸馏的训练制度始终比其他训练方案（如常规训练、更大数据集的预训练和对抗训练）提供更好的不确定性估计。此外，我们发现了一类ViT模型，在不确定性估计性能方面优于其他任何模型。例如，我们发现在ImageNet数据集中，一个ViT模型在47%的覆盖率下实现了前所未有的99%的顶级选择性准确度（且在80%的覆盖率下达到95%的顶级准确度），而竞争的EfficientNet-V2-XL无法在任何覆盖率水平上获得这些准确度约束。我们的姊妹论文也在ICLR 2023上发表（《一种用于基准测试类别外分布检测的框架及其在ImageNet上的应用》），考察了这些分类器在外类别分布环境中的性能。

[No.122] Strategic Classification with Graph Neural Networks
来源: ICLR 2023
命中: classif
标题: 基于图神经网络的战略分类
类别: 
作者: Itay Eilat;Ben Finkelshtein;Chaim Baskin;Nir Rosenfeld;
机构: Technion-Israel Institute of Technology;University of Oxford;Technion - Israel Institute of Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2023/poster/12001
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2205.15765
英摘: Strategic classification studies learning in settings where users can modify their features to obtain favorable predictions. Most current works focus on simple classifiers that trigger independent user responses. Here we examine the implications of learning with more elaborate models that break the independence assumption. Motivated by the idea that applications of strategic classification are often social in nature, we focus on \emph{graph neural networks}, which make use of social relations between users to improve predictions. Using a graph for learning introduces inter-user dependencies in prediction; our key point is that strategic users can exploit these to promote their goals. As we show through analysis and simulation, this can work either against the system -- or for it. Based on this, we propose a differentiable framework for strategically-robust learning of graph-based classifiers. Experiments on several real networked datasets demonstrate the utility of our approach.
中摘: 战略分类研究了用户可以修改其特征以获得有利预测的学习环境。目前大多数工作集中在触发独立用户响应的简单分类器上。在这里，我们探讨了使用更为复杂的模型（这些模型打破了独立性假设）进行学习的影响。受战略分类应用往往是社会性的这一想法的启发，我们专注于图神经网络，这种模型利用用户之间的社会关系来提高预测准确性。通过使用图来进行学习，引入了用户间的依赖性；我们的关键观点是，战略性用户可以利用这些依赖性来促进自己的目标。正如我们在分析和模拟中所示，这既可以对系统不利——也可以对其有利。基于此，我们提出了一种可微分框架，用于实现基于图的战略鲁棒性学习分类器。在多个真实网络数据集上的实验表明，我们方法的有效性。

[No.123] Denoising Masked Autoencoders Help Robust Classification
来源: ICLR 2023
命中: classif
标题: 去噪掩码自编码器助力稳健分类
类别: 
作者: QuanLin Wu;Hang Ye;Yuntian Gu;Huishuai Zhang;Liwei Wang;Di He;
机构: Peking University;Microsoft;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2023/poster/12054
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2210.06983
英摘: In this paper, we propose a new self-supervised method, which is called Denoising Masked AutoEncoders (DMAE), for learning certified robust classifiers of images. In DMAE, we corrupt each image by adding Gaussian noises to each pixel value and randomly masking several patches. A Transformer-based encoder-decoder model is then trained to reconstruct the original image from the corrupted one. In this learning paradigm, the encoder will learn to capture relevant semantics for the downstream tasks, which is also robust to Gaussian additive noises. We show that the pre-trained encoder can naturally be used as the base classifier in Gaussian smoothed models, where we can analytically compute the certified radius for any data point. Although the proposed method is simple, it yields significant performance improvement in downstream classification tasks. We show that the DMAE ViT-Base model, which just uses 1/10 parameters of the model developed in recent work arXiv:2206.10550, achieves competitive or better certified accuracy in various settings. The DMAE ViT-Large model significantly surpasses all previous results, establishing a new state-of-the-art on ImageNet dataset. We further demonstrate that the pre-trained model has good transferability to the CIFAR-10 dataset, suggesting its wide adaptability. Models and code are available at https://github.com/quanlin-wu/dmae.
中摘: 在本文中，我们提出了一种新的自监督方法，称为去噪掩码自动编码器（DMAE），用于学习具有认证鲁棒性的图像分类器。在DMAE中，我们通过向每个像素值添加高斯噪声来破坏每张图像，并随机遮盖几个patches。然后，使用基于Transformer的编码器-解码器模型训练从被破坏的图像重建原始图像。在这种学习范式中，编码器将学会捕获与下游任务相关的语义信息，同时也能够抵抗高斯噪声的干扰。我们展示了预训练的编码器可以自然地用作高斯平滑模型中的基础分类器，在这种模型中，我们可以对任意数据点进行解析计算，得到其认证半径。尽管所提出的这种方法很简单，但在下游分类任务中却能显著提高性能。我们证明了DMAE ViT-Base模型，仅使用了近期工作arXiv:2206.10550中开发的模型参数的1/10，便在各种设置中实现了竞争力甚至更好的认证准确性。DMAE ViT-Large模型则大幅超越了所有先前的结果，在ImageNet数据集上建立了新的最先进的性能。此外，我们还进一步展示了预训练模型在CIFAR-10数据集上的良好的迁移性，这表明其具有广泛的适应能力。相关模型和代码可在https://github.com/quanlin-wu/dmae获取。

[No.125] Towards Better Selective Classification
来源: ICLR 2023
命中: classif
标题: 更好的选择性分类方法
类别: 
作者: Leo Feng;Mohamed Osama Ahmed;Hossein Hajimirsadeghi;Amir H. Abdi;
机构: Quebec Artificial Intelligence Institute;Borealis AI;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2023/poster/11355
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2206.09034
英摘: We tackle the problem of Selective Classification where the objective is to achieve the best performance on a predetermined ratio (coverage) of the dataset. Recent state-of-the-art selective methods come with architectural changes either via introducing a separate selection head or an extra abstention logit. In this paper, we challenge the aforementioned methods. The results suggest that the superior performance of state-of-the-art methods is owed to training a more generalizable classifier rather than their proposed selection mechanisms. We argue that the best performing selection mechanism should instead be rooted in the classifier itself. Our proposed selection strategy uses the classification scores and achieves better results by a significant margin, consistently, across all coverages and all datasets, without any added compute cost. Furthermore, inspired by semi-supervised learning, we propose an entropy-based regularizer that improves the performance of selective classification methods. Our proposed selection mechanism with the proposed entropy-based regularizer achieves new state-of-the-art results.
中摘: 我们在选择性分类问题上处理这一问题，目标是在数据集中达到预设比例（覆盖范围）的最佳性能。最近的先进选择性方法通常伴随着架构上的变化，要么通过引入单独的选择分支，要么增加额外的弃权logit。在本文中，我们挑战了上述方法。研究结果表明，最先进的方法的优越性能归功于训练出更具泛化能力的分类器，而不是它们提出的选择机制。我们认为，最佳的选择机制应该根植于分类器本身。我们提出的选机制利用分类分数，并且在所有覆盖范围和所有数据集上均能显著提高性能，且无需增加计算成本。此外，受半监督学习的启发，我们提出了一种基于熵的正则化器，以改进选择性分类方法的性能。结合我们提出的基于熵的正则化器的选择机制达到了新的最先进的性能。

[No.126] FIFA: Making Fairness More Generalizable in Classifiers Trained on Imbalanced Data
来源: ICLR 2023
命中: classif
标题: FIFA：在不平衡数据训练的分类器中实现更广泛的公平性
类别: 
作者: Zhun Deng;Jiayao Zhang;Linjun Zhang;Ting Ye;Yates Coley;Weijie J Su;James Zou;
机构: Columbia University;University of Pennsylvania;Rutgers University;University of Washington;Stanford University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2023/poster/11119
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2206.02792
英摘: Algorithmic fairness plays an important role in machine learning and imposing fairness constraints during learning is a common approach. However, many datasets are imbalanced in certain label classes (e.g. "healthy") and sensitive subgroups (e.g. "older patients"). Empirically, this imbalance leads to a lack of generalizability not only of classification, but also of fairness properties, especially in over-parameterized models. For example, fairness-aware training may ensure equalized odds (EO) on the training data, but EO is far from being satisfied on new users. In this paper, we propose a theoretically-principled, yet Flexible approach that is Imbalance-Fairness-Aware (FIFA). Specifically, FIFA encourages both classification and fairness generalization and can be flexibly combined with many existing fair learning methods with logits-based losses. While our main focus is on EO, FIFA can be directly applied to achieve equalized opportunity (EqOpt); and under certain conditions, it can also be applied to other fairness notions. We demonstrate the power of FIFA by combining it with a popular fair classification algorithm, and the resulting algorithm achieves significantly better fairness generalization on several real-world datasets.
中摘: 算法公平性在机器学习中扮演着重要角色，在学习过程中施加公平性约束是一种常见的方法。然而，许多数据集在某些标签类别（例如“健康”）和敏感子群体（例如“老年患者”）中存在不平衡问题。从经验上看，这种不平衡不仅会导致分类的一般化能力不足，还会对公平性属性产生影响，尤其是在参数过多的模型中。例如，具有公平意识的训练可能在训练数据上确保了同等机会（EO），但在新用户上却远未达到EO的要求。在本文中，我们提出了一种理论指导下的、同时具备灵活性的方法——不平衡公平感知（Imbalance-Fairness-Aware，简称FIFA）。具体来说，FIFA鼓励分类和公平性的泛化，并可以灵活地与许多现有的基于逻辑概率损失的公平学习方法结合使用。虽然我们的主要关注点是EO，但FIFA可以直接应用于实现同等机会（EqOpt）；在某些条件下，它还可以应用于其他公平性概念。我们通过将FIFA与一种流行的公平分类算法结合，展示了FIFA的强大功能，最终生成的算法在多个实际数据集上实现了显著更好的公平性泛化能力。

[No.127] Temporal Coherent Test Time Optimization for Robust Video Classification
来源: ICLR 2023
命中: classif
标题: 时空一致性测试时间优化以实现稳健的视频分类
类别: 
作者: Chenyu Yi;SIYUAN YANG;Yufei Wang;Haoliang Li;Yap-peng Tan;Alex Kot;
机构: Nanyang Technological University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2023/poster/11570
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2302.14309
英摘: Deep neural networks are likely to fail when the test data is corrupted in real-world deployment (e.g., blur, weather, etc.). Test-time optimization is an effective way that adapts models to generalize to corrupted data during testing, which has been shown in the image domain. However, the techniques for improving video classification corruption robustness remain few. In this work, we propose a Temporal Coherent Test-time Optimization framework (TeCo) to utilize spatio-temporal information in test-time optimization for robust video classification. To exploit information in video with self-supervised learning, TeCo uses global content from video clips and optimizes models for entropy minimization. TeCo minimizes the entropy of the prediction based on the global content from video clips. Meanwhile, it also feeds local content to regularize the temporal coherence at the feature level. TeCo retains the generalization ability of various video classification models and achieves significant improvements in corruption robustness across Mini Kinetics-C and Mini SSV2-C. Furthermore, TeCo sets a new baseline in video classification corruption robustness via test-time optimization.
中摘: 深度神经网络在实际部署中当测试数据受到污染（例如模糊、天气等）时可能会失效。测试时优化是一种有效的方法，在测试过程中适应模型以泛化到受污染的数据，这种方法已经在图像领域得到了验证。然而，提高视频分类对污染的鲁棒性的方法仍然很少。在这项工作中，我们提出了一种时空一致的测试时优化框架（TeCo），旨在利用视频片段中的时空信息进行测试时优化，以实现鲁棒的视频分类。为了从视频中提取信息并使用自监督学习，TeCo 利用了视频片段的全局内容，并优化模型以最小化预测的熵。TeCo 基于视频片段的全局内容来最小化预测的熵。同时，它还通过特征级别输入局部内容来规范时间一致性。TeCo 保留了各种视频分类模型的一般化能力，并在Mini Kinetics-C和Mini SSV2-C上实现了显著的鲁棒性改进。此外，TeCo 通过测试时优化为视频分类设定了新的鲁棒性基准。

[No.128] Benign Overfitting in Classification: Provably Counter Label Noise with Larger Models
来源: ICLR 2023
命中: classif
标题: 分类中的良性过拟合：更大模型证明性地对抗标签噪声
类别: 
作者: Kaiyue Wen;Jiaye Teng;Jingzhao Zhang;
机构: Stanford University;Tsinghua University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2023/poster/11667
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2206.00501
英摘: Studies on benign overfitting provide insights for the success of overparameterized deep learning models. In this work, we examine whether overfitting is truly benign in real-world classification tasks. We start with the observation that a ResNet model overfits benignly on Cifar10 but not benignly on ImageNet. To understand why benign overfitting fails in the ImageNet experiment, we theoretically analyze benign overfitting under a more restrictive setup where the number of parameters is not significantly larger than the number of data points. Under this mild overparameterization setup, our analysis identifies a phase change: unlike in the previous heavy overparameterization settings, benign overfitting can now fail in the presence of label noise. Our analysis explains our empirical observations, and is validated by a set of control experiments with ResNets. Our work highlights the importance of understanding implicit bias in underfitting regimes as a future direction.
中摘: 关于良性过拟合的研究为过参数化深度学习模型的成功提供了见解。在这项工作中，我们探讨了良性过拟合是否真正适用于现实世界的分类任务。我们观察到，ResNet模型在Cifar10数据集上以良性方式过拟合，但在ImageNet数据集上并非如此。为了理解为什么良性过拟合在ImageNet实验中失败，我们在参数数量不显著大于数据点数量的情况下对良性过拟合进行了理论分析。在这一较为温和的过参数化设置下，我们的分析发现了一个相变现象：与之前的严重过参数化设置不同，在存在标签噪声的情况下，良性过拟合现在也可能失败。我们的分析解释了我们的实证观察，并通过一系列针对ResNet的控制实验得到了验证。我们的工作强调了在未来研究方向中理解欠拟合状态下隐含偏置的重要性。

[No.130] Out-of-distribution Representation Learning for Time Series Classification
来源: ICLR 2023
命中: classif
标题: 离分布表示学习在时间序列分类中的应用
类别: 
作者: Wang Lu;Jindong Wang;Xinwei Sun;Yiqiang Chen;Xing Xie;
机构: Fudan University;Chinese Academy of Sciences;Microsoft;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2023/poster/11714
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2209.07027
英摘: Time series classification is an important problem in real world. Due to its non-stationary property that the distribution changes over time, it remains challenging to build models for generalization to unseen distributions. In this paper, we propose to view the time series classification problem from the distribution perspective. We argue that the temporal complexity attributes to the unknown latent distributions within. To this end, we propose DIVERSIFY to learn generalized representations for time series classification. DIVERSIFY takes an iterative process: it first obtains the worst-case distribution scenario via adversarial training, then matches the distributions of the obtained sub-domains. We also present some theoretical insights. We conduct experiments on gesture recognition, speech commands recognition, wearable stress and affect detection, and sensor-based human activity recognition with a total of seven datasets in different settings. Results demonstrate that DIVERSIFY significantly outperforms other baselines and effectively characterizes the latent distributions by qualitative and quantitative analysis. Code is available at: https://github.com/microsoft/robustlearn.
中摘: 时间序列分类在现实世界中是一个重要的问题。由于其非平稳性质，即分布随时间变化，构建能够泛化到未见分布的模型仍然具有挑战性。在本文中，我们提出从分布的角度看待时间序列分类问题。我们认为时间复杂性来源于未知的潜在分布。为此，我们提出了DIVERSIFY方法来学习时间序列分类的一般化表示。DIVERSIFY采用迭代过程：首先通过对抗训练获得最坏情况的分布场景，然后匹配所获得子域的分布。我们还提供了一些理论见解。我们在手势识别、语音命令识别、可穿戴设备压力和情绪检测以及基于传感器的人类活动识别等七个不同设置的数据集中进行了实验。结果显示，DIVERSIFY显著优于其他基线方法，并且通过定性和定量分析有效地表征了潜在分布。代码可在以下地址获取：https://github.com/microsoft/robustlearn。

[No.131] Decoupled Training for Long-Tailed Classification With Stochastic Representations
来源: ICLR 2023
命中: classif
标题: 长尾分类中的解耦训练与随机表示
类别: 
作者: Giung Nam;Sunguk Jang;Juho Lee;
机构: Korea Advanced Institute of Science and Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2023/poster/11593
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2304.09426
英摘: Decoupling representation learning and classifier learning has been shown to be effective in classification with long-tailed data. There are two main ingredients in constructing a decoupled learning scheme; 1) how to train the feature extractor for representation learning so that it provides generalizable representations and 2) how to re-train the classifier that constructs proper decision boundaries by handling class imbalances in long-tailed data. In this work, we first apply Stochastic Weight Averaging (SWA), an optimization technique for improving the generalization of deep neural networks, to obtain better generalizing feature extractors for long-tailed classification. We then propose a novel classifier re-training algorithm based on stochastic representation obtained from the SWA-Gaussian, a Gaussian perturbed SWA, and a self-distillation strategy that can harness the diverse stochastic representations based on uncertainty estimates to build more robust classifiers. Extensive experiments on CIFAR10/100-LT, ImageNet-LT, and iNaturalist-2018 benchmarks show that our proposed method improves upon previous methods both in terms of prediction accuracy and uncertainty estimation.
中摘: 在处理长尾数据分类时，分离表示学习和分类器学习已被证明是有效的。构建一个分离学习方案主要有两个关键要素：1）如何训练特征提取器以进行表示学习，使其提供可泛化的表示；2）如何重新训练分类器，使其能够通过处理长尾数据中的类别不平衡来构建适当的决策边界。在本工作中，我们首先应用随机权重平均（SWA），这是一种改进深度神经网络泛化能力的优化技术，以获得更好的泛化特征提取器。然后，我们提出了一种基于SWA高斯分布（即加高斯噪声的SWA）和自我蒸馏策略的新颖分类器重新训练算法。该算法可以根据不确定性估计利用多样化的随机表示来构建更稳健的分类器。在CIFAR10/100-LT、ImageNet-LT和iNaturalist-2018基准测试上的广泛实验表明，我们提出的方法在预测准确性和不确定性估计方面都优于之前的方法。

[No.132] FaiREE: fair classification with finite-sample and distribution-free guarantee
来源: ICLR 2023
命中: classif
标题: FairREE：基于有限样本且无分布假设的公平分类
类别: 
作者: Puheng Li;James Zou;Linjun Zhang;
机构: Peking University;Stanford University;Rutgers University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2023/poster/11545
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2211.15072
英摘: Algorithmic fairness plays an increasingly critical role in machine learning research. Several group fairness notions and algorithms have been proposed. However, the fairness guarantee of existing fair classification methods mainly depends on specific data distributional assumptions, often requiring large sample sizes, and fairness could be violated when there is a modest number of samples, which is often the case in practice. In this paper, we propose FaiREE, a fair classification algorithm that can satisfy group fairness constraints with finite-sample and distribution-free theoretical guarantees. FaiREE can be adapted to satisfy various group fairness notions (e.g., Equality of Opportunity, Equalized Odds, Demographic Parity, etc.) and achieve the optimal accuracy. These theoretical guarantees are further supported by experiments on both synthetic and real data. FaiREE is shown to have favorable performance over state-of-the-art algorithms.
中摘: 算法公平性在机器学习研究中发挥着越来越关键的作用。已经提出了几种群体公平性概念和算法。然而，现有公平分类方法的公平性保证主要依赖于特定的数据分布假设，通常需要大量样本，而在实践中当样本数量仅为适度时，公平性可能会被违反。在本文中，我们提出了一种名为FaiREE的公平分类算法，该算法能够在有限样本量和无分布假设的情况下满足群体公平性约束，并提供理论上的公平性保证。FaiREE可以适应满足各种群体公平性概念（例如机会平等、同等概率、人口平等性等），并实现最优准确性。这些理论上的保证进一步得到了合成数据和真实数据实验的支持。FaiREE的表现优于现有的先进算法。

[No.133] TextShield: Beyond Successfully Detecting Adversarial Sentences in text classification
来源: ICLR 2023
命中: classif
标题: TextShield：超越文本分类中成功检测对抗句子
类别: 
作者: Lingfeng Shen;Ze Zhang;Haiyun Jiang;Ying Chen;
机构: Johns Hopkins University;Fudan University;Tencent;China Agricultural University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2023/poster/10789
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2302.02023
英摘: Adversarial attack serves as a major challenge for neural network models in NLP, which precludes the model's deployment in safety-critical applications. A recent line of work, detection-based defense, aims to distinguish adversarial sentences from benign ones. However, {the core limitation of previous detection methods is being incapable of giving correct predictions on adversarial sentences unlike defense methods from other paradigms.} To solve this issue, this paper proposes TextShield: (1) we discover a link between text attack and saliency information, and then we propose a saliency-based detector, which can effectively detect whether an input sentence is adversarial or not. (2) We design a saliency-based corrector, which converts the detected adversary sentences to benign ones. By combining the saliency-based detector and corrector, TextShield extends the detection-only paradigm to a detection-correction paradigm, thus filling the gap in the existing detection-based defense. Comprehensive experiments show that (a) TextShield consistently achieves higher or comparable performance than state-of-the-art defense methods across various attacks on different benchmarks. (b) our saliency-based detector outperforms existing detectors for detecting adversarial sentences.
中摘: 对抗攻击是自然语言处理（NLP）神经网络模型面临的主要挑战之一，这妨碍了模型在关键安全应用中的部署。近期的研究方向是基于检测的防御方法，旨在区分对抗性句子和良性句子。然而，之前检测方法的核心局限在于无法在预测对抗性句子时给出正确的结果，不同于其他范式的防御方法。为解决这一问题，本文提出了TextShield：（1）我们发现文本攻击与显著性信息之间的关联，并提出了一种基于显著性的检测器，该检测器能够有效判断输入句子是否为对抗性句子。（2）我们设计了一种基于显著性的修正器，将检测出的对抗性句子转换为良性句子。通过结合基于显著性的检测器和修正器，TextShield 将仅检测的范式扩展到了检测-修正范式，从而填补了现有基于检测的防御方法中的空白。综合实验表明，（a）TextShield 在不同基准上的各种攻击下的性能均高于或可媲美最先进的防御方法。（b）我们的基于显著性的检测器在检测对抗性句子方面优于现有的检测器。

[No.134] Cross-Level Distillation and Feature Denoising for Cross-Domain Few-Shot Classification
来源: ICLR 2023
命中: classif
标题: 跨层次蒸馏与特征去噪在跨领域少样本分类中的应用
类别: 
作者: Hao ZHENG;Runqi Wang;Jianzhuang Liu;Asako Kanezaki;
机构: Tokyo Institute of Technology;Huawei;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2023/poster/12203
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.02392
英摘: The conventional few-shot classification aims at learning a model on a large labeled base dataset and rapidly adapting to a target dataset that is from the same distribution as the base dataset. However, in practice, the base and the target datasets of few-shot classification are usually from different domains, which is the problem of cross-domain few-shot classification. We tackle this problem by making a small proportion of unlabeled images in the target domain accessible in the training stage. In this setup, even though the base data are sufficient and labeled, the large domain shift still makes transferring the knowledge from the base dataset difficult. We meticulously design a cross-level knowledge distillation method, which can strengthen the ability of the model to extract more discriminative features in the target dataset by guiding the network's shallow layers to learn higher-level information. Furthermore, in order to alleviate the overfitting in the evaluation stage, we propose a feature denoising operation which can reduce the feature redundancy and mitigate overfitting. Our approach can surpass the previous state-of-the-art method, Dynamic-Distillation, by 5.44% on 1-shot and 1.37% on 5-shot classification tasks on average in the BSCD-FSL benchmark. The implementation code will be available at https://github.com/jarucezh/cldfd.
中摘: 传统的少样本分类旨在在一个大型带标签的基础数据集上学习一个模型，并在目标数据集快速适应时，该目标数据集与基础数据集来自相同的分布。然而，在实践中，少样本分类的基础数据集和目标数据集通常来自不同的领域，这就是少样本跨域分类的问题。我们通过在训练阶段使目标域的一部分未标记图像可访问来解决这个问题。在这种设置下，尽管基础数据足够且带标签，但大规模的领域转移仍然使得从基础数据集迁移知识变得困难。我们精心设计了一种跨层级的知识蒸馏方法，可以通过引导网络的浅层层学习更高层次的信息，从而增强模型在目标数据集中提取更具区分性的特征的能力。此外，为了缓解评估阶段的过拟合问题，我们提出了一种特征去噪操作，可以减少特征冗余并减轻过拟合。我们的方法在BSCD-FSL基准测试中的1-shot和5-shot分类任务中平均分别超越了之前的最佳方法Dynamic-Distillation 5.44%和1.37%。实施代码将在https://github.com/jarucezh/cldfd上提供。

[No.135] A Multi-Grained Self-Interpretable Symbolic-Neural Model For Single/Multi-Labeled Text Classification
来源: ICLR 2023
命中: classif
标题: 一种多粒度自解释符号-神经模型用于单标签/多标签文本分类
类别: 
作者: Xiang Hu;XinYu KONG;Kewei Tu;
机构: Alibaba Group;ShanghaiTech University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2023/poster/12223
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2303.02860
英摘: Deep neural networks based on layer-stacking architectures have historically suffered from poor inherent interpretability. Meanwhile, symbolic probabilistic models function with clear interpretability, but how to combine them with neural networks to enhance their performance remains to be explored. In this paper, we try to marry these two systems for text classification via a structured language model. We propose a Symbolic-Neural model that can learn to explicitly predict class labels of text spans from a constituency tree without requiring any access to span-level gold labels. As the structured language model learns to predict constituency trees in a self-supervised manner, only raw texts and sentence-level labels are required as training data, which makes it essentially a general constituent-level self-interpretable classification model. Our experiments demonstrate that our approach could achieve good prediction accuracy in downstream tasks. Meanwhile, the predicted span labels are consistent with human rationales to a certain degree.
中摘: 基于层堆叠架构的深度神经网络历史上在固有可解释性方面表现不佳。与此同时，符号概率模型具有明确的可解释性，但如何将它们与神经网络结合以提升性能仍有待探索。在本文中，我们尝试通过结构化语言模型将这两种系统结合起来用于文本分类。我们提出了一种符号-神经模型，该模型能够学习从构成树中显式地预测文本片段的类别标签，而无需访问任何片段级别的金标准标签。由于结构化语言模型以自监督的方式学习预测构成树，因此仅需原始文本和句子级别的标签作为训练数据，这使其本质上成为一种通用的构成级别自我可解释分类模型。我们的实验表明，该方法在下游任务中能够实现良好的预测准确性。同时，预测出的片段标签在一定程度上与人类的推理一致。

[No.136] Spiking Convolutional Neural Networks for Text Classification
来源: ICLR 2023
命中: classif
标题: 基于尖峰卷积神经网络的文本分类
类别: 
作者: Changze Lv;Jianhan Xu;Xiaoqing Zheng;
机构: Fudan University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2023/poster/11720
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.19230
英摘: Spiking neural networks (SNNs) offer a promising pathway to implement deep neural networks (DNNs) in a more energy-efficient manner since their neurons are sparsely activated and inferences are event-driven. However, there have been very few works that have demonstrated the efficacy of SNNs in language tasks partially because it is non-trivial to represent words in the forms of spikes and to deal with variable-length texts by SNNs. This work presents a "conversion + fine-tuning" two-step method for training SNNs for text classification and proposes a simple but effective way to encode pre-trained word embeddings as spike trains. We show empirically that after fine-tuning with surrogate gradients, the converted SNNs achieve comparable results to their DNN counterparts with much less energy consumption across multiple datasets for both English and Chinese. We also show that such SNNs are more robust to adversarial attacks than DNNs.
中摘: 脉冲神经网络（SNNs）提供了一种更具能效性的实现深度神经网络（DNNs）的方法，因为它们的神经元稀疏激活且推理是由事件驱动的。然而，由于将单词表示为脉冲形式并处理不同长度的文本对于SNNs来说并不简单，因此在语言任务中很少有研究表明SNNs的有效性。本工作提出了一种“转换+微调”两步方法来训练SNNs进行文本分类，并提出了一种简单而有效的方法，将预训练的词嵌入编码为脉冲序列。我们通过实验证明，在对多种英语和中文数据集进行微调后，转换得到的SNNs在能耗方面远低于其DNN对应模型，但仍能达到相当的性能。此外，我们还证明了这样的SNNs相较于DNNs具有更好的对抗攻击鲁棒性。

[No.137] Distributionally Robust Post-hoc Classifiers under Prior Shifts
来源: ICLR 2023
命中: classif
标题: 分布鲁棒性事后分类器在先验偏移下的应用
类别: 
作者: Jiaheng Wei;Harikrishna Narasimhan;Ehsan Amid;Wen-Sheng Chu;Yang Liu;Abhishek Kumar;
机构: ByteDance;Google;University of California, Santa Cruz;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2023/poster/11787
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2309.08825
英摘: The generalization ability of machine learning models degrades significantly when the test distribution shifts away from the training distribution. We investigate the problem of training models that are robust to shifts caused by changes in the distribution of class-priors or group-priors. The presence of skewed training priors can often lead to the models overfitting to spurious features. Unlike existing methods, which optimize for either the worst or the average performance over classes or groups, our work is motivated by the need for finer control over the robustness properties of the model. We present an extremely lightweight post-hoc approach that performs scaling adjustments to predictions from a pre-trained model, with the goal of minimizing a distributionally robust loss around a chosen target distribution. These adjustments are computed by solving a constrained optimization problem on a validation set and applied to the model during test time. Our constrained optimization objective is inspired by a natural notion of robustness to controlled distribution shifts. Our method comes with provable guarantees and empirically makes a strong case for distributional robust post-hoc classifiers. An empirical implementation is available at https://github.com/weijiaheng/Drops.
中摘: 机器学习模型在测试分布偏离训练分布时，其泛化能力会显著下降。我们研究了训练能够抵御由类别先验或群体先验分布变化引起的变化的模型的问题。训练数据中的偏斜先验往往会导致模型过度拟合到虚假特征。与现有的方法不同，这些方法优化的是整个类别或群体中最差或平均的表现，我们的工作旨在对模型的鲁棒性属性进行更精细的控制。我们提出了一种极其轻量级的后处理方法，该方法通过对预训练模型的预测进行缩放调整，以最小化围绕选定目标分布的分布鲁棒损失。这些调整通过在验证集上求解受限优化问题来计算，并在测试时应用于模型。我们的受限优化目标受到对受控分布转移具有自然鲁棒性的概念的启发。我们的方法具有可证明的保证，并且从实验上强烈支持分布鲁棒的后处理分类器。实验实现可在https://github.com/weijiaheng/Drops找到。

[No.138] Suitability Filter: A Statistical Framework for Classifier Evaluation in Real-World Deployment Settings
来源: ICML 2025
命中: classif
标题: 适配性过滤器：在实际部署环境中分类器评估的统计框架
类别: social aspects->everything else
作者: Angéline Pouget;Mohammad Yaghini;Stephan Rabanser;Nicolas Papernot;
机构: Google;;University of Toronto;
类型: Oral
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45090
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.22356
英摘: Deploying machine learning models in safety-critical domains poses a key challenge: ensuring reliable model performance on downstream user data without access to ground truth labels for direct validation. We propose the suitability filter, a novel framework designed to detect performance deterioration by utilizing suitability signals -- model output features that are sensitive to covariate shifts and indicative of potential prediction errors. The suitability filter evaluates whether classifier accuracy on unlabeled user data shows significant degradation compared to the accuracy measured on the labeled test dataset. Specifically, it ensures that this degradation does not exceed a pre-specified margin, which represents the maximum acceptable drop in accuracy. To achieve reliable performance evaluation, we aggregate suitability signals for both test and user data and compare these empirical distributions using statistical hypothesis testing, thus providing insights into decision uncertainty. Our modular method adapts to various models and domains. Empirical evaluations across different classification tasks demonstrate that the suitability filter reliably detects performance deviations due to covariate shift. This enables proactive mitigation of potential failures in high-stakes applications.
中摘: 在安全关键领域部署机器学习模型面临着一个关键挑战：确保模型在下游用户数据上的可靠性能，而无需直接访问地面真实标签进行验证。我们提出了一种新颖的框架——适合性过滤器，该框架旨在通过利用适合性信号（对协变量偏移敏感且能指示潜在预测错误的模型输出特征）来检测性能下降。具体来说，适合性过滤器评估未标记用户数据上的分类器准确性是否显著下降，与在标记测试数据集上测量的准确性进行比较。它确保这种下降不超过预设的阈值，即最大可接受的准确性下降幅度。为了实现可靠的性能评估，适合性过滤器聚合了测试数据和用户数据的适合性信号，并使用统计假设检验比较这些经验分布，从而提供决策不确定性方面的见解。我们的模块化方法适用于各种模型和领域。在不同分类任务上的实证评估表明，适合性过滤器能够可靠地检测由于协变量偏移导致的性能偏差，这有助于在高风险应用中主动缓解潜在故障。

[No.140] Principled Algorithms for Optimizing Generalized Metrics in Binary Classification
来源: ICML 2025
命中: classif
标题: 基于原则的优化通用度量算法在二元分类中的应用
类别: general machine learning->supervised learning
作者: Anqi Mao;Mehryar Mohri;Yutao Zhong;
机构: New York University;Google;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/44895
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.23133
英摘: In applications with significant class imbalance or asymmetric costs, metrics such as the $F_β$-measure, AM measure, Jaccard similarity coefficient, and weighted accuracy offer more suitable evaluation criteria than standard binary classification loss. However, optimizing these metrics present significant computational and statistical challenges. Existing approaches often rely on the characterization of the Bayes-optimal classifier, and use threshold-based methods that first estimate class probabilities and then seek an optimal threshold. This leads to algorithms that are not tailored to restricted hypothesis sets and lack finite-sample performance guarantees. In this work, we introduce principled algorithms for optimizing generalized metrics, supported by $H$-consistency and finite-sample generalization bounds. Our approach reformulates metric optimization as a generalized cost-sensitive learning problem, enabling the design of novel surrogate loss functions with provable $H$-consistency guarantees. Leveraging this framework, we develop new algorithms, METRO (Metric Optimization), with strong theoretical performance guarantees. We report the results of experiments demonstrating the effectiveness of our methods compared to prior baselines.
中摘: 在具有显著类别不平衡或不对称成本的应用中，如$F_β$-度量、AM度量、雅卡德相似系数和加权准确率等指标比标准二分类损失提供了更合适的评估标准。然而，优化这些指标会面临重大的计算和统计挑战。现有方法通常依赖于贝叶斯最优分类器的表征，并使用基于阈值的方法首先估计类别概率，然后寻找最优阈值。这导致了不适合限制性假设集的算法，并缺乏有限样本性能保证。在本文中，我们引入了基于$H$-一致性及有限样本泛化界的原则性算法，用于优化广义指标。我们的方法将指标优化重新表述为广义成本敏感学习问题，使得能够设计出具有可证明$H$-一致性保证的新替代损失函数。利用这一框架，我们开发了新的算法METRO（Metric Optimization），并报告了实验结果，展示了与先前基准相比，我们方法的有效性。

[No.142] Statistical Query Hardness of Multiclass Linear Classification with Random Classification Noise
来源: ICML 2025
命中: classif
标题: 统计查询下的多类线性分类随机分类噪声 hardness
类别: theory->learning theory
作者: Ilias Diakonikolas;Mingchen Ma;Lisheng Ren;Christos Tzamos;
机构: University of Wisconsin-Madison;University of Athens;
类型: Oral
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45937
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.11413
英摘: We study the task of Multiclass Linear Classification (MLC) in the distribution-free PAC model with Random Classification Noise (RCN). Specifically, the learner is given a set of labeled examples $(x, y)$, where $x$ is drawn from an unknown distribution on $R^d$ and the labels are generated by a multiclass linear classifier corrupted with RCN. That is, the label $y$ is flipped from $i$ to $j$ with probability $H_{ij}$ according to a known noise matrix $H$ with non-negative separation $σ: = \min_{i \neq j} H_{ii}-H_{ij}$. The goal is to compute a hypothesis with small 0-1 error. For the special case of two labels, prior work has given polynomial-time algorithms achieving the optimal error. Surprisingly, little is known about the complexity of this task even for three labels. As our main contribution, we show that the complexity of MLC with RCN becomes drastically different in the presence of three or more labels. Specifically, we prove super-polynomial Statistical Query (SQ) lower bounds for this problem. In more detail, even for three labels and constant separation, we give a super-polynomial lower bound on the complexity of any SQ algorithm achieving optimal error. For a larger number of labels and smaller separation, we show a super-polynomial SQ lower bound even for the weaker goal of achieving any constant factor approximation to the optimal loss or even beating the trivial hypothesis.
中摘: 我们在无分布假设的PAC模型中研究了随机分类噪声下的多类线性分类（MLC）任务。具体来说，学习者得到了一组带标签的例子$(x, y)$，其中$x$来自未知的$d$维分布，而标签是由一个被随机分类噪声（RCN）污染的多类线性分类器生成的。也就是说，标签$y$从类别$i$被翻转到类别$j$的概率为已知噪声矩阵$H$中的$H_{ij}$，且该噪声矩阵具有非负分离度$\sigma := \min_{i \neq j} H_{ii} - H_{ij}$。目标是计算出一个具有小0-1错误率的假设。对于特殊情况的两类别标签，先前的工作已经提供了实现最优错误率的多项式时间算法。令人惊讶的是，即使在三类别标签的情况下，关于此任务复杂性的了解也非常有限。作为我们的主要贡献，我们证明了在存在三个或更多类别时，MLC任务在随机分类噪声下的复杂性变得截然不同。具体而言，我们证明了即使对于三类别标签和常数分离度，任何实现最优错误率的统计查询（SQ）算法的复杂性也存在超多项式的下界。对于更多的类别标签和较小的分离度，我们甚至证明了即使在更弱的目标下，即达到最优损失的某个常数因子近似或甚至击败平凡假设，其SQ算法的复杂性也存在超多项式的下界。

[No.143] Learning Soft Sparse Shapes for Efficient Time-Series Classification
来源: ICML 2025
命中: classif
标题: 学习软稀疏形状以实现高效时间序列分类
类别: deep learning->sequential models time series
作者: Zhen Liu;Yicheng Luo;Boyuan Li;Emadeldeen Eldele;Min Wu;Qianli Ma;
机构: South China University of Technology;Agency for Science, Technology and Research;Institute for Infocomm Research;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/46130
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.06892
英摘: Shapelets are discriminative subsequences (or shapes) with high interpretability in time series classification. Due to the time-intensive nature of shapelet discovery, existing shapelet-based methods mainly focus on selecting discriminative shapes while discarding others to achieve candidate subsequence sparsification. However, this approach may exclude beneficial shapes and overlook the varying contributions of shapelets to classification performance. To this end, we propose a Soft sparse Shapes (SoftShape) model for efficient time series classification. Our approach mainly introduces soft shape sparsification and soft shape learning blocks. The former transforms shapes into soft representations based on classification contribution scores, merging lower-scored ones into a single shape to retain and differentiate all subsequence information. The latter facilitates intra- and inter-shape temporal pattern learning, improving model efficiency by using sparsified soft shapes as inputs. Specifically, we employ a learnable router to activate a subset of class-specific expert networks for intra-shape pattern learning. Meanwhile, a shared expert network learns inter-shape patterns by converting sparsified shapes into sequences. Extensive experiments show that SoftShape outperforms state-of-the-art methods and produces interpretable results.
中摘: 形状子序列（或形状）在时间序列分类中具有高度可解释性。由于形状子序列发现过程耗时，现有的基于形状子序列的方法主要集中在选择具有区分性的形状并丢弃其他形状以实现候选子序列的稀疏化。然而，这种方法可能会排除有益的形状，并忽视形状子序列对分类性能的不同贡献。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了一种高效的软稀疏形状（SoftShape）模型。我们的方法主要引入了软形状稀疏化和软形状学习模块。前者基于分类贡献分数将形状转换为软表示，合并低评分的形状为单一形状，从而保留和区分所有子序列的信息。后者促进了形状内和形状间的时间模式学习，通过使用稀疏化的软形状作为输入来提高模型效率。具体而言，我们采用可学习的路由器激活特定类别的专家网络进行形状内模式学习。同时，共享的专家网络通过将稀疏化的形状转换为序列来学习形状间的模式。广泛的实验表明，SoftShape 在性能上优于现有最先进的方法，并且能够生成可解释的结果。

[No.148] Runtime Analysis of Evolutionary NAS for Multiclass Classification
来源: ICML 2025
命中: classif
标题: 多类分类任务中进化NAS的运行时分析
类别: theory->deep learning
作者: Zeqiong Lv;Chao Qian;Yun Liu;Jiahao Fan;Yanan Sun;
机构: ;Nanjing University;Sichuan University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/44728
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.06019
英摘: Evolutionary neural architecture search (ENAS) is a key part of evolutionary machine learning, which commonly utilizes evolutionary algorithms (EAs) to automatically design high-performing deep neural architectures. During past years, various ENAS methods have been proposed with exceptional performance. However, the theory research of ENAS is still in the infant. In this work, we step for the runtime analysis, which is an essential theory aspect of EAs, of ENAS upon multiclass classification problems. Specifically, we first propose a benchmark to lay the groundwork for the analysis. Furthermore, we design a two-level search space, making it suitable for multiclass classification problems and consistent with the common settings of ENAS. Based on both designs, we consider (1+1)-ENAS algorithms with one-bit and bit-wise mutations, and analyze their upper and lower bounds on the expected runtime. We prove that the algorithm using both mutations can find the optimum with the expected runtime upper bound of $O(rM\ln{rM})$ and lower bound of $Ω(rM\ln{M})$. This suggests that a simple one-bit mutation may be greatly considered, given that most state-of-the-art ENAS methods are laboriously designed with the bit-wise mutation. Empirical studies also support our theoretical proof.
中摘: 进化神经架构搜索（ENAS）是进化机器学习的关键部分，通常利用进化算法（EAs）自动设计高性能的深度神经网络架构。在过去几年中，各种ENAS方法被提出并表现出卓越的性能。然而，ENAS的理论研究仍然处于初级阶段。在这项工作中，我们对ENAS进行了运行时分析，这是EAs的重要理论方面之一，特别是在多类分类问题上。具体来说，我们首先提出了一个基准，为分析奠定基础。此外，我们设计了一个两层搜索空间，使其适用于多类分类问题，并与ENAS常见的设置保持一致。基于这两种设计，我们考虑了一种(1+1)-ENAS算法，该算法使用了一比特和位操作突变，并分析了它们在期望运行时上的上界和下界。我们证明，使用两种突变的算法可以在期望运行时的上界为$O(rM\ln{rM})$和下界为$\Omega(rM\ln{M})$的情况下找到最优解。这表明，在大多数最先进的ENAS方法中，虽然使用了位操作突变，但简单的一比特突变也应得到极大的重视。实验研究也支持我们的理论证明。

[No.151] Focal-SAM: Focal Sharpness-Aware Minimization for Long-Tailed Classification
来源: ICML 2025
命中: classif
标题: 焦点-SAM：针对长尾分类的焦点锐化感知最小化
类别: general machine learning->supervised learning
作者: Sicong Li;Qianqian Xu;Zhiyong Yang;Zitai Wang;Linchao Zhang;Xiaochun Cao;Qingming Huang;
机构: Chinese Academy of Sciences;University of Chinese Academy of Sciences;Artificial Intelligence Institute of China Electronics Technology Group Corporation;Sun Yat-sen University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/44211
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.01660
英摘: Real-world datasets often follow a long-tailed distribution, making generalization to tail classes difficult. Recent methods resorted to long-tail variants of Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM), such as ImbSAM and CC-SAM, to improve generalization by flattening the loss landscape. However, these attempts face a trade-off between computational efficiency and control over the loss landscape. On the one hand, ImbSAM is efficient but offers only coarse control as it excludes head classes from the SAM process. On the other hand, CC-SAM provides fine-grained control through class-dependent perturbations but at the cost of efficiency due to multiple backpropagations. Seeing this dilemma, we introduce Focal-SAM, which assigns different penalties to class-wise sharpness, achieving fine-grained control without extra backpropagations, thus maintaining efficiency. Furthermore, we theoretically analyze Focal-SAM's generalization ability and derive a sharper generalization bound. Extensive experiments on both traditional and foundation models validate the effectiveness of Focal-SAM.
中摘: 真实世界的数据集通常遵循长尾分布，这使得泛化到尾部类别的任务变得困难。最近的方法采用了长尾版本的Sharpness-Aware Minimization（SAM），如ImbSAM和CC-SAM，通过平坦化损失景观来提高泛化能力。然而，这些方法在计算效率和对损失景观的控制之间面临权衡。一方面，ImbSAM计算效率较高，但仅能提供粗略控制，因为它将头类排除在SAM过程中；另一方面，CC-SAM通过类别相关的扰动提供了精细控制，但由于多次反向传播，其效率较低。面对这一困境，我们引入了Focal-SAM，它根据不同类别的sharpness分配不同的惩罚，从而在无需额外反向传播的情况下实现精细控制，保持了计算效率。此外，我们还从理论上分析了Focal-SAM的泛化能力，并推导出了更紧的泛化界。广泛的实验验证了Focal-SAM的有效性，无论是传统的模型还是基础模型。

[No.152] Should Decision-Makers Reveal Classifiers in Online Strategic Classification?
来源: ICML 2025
命中: classif
标题: 在线战略分类中决策者应披露分类器吗？
类别: theory->game theory
作者: Han Shao;Shuo Xie;Kunhe Yang;
机构: Harvard University;Toyota Technological Institute at Chicago;University of California, Berkeley;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45611
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.01936
英摘: Strategic classification addresses a learning problem where a decision-maker implements a classifier over agents who may manipulate their features in order to receive favorable predictions. In the standard model of online strategic classification, in each round, the decision-maker implements and publicly reveals a classifier, after which agents perfectly best respond based on this knowledge. However, in practice, whether to disclose the classifier is often debated -- some decision-makers believe that hiding the classifier can prevent misclassification errors caused by manipulation.
  In this paper, we formally examine how limiting the agents' access to the current classifier affects the decision-maker's performance. Specifically, we consider an extended online strategic classification setting where agents lack direct knowledge about the current classifier and instead manipulate based on a weighted average of historically implemented classifiers. Our main result shows that in this setting, the decision-maker incurs $(1-γ)^{-1}$ or $k_{\text{in}}$ times more mistakes compared to the full-knowledge setting, where $k_{\text{in}}$ is the maximum in-degree of the manipulation graph (representing how many distinct feature vectors can be manipulated to appear as a single one), and $γ$ is the discount factor indicating agents' memory of past classifiers. Our results demonstrate how withholding access to the classifier can backfire and degrade the decision-maker's performance in online strategic classification.
中摘: 战略分类解决了一个学习问题，其中决策者需要对可能操控其特征以获得有利预测的代理实施分类器。在标准的在线战略分类模型中，每一轮中，决策者都会实施并公开一个分类器，随后代理会基于这些信息进行完美的最优响应。然而，在实践中，是否披露分类器往往存在争议——一些决策者认为隐藏分类器可以防止由于操控导致的误分类错误。

在本文中，我们正式研究了限制代理访问当前分类器对其性能的影响。具体来说，我们考虑了一个扩展的在线战略分类设置，在这个设置中，代理没有直接了解当前分类器的知识，而是根据历史上实施的分类器的加权平均值来进行操控。我们的主要结果显示，在这种设置下，决策者犯的错误数量是完全知情设置下的$(1-\gamma)^{-1}$或$k_{\text{in}}$倍，其中$k_{\text{in}}$是操控图的最大入度（表示多少种不同的特征向量可以通过操控表现为单一的特征向量），$\gamma$是折扣因子，反映了代理对过去分类器的记忆程度。我们的结果表明，隐瞒分类器的访问权限可能会适得其反，从而降低决策者在在线战略分类中的表现。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

[No.154] TabPFN Unleashed: A Scalable and Effective Solution to Tabular Classification Problems
来源: ICML 2025
命中: classif
标题: TabPFN 解锁：一个适用于表格分类问题的可扩展且有效的解决方案
类别: deep learning
作者: Siyang Liu;Han-Jia Ye;
机构: Nanjing University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/46442
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.02527
英摘: TabPFN has emerged as a promising in-context learning model for tabular data, capable of directly predicting the labels of test samples given labeled training examples. It has demonstrated competitive performance, particularly on small-scale classification tasks. However, despite its effectiveness, TabPFN still requires further refinement in several areas, including handling high-dimensional features, aligning with downstream datasets, and scaling to larger datasets. In this paper, we revisit existing variants of TabPFN and observe that most approaches focus either on reducing bias or variance, often neglecting the need to address the other side, while also increasing inference overhead. To fill this gap, we propose Beta (Bagging and Encoder-based Fine-tuning for TabPFN Adaptation), a novel and effective method designed to minimize both bias and variance. To reduce bias, we introduce a lightweight encoder to better align downstream tasks with the pre-trained TabPFN. By increasing the number of encoders in a lightweight manner, Beta mitigate variance, thereby further improving the model's performance. Additionally, bootstrapped sampling is employed to further reduce the impact of data perturbations on the model, all while maintaining computational efficiency during inference. Our approach enhances TabPFN's ability to handle high-dimensional data and scale to larger datasets. Experimental results on over 200 benchmark classification datasets demonstrate that Beta either outperforms or matches state-of-the-art methods.
中摘: TabPFN 已经成为一种有前景的针对表格数据的增量学习模型，能够直接根据标记的训练样本预测测试样本的标签。它在小型分类任务上展示了竞争力，然而，尽管效果显著，TabPFN 在处理高维特征、与下游数据集对齐以及扩展到更大数据集方面仍需进一步完善。在本文中，我们重新审视现有的 TabPFN 变体，并观察到大多数方法要么专注于减少偏差，要么专注于减少方差，往往忽视了需要同时解决另一端的问题，同时也增加了推理开销。为了填补这一空白，我们提出了一种名为 Beta（基于袋装和编码器微调的 TabPFN 调整方法）的新颖且有效的方法，旨在同时最小化偏差和方差。为了减少偏差，我们引入了一个轻量级编码器来更好地将下游任务与预训练的 TabPFN 对齐。通过以轻量级的方式增加编码器的数量，Beta 减少了方差，从而进一步提高了模型的性能。此外，我们采用了自助采样技术，以进一步减少数据扰动对模型的影响，同时保持推理过程中的计算效率。我们的方法增强了 TabPFN 处理高维数据和扩展到更大数据集的能力。实验结果表明，在超过 200 个基准分类数据集上，Beta 或者优于或者匹配最先进的方法。

[No.156] Direct Prediction Set Minimization via Bilevel Conformal Classifier Training
来源: ICML 2025
命中: classif
标题: 直接预测集最小化通过双层一致分类器训练
类别: general machine learning
作者: Yuanjie Shi;Hooman Shahrokhi;Xuesong Jia;Xiongzhi Chen;Jana Doppa;Yan Yan;
机构: Washington State University;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45700
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.06599
英摘: Conformal prediction (CP) is a promising uncertainty quantification framework which works as a wrapper around a black-box classifier to construct prediction sets (i.e., subset of candidate classes) with provable guarantees. However, standard calibration methods for CP tend to produce large prediction sets which makes them less useful in practice. This paper considers the problem of integrating conformal principles into the training process of deep classifiers to directly minimize the size of prediction sets. We formulate conformal training as a bilevel optimization problem and propose the {\em Direct Prediction Set Minimization (DPSM)} algorithm to solve it. The key insight behind DPSM is to minimize a measure of the prediction set size (upper level) that is conditioned on the learned quantile of conformity scores (lower level). We analyze that DPSM has a learning bound of $O(1/\sqrt{n})$ (with $n$ training samples), while prior conformal training methods based on stochastic approximation for the quantile has a bound of $Ω(1/s)$ (with batch size $s$ and typically $s \ll \sqrt{n}$). Experiments on various benchmark datasets and deep models show that DPSM significantly outperforms the best prior conformal training baseline with $20.46\%\downarrow$ in the prediction set size and validates our theory.
中摘: 自适应预测集最小化（DPSM）算法是一种有前途的不确定性量化框架，它可以作为一个包装器围绕黑盒分类器，以构建具有可证明保证的预测集（即候选类别的子集）。然而，标准的自适应预测集校准方法往往会生成较大的预测集，这在实践中使其不太有用。本文考虑了将自适应原则集成到深度分类器的训练过程中的问题，以直接最小化预测集的大小。我们形式化地将自适应预测集训练表述为一个多层优化问题，并提出了DPSM算法来解决它。DPSM的核心洞察在于最小化预测集大小的一种度量（上层），该度量依赖于学习到的符合分数分位数（下层）。我们分析表明，DPSM的学习界为$O(1/\sqrt{n})$（其中$n$为训练样本数量），而基于随机近似计算分位数的先前自适应预测集训练方法的学习界为$Ω(1/s)$（其中$s$为批次大小，通常$s \ll \sqrt{n}$）。在各种基准数据集和深度模型上的实验表明，DPSM显著优于先前的最佳自适应预测集训练基线，预测集大小减少了$20.46\%$，并验证了我们的理论。

[No.157] Improving Continual Learning Performance and Efficiency with Auxiliary Classifiers
来源: ICML 2025
命中: classif
标题: 使用辅助分类器提高持续学习性能和效率
类别: general machine learning->transfer multitask and metalearning
作者: Filip Szatkowski;Yaoyue Zheng;Fei Yang;Tomasz Trzcinski;Bartłomiej Twardowski;Joost van de Weijer;
机构: Institute for Development, Economic Analysis, and Simulation (IDEAS);Warsaw University of Technology;Amazon;Universitat Autónoma de Barcelona;Xi'an Jiao Tong University;Nankai University;Universitat Autònoma de Barcelona;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/43813
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.07404
英摘: Continual learning is crucial for applying machine learning in challenging, dynamic, and often resource-constrained environments. However, catastrophic forgetting - overwriting previously learned knowledge when new information is acquired - remains a major challenge. In this work, we examine the intermediate representations in neural network layers during continual learning and find that such representations are less prone to forgetting, highlighting their potential to accelerate computation. Motivated by these findings, we propose to use auxiliary classifiers(ACs) to enhance performance and demonstrate that integrating ACs into various continual learning methods consistently improves accuracy across diverse evaluation settings, yielding an average 10% relative gain. We also leverage the ACs to reduce the average cost of the inference by 10-60% without compromising accuracy, enabling the model to return the predictions before computing all the layers. Our approach provides a scalable and efficient solution for continual learning.
中摘: 持续学习在应用机器学习于挑战性、动态且常常资源受限的环境中至关重要。然而，灾难性遗忘——当新信息被获取时覆盖之前学到的知识——仍然是一个主要挑战。在这项工作中，我们研究了持续学习过程中神经网络层中的中间表示，并发现这些表示不太容易忘记，突显了它们加速计算的潜力。受此发现的启发，我们提出使用辅助分类器（ACs）来提升性能，并证明将ACs整合到各种持续学习方法中能够在多种评估设置中一致地提高准确率，平均获得10%的相对增益。我们还利用ACs减少了推理的平均成本10-60%，而不牺牲准确率，使模型能够在计算所有层之前返回预测结果。我们的方法为持续学习提供了一个可扩展且高效的解决方案。
