[No.158] Disparate Conditional Prediction in Multiclass Classifiers
来源: ICML 2025
命中: classif
标题: 多类分类器中的异质条件预测
类别: social aspects->fairness
作者: Sivan Sabato;Eran Treister;Elad Yom-Tov;
机构: McMaster University;Ben-Gurion University of the Negev;Bar-Ilan University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45683
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2206.03234
英摘: We propose methods for auditing multiclass classifiers for fairness under multiclass equalized odds,by estimating the deviation from equalized odds when the classifier is not completely fair. We generalize to multiclass classifiers the measure of Disparate Conditional Prediction (DCP), originally suggested by Sabato & Yom-Tov (2020) for binary classifiers. DCP is defined as the fraction of the population for which the classifier predicts with conditional prediction probabilities that differ from the closest common baseline. We provide new local-optimization methods for estimating the multiclass DCPunder two different regimes,one in which the conditional confusion matrices for each protected sub-population are known, and one in which these cannot be estimated, for instance, because the classifier is inaccessible or because good-quality individual-level data is not available. These methods can be used to detect classifiers that likely treat a significant fraction of the population unfairly. Experiments demonstrate the accuracy of the methods. Code is provided at https://github.com/sivansabato/DCPmulticlass.
中摘: 我们提出了审计多类别分类器公平性的方法，在分类器不完全公平的情况下估计其与多类别平等机会之间的偏差。我们将原始由Sabato和Yom-Tov（2020年）为二分类器建议的差异条件预测（DCP）度量推广到了多类别分类器。DCP定义为那些分类器预测条件概率与最近的共同基线不同的群体所占的比例。我们提供了两种新的局部优化方法来估计多类别DCP，在这两种方法中，每种受保护子群体的条件混淆矩阵可能已知，也可能无法估计，例如，因为分类器不可访问或缺乏高质量的个体水平数据。这些方法可用于检测可能不公平对待显著部分人群的分类器。实验表明了这些方法的准确性。代码可在https://github.com/sivansabato/DCPmulticlass获取。

[No.159] A Market for Accuracy: Classification Under Competition
来源: ICML 2025
命中: classif
标题: 准确性的市场：在竞争中的分类

作者: Ohad Einav;Nir Rosenfeld;

英摘: Machine learning models play a key role for service providers looking to gain market share in consumer markets. However, traditional learning approaches do not take into account the existence of additional providers, who compete with each other for consumers. Our work aims to study learning in this market setting, as it affects providers, consumers, and the market itself. We begin by analyzing such markets through the lens of the learning objective, and show that accuracy cannot be the only consideration. We then propose a method for classification under competition, so that a learner can maximize market share in the presence of competitors. [No.160] FIC-TSC: Learning Time Series Classification with Fisher Information Constraint
来源: ICML 2025
命中: classif
标题: FIC-TSC：带费舍尔信息约束的时间序列分类学习
类别: applications->time series
作者: Xiwen Chen;Wenhui Zhu;Peijie Qiu;Hao Wang;Huayu Li;ZIHAN LI;Yalin Wang;Aristeidis Sotiras;Abolfazl Razi;

英摘: Analyzing time series data is crucial to a wide spectrum of applications, including economics, online marketplaces, and human healthcare. In particular, time series classification plays an indispensable role in segmenting different phases in stock markets, predicting customer behavior, and classifying worker actions and engagement levels. These aspects contribute significantly to the advancement of automated decision-making and system optimization in real-world applications. However, there is a large consensus that time series data often suffers from domain shifts between training and test sets, which dramatically degrades the classification performance. Despite the success of (reversible) instance normalization in handling the domain shifts for time series regression tasks, its performance in classification is unsatisfactory. In this paper, we propose \textit{FIC-TSC}, a training framework for time series classification that leverages Fisher information as the constraint. We theoretically and empirically show this is an efficient and effective solution to guide the model converge toward flatter minima, which enhances its generalizability to distribution shifts. We rigorously evaluate our method on 30 UEA multivariate and 85 UCR univariate datasets. Our empirical results demonstrate the superiority of the proposed method over 14 recent state-of-the-art methods.
中摘: 分析时间序列数据在广泛的应用领域中至关重要，包括经济学、在线市场和人类健康护理。特别是，时间序列分类在股票市场分段不同阶段、预测客户行为以及分类工人的行动和参与水平方面发挥着不可或缺的作用。这些方面对实际应用中的自动化决策和系统优化有重大贡献。然而，普遍认为时间序列数据在训练集和测试集之间经常存在领域偏移问题，这极大地降低了分类性能。尽管可逆实例归一化在处理时间序列回归任务中的领域偏移方面取得了成功，但在分类任务中的表现却不尽如人意。在本文中，我们提出了一种名为\textit{FIC-TSC}的时间序列分类训练框架，该框架利用费舍尔信息作为约束条件。我们从理论和实验上证明，这种方法能有效地引导模型向更平坦的极小值收敛，从而提高其对分布偏移的泛化能力。我们严格评估了该方法在30个UEA多变量和85个UCR单变量数据集上的性能。我们的实验结果表明，所提出的方法优于14种最近的先进方法。

[No.161] On Temperature Scaling and Conformal Prediction of Deep Classifiers
来源: ICML 2025
命中: classif
标题: 温度归一化与深度分类器的齐性预测
类别: deep learning->everything else
作者: Lahav Dabah;Tom Tirer;
机构: Bar-Ilan University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/44049
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.05806
英摘: In many classification applications, the prediction of a deep neural network (DNN) based classifier needs to be accompanied by some confidence indication. Two popular approaches for that aim are: 1) Calibration: modifies the classifier's softmax values such that the maximal value better estimates the correctness probability; and 2) Conformal Prediction (CP): produces a prediction set of candidate labels that contains the true label with a user-specified probability, guaranteeing marginal coverage but not, e.g., per class coverage. In practice, both types of indications are desirable, yet, so far the interplay between them has not been investigated. Focusing on the ubiquitous Temperature Scaling (TS) calibration, we start this paper with an extensive empirical study of its effect on prominent CP methods. We show that while TS calibration improves the class-conditional coverage of adaptive CP methods, surprisingly, it negatively affects their prediction set sizes. Motivated by this behavior, we explore the effect of TS on CP beyond its calibration application and reveal an intriguing trend under which it allows to trade prediction set size and conditional coverage of adaptive CP methods. Then, we establish a mathematical theory that explains the entire non-monotonic trend. Finally, based on our experiments and theory, we offer simple guidelines for practitioners to effectively combine adaptive CP with calibration, aligned with user-defined goals.
中摘: 在许多分类应用中，深度神经网络（DNN）分类器的预测需要伴随一些置信度指示。两种常见的方法是：1）校准：修改分类器的softmax值，使得最大值更好地估计正确性概率；和2）可信预测（CP）：生成一个候选标签的预测集，该集合以用户指定的概率包含真实标签，保证边际覆盖但不保证每类覆盖。目前，这两种指示都是实际中所期望的，然而，迄今为止它们之间的相互作用尚未被研究。本文以广泛使用的温度校准（Temperature Scaling, TS）为切入点，首先进行了对主流可信预测方法效果的详尽实证研究。我们发现，虽然TS校准可以提高自适应可信预测方法的类别条件覆盖，但出乎意料的是，它却会负面影响这些方法的预测集大小。受这种行为的启发，我们进一步探讨了TS对可信预测的影响，并揭示了一种有趣的趋势，即TS允许在自适应可信预测方法的预测集大小和条件覆盖之间进行权衡。然后，我们建立了一个数学理论来解释整个非单调趋势。最后，基于我们的实验和理论，我们为实践者提供了简单指南，以有效地结合自适应可信预测与校准，从而实现用户定义的目标。

[No.162] To Each Metric Its Decoding: Post-Hoc Optimal Decision Rules of Probabilistic Hierarchical Classifiers
来源: ICML 2025
命中: classif
标题: 为每种度量其解码之道：概率层次分类器的后验最优决策规则
类别: general machine learning->evaluation
作者: Roman Plaud;Alexandre Perez-Lebel;Matthieu Labeau;Antoine Saillenfest;Thomas Bonald;
机构: Télécom ParisTech;;onepoint;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/46401
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.01552
英摘: Hierarchical classification offers an approach to incorporate the concept of mistake severity by leveraging a structured, labeled hierarchy. However, decoding in such settings frequently relies on heuristic decision rules, which may not align with task-specific evaluation metrics. In this work, we propose a framework for the optimal decoding of an output probability distribution with respect to a target metric. We derive optimal decision rules for increasingly complex prediction settings, providing universal algorithms when candidates are limited to the set of nodes. In the most general case of predicting a subset of nodes, we focus on rules dedicated to the hierarchical $hF_β$ scores, tailored to hierarchical settings. These results highlight the potential of our methods to enhance the performance and reliability of hierarchical classifiers in real-world applications. [No.164] Learning Classifiers That Induce Markets
来源: ICML 2025
命中: classif
标题: 学习构建市场分类器
类别: social aspects->robustness
作者: Yonatan Sommer;Ivri Hikri;Lotan Amit;Nir Rosenfeld;
机构: Technion - Israel Institute of Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45851
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.20012
英摘: When learning is used to inform decisions about humans, such as for loans, hiring, or admissions, this can incentivize users to strategically modify their features, at a cost, to obtain positive predictions. The common assumption is that the function governing costs is exogenous, fixed, and predetermined. We challenge this assumption, and assert that costs can emerge as a result of deploying a classifier. Our idea is simple: when users seek positive predictions, this creates demand for important features; and if features are available for purchase, then a market will form, and competition will give rise to prices. We extend the strategic classification framework to support this notion, and study learning in a setting where a classifier can induce a market for features. We present an analysis of the learning task, devise an algorithm for computing market prices, propose a differentiable learning framework, and conduct experiments to explore our novel setting and approach.
中摘: 当学习被用来为人类做出贷款、招聘或录取等决策时，这可能会激励用户有策略地修改自己的特征，以获得积极的预测结果。普遍的假设是，成本函数是外生的、固定的并且预先确定的。我们挑战这一假设，并主张成本可能在部署分类器后逐渐形成。我们的想法很简单：当用户寻求积极的预测结果时，这会创造对重要特征的需求；如果这些特征可以购买，那么就会形成一个市场，竞争会导致价格的产生。我们扩展了战略分类框架来支持这一观点，并研究了一个分类器能够诱导特征市场的学习环境。我们对学习任务进行了分析，设计了一种计算市场价格的算法，提出了一个可微学习框架，并进行了实验以探索我们的新型设置和方法。

[No.165] A Classification View on Meta Learning Bandits
来源: ICML 2025
命中: classif
标题: 元学习 bandit 的分类视角
类别: theory->online learning and bandits
作者: Mirco Mutti;Jeongyeol Kwon;Shie Mannor;Aviv Tamar;
机构: Technion - Israel Institute of Technology;University of Wisconsin-Madison;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/44061
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.04505
英摘: Contextual multi-armed bandits are a popular choice to model sequential decision-making. E.g., in a healthcare application we may perform various tests to asses a patient condition (exploration) and then decide on the best treatment to give (exploitation). When humans design strategies, they aim for the exploration to be fast, since the patient's health is at stake, and easy to interpret for a physician overseeing the process. However, common bandit algorithms are nothing like that: The regret caused by exploration scales with $\sqrt{H}$ over $H$ rounds and decision strategies are based on opaque statistical considerations. In this paper, we use an original classification view to meta learn interpretable and fast exploration plans for a fixed collection of bandits $\mathbb{M}$. The plan is prescribed by an interpretable decision tree probing decisions' payoff to classify the test bandit. The test regret of the plan in the stochastic and contextual setting scales with $O (λ^{-2} C_λ (\mathbb{M}) \log^2 (MH))$, being $M$ the size of $\mathbb{M}$, $λ$ a separation parameter over the bandits, and $C_λ(\mathbb{M})$ a novel classification-coefficient that fundamentally links meta learning bandits with classification. Through a nearly matching lower bound, we show that $C_λ(\mathbb{M})$ inherently captures the complexity of the setting.
中摘: 上下文多臂老虎机是建模序列决策的一种流行选择。例如，在医疗应用中，我们可能会进行各种测试来评估患者的状况（探索），然后根据这些测试结果决定给予最佳治疗方案（利用）。当人类设计策略时，他们希望探索过程既快速又易于医生理解，因为患者的健康状况至关重要。然而，常见的老虎机算法并非如此：由于探索引起的遗憾随轮次 \(H\) 的增加按 \(\sqrt{H}\) 的比例增长，而决策策略基于不透明的统计考量。在本文中，我们采用一种原始的分类视角，元学习一个可解释且快速的探索计划，适用于固定集合的老虎机 \(\mathbb{M}\)。该计划通过一个可解释的决策树来探查决策的收益，从而对测试老虎机进行分类。在随机和上下文环境中，该计划的测试遗憾与 \(O(λ^{-2} C_λ(\mathbb{M}) \log^2(MH))\) 成比例，其中 \(M\) 是 \(\mathbb{M}\) 的大小，\(λ\) 是一个区分参数，而 \(C_λ(\mathbb{M})\) 是一个新颖的分类系数，它从根本上将元学习老虎机与分类联系起来。通过几乎匹配的下界，我们证明了 \(C_λ(\mathbb{M})\) 内在地捕获了环境的复杂性。

英摘: Nonparametric estimation using uniform-width binning is a standard approach for evaluating the calibration performance of machine learning models. Additionally, many parametric recalibration algorithms lack theoretical guarantees for their generalization performance. To address these issues, we conduct a generalization analysis of calibration error using the probably approximately correct Bayes framework. [No.170] EnsLoss: Stochastic Calibrated Loss Ensembles for Preventing Overfitting in Classification
来源: ICML 2025
命中: classif
标题: EnsLoss：防止分类过拟合的校准随机损失集成
类别: general machine learning->supervised learning
作者: Ben Dai;
机构: Chinese University of Hong Kong;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/43623
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.00908
英摘: Empirical risk minimization (ERM) with a computationally feasible surrogate loss is a widely accepted approach for classification. Notably, the convexity and calibration (CC) properties of a loss function ensure consistency of ERM in maximizing accuracy, thereby offering a wide range of options for surrogate losses. In this article, we propose a novel ensemble method, namely EnsLoss, which extends the ensemble learning concept to combine loss functions within the ERM framework. A key feature of our method is the consideration on preserving the "legitimacy" of the combined losses, i.e., ensuring the CC properties. Specifically, we first transform the CC conditions of losses into loss-derivatives, thereby bypassing the need for explicit loss functions and directly generating calibrated loss-derivatives. Therefore, inspired by Dropout, EnsLoss enables loss ensembles through one training process with doubly stochastic gradient descent (i.e., random batch samples and random calibrated loss-derivatives). We theoretically establish the statistical consistency of our approach and provide insights into its benefits. The numerical effectiveness of EnsLoss compared to fixed loss methods is demonstrated through experiments on a broad range of 14 OpenML tabular datasets and 46 image datasets with various deep learning architectures. Python repository and source code are available on GitHub at https://github.com/statmlben/ensloss.
中摘: 经验风险最小化（ERM）结合可计算的有效替代损失函数是一种广泛接受的分类方法。值得注意的是，损失函数的凸性和校准（CC）性质确保了ERM在最大化准确率方面的一致性，从而提供了广泛的选择范围用于替代损失函数。本文提出了一种新颖的集成方法，即EnsLoss方法，该方法将集成学习的概念扩展到ERM框架内，以结合损失函数。我们方法的关键特征是考虑保留“综合损失的合法性”，即确保CC性质。具体而言，我们首先将损失的CC条件转换为损失导数，从而绕过了显式损失函数的需求，并直接生成校准后的损失导数。因此，受Dropout启发，EnsLoss通过使用双重随机梯度下降（即随机批量样本和随机校准损失导数）的一个训练过程实现了损失函数的集成。我们从理论上建立了该方法的统计一致性，并提供了其优势的见解。通过在14个OpenML表格数据集和46个具有各种深度学习架构的图像数据集上的实验，展示了EnsLoss相较于固定损失方法的数值有效性。Python代码库和源代码可在GitHub上获取：https://github.com/statmlben/ensloss。

[No.173] Offline-to-Online Reinforcement Learning with Classifier-Free Diffusion Generation
来源: ICML 2025
命中: classif
标题: 离线到在线强化学习与无分类器扩散生成
类别: reinforcement learning
作者: Xiao Huang;Xu Liu;Enze Zhang;Tong Yu;Shuai Li;
机构: University of California, Los Angeles;Shanghai Jiao Tong University;Adobe;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/46491
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.06806
英摘: Offline-to-online Reinforcement Learning (O2O RL) aims to perform online fine-tuning on an offline pre-trained policy to minimize costly online interactions. Existing work used offline datasets to generate data that conform to the online data distribution for data augmentation. However, generated data still exhibits a gap with the online data, limiting overall performance. To address this, we propose a new data augmentation approach, Classifier-Free Diffusion Generation (CFDG). Without introducing additional classifier training overhead, CFDG leverages classifier-free guidance diffusion to significantly enhance the generation quality of offline and online data with different distributions. Additionally, it employs a reweighting method to enable more generated data to align with the online data, enhancing performance while maintaining the agent's stability. Experimental results show that CFDG outperforms replaying the two data types or using a standard diffusion model to generate new data. Our method is versatile and can be integrated with existing offline-to-online RL algorithms. By implementing CFDG to popular methods IQL, PEX and APL, we achieve a notable 15% average improvement in empirical performance on the D4RL benchmark such as MuJoCo and AntMaze.
中摘: Offline-to-online 强化学习（O2O RL）旨在对离线预训练策略进行在线微调，以减少昂贵的在线交互。现有工作使用离线数据集生成符合在线数据分布的数据以进行数据增强。然而，生成的数据仍然与在线数据存在差距，限制了整体性能。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了一种新的数据增强方法——无分类器扩散生成（Classifier-Free Diffusion Generation，CFDG）。CFDG 不引入额外的分类器训练开销，而是利用无分类器引导的扩散机制显著提高具有不同分布的离线和在线数据的生成质量。此外，它还采用了一种加权方法，使更多的生成数据能够与在线数据对齐，从而在保持智能体稳定性的前提下提升性能。实验结果显示，CFDG 在性能上优于简单地回放这两种数据类型或使用标准扩散模型生成新数据。我们的方法具有灵活性，并可以与现有的 O2O RL 算法集成。通过将 CFDG 应用于流行的算法 IQL、PEX 和 APL，在 D4RL 基准测试中的 MuJoCo 和 AntMaze 等任务上，我们实现了显著的平均性能改进，达到了约 15%。

[No.175] Semantic Shift Estimation via Dual-Projection and Classifier Reconstruction for Exemplar-Free Class-Incremental Learning
来源: ICML 2025
命中: classif
标题: 基于双投影和分类器重构的语义转换估计在无示例增量学习中的应用
类别: deep learning
作者: Run He;Di Fang;Yicheng Xu;Yawen Cui;Ming Li;Cen Chen;Ziqian Zeng;Huiping Zhuang;
机构: South China University of Technology;Bosch (China) Investment Ltd.;Institute of Science, Tokyo;Hong Kong Polytechnic University;Guangming Laboratory;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/43787
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.05423
英摘: Exemplar-Free Class-Incremental Learning (EFCIL) aims to sequentially learn from distinct categories without retaining exemplars but easily suffers from catastrophic forgetting of learned knowledge. While existing EFCIL methods leverage knowledge distillation to alleviate forgetting, they still face two critical challenges: semantic shift and decision bias. Specifically, the embeddings of old tasks shift in the embedding space after learning new tasks, and the classifier becomes biased towards new tasks due to training solely with new data, hindering the balance between old and new knowledge. To address these issues, we propose the Dual-Projection Shift Estimation and Classifier Reconstruction (DPCR) approach for EFCIL. DPCR effectively estimates semantic shift through a dual-projection, which combines a learnable transformation with a row-space projection to capture both task-wise and category-wise shifts. Furthermore, to mitigate decision bias, DPCR employs ridge regression to reformulate a classifier reconstruction process. This reconstruction exploits previous in covariance and prototype of each class after calibration with estimated shift, thereby reducing decision bias. Extensive experiments demonstrate that, on various datasets, DPCR effectively balances old and new tasks, outperforming state-of-the-art EFCIL methods. Our codes are available at https://github.com/RHe502/ICML25-DPCR.
中摘: 无范例类增量学习（EFCIL）旨在无需保留示例的情况下顺序学习不同的类别，但容易遭受已学知识的灾难性遗忘。尽管现有的EFCIL方法利用知识蒸馏来减轻遗忘问题，它们仍然面临两个关键挑战：语义偏移和决策偏差。具体来说，在学习新任务后，旧任务的嵌入在嵌入空间中会发生偏移，而分类器由于仅使用新数据进行训练，倾向于新任务，从而妨碍了旧知识和新知识之间的平衡。为了解决这些问题，我们提出了双投影语义偏移估计与分类器重构（DPCR）方法，以应对EFCIL。DPCR通过双投影有效估计语义偏移，该方法结合了一个可学习的变换与行空间投影，以捕捉任务间和类别间的偏移。此外，为了减轻决策偏差，DPCR采用岭回归重新构建分类器重构过程。这一重构过程在估计偏移后的校准过程中利用了每个类别的协方差和原型，从而减少了决策偏差。广泛的经验研究表明，在各种数据集上，DPCR有效地平衡了旧任务和新任务，优于最先进的EFCIL方法。我们的代码可在https://github.com/RHe502/ICML25-DPCR获取。

[No.176] When Do LLMs Help With Node Classification? A Comprehensive Analysis

标题: 当大型语言模型在节点分类任务中提供帮助？全面分析
类别: deep learning->graph neural networks
作者: Xixi Wu;Yifei Shen;Fangzhou Ge;Caihua Shan;Yizhu Jiao;Xiangguo Sun;Hong Cheng;
机构: Chinese University of Hong Kong;Microsoft;;University of Illinois Urbana-Champaign;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45461
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.00829
英摘: Node classification is a fundamental task in graph analysis, with broad applications across various fields. Recent breakthroughs in Large Language Models (LLMs) have enabled LLM-based approaches for this task. Although many studies demonstrate the impressive performance of LLM-based methods, the lack of clear design guidelines may hinder their practical application. In this work, we aim to establish such guidelines through a fair and systematic comparison of these algorithms. As a first step, we developed LLMNodeBed, a comprehensive codebase and testbed for node classification using LLMs. It includes 10 homophilic datasets, 4 heterophilic datasets, 8 LLM-based algorithms, 8 classic baselines, and 3 learning paradigms. Subsequently, we conducted extensive experiments, training and evaluating over 2,700 models, to determine the key settings (e.g., learning paradigms and homophily) and components (e.g., model size and prompt) that affect performance. Our findings uncover 8 insights, e.g., (1) LLM-based methods can significantly outperform traditional methods in a semi-supervised setting, while the advantage is marginal in a supervised setting; (2) Graph Foundation Models can beat open-source LLMs but still fall short of strong LLMs like GPT-4o in a zero-shot setting. We hope that the release of LLMNodeBed, along with our insights, will facilitate reproducible research and inspire future studies in this field. Codes and datasets are released at \href{https://llmnodebed.github.io/}{\texttt{https://llmnodebed.github.io/}}.
中摘: 节点分类是图分析中的一个基本任务，具有广泛的应用领域。近年来，大型语言模型（LLMs）的突破使得基于LLM的方法能够应用于这一任务。尽管许多研究展示了LLM基方法的出色性能，但缺乏明确的设计指南可能阻碍其实际应用。在这项工作中，我们旨在通过公平且系统的算法比较来建立这些指南。作为第一步，我们开发了LLMNodeBed，这是一个全面的代码库和实验平台，用于使用LLM进行节点分类。它包括10个同质数据集、4个异质数据集、8种LLM基算法、8种经典基准以及3种学习范式。随后，我们进行了广泛的实验，训练并评估了超过2700个模型，以确定影响性能的关键设置（例如，学习范式和同质性）和组件（例如，模型大小和提示）。我们的发现揭示了8条见解，例如：（1）在半监督设置中，LLM基方法可以显著超越传统方法，而在监督设置中的优势则微乎其微；（2）图基础模型可以在零样本设置中击败开源的LLM，但在与GPT-4o这样的强大LLM相比时仍处于劣势。我们希望LLMNodeBed的发布，连同我们的见解，能够促进可重复的研究，并激发该领域的未来研究。代码和数据集可在\href{https://llmnodebed.github.io/}{https://llmnodebed.github.io/}获取。

[No.177] SCISSOR: Mitigating Semantic Bias through Cluster-Aware Siamese Networks for Robust Classification
来源: ICML 2025
命中: classif
标题: 剪刀：通过集群感知双胞胎网络减轻语义偏见以实现稳健分类
类别: social aspects->robustness
作者: Shuo Yang;Bardh Prenkaj;Gjergji Kasneci;
机构: ;Technische Universität München;University of Tuebingen;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45266
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.14587
英摘: Shortcut learning undermines model generalization to out-of-distribution data. While the literature attributes shortcuts to biases in superficial features, we show that imbalances in the semantic distribution of sample embeddings induce spurious semantic correlations, compromising model robustness. To address this issue, we propose SCISSOR (Semantic Cluster Intervention for Suppressing ShORtcut), a Siamese network-based debiasing approach that remaps the semantic space by discouraging latent clusters exploited as shortcuts. Unlike prior data-debiasing approaches, SCISSOR eliminates the need for data augmentation and rewriting. We evaluate SCISSOR on 6 models across 4 benchmarks: Chest-XRay and Not-MNIST in computer vision, and GYAFC and Yelp in NLP tasks. Compared to several baselines, SCISSOR reports +5.3 absolute points in F1 score on GYAFC, +7.3 on Yelp, +7.7 on Chest-XRay, and +1 on Not-MNIST. SCISSOR is also highly advantageous for lightweight models with ~9.5% improvement on F1 for ViT on computer vision datasets and ~11.9% for BERT on NLP. Our study redefines the landscape of model generalization by addressing overlooked semantic biases, establishing SCISSOR as a foundational framework for mitigating shortcut learning and fostering more robust, bias-resistant AI systems.
中摘: 快捷学习会削弱模型对未标记数据的泛化能力。虽然文献将快捷学习归因于表层特征上的偏差，但我们表明，样本嵌入在语义分布上的不平衡会导致虚假的语义相关性，从而损害模型的稳健性。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了一种基于Siamese网络的去偏方法——SCISSOR（语义簇干预以抑制快捷），该方法通过抑制被用作快捷的潜在语义簇来重新映射语义空间。与之前的去数据偏差方法不同，SCISSOR 消除了数据增强和重写的需求。我们在4个基准上的6个模型上评估了SCISSOR：计算机视觉领域的Chest-XRay和Not-MNIST，以及自然语言处理领域的GYAFC和Yelp。与几个基线相比，SCISSOR 在GYAFC 上报告了5.3个绝对点的F1分数提升，在Yelp 上提升了7.3个点，在Chest-XRay 上提升了7.7个点，在Not-MNIST 上提升了1个点。对于轻量级模型，SCISSOR 也有显著优势，计算机视觉数据集上的ViT模型F1分数提高了约9.5%，而NLP任务中的BERT模型则提高了约11.9%。我们的研究通过解决被忽视的语义偏差，重新定义了模型泛化的景观，并确立了SCISSOR 作为减轻快捷学习和促进更稳健、更具抗偏性的AI系统的基础框架。

[No.179] $f$-Divergence Based Classification: Beyond the Use of Cross-Entropy
来源: ICML 2024
命中: classif
标题: 基于$f$散度的分类：超越交叉熵的应用
类别: 
作者: Nicola Novello;Andrea M Tonello;
机构: Alpen-Adria-Universität Klagenfurt;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/34057
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.01268
英摘: In deep learning, classification tasks are formalized as optimization problems often solved via the minimization of the cross-entropy. However, recent advancements in the design of objective functions allow the usage of the $f$-divergence to generalize the formulation of the optimization problem for classification. We adopt a Bayesian perspective and formulate the classification task as a maximum a posteriori probability problem. We propose a class of objective functions based on the variational representation of the $f$-divergence. Furthermore, driven by the challenge of improving the state-of-the-art approach, we propose a bottom-up method that leads us to the formulation of an objective function corresponding to a novel $f$-divergence referred to as shifted log (SL). We theoretically analyze the objective functions proposed and numerically test them in three application scenarios: toy examples, image datasets, and signal detection/decoding problems. The analyzed scenarios demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach and that the SL divergence achieves the highest classification accuracy in almost all the considered cases.
中摘: 在深度学习中，分类任务通常被形式化为优化问题，并通过最小化交叉熵来求解。然而，最近在目标函数设计方面的进展使得可以使用$f$-散度来泛化分类优化问题的表述。我们从贝叶斯视角出发，将分类任务形式化为最大后验概率问题。我们提出了一类基于$f$-散度变分表示的目标函数。此外，受提高现有最佳方法性能的挑战驱动，我们提出了一种自底向上的方法，从而导出了一个与新型$f$-散度“移位对数”（SL）相对应的目标函数。我们对提出的这些目标函数进行了理论分析，并在三种应用场景中进行了数值测试：玩具示例、图像数据集以及信号检测/解码问题。分析的结果表明，所提出的方法的有效性，且SL散度在几乎所有考虑的情况下都能实现最高的分类准确性。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.180] A New Computationally Efficient Algorithm to solve Feature Selection for Functional Data Classification in High-dimensional Spaces
来源: ICML 2024
命中: classif
标题: 一种在高维空间中解决功能性数据分类特征选择的新高效算法
类别: 
作者: Tobia Boschi;Francesca Bonin;Rodrigo Ordonez-Hurtado;Alessandra Pascale;Jonathan P Epperlein;
机构: International Business Machines Corporation;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/33700
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.05765
英摘: This paper introduces a novel methodology for Feature Selection for Functional Classification, FSFC, that addresses the challenge of jointly performing feature selection and classification of functional data in scenarios with categorical responses and multivariate longitudinal features. FSFC tackles a newly defined optimization problem that integrates logistic loss and functional features to identify the most crucial variables for classification. To address the minimization procedure, we employ functional principal components and develop a new adaptive version of the Dual Augmented Lagrangian algorithm. The computational efficiency of FSFC enables handling high-dimensional scenarios where the number of features may considerably exceed the number of statistical units. Simulation experiments demonstrate that FSFC outperforms other machine learning and deep learning methods in computational time and classification accuracy. Furthermore, the FSFC feature selection capability can be leveraged to significantly reduce the problem's dimensionality and enhance the performances of other classification algorithms. The efficacy of FSFC is also demonstrated through a real data application, analyzing relationships between four chronic diseases and other health and demographic factors.
中摘: 本文介绍了一种新的特征选择方法——功能分类的特征选择方法（FSFC），该方法解决了在具有类别响应和多变量纵向特征的情况下同时进行特征选择和功能数据分类的挑战。FSFC处理了一个新定义的优化问题，将逻辑损失和功能特征结合起来，以识别分类中最关键的变量。为了应对最小化过程，我们采用了功能性主成分，并开发了一种新的双增广拉格朗日算法的适应性版本。FSFC的计算效率使其能够处理高维场景，其中特征的数量可能远远超过统计单位的数量。模拟实验表明，FSFC在计算时间和分类准确性方面优于其他机器学习和深度学习方法。此外，FSFC的特征选择能力可以显著降低问题的维度，并增强其他分类算法的性能。通过实际数据应用，FSFC还被用于分析四种慢性疾病与其他健康和人口统计因素之间的关系。

[No.192] Continual Learning in Linear Classification on Separable Data
来源: ICML 2023
命中: classif
标题: 线性分类在可分数据上的持续学习
类别: 
作者: Itay Evron;Edward Moroshko;Gon Buzaglo;Maroun Khriesh;Badea Marjieh;Nathan Srebro;Daniel Soudry;
机构: Amazon;Technion – Israel Institute of Technology;Technion - Israel Institute of Technology;University of Chicago;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2023/poster/24746
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.03534
英摘: We analyze continual learning on a sequence of separable linear classification tasks with binary labels. We show theoretically that learning with weak regularization reduces to solving a sequential max-margin problem, corresponding to a special case of the Projection Onto Convex Sets (POCS) framework. We then develop upper bounds on the forgetting and other quantities of interest under various settings with recurring tasks, including cyclic and random orderings of tasks. We discuss several practical implications to popular training practices like regularization scheduling and weighting. We point out several theoretical differences between our continual classification setting and a recently studied continual regression setting.
中摘: 我们分析了一系列可分线性分类任务上的持续学习，这些任务具有二进制标签。理论上，我们证明了在弱正则化下进行学习等同于解决一个顺序最大边际问题，这对应于投影到凸集（POCS）框架的一个特殊情形。随后，我们在不同设置下发展了遗忘及其他感兴趣量的上界，包括循环和随机的任务排列顺序。我们讨论了几种实用含义，涉及流行的训练实践如正则化调度和权重分配。我们指出了我们所研究的持续分类设置与最近研究的持续回归设置之间的几个理论差异。

[No.193] Differential Privacy has Bounded Impact on Fairness in Classification
来源: ICML 2023
命中: classif
标题: 差分隐私对分类公平性的影响是有限的
类别: 
作者: Paul Mangold;Michaël Perrot;Aurélien Bellet;Marc Tommasi;
机构: INRIA;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2023/poster/24848
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2210.16242
英摘: We theoretically study the impact of differential privacy on fairness in classification. We prove that, given a class of models, popular group fairness measures are pointwise Lipschitz-continuous with respect to the parameters of the model. This result is a consequence of a more general statement on accuracy conditioned on an arbitrary event (such as membership to a sensitive group), which may be of independent interest. We use this Lipschitz property to prove a non-asymptotic bound showing that, as the number of samples increases, the fairness level of private models gets closer to the one of their non-private counterparts. This bound also highlights the importance of the confidence margin of a model on the disparate impact of differential privacy.
中摘: 我们理论研究了差分隐私对分类公平性的影响。我们证明，在给定一类模型的情况下，流行的群体公平性指标在模型参数的变化下是点Lipschitz连续的。这一结果是关于条件准确性（例如，基于敏感群体的成员身份）的一个更一般的陈述的一个推论，这个陈述本身可能具有独立的研究价值。我们利用这一Lipschitz性质证明了一个非渐近界，表明随着样本数量的增加，私人模型的公平水平会越来越接近其非私人版本的公平水平。这一界也突显了模型的信心边际对于差分隐私不同影响的重要性。

[No.194] Distribution Free Prediction Sets for Node Classification
来源: ICML 2023
命中: classif
标题: 节点分类的无分布预测集
类别: 
作者: Jase Clarkson;
机构: University of Oxford;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2023/poster/23802
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2211.14555
英摘: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) are able to achieve high classification accuracy on many important real world datasets, but provide no rigorous notion of predictive uncertainty. Quantifying the confidence of GNN models is difficult due to the dependence between datapoints induced by the graph structure. We leverage recent advances in conformal prediction to construct prediction sets for node classification in inductive learning scenarios. We do this by taking an existing approach for conformal classification that relies on \textit{exchangeable} data and modifying it by appropriately weighting the conformal scores to reflect the network structure. We show through experiments on standard benchmark datasets using popular GNN models that our approach provides tighter and better calibrated prediction sets than a naive application of conformal prediction.
中摘: 图神经网络（GNNs）能够在许多重要的现实世界数据集中实现高分类准确性，但它们无法提供严格的预测不确定性概念。由于图结构引起的样本之间的依赖性，量化GNN模型的信心具有困难。我们利用最近在自适应预测方面的进展，通过一个现有的自适应分类方法来构建节点分类的预测集，并在此基础上适当加权自适应评分以反映网络结构。通过在标准基准数据集上使用流行GNN模型进行实验，我们展示了我们的方法比直接应用自适应预测提供了更紧致且更好的校准预测集。

[No.195] Evolutionary Multi-View Classification via Eliminating Individual Fitness Bias

[No.196] Robust Graph Condensation via Classification Complexity Mitigation
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: classif
标题: 稳健的图凝聚通过分类复杂性缓解
类别: deep learning
作者: Jiayi Luo;Qingyun Sun;Beining Yang;Haonan Yuan;Xingcheng Fu;Yanbiao Ma;Jianxin Li;Philip S. Yu;
机构: Zhongguancun Academy;Beihang University;;Guangxi Normal University;Renmin University of China;Xidian University;University of Illinois at Chicago;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=vATe64ktAo
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.26451
英摘: Graph condensation (GC) has gained significant attention for its ability to synthesize smaller yet informative graphs. However, existing studies often overlook the robustness of GC in scenarios where the original graph is corrupted. In such cases, we observe that the performance of GC deteriorates significantly, while existing robust graph learning technologies offer only limited effectiveness. Through both empirical investigation and theoretical analysis, we reveal that GC is inherently an intrinsic-dimension-reducing process, synthesizing a condensed graph with lower classification complexity. Although this property is critical for effective GC performance, it remains highly vulnerable to adversarial perturbations. To tackle this vulnerability and improve GC robustness, we adopt the geometry perspective of graph data manifold and propose a novel Manifold-constrained Robust Graph Condensation framework named MRGC. Specifically, we introduce three graph data manifold learning modules that guide the condensed graph to lie within a smooth, low-dimensional manifold with minimal class ambiguity, thereby preserving the classification complexity reduction capability of GC and ensuring robust performance under universal adversarial attacks. Extensive experiments demonstrate the robustness of \ModelName\ across diverse attack scenarios.
中摘: 图凝聚（GC）因其能够合成更小但更具信息量的图而受到了广泛关注。然而，现有研究往往忽视了在原始图被篡改的情况下，GC的鲁棒性问题。在这种情况下，我们观察到GC的性能显著下降，而现有的鲁棒图学习技术只能提供有限的有效性。通过实证研究和理论分析，我们揭示了GC本质上是一个降低内在维度的过程，它合成一个具有较低分类复杂性的浓缩图。尽管这一特性对于有效的GC性能至关重要，但它仍然高度易受对抗性扰动的影响。为了应对这一脆弱性并提高GC的鲁棒性，我们采用了图数据流形的几何视角，并提出了一种新的Manifold-constrained Robust Graph Condensation框架，命名为MRGC。具体来说，我们引入了三个图数据流形学习模块，以引导浓缩图位于一个光滑、低维度且类间模糊度最小的流形上，从而保留GC的分类复杂性减少能力，并确保在普遍对抗攻击下的稳健性能。广泛的实验表明，\ModelName\ 在多种攻击场景下都具有鲁棒性。

[No.199] Towards Understanding the Mechanisms of Classifier-Free Guidance
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: classif
标题: 理解无分类引导机制
类别: deep learning
作者: Xiang Li;Rongrong Wang;Qing Qu;
机构: University of Michigan;Michigan State University;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=bRAm7A02Qm
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.19210
英摘: Classifier-free guidance (CFG) is a core technique powering state-of-the-art image generation systems, yet its underlying mechanisms remain poorly understood. In this work, we begin by analyzing CFG in a simplified linear diffusion model, where we show its behavior closely resembles that observed in the nonlinear case. Our analysis reveals that linear CFG improves generation quality via three distinct components: (i) a mean-shift term that approximately steers samples in the direction of class means, (ii) a positive Contrastive Principal Components (CPC) term that amplifies class-specific features, and (iii) a negative CPC term that suppresses generic features prevalent in unconditional data. We then verify these insights in real-world, nonlinear diffusion models: over a broad range of noise levels, linear CFG resembles the behavior of its nonlinear counterpart. Although the two eventually diverge at low noise levels, we discuss how the insights from the linear analysis still shed light on the CFG's mechanism in the nonlinear regime.
中摘: 无分类引导（Classifier-Free Guidance, CFG）是推动最先进的图像生成系统的核心技术，但其背后的机制仍然不甚明了。在本项工作中，我们首先在简化的一维扩散模型中分析了CFG，展示了其行为与非线性情况下的观察结果高度相似。我们的分析揭示了CFG通过三个不同的组件来提高生成质量：(i) 平均偏移项，该项大致引导样本朝类别平均值方向移动；(ii) 正向对比主成分（Contrastive Principal Components, CPC）项，该项增强了特定类别的特征；(iii) 负向CPC项，该项抑制了在无条件数据中普遍存在的通用特征。然后，我们在现实世界的非线性扩散模型中验证了这些见解：在广泛的噪声水平范围内，线性CFG的行为与其非线性对应物相似。尽管两者在低噪声水平下最终会有所不同，但我们讨论了线性分析中的这些见解如何仍能照亮CFG机制在非线性域中的运作方式。

[No.201] Adaptive Classifier-Free Guidance via Dynamic Low-Confidence Masking
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: classif
标题: 自适应无分类器自由引导通过动态低置信度掩码
类别: deep learning
作者: Pengxiang Li;Shilin Yan;Jiayin Cai;Renrui Zhang;Ruichuan An;Ziyu Guo;Xiaowei Gao;
机构: Hong Kong Polytechnic University;Dalian University of Technology;Alibaba Group;Xiaohongshu;Chinese University of Hong Kong;Peking University;Xi'an Jiao Tong University;Imperial College London;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=0EcomthIKh
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.20199
英摘: Classifier-Free Guidance (CFG) significantly enhances controllability in generative models by interpolating conditional and unconditional predictions. However, standard CFG often employs a static unconditional input, which can be suboptimal for iterative generation processes where model uncertainty varies dynamically. We introduce Adaptive Classifier-Free Guidance (A-CFG), a novel method that tailors the unconditional input by leveraging the model's instantaneous predictive confidence. At each step of an iterative (masked) diffusion language model, A-CFG identifies tokens in the currently generated sequence for which the model exhibits low confidence. These tokens are temporarily re-masked to create a dynamic, localized unconditional input. This focuses CFG's corrective influence precisely on areas of ambiguity, leading to more effective guidance. We integrate A-CFG into a state-of-the-art masked diffusion language model and demonstrate its efficacy. Experiments on diverse language generation benchmarks show that A-CFG yields substantial improvements over standard CFG, achieving, for instance, a 3.9 point gain on GPQA. Our work highlights the benefit of dynamically adapting guidance mechanisms to model uncertainty in iterative generation.
中摘: 分类器自由引导（CFG）通过插值条件性和无条件预测显著增强了生成模型的可控性。然而，标准的CFG通常采用静态的无条件输入，这在模型不确定性动态变化的迭代生成过程中可能不是最优选择。我们引入了自适应分类器自由引导（A-CFG），这是一种新颖的方法，通过利用模型即时的预测置信度来定制无条件输入。在迭代（掩码）扩散语言模型的每一步中，A-CFG会识别出当前生成序列中模型置信度低的标记，并暂时重新掩码这些标记以创建一个动态、局部化的无条件输入。这种方法将CFG的矫正影响精确地集中在模糊区域上，从而实现更有效的引导。我们将A-CFG整合到最先进的掩码扩散语言模型中，并展示了其有效性。实验表明，A-CFG在各种语言生成基准测试中相较于标准的CFG取得了显著改进，例如在GPQA上的得分提高了3.9分。我们的研究突显了根据模型不确定性动态调整引导机制的好处。

[No.203] Rethinking Multimodal Learning from the Perspective of Mitigating Classification Ability Disproportion
来源: NeurIPS 2025

英摘: Multimodal learning (MML) is significantly constrained by modality imbalance, leading to suboptimal performance in practice. While existing approaches primarily focus on balancing the learning of different modalities to address this issue, they fundamentally overlook the inherent disproportion in model classification ability, which serves as the primary cause of this phenomenon. In this paper, we propose a novel multimodal learning approach to dynamically balance the classification ability of weak and strong modalities by incorporating the principle of boosting. Concretely, we first propose a sustained boosting algorithm in multimodal learning by simultaneously optimizing the classification and residual errors. Subsequently, we introduce an adaptive classifier assignment strategy to dynamically facilitate the classification performance of the weak modality. Furthermore, we theoretically analyze the convergence property of the cross-modal gap function, ensuring the effectiveness of the proposed boosting scheme. To this end, the classification ability of strong and weak modalities is expected to be balanced, thereby mitigating the imbalance issue. Empirical experiments on widely used datasets reveal the superiority of our method through comparison with various state-of-the-art (SOTA) multimodal learning baselines. [No.205] Stealthy Yet Effective: Distribution-Preserving Backdoor Attacks on Graph Classification
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: classif
标题: 隐秘而有效：基于分布保持的图分类后门攻击
类别: deep learning
作者: Xiaobao Wang;Ruoxiao Sun;Yujun Zhang;Bingdao Feng;Dongxiao He;Luzhi Wang;Di Jin;
机构: Tianjin University;Dalian Maritime University;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=EpEluUcQ6A
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.26032
英摘: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have demonstrated strong performance across tasks such as node classification, link prediction, and graph classification, but remain vulnerable to backdoor attacks that implant imperceptible triggers during training to control predictions. While node-level attacks exploit local message passing, graph-level attacks face the harder challenge of manipulating global representations while maintaining stealth. We identify two main sources of anomaly in existing graph classification backdoor methods: structural deviation from rare subgraph triggers and semantic deviation caused by label flipping, both of which make poisoned graphs easily detectable by anomaly detection models. To address this, we propose DPSBA, a clean-label backdoor framework that learns in-distribution triggers via adversarial training guided by anomaly-aware discriminators. DPSBA effectively suppresses both structural and semantic anomalies, achieving high attack success while significantly improving stealth. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets validate that DPSBA achieves a superior balance between effectiveness and detectability compared to state-of-the-art baselines.
中摘: 图神经网络（GNNs）在节点分类、链接预测和图分类等任务上展现了强大的性能，但在训练过程中植入不可感知触发器以控制预测结果的后门攻击下仍然存在漏洞。虽然节点级攻击利用局部消息传递，图级攻击则面临更艰巨的挑战——在保持隐蔽性的同时操控全局表示。我们发现现有图分类后门方法中的两大异常来源：结构上的偏差源于稀有子图触发器，语义上的偏差则由标签翻转引起，这两种偏差使得中毒图容易被异常检测模型识别出来。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了一种名为DPSBA的干净标签后门框架，通过异常感知判别器引导的对抗训练来学习分布内触发器。DPSBA有效地抑制了结构和语义上的异常，实现了高成功率的攻击同时显著提高了隐蔽性。在真实数据集上的广泛实验验证了DPSBA在有效性和可检测性之间取得了优于最先进的基线方法的优异平衡。

[No.206] Multiclass Loss Geometry Matters for Generalization of Gradient Descent in Separable Classification
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: classif
标题: 多类损失几何结构对可分分类中梯度下降泛化的意义
类别: theory
作者: Matan Schliserman;Tomer Koren;
机构: Tel Aviv University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=FBjTMq2Rby
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.22359
英摘: We study the generalization performance of unregularized gradient methods for separable linear classification. While previous work mostly deal with the binary case, we focus on the multiclass setting with $k$ classes and establish novel population risk bounds for Gradient Descent for loss functions that decay to zero. In this setting, we show risk bounds that reveal that convergence rates are crucially influenced by the geometry of the loss template, as formalized by Wang and Scott (2024), rather than of the loss function itself. Particularly, we establish risk upper bounds that holds for any decay rate of the loss whose template is smooth with respect to the $p$-norm. In the case of exponentially decaying losses, our results indicates a contrast between the $p=\infty$ case, where the risk exhibits a logarithmic dependence on $k$, and $p=2$ where the risk scales linearly with $k$. To establish this separation formally, we also prove a lower bound in the latter scenario, demonstrating that the polynomial dependence on $k$ is unavoidable. Central to our analysis is a novel bound on the Rademacher complexity of low-noise vector-valued linear predictors with a loss template smooth w.r.t.~general $p$-norms.
中摘: 我们研究了可分线性分类中未正则化梯度方法的一般化性能。虽然以往的工作主要关注二分类情况，我们则集中在具有$k$个类别的多分类设置，并为损失函数在零处衰减至零的情况建立了新的总体风险界。在这个设置中，我们展示了风险界揭示了收敛速率受到损失模板几何形状的影响，而不是损失函数本身，正如Wang和Scott（2024）所正式化的形式。特别是，我们建立了适用于任何相对于$p$范数光滑的损失模板的风险上界。对于指数衰减损失，在$p=\infty$的情况下，风险表现出对$k$的对数依赖性；而在$p=2$的情况下，风险随$k$线性增长。为了正式证明这种分离，我们还在后者情况下证明了一个下界，表明对$k$的多项式依赖性不可避免。我们分析的核心在于一个新的关于相对于一般$p$范数光滑的向量值线性预测器的Rademacher复杂性的上界。

[No.207] Simple and Effective Specialized Representations for Fair Classifiers
来源: NeurIPS 2025

英摘: Fair classification is a critical challenge that has gained increasing importance due to international regulations and its growing use in high-stakes decision-making settings. Existing methods often rely on adversarial learning or distribution matching across sensitive groups; however, adversarial learning can be unstable, and distribution matching can be computationally intensive. To address these limitations, we propose a novel approach based on the characteristic function distance. Our method ensures that the learned representation contains minimal sensitive information while maintaining high effectiveness for downstream tasks. [No.208] Incremental Sequence Classification with Temporal Consistency
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: classif

作者: Lucas Maystre;Gabriel Barello;Tudor Berariu;Aleix Cambray;Rares Dolga;Alvaro Ortega Gonzalez;Andrei Cristian Nica;David Barber;

英摘: We address the problem of incremental sequence classification, where predictions are updated as new elements in the sequence are revealed. [No.209] Conservative classifiers do consistently well with improving agents: characterizing statistical and online learning
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: classif
标题: 保守分类器在改进代理方面表现一致良好：描述统计学习和在线学习特征
类别: theory
作者: Dravyansh Sharma;Alec Sun;
机构: Toyota Technological Institute at Chicago;University of Chicago;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=Zc5Ntjt5Ul
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.05252
英摘: Machine learning is now ubiquitous in societal decision-making, for example in evaluating job candidates or loan applications, and it is increasingly important to take into account how classified agents will react to the learning algorithms. The majority of recent literature on strategic classification has focused on reducing and countering deceptive behaviors by the classified agents, but recent work of Attias et al. identifies surprising properties of learnability when the agents genuinely improve in order to attain the desirable classification, such as smaller generalization error than standard PAC-learning. In this paper we characterize so-called learnability with improvements across multiple new axes. We introduce an asymmetric variant of minimally consistent concept classes and use it to provide an exact characterization of proper learning with improvements in the realizable setting. While prior work studies learnability only under general, arbitrary agent improvement regions, we give positive results for more natural Euclidean ball improvement sets. In particular, we characterize improper learning under a mild generative assumption on the data distribution. We further show how to learn in more challenging settings, achieving lower generalization error under well-studied bounded noise models and obtaining mistake bounds in realizable and agnostic online learning. We resolve open questions posed by Attias et al. for both proper and improper learning.
中摘: 机器学习现在在社会决策制定中无处不在，例如在评估求职者或贷款申请时。越来越多的研究表明，在考虑分类代理对学习算法的反应时，必须将其纳入考量。近期关于战略分类的研究主要集中在减少和对抗代理的欺骗行为上，但Attias等人最近的工作揭示了当代理真正改进以获得理想的分类时，可学习性表现出令人惊讶的性质，例如比标准PAC学习更小的泛化误差。在这篇论文中，我们从多个新的维度来表征所谓的“改进可学习性”。我们引入了一种不对称的最小一致概念类变体，并利用它在可实现的情况下提供了精确的改进正确学习表征。而先前的工作仅研究了代理改进区域的一般任意情况下的可学习性，我们则针对更自然的欧几里得球形改进集给出了积极的结果。特别是，我们在轻度生成假设的数据分布下表征了不正确学习。此外，我们展示了如何在更具挑战性的环境中进行学习，实现了在广泛研究的有界噪声模型下更低的泛化误差，并获得了可实现和非一致在线学习中的错误边界。我们解决了Attias等人提出的相关正确学习和不正确学习的开放问题。

[No.210] Improved Balanced Classification with Theoretically Grounded Loss Functions
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: classif
标题: 基于理论支撑的损失函数改进的平衡分类方法
类别: theory
作者: Corinna Cortes;Mehryar Mohri;Yutao Zhong;
机构: Google;New York University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=SkZUo6Xg61
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.23947
英摘: The balanced loss is a widely adopted objective for multi-class classification under class imbalance. By assigning equal importance to all classes, regardless of their frequency, it promotes fairness and ensures that minority classes are not overlooked. However, directly minimizing the balanced classification loss is typically intractable, which makes the design of effective surrogate losses a central question. This paper introduces and studies two advanced surrogate loss families: Generalized Logit-Adjusted (GLA) loss functions and Generalized Class-Aware weighted (GCA) losses. GLA losses generalize Logit-Adjusted losses, which shift logits based on class priors, to the broader general cross-entropy loss family. GCA loss functions extend the standard class-weighted losses, which scale losses inversely by class frequency, by incorporating class-dependent confidence margins and extending them to the general cross-entropy family. We present a comprehensive theoretical analysis of consistency for both loss families. We show that GLA losses are Bayes-consistent, but only $H$-consistent for complete (i.e., unbounded) hypothesis sets. Moreover, their $H$-consistency bounds depend inversely on the minimum class probability, scaling at least as $1/\mathsf p_{\min}$. In contrast, GCA losses are $H$-consistent for any hypothesis set that is bounded or complete, with $H$-consistency bounds that scale more favorably as $1/\sqrt{\mathsf p_{\min}}$, offering significantly stronger theoretical guarantees in imbalanced settings. We report the results of experiments demonstrating that, empirically, both the GCA losses with calibrated class-dependent confidence margins and GLA losses can greatly outperform straightforward class-weighted losses as well as the LA losses. GLA generally performs slightly better in common benchmarks, whereas GCA exhibits a slight edge in highly imbalanced settings.
中摘: 平衡损失在处理类别不平衡的多分类问题时被广泛采用为目标函数。通过赋予所有类别相同的权重，无论其频率如何，它促进了公平性并确保少数类不会被忽视。然而，直接最小化平衡分类损失通常是不可行的，这使得有效替代损失的设计成为核心问题。本文介绍了并研究了两种先进的替代损失家族：广义逻辑调整（GLA）损失函数和广义类别感知加权（GCA）损失。GLA损失将基于类别先验进行逻辑调整的Logit-Adjusted损失泛化为更广泛的交叉熵损失家族。GCA损失函数扩展了标准类别加权损失（即按类别频率反向缩放损失），通过引入类别依赖的置信度边界，并将其扩展到一般的交叉熵损失家族。我们对这两种损失家族的一致性进行了全面的理论分析。我们表明，GLA损失是贝叶斯一致的，但对于完全（即无界）假设集来说，只能是$H$-一致。此外，它们的$H$-一致性界限与最小类别概率成反比，缩放至少为$1/\mathsf p_{\min}$。相比之下，GCA损失对于任何有界或完全的假设集都是$H$-一致的，其$H$-一致性界限以更可喜的方式缩放为$1/\sqrt{\mathsf p_{\min}}$，在不平衡设置中提供了显著更强的理论保证。我们在实验中报告的结果显示，从经验上看，GCA损失带有校准的类别依赖置信度边界和GLA损失都能显著优于简单的类别加权损失以及LA损失。GLA在常见基准测试中通常表现稍好，而GCA在高度不平衡的设置中则略占优势。

[No.212] MAPLE: Multi-scale Attribute-enhanced Prompt Learning for Few-shot Whole Slide Image Classification
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: classif
标题: MAPLE：多尺度属性增强提示学习在少量样本全切片图像分类中的应用
类别: applications
作者: Junjie Zhou;WEI SHAO;Yagao Yue;Wei Mu;Peng Wan;Qi Zhu;Daoqiang Zhang;
机构: Nanjing University of Aeronautics and Astronautics;Beihang University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=yHi8Ao6GAe
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.25863
英摘: Prompt learning has emerged as a promising paradigm for adapting pre-trained vision-language models (VLMs) to few-shot whole slide image (WSI) classification by aligning visual features with textual representations, thereby reducing annotation cost and enhancing model generalization. Nevertheless, existing methods typically rely on slide-level prompts and fail to capture the subtype-specific phenotypic variations of histological entities (\emph{e.g.,} nuclei, glands) that are critical for cancer diagnosis. To address this gap, we propose Multi-scale Attribute-enhanced Prompt Learning (\textbf{MAPLE}), a hierarchical framework for few-shot WSI classification that jointly integrates multi-scale visual semantics and performs prediction at both the entity and slide levels. Specifically, we first leverage large language models (LLMs) to generate entity-level prompts that can help identify multi-scale histological entities and their phenotypic attributes, as well as slide-level prompts to capture global visual descriptions. Then, an entity-guided cross-attention module is proposed to generate entity-level features, followed by aligning with their corresponding subtype-specific attributes for fine-grained entity-level prediction. To enrich entity representations, we further develop a cross-scale entity graph learning module that can update these representations by capturing their semantic correlations within and across scales. The refined representations are then aggregated into a slide-level representation and aligned with the corresponding prompts for slide-level prediction. Finally, we combine both entity-level and slide-level outputs to produce the final prediction results. Results on three cancer cohorts confirm the effectiveness of our approach in addressing few-shot pathology diagnosis tasks.
中摘: 提示学习已经 emerged 作为一种有前景的方法，用于通过将视觉特征与文本表示对齐来适应预训练的视觉-语言模型（VLMs）进行少量样本全切片图像（WSI）分类，从而降低标注成本并增强模型泛化能力。然而，现有方法通常依赖于切片区级提示，无法捕捉到对于癌症诊断至关重要的组织学实体（例如，核、腺体）的亚型特异性表型变异。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了一种多尺度属性增强提示学习框架（MAPLE），这是一种用于少量样本WSI分类的分层架构，可以同时集成多尺度视觉语义并在实体和切片区级进行预测。具体而言，我们首先利用大型语言模型（LLMs）生成实体级别的提示，以帮助识别多尺度组织学实体及其表型属性，并生成切片区级提示来捕捉全局视觉描述。接着，我们提出了一种实体导向的交叉注意力模块来生成实体级别的特征，并将这些特征与其对应的亚型特异性属性对齐，以实现细粒度的实体级别预测。为了丰富实体表示，我们进一步开发了一个跨尺度实体图学习模块，该模块可以通过捕捉不同尺度内的语义相关性来更新这些表示。经过细化的表示随后被聚合为切片区级表示，并与相应的提示对齐以进行切片区级预测。最后，我们将实体级别和切片区级的输出结合起来，产生最终的预测结果。在三个癌症队列上的结果证实了我们的方法在解决少量样本病理诊断任务方面的有效性。

[No.213] Enhancing Graph Classification Robustness with Singular Pooling
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: classif
标题: 通过奇异池化增强图分类的鲁棒性
类别: deep learning
作者: Sofiane ENNADIR;Oleg Smirnov;Yassine ABBAHADDOU;Lele Cao;Johannes F. Lutzeyer;
机构: Microsoft (King - ABK);KTH Royal Institute of Technology;Microsoft;Ecole Polytechnique;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.22643
英摘: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have achieved strong performance across a range of graph representation learning tasks, yet their adversarial robustness in graph classification remains underexplored compared to node classification. While most existing defenses focus on the message-passing component, this work investigates the overlooked role of pooling operations in shaping robustness. We present a theoretical analysis of standard flat pooling methods (sum, average and max), deriving upper bounds on their adversarial risk and identifying their vulnerabilities under different attack scenarios and graph structures. Motivated by these insights, we propose \textit{Robust Singular Pooling (RS-Pool)}, a novel pooling strategy that leverages the dominant singular vector of the node embedding matrix to construct a robust graph-level representation. We theoretically investigate the robustness of RS-Pool and interpret the resulting bound leading to improved understanding of our proposed pooling operator. While our analysis centers on Graph Convolutional Networks (GCNs), RS-Pool is model-agnostic and can be implemented efficiently via power iteration. Empirical results on real-world benchmarks show that RS-Pool provides better robustness than the considered pooling methods when subject to state-of-the-art adversarial attacks while maintaining competitive clean accuracy. Our code is publicly available at:\href{https://github.com/king/rs-pool}{https://github.com/king/rs-pool}.
中摘: 图神经网络（GNNs）在各种图表示学习任务中表现出强大的性能，但在图分类任务中的对抗鲁棒性方面与节点分类相比仍然被研究不足。尽管大多数现有的防御措施集中在消息传递组件上，这项工作探讨了池化操作在塑造鲁棒性方面被忽视的作用。我们对标准的平坦池化方法（求和、平均和最大值）进行了理论分析，推导出它们的对抗风险上限，并在不同攻击场景和图结构下识别出了它们的脆弱性。受到这些见解的启发，我们提出了**稳健奇异池化（RS-Pool）**，这是一种新颖的池化策略，利用节点嵌入矩阵中的主导奇异向量来构建稳健的图级表示。我们从理论上研究了RS-Pool的鲁棒性，并对其结果进行了解释，从而提高了我们所提出的池化操作的理解。虽然我们的分析主要集中在图卷积网络（GCNs）上，但RS-Pool是一种模型无关的方法，可以通过幂迭代高效实现。在现实世界的基准测试上的实证结果显示，在遭受最先进的对抗攻击时，RS-Pool提供了比考虑的池化方法更好的鲁棒性，同时保持了竞争力的干净准确率。我们的代码可在以下链接公开获取：[https://github.com/king/rs-pool](https://github.com/king/rs-pool)。

[No.214] Towards a Golden Classifier-Free Guidance Path via Foresight Fixed Point Iterations
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: classif
标题: 通过前瞻固定点迭代实现的金钥匙无分类指导路径
类别: deep learning
作者: Kaibo Wang;Jianda Mao;Tong Wu;Yang Xiang;
机构: Hong Kong University of Science and Technology;;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=yf8O4xEB4T
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.21512
英摘: Classifier-Free Guidance (CFG) is an essential component of text-to-image diffusion models, and understanding and advancing its operational mechanisms remains a central focus of research. Existing approaches stem from divergent theoretical interpretations, thereby limiting the design space and obscuring key design choices. To address this, we propose a unified perspective that reframes conditional guidance as fixed point iterations, seeking to identify a golden path where latents produce consistent outputs under both conditional and unconditional generation. We demonstrate that CFG and its variants constitute a special case of single-step short-interval iteration, which is theoretically proven to exhibit inefficiency. To this end, we introduce Foresight Guidance (FSG), which prioritizes solving longer-interval subproblems in early diffusion stages with increased iterations. Extensive experiments across diverse datasets and model architectures validate the superiority of FSG over state-of-the-art methods in both image quality and computational efficiency. Our work offers novel perspectives for conditional guidance and unlocks the potential of adaptive design.
中摘: 分类器无关引导（CFG）是文本到图像扩散模型中的一个关键组成部分，理解并推进其操作机制仍然是研究的核心焦点。现有的方法源于不同的理论解释，这限制了设计空间，并模糊了关键设计选择。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了一种统一的观点，将条件引导重新定义为固定点迭代，旨在找到一条“黄金路径”，使得潜在变量在有条件和无条件生成时都能产生一致的输出。我们证明了CFG及其变体构成单步短间隔迭代的一个特殊案例，从理论上讲，这种迭代方式表现出低效性。为此，我们引入了前瞻引导（FSG），它在早期扩散阶段通过增加迭代次数优先解决较长间隔的子问题。广泛的实验表明，在不同数据集和模型架构上，FSG在图像质量和计算效率方面均优于最先进的方法。我们的工作提供了关于条件引导的新视角，并解锁了适应性设计的潜力。

[No.217] Split conformal classification with unsupervised calibration
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: classif
标题: 无监督校准的分割共形分类
类别: theory
作者: Santiago Mazuelas;

类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=ewP2Gx85wb
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.07185
英摘: Methods for split conformal prediction leverage calibration samples to transform any prediction rule into a set-prediction rule that complies with a target coverage probability. Existing methods provide remarkably strong performance guarantees with minimal computational costs. However, they require to use calibration samples composed by labeled examples different to those used for training. This requirement can be highly inconvenient, as it prevents the use of all labeled examples for training and may require acquiring additional labels solely for calibration. This paper presents an effective methodology for split conformal prediction with unsupervised calibration for classification tasks. In the proposed approach, set-prediction rules are obtained using unsupervised calibration samples together with supervised training samples previously used to learn the classification rule. Theoretical and experimental results show that the presented methods can achieve performance comparable to that with supervised calibration, at the expenses of a moderate degradation in performance guarantees and computational efficiency.
中摘: 分拆自适应预测方法利用校准样本将任何预测规则转换为符合目标覆盖概率的集合预测规则。现有方法在几乎不增加计算成本的情况下提供了极其强大的性能保证。然而，这些方法需要使用与训练样本不同的带标签示例作为校准样本。这一要求可能非常不便，因为它阻止了使用所有带标签的示例进行训练，并且可能需要额外获取标签以用于校准。本文提出了一种有效的分拆自适应预测方法，适用于分类任务，该方法通过结合监督训练样本和之前用于学习分类规则的无监督校准样本来获得集合预测规则。理论和实验结果表明，所提出的这些方法可以在适度牺牲性能保证和计算效率的情况下达到与监督校准相当的性能。

[No.218] Optimal Rates for Generalization of Gradient Descent for Deep ReLU Classification
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: classif
标题: 深度ReLU分类中梯度下降泛化的最优率
类别: theory
作者: Yuanfan Li;Yunwen Lei;Zheng-Chu Guo;Yiming Ying;
机构: Zhejiang University;University of Hong Kong;University of Sydney;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=7VN0iICXZj
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.02779
英摘: Recent advances have significantly improved our understanding of the generalization performance of gradient descent (GD) methods in deep neural networks. A natural and fundamental question is whether GD can achieve generalization rates comparable to the minimax optimal rates established in the kernel setting. Existing results either yield suboptimal rates of $O(1/\sqrt{n})$, or focus on networks with smooth activation functions, incurring exponential dependence on network depth $L$. In this work, we establish optimal generalization rates for GD with deep ReLU networks by carefully trading off optimization and generalization errors, achieving only polynomial dependence on depth. Specifically, under the assumption that the data are NTK separable from the margin $γ$, we prove an excess risk rate of $\widetilde{O}(L^4 (1 + γL^2) / (n γ^2))$, which aligns with the optimal SVM-type rate $\widetilde{O}(1 / (n γ^2))$ up to depth-dependent factors. A key technical contribution is our novel control of activation patterns near a reference model, enabling a sharper Rademacher complexity bound for deep ReLU networks trained with gradient descent.
中摘: 最近的研究显著提高了我们对梯度下降（GD）方法在深度神经网络中的泛化性能的理解。一个自然且基本的问题是，GD是否能够达到与核设置中建立的最小最大最优速率相媲美的泛化速率。现有的结果要么给出次优的 $O(1/\sqrt{n})$ 速率，要么集中在具有光滑激活函数的网络上，导致深度 $L$ 的指数依赖关系。在本文中，我们通过仔细权衡优化误差和泛化误差，为深度ReLU网络中的GD方法建立了最优泛化速率，实现了仅与深度相关的多项式依赖关系。具体来说，在假设数据从边缘 $\gamma$ 可以被NTK可分的情况下，我们证明了过拟合风险率为 $\widetilde{O}(L^4 (1 + \gamma L^2) / (n \gamma^2))$，这与最优的支持向量机（SVM）类型速率 $\widetilde{O}(1 / (n \gamma^2))$ 在深度依赖因子上保持一致。一个关键技术贡献是我们提出了新的方法来控制参考模型附近的激活模式，从而为通过梯度下降训练的深度ReLU网络提供了一个更精确的Rademacher复杂性界。

[No.219] ShapeX: Shapelet-Driven Post Hoc Explanations for Time Series Classification Models
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: classif
标题: ShapeX：基于形状let的后验解释时间序列分类模型
类别: deep learning
作者: Bosong Huang;Ming Jin;Yuxuan Liang;Johan Barthelemy;Debo Cheng;Qingsong Wen;Chenghao Liu;Shirui Pan;
机构: Griffith University;Hong Kong University of Science and Technology;University of Wollongong;NVIDIA;;Salesforce;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=EAtSPyQ09Z
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.20084
英摘: Explaining time series classification models is crucial, particularly in high-stakes applications such as healthcare and finance, where transparency and trust play a critical role. Although numerous time series classification methods have identified key subsequences, known as shapelets, as core features for achieving state-of-the-art performance and validating their pivotal role in classification outcomes, existing post-hoc time series explanation (PHTSE) methods primarily focus on timestep-level feature attribution. These explanation methods overlook the fundamental prior that classification outcomes are predominantly driven by key shapelets. To bridge this gap, we present ShapeX, an innovative framework that segments time series into meaningful shapelet-driven segments and employs Shapley values to assess their saliency. At the core of ShapeX lies the Shapelet Describe-and-Detect (SDD) framework, which effectively learns a diverse set of shapelets essential for classification. We further demonstrate that ShapeX produces explanations which reveal causal relationships instead of just correlations, owing to the atomicity properties of shapelets. Experimental results on both synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that ShapeX outperforms existing methods in identifying the most relevant subsequences, enhancing both the precision and causal fidelity of time series explanations.
中摘: 时间序列分类模型的解释至关重要，尤其是在医疗保健和金融等高风险应用领域，透明度和信任起着关键作用。尽管许多时间序列分类方法已经识别出关键子序列，即形状轮廓（shapelets），作为实现最先进的性能并验证其在分类结果中的核心作用的关键特征，现有的事后时间序列解释（PHTSE）方法主要集中在时间步长级别的特征归因上。这些解释方法忽视了一个基本前提：分类结果主要是由关键形状轮廓驱动的。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了ShapeX这一创新框架，该框架将时间序列分割成有意义的形状轮廓驱动片段，并使用Shapley值来评估这些片段的重要性。ShapeX的核心在于形状轮廓描述与检测（SDD）框架，该框架能够有效地学习对分类至关重要的多样化形状轮廓。此外，我们还证明了由于形状轮廓的原子性特性，ShapeX能够产生揭示因果关系而非仅仅相关性的解释。在合成数据集和真实世界数据集上的实验结果表明，ShapeX在识别最相关的子序列方面优于现有方法，提高了时间序列解释的精确性和因果忠实度。

[No.220] Parameter-Free Hypergraph Neural Network for Few-Shot Node Classification

英摘: Few-shot node classification on hypergraphs requires models that generalize from scarce labels while capturing high-order structures. Existing hypergraph neural networks (HNNs) effectively encode such structures but often suffer from overfitting and scalability issues due to complex, black-box architectures. In this work, we propose ZEN (Zero-Parameter Hypergraph Neural Network), a fully linear and parameter-free model that achieves both expressiveness and efficiency. Built upon a unified formulation of linearized HNNs, ZEN introduces a tractable closed-form solution for the weight matrix and a redundancy-aware propagation scheme to avoid iterative training and to eliminate redundant self information. 中摘: 基于超图的少量示例节点分类需要模型能够在稀缺标签的情况下泛化并捕捉高阶结构。现有的超图神经网络（HNNs）能够有效编码这些结构，但由于其复杂的黑盒架构，常常会遭受过拟合和可扩展性问题。在本文中，我们提出了一种名为ZEN（零参数超图神经网络）的完全线性和无参数模型，该模型同时实现了表达能力和效率。ZEN基于线性化HNNs的统一公式，引入了一个可处理的闭式解来解决权重矩阵，并采用一种意识到冗余性的传播方案，以避免迭代训练并消除冗余的自我信息。在11个真实世界的超图基准测试上，ZEN在分类准确性方面始终优于8种基线模型，在最快速的竞争者上实现了高达696倍的速度提升。此外，ZEN的决策过程是完全可解释的，可以提供有关数据集特性的见解。我们的代码和数据集完全可在https://github.com/chaewoonbae/ZEN获取。

[No.221] On the Robustness of Transformers against Context Hijacking for Linear Classification
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: classif
标题: 对抗上下文劫持的Transformer在线性分类中的鲁棒性
类别: theory
作者: Tianle Li;Chenyang Zhang;Xingwu Chen;Yuan Cao;Difan Zou;
机构: University of Hong Kong;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=gb2wOj7V74
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.15609
英摘: Transformer-based Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated powerful in-context learning capabilities. However, their predictions can be disrupted by factually correct context, a phenomenon known as context hijacking, revealing a significant robustness issue. To understand this phenomenon theoretically, we explore an in-context linear classification problem based on recent advances in linear transformers. In our setup, context tokens are designed as factually correct query-answer pairs, where the queries are similar to the final query but have opposite labels. Then, we develop a general theoretical analysis on the robustness of the linear transformers, which is formulated as a function of the model depth, training context lengths, and number of hijacking context tokens. A key finding is that a well-trained deeper transformer can achieve higher robustness, which aligns with empirical observations. We show that this improvement arises because deeper layers enable more fine-grained optimization steps, effectively mitigating interference from context hijacking. This is also well supported by our numerical experiments. Our findings provide theoretical insights into the benefits of deeper architectures and contribute to enhancing the understanding of transformer architectures.
中摘: 基于Transformer的大语言模型（LLMs）展示了强大的上下文内学习能力。然而，它们的预测可能会被事实正确的上下文所干扰，这种现象被称为“上下文劫持”，揭示了一个重要的鲁棒性问题。为了从理论上理解这一现象，我们基于近期线性Transformer的发展探索了一个基于上下文的线性分类问题。在我们的设置中，上下文标记被设计为事实正确的查询-答案对，其中查询类似于最终查询但标签相反。然后，我们发展了一种关于线性Transformer鲁棒性的通用理论分析，该分析形式化地表示为模型深度、训练上下文长度和劫持上下文标记的数量的函数。一个关键发现是，经过良好训练的更深的Transformer可以实现更高的鲁棒性，这与实验观察一致。我们证明了这种改进是因为深层层能够进行更精细的优化步骤，有效地减轻了上下文劫持的干扰。我们的数值实验也很好地支持了这一点。我们的研究提供了关于更深架构优势的理论见解，并有助于增强对Transformer架构的理解。

[No.223] Rethinking Tokenized Graph Transformers for Node Classification
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: classif
标题: 重新思考分词图变换器在节点分类中的应用
类别: deep learning
作者: Jinsong Chen;Chenyang Li;Gaichao Li;John E. Hopcroft;Kun He;
机构: Central China Normal University;Huazhong University of Science and Technology;Cornell University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=ofBqR4l0TD
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.08101
英摘: Node tokenized graph Transformers (GTs) have shown promising performance in node classification. The generation of token sequences is the key module in existing tokenized GTs which transforms the input graph into token sequences, facilitating the node representation learning via Transformer. In this paper, we observe that the generations of token sequences in existing GTs only focus on the first-order neighbors on the constructed similarity graphs, which leads to the limited usage of nodes to generate diverse token sequences, further restricting the potential of tokenized GTs for node classification. To this end, we propose a new method termed SwapGT. SwapGT first introduces a novel token swapping operation based on the characteristics of token sequences that fully leverages the semantic relevance of nodes to generate more informative token sequences. Then, SwapGT leverages a Transformer-based backbone to learn node representations from the generated token sequences. Moreover, SwapGT develops a center alignment loss to constrain the representation learning from multiple token sequences, further enhancing the model performance. Extensive empirical results on various datasets showcase the superiority of SwapGT for node classification.
中摘: 节点分词图变换器（Node Tokenized Graph Transformers, GTs）在节点分类任务中展现出了令人鼓舞的性能。现有分词GTs中的关键模块是生成分词序列，该模块将输入图转换为分词序列，从而通过Transformer促进节点表示学习。本文中，我们观察到现有GTs在生成分词序列时仅关注构建的相似性图上的邻近节点，这限制了节点用于生成多样化分词序列的能力，进一步限制了分词GTs在节点分类中的潜力。为此，我们提出了一种名为SwapGT的新方法。SwapGT首先引入了一种基于分词序列特征的新型分词交换操作，充分利用节点之间的语义相关性来生成更具信息量的分词序列。然后，SwapGT利用基于Transformer的骨干网络从生成的分词序列中学习节点表示。此外，SwapGT开发了一种中心对齐损失，以约束多分词序列的表示学习，进一步提升模型性能。在各种数据集上的广泛实验证明了SwapGT在节点分类任务中的优越性。

[No.226] Unlabeled Data Improves Fine-Grained Image Zero-shot Classification with Multimodal LLMs
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: classif
标题: 未标注数据提高多模态大语言模型在细粒度图像零样本分类中的性能
类别: applications
作者: Yunqi Hong;Sohyun An;Andrew Bai;Neil Lin;Cho-Jui Hsieh;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.03195
英摘: Despite Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) showing promising results on general zero-shot image classification tasks, fine-grained image classification remains challenging. It demands precise attention to subtle visual details to distinguish between visually similar subcategories--details that MLLMs may easily overlook without explicit guidance. To address this, we introduce AutoSEP, an iterative self-supervised prompt learning framework designed to enhance MLLM fine-grained classification capabilities in a fully unsupervised manner. Our core idea is to leverage unlabeled data to learn a description prompt that guides MLLMs in identifying crucial discriminative features within an image, and boosts classification accuracy. We developed an automatic self-enhancing prompt learning framework called AutoSEP to iteratively improve the description prompt using unlabeled data, based on instance-level classification scoring function. AutoSEP only requires black-box access to MLLMs, eliminating the need for any training or fine-tuning. We evaluate our approach on multiple fine-grained classification datasets. It consistently outperforms other unsupervised baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness of our self-supervised optimization framework. Notably, AutoSEP on average improves 13 percent over standard zero-shot classification and 5 percent over the best-performing baselines. Code is available at: https://github.com/yq-hong/AutoSEP
中摘: 尽管多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在通用零样本图像分类任务上显示出了令人鼓舞的结果，但细粒度图像分类仍然具有挑战性。它要求对微妙的视觉细节给予精确的关注，以区分视觉上相似的子类别——这些细节在没有明确指导的情况下，MLLMs可能会轻易忽略。为了解决这个问题，我们引入了AutoSEP，这是一种迭代自监督提示学习框架，旨在以完全无监督的方式增强MLLMs的细粒度分类能力。我们的核心思想是利用未标记的数据来学习一个描述提示，该提示可以引导MLLMs识别图像中的关键区分特征，并提升分类准确性。我们开发了一个自动自我增强提示学习框架AutoSEP，基于实例级分类评分函数，迭代地使用未标记数据改进描述提示。AutoSEP 只需要对MLLMs进行黑盒访问，无需任何训练或微调。我们在多个细粒度分类数据集上评估了我们的方法。结果显示，它始终优于其他无监督基准，证明了我们自监督优化框架的有效性。值得注意的是，与标准零样本分类相比，AutoSEP 平均提高了13个百分点；与表现最佳的基准相比，提高了5个百分点。代码可在以下链接获取：https://github.com/yq-hong/AutoSEP

[No.228] Geometric Imbalance in Semi-Supervised Node Classification
来源: NeurIPS 2025

标题: 半监督节点分类中的几何不平衡问题
类别: social and economic aspects of machine learning
作者: Liang Yan;Shengzhong Zhang;Bisheng Li;Menglin Yang;Chen Yang;Min Zhou;Weiyang Ding;Yutong Xie;Zengfeng Huang;

英摘: Class imbalance in graph data presents a significant challenge for effective node classification, particularly in semi-supervised scenarios. In this work, we formally introduce the concept of geometric imbalance, which captures how message passing on class-imbalanced graphs leads to geometric ambiguity among minority-class nodes in the riemannian manifold embedding space. We provide a rigorous theoretical analysis of geometric imbalance on the riemannian manifold and propose a unified framework that explicitly mitigates it through pseudo-label alignment, node reordering, and ambiguity filtering. Extensive experiments on diverse benchmarks show that our approach consistently outperforms existing methods, especially under severe class imbalance. Our findings offer new theoretical insights and practical tools for robust semi-supervised node classification.
中摘: 在图数据中存在类别不平衡问题，这为有效的节点分类带来了重大挑战，尤其是在半监督场景中。在本文中，我们正式引入了几何不平衡的概念，该概念捕捉了类别不平衡的图上消息传递如何在黎曼流形嵌入空间中导致少数类节点之间的几何模糊性。我们对几何不平衡在黎曼流形上的严格理论分析提出了一个统一框架，通过伪标签对齐、节点重排序和模糊性过滤显式地缓解这一问题。我们在多种基准上的广泛实验表明，我们的方法在所有情况下都能一致地优于现有方法，尤其是在类别极度不平衡的情况下。我们的研究提供了新的理论见解和实用工具，以实现稳健的半监督节点分类。

[No.230] Latent Zoning Network: A Unified Principle for Generative Modeling, Representation Learning, and Classification
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: classif
标题: 潜在分区网络：生成建模、表示学习和分类的统一原则
类别: general machine learning
作者: Zinan Lin;Enshu Liu;Xuefei Ning;Junyi Zhu;Wenyu Wang;Sergey Yekhanin;
机构: Microsoft;Tsinghua University;Samsung;Google;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=8KuKSKLott
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.15591
英摘: Generative modeling, representation learning, and classification are three core problems in machine learning (ML), yet their state-of-the-art (SoTA) solutions remain largely disjoint. In this paper, we ask: Can a unified principle address all three? Such unification could simplify ML pipelines and foster greater synergy across tasks. We introduce Latent Zoning Network (LZN) as a step toward this goal. At its core, LZN creates a shared Gaussian latent space that encodes information across all tasks. Each data type (e.g., images, text, labels) is equipped with an encoder that maps samples to disjoint latent zones, and a decoder that maps latents back to data. ML tasks are expressed as compositions of these encoders and decoders: for example, label-conditional image generation uses a label encoder and image decoder; image embedding uses an image encoder; classification uses an image encoder and label decoder. We demonstrate the promise of LZN in three increasingly complex scenarios: (1) LZN can enhance existing models (image generation): When combined with the SoTA Rectified Flow model, LZN improves FID on CIFAR10 from 2.76 to 2.59-without modifying the training objective. (2) LZN can solve tasks independently (representation learning): LZN can implement unsupervised representation learning without auxiliary loss functions, outperforming the seminal MoCo and SimCLR methods by 9.3% and 0.2%, respectively, on downstream linear classification on ImageNet. (3) LZN can solve multiple tasks simultaneously (joint generation and classification): With image and label encoders/decoders, LZN performs both tasks jointly by design, improving FID and achieving SoTA classification accuracy on CIFAR10. The code and trained models are available at https://github.com/microsoft/latent-zoning-networks. The project website is at https://zinanlin.me/blogs/latent_zoning_networks.html.
中摘: 生成模型、表征学习和分类是机器学习（ML）中的三个核心问题，但它们最先进的（SoTA）解决方案仍然相对独立。在本文中，我们提出一个问题：是否可以有一个统一的原则来解决所有这三个问题？这样的统一可以简化ML管道，并促进任务之间的更大协同作用。我们引入了潜空间分区网络（Latent Zoning Network，简称LZN），作为这一目标的一步。其核心在于创建一个共享的高斯潜空间，该空间能够跨所有任务编码信息。每种数据类型（例如，图像、文本、标签）都配备了一个编码器，将样本映射到不同的潜空间区域，以及一个解码器，将潜变量重新映射回数据。ML任务通过这些编码器和解码器的组合来表达：例如，条件标签图像生成使用标签编码器和图像解码器；图像嵌入使用图像编码器；分类则使用图像编码器和标签解码器。我们在三个逐渐复杂的情景中展示了LZN的潜力：（1）LZN可以增强现有模型（图像生成）：当与最先进的修正流模型结合时，LZN在CIFAR10上的FID得分从2.76提高到2.59，而无需修改训练目标。（2）LZN可以独立解决任务（表征学习）：LZN可以在没有辅助损失函数的情况下实现无监督的表征学习，分别在ImageNet下游线性分类任务上比MoCo和SimCLR方法高出9.3%和0.2%。（3）LZN可以同时解决多个任务（联合生成和分类）：通过设计中的图像和标签编码器/解码器，LZN能够同时执行这两个任务，在CIFAR10上提高FID得分并达到最先进的分类准确率。相关代码和训练模型可在https://github.com/microsoft/latent-zoning-networks获取。该项目网站位于https://zinanlin.me/blogs/latent_zoning_networks.html。

[No.231] Quantifying Uncertainty in Error Consistency: Towards Reliable Behavioral Comparison of Classifiers
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: classif
标题: 量化误差一致性中的不确定性：迈向可靠的分类器行为比较
类别: neuroscience and cognitive science
作者: Thomas Klein;Sascha Meyen;Wieland Brendel;Felix A. Wichmann;Kristof Meding;
机构: ;Eberhard Karls University of Tübingen;ELLIS Institute;University of Tübingen;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=ngBOb9wSYN
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.06645
英摘: Benchmarking models is a key factor for the rapid progress in machine learning (ML) research. Thus, further progress depends on improving benchmarking metrics. A standard metric to measure the behavioral alignment between ML models and human observers is error consistency (EC). EC allows for more fine-grained comparisons of behavior than other metrics such as accuracy, and has been used in the influential Brain-Score benchmark to rank different DNNs by their behavioral consistency with humans. Previously, EC values have been reported without confidence intervals. However, empirically measured EC values are typically noisy -- thus, without confidence intervals, valid benchmarking conclusions are problematic. Here we improve on standard EC in two ways: First, we show how to obtain confidence intervals for EC using a bootstrapping technique, allowing us to derive significance tests for EC. Second, we propose a new computational model relating the EC between two classifiers to the implicit probability that one of them copies responses from the other. This view of EC allows us to give practical guidance to scientists regarding the number of trials required for sufficiently powerful, conclusive experiments. Finally, we use our methodology to revisit popular NeuroAI-results. We find that while the general trend of behavioral differences between humans and machines holds up to scrutiny, many reported differences between deep vision models are statistically insignificant. Our methodology enables researchers to design adequately powered experiments that can reliably detect behavioral differences between models, providing a foundation for more rigorous benchmarking of behavioral alignment.
中摘: 模型基准测试是机器学习（ML）研究快速进步的关键因素。因此，进一步的进步取决于改进基准测试指标。衡量机器学习模型与人类观察者行为一致性的一个标准指标是误差一致性（EC）。EC 允许进行比准确率等其他指标更为细致的行为比较。它已被用于有影响力的 Brain-Score 基准测试中，根据其行为一致性排名不同的深度神经网络（DNNs）。此前，EC 值通常没有报告置信区间。然而，实际测量的 EC 值通常是噪声较大的，因此在没有置信区间的前提下，基准测试结论的有效性存在问题。在这里，我们通过两种方式改进了标准的 EC：首先，我们展示了如何使用自助法技术获得 EC 的置信区间，从而可以推导出 EC 的显著性检验；其次，我们提出了一种新的计算模型，将两个分类器之间的 EC 关系与其中一个分类器是否从另一个分类器复制响应的隐含概率联系起来。这种对 EC 的看法为科学家提供了关于进行足够强大且可靠的实验所需试次数量的实际指导。最后，我们利用我们的方法重新审视了流行的 NeuroAI 结果。我们发现，在严格的审查下，人类和机器之间行为差异的一般趋势仍然存在，但许多报告的深度视觉模型之间的差异在统计上并不显著。我们的方法使研究人员能够设计出足够强大的实验，以可靠地检测模型之间的行为差异，为更严谨的行为一致性基准测试奠定了基础。

[No.232] Concentration and excess risk bounds for imbalanced classification with synthetic oversampling
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: classif
标题: 合成过采样在不平衡分类中的集中度和超额风险界
类别: theory
作者: Touqeer Ahmad;Mohammadreza Mousavi Kalan;François Portier;Gilles Stupfler;
机构: Ecole Nationale de la Statistique et de l'Analyse de l'information;Université d'Angers;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=ajBIcOBcKV
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.20472
英摘: Synthetic oversampling of minority examples using SMOTE and its variants is a leading strategy for addressing imbalanced classification problems. Despite the success of this approach in practice, its theoretical foundations remain underexplored. We develop a theoretical framework to analyze the behavior of SMOTE and related methods when classifiers are trained on synthetic data. We first derive a uniform concentration bound on the discrepancy between the empirical risk over synthetic minority samples and the population risk on the true minority distribution. We then provide a nonparametric excess risk guarantee for kernel-based classifiers trained using such synthetic data. These results lead to practical guidelines for better parameter tuning of both SMOTE and the downstream learning algorithm. Numerical experiments are provided to illustrate and support the theoretical findings
中摘: 使用SMOTE和其变体对少数类样本进行合成过采样是一种解决不平衡分类问题的主要策略。尽管在实践中这种方法取得了成功，但其理论基础仍然没有得到充分探索。我们建立了一个理论框架来分析在使用合成数据训练分类器时SMOTE及其相关方法的行为。首先，我们推导出一个统一的集中不等式，用于分析合成少数类样本上的经验风险与真实少数类分布上的总体风险之间的差异。然后，我们为使用此类合成数据训练的核基分类器提供了非参数超额风险保证。这些结果导致了改进SMOTE及其下游学习算法参数调优的实用指南。数值实验被提供以说明并支持理论发现。

[No.233] Breaking the Gradient Barrier: Unveiling Large Language Models for Strategic Classification
来源: NeurIPS 2025

英摘: Strategic classification~(SC) explores how individuals or entities modify their features strategically to achieve favorable classification outcomes.
