中摘: 战略分类（SC）研究个体或实体如何战略性地修改其特征以实现有利的分类结果。然而，现有的SC方法，大多基于线性模型或浅层神经网络，在应用于具有显著增加规模的实际数据集时，在金融服务业和互联网领域尤其如此，面临着可扩展性和容量的重大限制。在这篇论文中，我们探讨了如何利用大规模语言模型来设计一个更可扩展和高效的SC框架，特别是在参与决策过程的个体数量不断增长的情况下。具体来说，我们引入了GLIM，这是一种基于上下文学习的无梯度SC方法。在自我注意力的前向传播过程中，GLIM隐式模拟了SC典型的两阶段优化过程，包括特征操纵和决策规则优化。无需微调大规模语言模型，我们提出的GLIM在动态战略环境中具有成本效益的适应优势。理论上，我们证明GLIM能够支持预训练的大规模语言模型适应广泛的策略操纵。通过在金融和互联网领域的现实世界和合成数据集上使用一系列预训练的大规模语言模型进行实验，我们验证了我们的方法，结果显示GLIM既具有稳健性又具有高效性，并提供了一个有效的解决方案，适用于大规模SC任务。

[No.234] CHiQPM: Calibrated Hierarchical Interpretable Image Classification

[No.237] DPA: A one-stop metric to measure bias amplification in classification datasets
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: classif
标题: DPA：一个一站式度量标准，用于测量分类数据集中的偏见放大
类别: social and economic aspects of machine learning
作者: Bhanu Tokas;Rahul Nair;Hannah Kerner;
机构: Arizona State University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=efuY2yYC1U
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.11060
英摘: Most ML datasets today contain biases. When we train models on these datasets, they often not only learn these biases but can worsen them -- a phenomenon known as bias amplification. Several co-occurrence-based metrics have been proposed to measure bias amplification in classification datasets. They measure bias amplification between a protected attribute (e.g., gender) and a task (e.g., cooking). These metrics also support fine-grained bias analysis by identifying the direction in which a model amplifies biases. However, co-occurrence-based metrics have limitations -- some fail to measure bias amplification in balanced datasets, while others fail to measure negative bias amplification. To solve these issues, recent work proposed a predictability-based metric called leakage amplification (LA). However, LA cannot identify the direction in which a model amplifies biases. We propose Directional Predictability Amplification (DPA), a predictability-based metric that is (1) directional, (2) works with balanced and unbalanced datasets, and (3) correctly identifies positive and negative bias amplification. DPA eliminates the need to evaluate models on multiple metrics to verify these three aspects. DPA also improves over prior predictability-based metrics like LA: it is less sensitive to the choice of attacker function (a hyperparameter in predictability-based metrics), reports scores within a bounded range, and accounts for dataset bias by measuring relative changes in predictability. Our experiments on well-known datasets like COMPAS (a tabular dataset), COCO, and ImSitu (image datasets) show that DPA is the most reliable metric to measure bias amplification in classification problems. To compare DPA with existing bias amplification metrics, we released a one-stop library of major bias amplification metrics at https://github.com/kerner-lab/Bias-Amplification.
中摘: 当今大多数机器学习（ML）数据集都包含偏见。当我们使用这些数据集训练模型时，模型不仅会学习这些偏见，还可能加剧它们，这一现象被称为偏见放大。已经提出了几种共现为基础的指标来衡量分类数据集中的偏见放大。这些指标通过识别受保护属性（例如，性别）与任务（例如，烹饪）之间的关系来测量偏见放大，并支持细粒度的偏见分析，以确定模型如何放大偏见的方向。然而，共现为基础的指标存在局限性——有些无法在平衡的数据集中测量偏见放大，而另一些则无法测量负向偏见放大。为了解决这些问题，最近的研究提出了一种基于可预测性的指标，称为泄漏放大（Leakage Amplification，LA），但它无法识别模型放大偏见的方向。我们提出了一种名为方向性可预测性放大（Directional Predictability Amplification，DPA）的基于可预测性的指标，该指标具有以下三个特点：（1）方向性；（2）适用于平衡和不平衡的数据集；（3）能够正确识别正向和负向偏见放大。DPA消除了评估模型需要在多个指标上进行验证的需求。与之前的基于可预测性的指标（如LA）相比，DPA也有所改进：它对攻击函数的选择（在基于可预测性的指标中是一个超参数）不太敏感，报告的分数处于限定范围内，并通过衡量可预测性相对变化来考虑数据集偏见。我们在著名的数据集（如COMPAS（表格数据集）、COCO和ImSitu（图像数据集））上的实验表明，DPA是最可靠的用于衡量分类问题中偏见放大的指标。为了将DPA与其他现有偏见放大指标进行比较，我们在https://github.com/kerner-lab/Bias-Amplification上发布了主要偏见放大指标的一站式库。

[No.238] Fairness-aware Bayes Optimal Functional Classification
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: classif
标题: 公平感知的贝叶斯最优功能分类

作者: Xiaoyu Hu;Gengyu Xue;Zhenhua Lin;Yi Yu;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.09471
英摘: Algorithmic fairness has become a central topic in machine learning, and mitigating disparities across different subpopulations has emerged as a rapidly growing research area. In this paper, we systematically study the classification of functional data under fairness constraints, ensuring the disparity level of the classifier is controlled below a pre-specified threshold. We propose a unified framework for fairness-aware functional classification, tackling an infinite-dimensional functional space, addressing key challenges from the absence of density ratios and intractability of posterior probabilities, and discussing unique phenomena in functional classification. We further design a post-processing algorithm, Fair Functional Linear Discriminant Analysis classifier (Fair-FLDA), which targets at homoscedastic Gaussian processes and achieves fairness via group-wise thresholding. Under weak structural assumptions on eigenspace, theoretical guarantees on fairness and excess risk controls are established. As a byproduct, our results cover the excess risk control of the standard FLDA as a special case, which, to the best of our knowledge, is first time seen. Our theoretical findings are complemented by extensive numerical experiments on synthetic and real datasets, highlighting the practicality of our designed algorithm.
中摘: 算法公平性已成为机器学习中的一个核心话题，减轻不同子人群之间的差异性也成为了一个迅速发展的研究领域。在本文中，我们系统地研究了在公平性约束下的功能性数据分类问题，确保分类器的差异水平被控制在一个预设的阈值之下。我们提出了一种统一的框架来实现公平的功能性分类，该框架处理无限维的功能性空间，并解决了由于缺乏密度比和后验概率不可解而导致的关键挑战，同时讨论了功能性分类中特有的现象。我们进一步设计了一种后处理算法——公平功能性线性判别分析分类器（Fair-FLDA），该算法针对同方差高斯过程，并通过组内阈值化实现了公平性。在对特征空间的弱结构假设下，我们建立了公平性和过拟合风险控制的理论保证。作为副产品，我们的结果涵盖了标准功能性线性判别分析分类器（FLDA）的过拟合风险控制，据我们所知，这是首次看到这种情况。我们的理论发现得到了大量合成数据集和真实数据集上的数值实验的支持，突显了我们设计的算法的实际应用价值。

[No.239] Exploring and Leveraging Class Vectors for Classifier Editing
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: classif
标题: 探索并利用类向量进行分类器编辑
类别: applications
作者: Jaeik Kim;Jaeyoung Do;
机构: Seoul National University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=jWrDyknUZ8
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.11268
英摘: Image classifiers play a critical role in detecting diseases in medical imaging and identifying anomalies in manufacturing processes. However, their predefined behaviors after extensive training make post hoc model editing difficult, especially when it comes to forgetting specific classes or adapting to distribution shifts. Existing classifier editing methods either focus narrowly on correcting errors or incur extensive retraining costs, creating a bottleneck for flexible editing. Moreover, such editing has seen limited investigation in image classification. To overcome these challenges, we introduce Class Vectors, which capture class-specific representation adjustments during fine-tuning. Whereas task vectors encode task-level changes in weight space, Class Vectors disentangle each class's adaptation in the latent space. We show that Class Vectors capture each class's semantic shift and that classifier editing can be achieved either by steering latent features along these vectors or by mapping them into weight space to update the decision boundaries. We also demonstrate that the inherent linearity and orthogonality of Class Vectors support efficient, flexible, and high-level concept editing via simple class arithmetic. Finally, we validate their utility in applications such as unlearning, environmental adaptation, adversarial defense, and adversarial trigger optimization.
中摘: 图像分类器在医学影像中的疾病检测和制造过程中异常识别中发挥着关键作用。然而，经过大量训练后它们预定义的行为使得事后模型编辑变得困难，尤其是在忘记特定类别或适应分布变化时。现有的分类器编辑方法要么专注于纠正错误，要么需要进行大量的重新训练，这成为灵活编辑的一个瓶颈。此外，这样的编辑在图像分类方面也受到了有限的研究。为克服这些挑战，我们引入了类向量（Class Vectors），它在微调过程中捕捉类别特定的表示调整。与任务向量编码权重空间的任务级变化不同，类向量将每个类别的适应性从潜在空间中分离出来。我们展示了类向量能够捕捉每个类别的语义变化，并且可以通过沿着这些向量引导潜在特征或将其映射到权重空间以更新决策边界来实现分类器编辑。我们还证明了类向量的固有线性和正交性支持通过简单的类别算术实现高效、灵活和高层次的概念编辑。最后，我们在诸如去学习、环境适应、对抗防御和对抗触发优化等应用中验证了类向量的实用性。

[No.240] Individual Fairness In Strategic Classification
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: classif
标题: 战略分类中的个体公平性
类别: social and economic aspects of machine learning
作者: Zhiqun Zuo;Mohammad Mahdi Khalili;

类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=sGY0TisiMB
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.05084
英摘: Strategic classification, where individuals modify their features to influence machine learning (ML) decisions, presents critical fairness challenges. While group fairness in this setting has been widely studied, individual fairness remains underexplored. We analyze threshold-based classifiers and prove that deterministic thresholds violate individual fairness. Then, we investigate the possibility of using a randomized classifier to achieve individual fairness. We introduce conditions under which a randomized classifier ensures individual fairness and leverage these conditions to find an optimal and individually fair randomized classifier through a linear programming problem. Additionally, we demonstrate that our approach can be extended to group fairness notions. Experiments on real-world datasets confirm that our method effectively mitigates unfairness and improves the fairness-accuracy trade-off.
中摘: 战略分类中，个体通过修改自己的特征来影响机器学习（ML）决策，这提出了关键的公平性挑战。虽然在这种情境下群体公平性已经得到了广泛研究，但个体公平性仍然没有得到充分探索。我们分析了阈值分类器，并证明了确定性阈值违反了个体公平性。然后，我们探讨了使用随机化分类器实现个体公平性的可能性。我们引入了确保随机化分类器实现个体公平性的条件，并利用这些条件通过线性规划问题找到一个最优且个体公平的随机化分类器。此外，我们展示了我们的方法可以扩展到群体公平性的概念。在实际数据集上的实验确认了我们的方法有效缓解了不公平现象，并改善了公平性和准确性的权衡。

[No.241] Convolution Goes Higher-Order: A Biologically Inspired Mechanism Empowers Image Classification
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: classif
标题: 高阶卷积：一种生物启发机制赋能图像分类
类别: deep learning
作者: Simone Azeglio;Olivier Marre;Peter Neri;Ulisse Ferrari;
机构: Ecole Normale Supérieure de Paris;Sorbonne Université - Faculté de Médecine;Sorbonne Université;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=u3aRwVkBv1
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.06740
英摘: We propose a novel approach to image classification inspired by complex nonlinear biological visual processing, whereby classical convolutional neural networks (CNNs) are equipped with learnable higher-order convolutions. Our model incorporates a Volterra-like expansion of the convolution operator, capturing multiplicative interactions akin to those observed in early and advanced stages of biological visual processing. We evaluated this approach on synthetic datasets by measuring sensitivity to testing higher-order correlations and performance in standard benchmarks (MNIST, FashionMNIST, CIFAR10, CIFAR100 and Imagenette). Our architecture outperforms traditional CNN baselines, and achieves optimal performance with expansions up to 3rd/4th order, aligning remarkably well with the distribution of pixel intensities in natural images. Through systematic perturbation analysis, we validate this alignment by isolating the contributions of specific image statistics to model performance, demonstrating how different orders of convolution process distinct aspects of visual information. Furthermore, Representational Similarity Analysis reveals distinct geometries across network layers, indicating qualitatively different modes of visual information processing. Our work bridges neuroscience and deep learning, offering a path towards more effective, biologically inspired computer vision models. It provides insights into visual information processing and lays the groundwork for neural networks that better capture complex visual patterns, particularly in resource-constrained scenarios.
中摘: 我们提出了一种受复杂非线性生物视觉处理启发的新颖图像分类方法，其中传统的卷积神经网络（CNN）被赋予了可学习的高阶卷积。我们的模型整合了卷积算子类似Volterra展开的形式，捕捉到了类似于生物视觉处理早期和晚期阶段观察到的乘性相互作用。我们在合成数据集上评估了这种方法，通过测量对测试高阶相关性的敏感度以及在标准基准测试（MNIST、FashionMNIST、CIFAR10、CIFAR100 和 Imagenette）中的表现来进行。我们的架构优于传统的CNN基线模型，并且在高阶展开至3/4阶时实现了最佳性能，这与自然图像中像素强度的分布高度一致。通过系统扰动分析，我们通过分离特定图像统计量对模型性能的贡献来验证这种一致性，展示了不同阶次的卷积如何处理视觉信息的不同方面。此外，表征相似性分析揭示了网络层之间不同的几何结构，表明了视觉信息处理的质性差异模式。我们的工作将神经科学与深度学习相结合，提供了一条更有效的、受生物学启发的计算机视觉模型的发展路径。它为理解视觉信息处理提供了见解，并为在资源受限场景下更好地捕捉复杂视觉模式奠定了神经网络的基础。

[No.242] Noise Matters: Optimizing Matching Noise for Diffusion Classifiers
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: classif
标题: 噪声很重要：优化扩散分类器中的匹配噪声
类别: deep learning
作者: Yanghao Wang;Long Chen;
机构: Hong Kong University of Science and Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=uG8bjnN1Nl
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.11330
英摘: Although today's pretrained discriminative vision-language models (e.g., CLIP) have demonstrated strong perception abilities, such as zero-shot image classification, they also suffer from the bag-of-words problem and spurious bias. To mitigate these problems, some pioneering studies leverage powerful generative models (e.g., pretrained diffusion models) to realize generalizable image classification, dubbed Diffusion Classifier (DC). Specifically, by randomly sampling a Gaussian noise, DC utilizes the differences of denoising effects with different category conditions to classify categories. Unfortunately, an inherent and notorious weakness of existing DCs is noise instability: different random sampled noises lead to significant performance changes. To achieve stable classification performance, existing DCs always ensemble the results of hundreds of sampled noises, which significantly reduces the classification speed. To this end, we firstly explore the role of noise in DC, and conclude that: there are some ``good noises'' that can relieve the instability. Meanwhile, we argue that these good noises should meet two principles: Frequency Matching and Spatial Matching. Regarding both principles, we propose a novel Noise Optimization method to learn matching (i.e., good) noise for DCs: NoOp. For frequency matching, NoOp first optimizes a dataset-specific noise: Given a dataset and a timestep t, optimize one randomly initialized parameterized noise. For Spatial Matching, NoOp trains a Meta-Network that adopts an image as input and outputs image-specific noise offset. The sum of optimized noise and noise offset will be used in DC to replace random noise. Extensive ablations on various datasets demonstrated the effectiveness of NoOp.
中摘: 尽管当今的预训练辨别型视觉-语言模型（例如CLIP）已经在零样本图像分类等方面展示了强大的感知能力，但它们也面临着“词袋”问题和虚假偏见。为缓解这些问题，一些开创性研究利用强大的生成模型（例如预训练扩散模型）实现了可泛化的图像分类，这种模型被称为扩散分类器（Diffusion Classifier，简称DC）。具体而言，通过随机采样高斯噪声，DC 利用不同类别条件下去噪效果的差异来进行分类。然而，现有的 DC 存在一个固有的、众所周知的弱点：噪声不稳定性；不同的随机噪声会导致显著的性能变化。为了实现稳定的分类性能，现有的 DC 总是将数百次采样噪声的结果进行集成，这极大地降低了分类速度。为此，我们首先探索了噪声在 DC 中的作用，并得出结论：存在一些“好的噪声”，可以缓解不稳定性。同时，我们认为这些好的噪声应该遵循两个原则：频率匹配和空间匹配。基于这两个原则，我们提出了一种新的噪声优化方法来学习适用于 DC 的匹配噪声（即“好的”噪声），这种方法称为 NoOp。对于频率匹配，NoOp 首先优化一个数据集特定的噪声：给定一个数据集和一个时间步 t，优化一个随机初始化的参数化噪声。而对于空间匹配，NoOp 训练了一个元网络，该网络以图像作为输入并输出图像特定的噪声偏移量。优化后的噪声与噪声偏移量之和将在 DC 中用于替代随机噪声。在各种数据集上的广泛消融实验表明了 NoOp 的有效性。

[No.243] Preference-driven Knowledge Distillation for Few-shot Node Classification
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: classif

英摘: Graph neural networks (GNNs) can efficiently process text-attributed graphs (TAGs) due to their message-passing mechanisms, but their training heavily relies on the human-annotated labels. Moreover, the complex and diverse local topologies of nodes of real-world TAGs make it challenging for a single mechanism to handle. Large language models (LLMs) perform well in zero-/few-shot learning on TAGs but suffer from a scalability challenge. Therefore, we propose a preference-driven knowledge distillation (PKD) framework to synergize the complementary strengths of LLMs and various GNNs for few-shot node classification. Specifically, we develop a GNN-preference-driven node selector that effectively promotes prediction distillation from LLMs to teacher GNNs. To further tackle nodes' intricate local topologies, we develop a node-preference-driven GNN selector that identifies the most suitable teacher GNN for each node, thereby facilitating tailored knowledge distillation from teacher GNNs to the student GNN. Extensive experiments validate the efficacy of our proposed framework in few-shot node classification on real-world TAGs. Our code is be available.
中摘: 图神经网络（GNNs）能够通过其消息传递机制高效地处理文本属性图（TAGs），但其训练过程高度依赖于人工标注的标签。此外，现实世界中TAGs节点复杂多样的局部拓扑结构使得单一机制难以应对。大型语言模型（LLMs）在处理TAGs的零样本/少样本学习任务中表现优异，但在可扩展性方面面临挑战。因此，我们提出了一种偏好驱动的知识蒸馏（PKD）框架，以协同利用LLMs和各种GNNs的优势进行少样本节点分类。具体而言，我们开发了一个GNN偏好驱动的节点选择器，有效促进了从LLMs到教师GNNs的预测蒸馏。为了进一步解决节点复杂的局部拓扑结构问题，我们还开发了一个节点偏好驱动的GNN选择器，为每个节点识别出最适合的教师GNN，从而促进针对学生GNN的个性化知识蒸馏。广泛的实验验证了我们提出的框架在现实世界TAGs的少样本节点分类任务中的有效性。我们的代码将对外开放。

[No.244] SuperCLIP: CLIP with Simple Classification Supervision
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: classif
标题: SuperCLIP：带有简单分类监督的CLIP
类别: deep learning
作者: Weiheng Zhao;Zilong Huang;Jiashi Feng;Xinggang Wang;
机构: Huazhong University of Science and Technology;ByteDance;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=EeIEvZlmVg
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.14480
英摘: Contrastive Language-Image Pretraining (CLIP) achieves strong generalization in vision-language tasks by aligning images and texts in a shared embedding space. However, recent findings show that CLIP-like models still underutilize fine-grained semantic signals in text, and this issue becomes even more pronounced when dealing with long and detailed captions. This stems from CLIP's training objective, which optimizes only global image-text similarity and overlooks token-level supervision - limiting its ability to achieve fine-grained visual-text alignment. To address this, we propose SuperCLIP, a simple yet effective framework that augments contrastive learning with classification-based supervision. By adding only a lightweight linear layer to the vision encoder, SuperCLIP leverages token-level cues to enhance visual-textual alignment - with just a 0.077% increase in total FLOPs, and no need for additional annotated data. Experiments show that SuperCLIP consistently improves zero-shot classification, image-text retrieval, and purely visual tasks. These gains hold regardless of whether the model is trained on original web data or rich re-captioned data, demonstrating SuperCLIP's ability to recover textual supervision in both cases. Furthermore, SuperCLIP alleviates CLIP's small-batch performance drop through classification-based supervision that avoids reliance on large batch sizes. Code and models will be made open source.
中摘: 对比语言-图像预训练（CLIP）通过在共享嵌入空间中对齐图像和文本，在视觉-语言任务中实现了强大的泛化能力。然而，最近的研究发现，CLIP 类型的模型仍然未能充分利用文本中的细粒度语义信号，特别是在处理长而详细的描述时，这一问题更为明显。这源于 CLIP 的训练目标，该目标仅优化全局图像-文本相似性，而忽略了基于标记的监督——限制了其实现细粒度的视觉-文本对齐的能力。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了一种简单而有效的框架 SuperCLIP，它将对比学习与分类监督相结合。通过仅向视觉编码器添加一个轻量级的线性层，SuperCLIP 利用基于标记的线索来增强视觉-文本对齐——仅增加了总 FLOPs 的 0.077%，且无需额外标注数据。实验表明，SuperCLIP 在零样本分类、图像-文本检索以及纯视觉任务上均能持续提升性能。这些增益在模型使用原始网络数据或丰富重新描述的数据进行训练时依然有效，展示了 SuperCLIP 能够在两种情况下恢复文本监督的能力。此外，SuperCLIP 通过基于分类的监督减少了 CLIP 在小批量训练时的表现下降，代码和模型将开源。

[No.245] Rethinking Approximate Gaussian Inference in Classification
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: classif
标题: 重新思考分类中的近似高斯推断
类别: probabilistic methods
作者: Bálint Mucsányi;Nathaël Da Costa;Philipp Hennig;
机构: Eberhard Karls University of Tübingen;University of Oxford;University of Tübingen;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=xPQyRmZ8hz
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.03366
英摘: In classification tasks, softmax functions are ubiquitously used as output activations to produce predictive probabilities. Such outputs only capture aleatoric uncertainty. To capture epistemic uncertainty, approximate Gaussian inference methods have been proposed. We develop a common formalism to describe such methods, which we view as outputting Gaussian distributions over the logit space. Predictives are then obtained as the expectations of the Gaussian distributions pushed forward through the softmax. However, such softmax Gaussian integrals cannot be solved analytically, and Monte Carlo (MC) approximations can be costly and noisy. We propose to replace the softmax activation by element-wise normCDF or sigmoid, which allows for the accurate sampling-free approximation of predictives. This also enables the approximation of the Gaussian pushforwards by Dirichlet distributions with moment matching. This approach entirely eliminates the runtime and memory overhead associated with MC sampling. We evaluate it combined with several approximate Gaussian inference methods (Laplace, HET, SNGP) on large- and small-scale datasets (ImageNet, CIFAR-100, CIFAR-10), demonstrating improved uncertainty quantification capabilities compared to softmax MC sampling. Our code is available at https://github.com/bmucsanyi/probit.
中摘: 在分类任务中，softmax函数广泛用作输出激活函数以生成预测概率。这类输出只能捕捉到 aleatoric 不确定性。为了捕捉 epistemic 不确定性，已经提出了近似高斯推断方法。我们开发了一种通用的形式化描述这些方法的方式，我们将这些方法视为在logit空间上输出高斯分布。然后，通过softmax将这些高斯分布推进得到预测值。然而，这样的softmax高斯积分无法解析求解，并且蒙特卡洛（MC）近似可能会非常昂贵且噪声较大。我们提议用逐元素的normCDF或sigmoid替换softmax激活函数，这允许我们通过精确的无采样近似来估计预测值。这种方法还使我们能够通过矩匹配将高斯推进近似为狄利克雷分布。这种方法完全消除了与MC采样相关的运行时和内存开销。我们在几种近似高斯推断方法（拉普拉斯法、HET、SNGP）与大型和小型数据集（ImageNet、CIFAR-100、CIFAR-10）结合使用时进行了评估，展示了相比softmax MC采样，其改进的不确定性量化能力。我们的代码可在https://github.com/bmucsanyi/probit获取。

[No.247] Transformers are almost optimal metalearners for linear classification
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: classif
标题: 变压器几乎是最优的元学习者，适用于线性分类任务
类别: theory
作者: Roey Magen;Gal Vardi;
机构: Weizmann Institute of Science;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=Smy93p3NCT
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.19797
英摘: Transformers have demonstrated impressive in-context learning (ICL) capabilities, raising the question of whether they can serve as metalearners that adapt to new tasks using only a small number of in-context examples, without any further training. While recent theoretical work has studied transformers' ability to perform ICL, most of these analyses do not address the formal metalearning setting, where the objective is to solve a collection of related tasks more efficiently than would be possible by solving each task individually. In this paper, we provide the first theoretical analysis showing that a simplified transformer architecture trained via gradient descent can act as a near-optimal metalearner in a linear classification setting. We consider a natural family of tasks where each task corresponds to a class-conditional Gaussian mixture model, with the mean vectors lying in a shared $k$-dimensional subspace of $R^d$. After training on a sufficient number of such tasks, we show that the transformer can generalize to a new task using only $O(k / R^4)$ in-context examples, where $R$ denotes the signal strength at test time. This performance (almost) matches that of an optimal learner that knows exactly the shared subspace and significantly outperforms any learner that only has access to the in-context data, which requires $Ω(d / R^4)$ examples to generalize. Importantly, our bounds on the number of training tasks and examples per task needed to achieve this result are independent of the ambient dimension $d$.
中摘: 变压器展示了令人印象深刻的上下文内学习（ICL）能力，引发了它们是否可以作为元学习器的问题，即仅使用少量上下文示例而无需进一步训练就能适应新任务。尽管近期的理论研究已经探讨了变压器进行上下文内学习的能力，但大多数分析并未涉及正式的元学习设置，在这种设置中，目标是在解决一组相关任务时比单独解决每个任务更高效。在本文中，我们首次提供了理论分析，证明通过梯度下降训练的一种简化变压器架构可以在线性分类设置中近似充当最优元学习器。我们考虑了一类自然的任务，其中每个任务对应于一个条件高斯混合模型，均值向量位于共享的$d$维空间中的$k$维子空间中。经过足够的此类任务的训练后，我们展示出，变压器仅需使用$O(k / R^4)$个上下文示例即可泛化到新任务，其中$R$表示测试时的信号强度。这一性能几乎与一个已知共享子空间的最优学习器相当，并且显著优于仅能访问上下文数据的学习器，后者需要$Ω(d / R^4)$个示例才能泛化。重要的是，我们的训练任务数量和每任务所需示例数的上界与环境维度$d$无关。

[No.248] Tackling Feature-Classifier Mismatch in Federated Learning via Prompt-Driven Feature Transformation
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: classif
标题: 通过提示驱动特征转换解决联邦学习中的特征分类器不匹配问题
类别: general machine learning
作者: Xinghao Wu;Xuefeng Liu;Jianwei Niu;Guogang Zhu;Mingjia Shi;Shaojie Tang;Jing Yuan;
机构: Beihang University;Behang University;University of Virginia;Houmo AI;National University of Singapore;;University of North Texas;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=vTJFQu5YXz
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.16139
英摘: In traditional Federated Learning approaches like FedAvg, the global model underperforms when faced with data heterogeneity. Personalized Federated Learning (PFL) enables clients to train personalized models to fit their local data distribution better. However, we surprisingly find that the feature extractor in FedAvg is superior to those in most PFL methods. More interestingly, by applying a linear transformation on local features extracted by the feature extractor to align with the classifier, FedAvg can surpass the majority of PFL methods. This suggests that the primary cause of FedAvg's inadequate performance stems from the mismatch between the locally extracted features and the classifier. While current PFL methods mitigate this issue to some extent, their designs compromise the quality of the feature extractor, thus limiting the full potential of PFL. In this paper, we propose a new PFL framework called FedPFT to address the mismatch problem while enhancing the quality of the feature extractor. FedPFT integrates a feature transformation module, driven by personalized prompts, between the global feature extractor and classifier. In each round, clients first train prompts to transform local features to match the global classifier, followed by training model parameters. This approach can also align the training objectives of clients, reducing the impact of data heterogeneity on model collaboration. Moreover, FedPFT's feature transformation module is highly scalable, allowing for the use of different prompts to tailor local features to various tasks. Leveraging this, we introduce a collaborative contrastive learning task to further refine feature extractor quality. Our experiments demonstrate that FedPFT outperforms state-of-the-art methods by up to 7.08%.
中摘: 在传统的联邦学习方法如FedAvg中，面对数据异质性时，全局模型表现不佳。个性化联邦学习（PFL）允许客户端训练出更适合本地数据分布的个性化模型。然而，我们惊讶地发现，在FedAvg中的特征提取器优于大多数PFL方法中的特征提取器。更有趣的是，通过在特征提取器提取的局部特征上应用线性变换，使其与分类器对齐，FedAvg可以超越大多数PFL方法。这表明FedAvg表现不足的主要原因在于局部提取的特征与分类器之间的不匹配。虽然当前的PFL方法在一定程度上缓解了这一问题，但它们的设计牺牲了特征提取器的质量，从而限制了PFL的全部潜力。在本文中，我们提出了一种新的PFL框架——FedPFT，以解决不匹配问题并提高特征提取器的质量。FedPFT在全局特征提取器和分类器之间集成了一个由个性化提示驱动的特征转换模块。在每一轮中，客户端首先训练提示以将局部特征转换为与全局分类器匹配，然后训练模型参数。这种方法还可以使客户端的训练目标更加一致，减少数据异质性对模型协作的影响。此外，FedPFT的特征转换模块具有高度可扩展性，可以使用不同的提示来根据各种任务定制局部特征。借助这一点，我们引入了一个协作对比学习任务，进一步细化特征提取器的质量。我们的实验结果显示，FedPFT相比于最先进的方法性能提高了高达7.08%。

[No.251] Diffusion-Classifier Synergy: Reward-Aligned Learning via Mutual Boosting Loop for FSCIL
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: classif
标题: 扩散分类协同效应：通过相互增强循环实现奖励对齐学习以应对多任务持续学习
类别: deep learning
作者: Ruitao Wu;Yifan Zhao;Guangyao Chen;Jia Li;
机构: Beihang University;Cornell University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=ub5QBBQ47S
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.03608
英摘: Few-Shot Class-Incremental Learning (FSCIL) challenges models to sequentially learn new classes from minimal examples without forgetting prior knowledge, a task complicated by the stability-plasticity dilemma and data scarcity. Current FSCIL methods often struggle with generalization due to their reliance on limited datasets. While diffusion models offer a path for data augmentation, their direct application can lead to semantic misalignment or ineffective guidance. This paper introduces Diffusion-Classifier Synergy (DCS), a novel framework that establishes a mutual boosting loop between diffusion model and FSCIL classifier. DCS utilizes a reward-aligned learning strategy, where a dynamic, multi-faceted reward function derived from the classifier's state directs the diffusion model. This reward system operates at two levels: the feature level ensures semantic coherence and diversity using prototype-anchored maximum mean discrepancy and dimension-wise variance matching, while the logits level promotes exploratory image generation and enhances inter-class discriminability through confidence recalibration and cross-session confusion-aware mechanisms. This co-evolutionary process, where generated images refine the classifier and an improved classifier state yields better reward signals, demonstrably achieves state-of-the-art performance on FSCIL benchmarks, significantly enhancing both knowledge retention and new class learning.
中摘: 少样本类增量学习（Few-Shot Class-Incremental Learning，简称FSCIL）挑战模型在无需遗忘先前知识的前提下，从少量示例中顺序学习新类。这一任务因稳定性与可塑性之间的矛盾以及数据稀缺性而变得复杂。当前的FSCIL方法往往由于依赖于有限的数据集而在泛化能力上遇到困难。虽然扩散模型为数据增强提供了一条途径，但直接应用它们可能会导致语义不匹配或无效指导。本文引入了扩散分类协同（Diffusion-Classifier Synergy，简称DCS），这是一种新颖的框架，它在扩散模型和FSCIL分类器之间建立了一个相互增强的循环。DCS采用了一种奖励对齐的学习策略，其中动态、多方面的奖励函数来源于分类器的状态，指导扩散模型。这个奖励系统在两个层面上运作：特征层面通过原型锚定的最大均值偏差和维度间方差匹配来确保语义一致性与多样性；逻辑层面则通过信心校准和跨会话混淆意识机制促进探索性图像生成并增强跨类别的区分能力。这种共生进化过程，在生成图像改进分类器的同时，一个优化后的分类器状态提供更好的奖励信号，已被证明在FSCIL基准测试中实现了最先进的性能，显著提升了知识保留能力和新类别的学习能力。

[No.252] What Does It Take to Build a Performant Selective Classifier?
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: classif
标题: 构建高性能选择性分类器需要什么？
类别: other
作者: Stephan Rabanser;Nicolas Papernot;
机构: Princeton University;University of Toronto;Google;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=pcwI5cNFJK
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.20242
英摘: Selective classifiers improve model reliability by abstaining on inputs the model deems uncertain. However, few practical approaches achieve the gold-standard performance of a perfect-ordering oracle that accepts examples exactly in order of correctness. Our work formalizes this shortfall as the selective-classification gap and present the first finite-sample decomposition of this gap to five distinct sources of looseness: Bayes noise, approximation error, ranking error, statistical noise, and implementation- or shift-induced slack. Crucially, our analysis reveals that monotone post-hoc calibration -- often believed to strengthen selective classifiers -- has limited impact on closing this gap, since it rarely alters the model's underlying score ranking. Bridging the gap therefore requires scoring mechanisms that can effectively reorder predictions rather than merely rescale them. We validate our decomposition on synthetic two-moons data and on real-world vision and language benchmarks, isolating each error component through controlled experiments. Our results confirm that (i) Bayes noise and limited model capacity can account for substantial gaps, (ii) only richer, feature-aware calibrators meaningfully improve score ordering, and (iii) data shift introduces a separate slack that demands distributionally robust training. Together, our decomposition yields a quantitative error budget as well as actionable design guidelines that practitioners can use to build selective classifiers which approximate ideal oracle behavior more closely.
中摘: 选择性分类器通过在模型认为不确定的输入上弃权来提高模型的可靠性。然而，很少有实际方法能够达到完美排序预言机的标准性能，即按正确顺序精确接受示例。我们的研究将这一不足正式化为选择性分类差距，并首次提出了这种差距的有限样本分解，分解为五个不同的松紧来源：贝叶斯噪声、逼近误差、排名误差、统计噪声以及实施或转移引起的松弛。关键的是，我们的分析揭示了单调后校准——通常被认为可以增强选择性分类器——对缩小这一差距的影响有限，因为这种校准极少改变模型的基本得分排名。因此，缩小差距需要能够有效重新排列预测的评分机制，而不仅仅是重新缩放它们。我们通过对合成的两月亮数据和真实世界的视觉和语言基准进行受控实验，验证了我们的分解方法，分别隔离了每个错误组件。实验结果证实了以下几点：(i) 贝叶斯噪声和模型容量限制可以解释显著的差距；(ii) 只有更丰富、特征感知的校准器能实质性地改进得分排序；(iii) 数据转移引入了一种独立的松弛，要求进行分布稳健的训练。综上所述，我们的分解不仅提供了一个定量的误差预算，还给出了可操作的设计指南，帮助实践者构建更接近理想预言机行为的选择性分类器。

[No.253] Incentivizing Desirable Effort Profiles in Strategic Classification: The Role of Causality and Uncertainty
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: classif
标题: 战略分类中激励 desirable 努力模式的作用：因果关系与不确定性的重要性
类别: social and economic aspects of machine learning
作者: Valia Efthymiou;Chara Podimata;Diptangshu Sen;Juba Ziani;
机构: Massachusetts Institute of Technology;Georgia Institute of Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=ioYzPQb7cL
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.06749
英摘: We study strategic classification in binary decision-making settings where agents can modify their features in order to improve their classification outcomes. Importantly, our work considers the causal structure across different features, acknowledging that effort in a given feature may affect other features. The main goal of our work is to understand \emph{when and how much agent effort is invested towards desirable features}, and how this is influenced by the deployed classifier, the causal structure of the agent's features, their ability to modify them, and the information available to the agent about the classifier and the feature causal graph.
  In the complete information case, when agents know the classifier and the causal structure of the problem, we derive conditions ensuring that rational agents focus on features favored by the principal. We show that designing classifiers to induce desirable behavior is generally non-convex, though tractable in special cases. We also extend our analysis to settings where agents have incomplete information about the classifier or the causal graph. While optimal effort selection is again a non-convex problem under general uncertainty, we highlight special cases of partial uncertainty where this selection problem becomes tractable. Our results indicate that uncertainty drives agents to favor features with higher expected importance and lower variance, potentially misaligning with principal preferences. Finally, numerical experiments based on a cardiovascular disease risk study illustrate how to incentivize desirable modifications under uncertainty.
中摘: 我们在二元决策制定的场景中研究战略性分类问题，其中代理可以修改其特征以改善分类结果。重要的是，我们的工作考虑了不同特征之间的因果结构，承认在某一特征上付出努力可能会影响其他特征。我们工作的主要目标是理解“代理会将多少努力投资于有利特征以及这种努力是如何受到部署的分类器、代理特征的因果结构、他们修改这些特征的能力以及代理关于分类器和特征因果图的信息可用性的影响”。

在完全信息的情况下，当代理知道分类器和问题的因果结构时，我们推导出确保理性代理专注于主方偏好的特征的条件。我们展示了设计分类器以诱导期望行为通常是非凸的问题，尽管在某些特殊情况下是可处理的。我们还将分析扩展到代理对分类器或因果图信息不完整的情况。虽然在一般不确定性下最佳努力选择仍然是一个非凸问题，但我们强调在部分不确定性的情况下，这种选择问题变得可处理。我们的结果显示，不确定性促使代理偏好具有更高预期重要性和更低方差的特征，这可能会与主方的偏好产生偏差。最后，基于心血管疾病风险的研究数值实验说明了如何在不确定性条件下激励期望的特征修改。

英摘: We consider binary classification restricted to a class of continuous piecewise linear functions whose decision boundaries are (possibly nonconvex) starshaped polyhedral sets, supported on a fixed polyhedral simplicial fan. [No.259] Embedding Principle of Homogeneous Neural Network for Classification Problem
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: classif
标题: 同构神经网络分类问题的嵌入原则
类别: deep learning
作者: Jiahan Zhang;Yaoyu Zhang;Tao Luo;
机构: Shanghai Jiao Tong University;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=4IIeoaNZ3a
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.12419
英摘: In this paper, we study the Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) points of the associated maximum-margin problem in homogeneous neural networks, including fully-connected and convolutional neural networks. In particular, We investigates the relationship between such KKT points across networks of different widths generated. We introduce and formalize the \textbf{KKT point embedding principle}, establishing that KKT points of a homogeneous network's max-margin problem ($P_Φ$) can be embedded into the KKT points of a larger network's problem ($P_{\tildeΦ}$) via specific linear isometric transformations. We rigorously prove this principle holds for neuron splitting in fully-connected networks and channel splitting in convolutional neural networks. Furthermore, we connect this static embedding to the dynamics of gradient flow training with smooth losses. We demonstrate that trajectories initiated from appropriately mapped points remain mapped throughout training and that the resulting $ω$-limit sets of directions are correspondingly mapped, thereby preserving the alignment with KKT directions dynamically when directional convergence occurs. We conduct several experiments to justify that trajectories are preserved. Our findings offer insights into the effects of network width, parameter redundancy, and the structural connections between solutions found via optimization in homogeneous networks of varying sizes.
中摘: 在本文中，我们研究了同质神经网络（包括全连接和卷积神经网络）相关最大边际问题的Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) 点。特别地，我们探讨了不同宽度网络生成的此类KKT点之间的关系。我们引入并正式化了**KKT点嵌入原则**，证明了一种同质网络的最大边际问题（$P_Φ$）的KKT点可以通过特定的线性等距变换嵌入到更大网络的问题（$P_{\tildeΦ}$）的KKT点中。我们严格证明了这一原则适用于全连接网络中的神经元分裂和卷积神经网络中的通道分裂。此外，我们将这种静态嵌入与光滑损失函数下的梯度流训练动态联系起来。我们展示，从适当映射的点出发的轨迹在整个训练过程中保持映射状态，并且方向收敛产生的$\omega$极限集方向也随之映射，从而在方向收敛时动态地保持与KKT方向的对齐。我们进行了多项实验来验证轨迹的保持情况。我们的发现为不同宽度网络的影响、参数冗余以及不同大小同质网络中通过优化找到的解之间的结构联系提供了见解。

[No.260] Gradient-Weight Alignment as a Train-Time Proxy for Generalization in Classification Tasks
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: classif
标题: 梯度权重对齐作为分类任务训练时的泛化代理
类别: general machine learning
作者: Florian A. Hölzl;Daniel Rueckert;Georgios Kaissis;
机构: ;Technische Universität München;Imperial College London;
类型: Poster

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.25480
英摘: Robust validation metrics remain essential in contemporary deep learning, not only to detect overfitting and poor generalization, but also to monitor training dynamics. In the supervised classification setting, we investigate whether interactions between training data and model weights can yield such a metric that both tracks generalization during training and attributes performance to individual training samples. We introduce Gradient-Weight Alignment (GWA), quantifying the coherence between per-sample gradients and model weights. We show that effective learning corresponds to coherent alignment, while misalignment indicates deteriorating generalization. GWA is efficiently computable during training and reflects both sample-specific contributions and dataset-wide learning dynamics. Extensive experiments show that GWA accurately predicts optimal early stopping, enables principled model comparisons, and identifies influential training samples, providing a validation-set-free approach for model analysis directly from the training data.
中摘: 稳健的验证指标在当今的深度学习中仍然至关重要，不仅能够检测过拟合和较差的泛化能力，还能监控训练动态。在监督分类设置中，我们研究了训练数据与模型权重之间的交互是否能产生这样一个指标：既能追踪训练过程中的泛化能力变化，又能将性能归因于个别训练样本。我们引入了梯度权重对齐（Gradient-Weight Alignment, GWA），量化了每个样本的梯度与模型权重之间的协调性。我们展示了有效的学习对应于协调的对齐，而对齐不协调则表明泛化能力下降。GWA 在训练过程中高效可计算，并反映了样本特定的贡献以及整个数据集的学习动态。大量实验表明，GWA 准确预测了最优的提前停止点，使模型比较具有原则性，还识别出了关键的训练样本，提供了一种无需验证集即可直接从训练数据进行模型分析的方法。

[No.261] Strategic Classification with Non-Linear Classifiers
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: classif
标题: 非线性分类器的战略分类
类别: social and economic aspects of machine learning
作者: Benyamin Trachtenberg;Nir Rosenfeld;
机构: Technion - Israel Institute of Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=Y9f6ZYmVCD
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.23443
英摘: In strategic classification, the standard supervised learning setting is extended to support the notion of strategic user behavior in the form of costly feature manipulations made in response to a classifier. While standard learning supports a broad range of model classes, the study of strategic classification has, so far, been dedicated mostly to linear classifiers. This work aims to expand the horizon by exploring how strategic behavior manifests under non-linear classifiers and what this implies for learning. We take a bottom-up approach showing how non-linearity affects decision boundary points, classifier expressivity, and model class complexity. Our results show how, unlike the linear case, strategic behavior may either increase or decrease effective class complexity, and that the complexity decrease may be arbitrarily large. Another key finding is that universal approximators (e.g., neural nets) are no longer universal once the environment is strategic. We demonstrate empirically how this can create performance gaps even on an unrestricted model class.
中摘: 在战略分类中，标准监督学习设置被扩展以支持用户以成本特征操作形式对分类器作出的战略行为。虽然标准学习支持广泛的模型类别，但迄今为止，战略分类的研究主要集中在线性分类器上。本项工作旨在通过探索非线性分类器下战略行为的表现及其对学习的影响来扩大研究范围。我们采取自下而上的方法，展示非线性如何影响决策边界点、分类器的表达能力和模型类的复杂度。我们的研究结果表明，在非线性情况下，战略行为可能会增加或减少有效的类复杂度，而且这种复杂度的减少可能是任意大的。另一个关键发现是，一旦环境具有战略性，通用逼近器（例如神经网络）将不再具有普遍性。我们通过实验证明，这可以在不受限制的模型类别上创造出性能差距。

[No.262] From Contextual Combinatorial Semi-Bandits to Bandit List Classification: Improved Sample Complexity with Sparse Rewards
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: classif
标题: 从上下文组合半 bandeits 到带列表分类的bandit问题：稀疏奖励下的改进样本复杂度
类别: theory
作者: Liad Erez;Tomer Koren;
机构: Tel Aviv University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=iEegka1gfw
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.09257
英摘: We study the problem of contextual combinatorial semi-bandits, where input contexts are mapped into subsets of size $m$ of a collection of $K$ possible actions. In each round, the learner observes the realized reward of the predicted actions. Motivated by prototypical applications of contextual bandits, we focus on the $s$-sparse regime where we assume that the sum of rewards is bounded by some value $s\ll K$. For example, in recommendation systems the number of products purchased by any customer is significantly smaller than the total number of available products. Our main result is for the $(ε,δ)$-PAC variant of the problem for which we design an algorithm that returns an $ε$-optimal policy with high probability using a sample complexity of $\tilde{O}((poly(K/m)+sm/ε^2) \log(|Π|/δ))$ where $Π$ is the underlying (finite) class and $s$ is the sparsity parameter. This bound improves upon known bounds for combinatorial semi-bandits whenever $s\ll K$, and in the regime where $s=O(1)$, the leading term is independent of $K$. Our algorithm is also computationally efficient given access to an ERM oracle for $Π$. Our framework generalizes the list multiclass classification problem with bandit feedback, which can be seen as a special case with binary reward vectors. In the special case of single-label classification corresponding to $s=m=1$, we prove an $O((K^7+1/ε^2)\log(|H|/δ))$ sample complexity bound, which improves upon recent results in this scenario. Additionally, we consider the regret minimization setting where data can be generated adversarially, and establish a regret bound of $\tilde O(|Π|+\sqrt{smT\log |Π|})$, extending the result of Erez et al. (2024) who consider the simpler single label classification setting.
中摘: 我们研究上下文组合半伯努利问题，其中输入上下文映射到大小为$m$的$K$个可能动作的子集。在每一轮中，学习者观察到所预测的动作实现的奖励。受上下文伯努利问题典型应用的启发，我们集中在$s$稀疏情况下，假设奖励之和被某个值$s\ll K$所限制。例如，在推荐系统中，任何客户的购买产品数量远小于可用产品的总数。我们的主要结果是针对$(ε,δ)$-PAC变体的问题，设计了一个算法，以高概率返回一个$ε$-最优策略，其样本复杂度为$\tilde{O}((poly(K/m)+sm/ε^2) \log(|Π|/δ))$，其中$Π$是潜在的（有限的）类别集合，$s$是稀疏参数。这一界在$s\ll K$的情况下改进了已知的组合半伯努利问题的界，并且在$s=O(1)$的条件下，主项与$K$无关。我们的算法在访问$Π$的ERM或acles时也是计算高效的。我们的框架扩展了带反馈列表多类分类问题，后者可以被视为二元奖励向量的特例。在单标签分类的特殊情况（即$s=m=1$）下，我们证明了一个$\tilde{O}((K^7+1/ε^2) \log(|H|/δ))$的样本复杂度界，这改进了该场景下的近期结果。此外，我们还考虑了数据可以对抗性生成的遗憾最小化设置，并建立了遗憾界$\tilde{O}(|Π|+\sqrt{smT\log |Π|})$，扩展了Erez等人（2024）的结果，他们考虑了更简单的单标签分类设置。

[No.263] Semi-Supervised Sparse Gaussian Classification: Provable Benefits of Unlabeled Data
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: classif
标题: 半监督稀疏高斯分类：未标记数据的可证明优势
类别: learning theory
作者: Eyar Azar;Boaz Nadler;
机构: Weizmann Institute of Science;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/94319
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.03335
英摘: The premise of semi-supervised learning (SSL) is that combining labeled and unlabeled data yields significantly more accurate models. Despite empirical successes, the theoretical understanding of SSL is still far from complete. In this work, we study SSL for high dimensional sparse Gaussian classification. To construct an accurate classifier a key task is feature selection, detecting the few variables that separate the two classes. % For this SSL setting, we analyze information theoretic lower bounds for accurate feature selection as well as computational lower bounds, assuming the low-degree likelihood hardness conjecture. % Our key contribution is the identification of a regime in the problem parameters (dimension, sparsity, number of labeled and unlabeled samples) where SSL is guaranteed to be advantageous for classification. Specifically, there is a regime where it is possible to construct in polynomial time an accurate SSL classifier. However, % any computationally efficient supervised or unsupervised learning schemes, that separately use only the labeled or unlabeled data would fail. Our work highlights the provable benefits of combining labeled and unlabeled data for {classification and} feature selection in high dimensions. We present simulations that complement our theoretical analysis.
中摘: 半监督学习（Semi-Supervised Learning，SSL）的前提是结合标记数据和未标记数据能够显著提高模型的准确性。尽管在实践中取得了成功，但对SSL的理论理解仍然不完整。在本文中，我们研究了SSL在高维稀疏高斯分类中的应用。要构建一个准确的分类器，关键任务是特征选择，即检测出能够区分两类变量的少数几个变量。对于这种SSL设置，我们分析了准确特征选择的信息论下界以及计算复杂性下界，假设低阶多项式似然性难题猜想成立。我们的主要贡献是在问题参数（维度、稀疏性、标记样本和未标记样本的数量）的某个范围内，证明SSL在分类中是有优势的。具体来说，在这个范围内，有可能在多项式时间内构造一个准确的SSL分类器。然而，任何仅使用标记数据或未标记数据的有效计算高效监督或无监督学习方案都会失败。我们的工作突显了在高维分类和特征选择中结合标记和未标记数据的可证明优势。我们还呈现了一些模拟实验来补充我们的理论分析。

[No.265] Overcoming Common Flaws in the Evaluation of Selective Classification Systems
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: classif
标题: 克服选择性分类系统评估中的常见缺陷
类别: evaluation
作者: Jeremias Traub;Till J. Bungert;Carsten T. Lüth;Michael Baumgartner;Klaus Maier-Hein;Lena Maier-hein;Paul F Jaeger;
机构: Deutsches Krebsforschungszentrum;German Cancer Research Center;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/96815
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.01032
英摘: Selective Classification, wherein models can reject low-confidence predictions, promises reliable translation of machine-learning based classification systems to real-world scenarios such as clinical diagnostics. While current evaluation of these systems typically assumes fixed working points based on pre-defined rejection thresholds, methodological progress requires benchmarking the general performance of systems akin to the $\mathrm{AUROC}$ in standard classification. In this work, we define 5 requirements for multi-threshold metrics in selective classification regarding task alignment, interpretability, and flexibility, and show how current approaches fail to meet them. We propose the Area under the Generalized Risk Coverage curve ($\mathrm{AUGRC}$), which meets all requirements and can be directly interpreted as the average risk of undetected failures. We empirically demonstrate the relevance of $\mathrm{AUGRC}$ on a comprehensive benchmark spanning 6 data sets and 13 confidence scoring functions. We find that the proposed metric substantially changes metric rankings on 5 out of the 6 data sets.
中摘: 选择性分类是一种模型可以在低置信度预测上拒绝的方法，它允诺将基于机器学习的分类系统可靠地转化为临床诊断等实际应用场景。当前对这些系统的评估通常假设固定的工作点，基于预定义的拒绝阈值，但方法学的进步需要像标准分类中的AUROC那样对标记系统的整体性能进行基准测试。在本文中，我们定义了选择性分类中多阈值指标的5项要求，涉及任务对齐、可解释性和灵活性，并展示了当前方法未能满足这些要求。我们提出了广义风险覆盖曲线下的面积（AUGRC）这一指标，该指标满足所有要求，并可以直接解释为未检测失败的平均风险。我们在涵盖6个数据集和13种置信度评分函数的综合基准上实证展示了AUGRC的相关性。我们发现，所提出的指标在6个数据集中的5个数据集上显著改变了指标排名。

[No.268] Fast Rates for Bandit PAC Multiclass Classification
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: classif
标题: 快速率下的带宽PAC多分类学习
类别: bandits
作者: Liad Erez;Alon Cohen;Tomer Koren;Yishay Mansour;Shay Moran;
机构: Tel Aviv University;Google;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/96490
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.12406
英摘: We study multiclass PAC learning with bandit feedback, where inputs are classified into one of $K$ possible labels and feedback is limited to whether or not the predicted labels are correct. Our main contribution is in designing a novel learning algorithm for the agnostic $(\varepsilon,δ)$-PAC version of the problem, with sample complexity of $O\big( (\operatorname{poly}(K) + 1 / \varepsilon^2) \log (|H| / δ) \big)$ for any finite hypothesis class $H$. In terms of the leading dependence on $\varepsilon$, this improves upon existing bounds for the problem, that are of the form $O(K/\varepsilon^2)$. We also provide an extension of this result to general classes and establish similar sample complexity bounds in which $\log |H|$ is replaced by the Natarajan dimension. This matches the optimal rate in the full-information version of the problem and resolves an open question studied by Daniely, Sabato, Ben-David, and Shalev-Shwartz (2011) who demonstrated that the multiplicative price of bandit feedback in realizable PAC learning is $Θ(K)$. We complement this by revealing a stark contrast with the agnostic case, where the price of bandit feedback is only $O(1)$ as $\varepsilon \to 0$. Our algorithm utilizes a stochastic optimization technique to minimize a log-barrier potential based on Frank-Wolfe updates for computing a low-variance exploration distribution over the hypotheses, and is made computationally efficient provided access to an ERM oracle over $H$.
中摘: 我们研究多类PAC学习中的带宽反馈问题，其中输入被分类为$K$个可能标签之一，并且反馈仅限于预测标签是否正确。我们的主要贡献在于设计了一种新的算法来解决无偏的$(\varepsilon, \delta)$-PAC版本的问题，该算法对于任何有限假设类$H$，其样本复杂度为$O\big( (\operatorname{poly}(K) + 1 / \varepsilon^2) \log (|H| / \delta) \big)$。在$\varepsilon$的主要依赖项方面，这一结果改进了现有问题的界，后者的形式为$O(K / \varepsilon^2)$。此外，我们还将这一结果扩展到一般类，并建立了类似的样本复杂度界，在这些界中，$\log |H|$被Natarajan维数所取代。这与信息完整版本的问题最优速率相匹配，并解决了Daniely、Sabato、Ben-David和Shalev-Shwartz（2011年）研究的一个开放问题，他们证明了在可实现的PAC学习中，带宽反馈的乘性价格为$Θ(K)$。相反，我们展示了在无偏情况下，随着$\varepsilon$趋近于0，带宽反馈的价格仅为$O(1)$。我们的算法利用了随机优化技术来最小化基于Frank-Wolfe更新计算低方差探索分布的对数障碍潜力，并且在可以访问$H$上的ERM或acles的情况下，该算法具有计算效率。

[No.269] Classification Diffusion Models: Revitalizing Density Ratio Estimation
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: classif
标题: 分类扩散模型：重振密度比率估计
类别: generative models
作者: Shahar Yadin;Noam Elata;Tomer Michaeli;
机构: Technion - Israel Institute of Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/94362
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.10095
英摘: A prominent family of methods for learning data distributions relies on density ratio estimation (DRE), where a model is trained to $\textit{classify}$ between data samples and samples from some reference distribution. DRE-based models can directly output the likelihood for any given input, a highly desired property that is lacking in most generative techniques. Nevertheless, to date, DRE methods have failed in accurately capturing the distributions of complex high-dimensional data, like images, and have thus been drawing reduced research attention in recent years. In this work we present $\textit{classification diffusion models}$ (CDMs), a DRE-based generative method that adopts the formalism of denoising diffusion models (DDMs) while making use of a classifier that predicts the level of noise added to a clean signal. Our method is based on an analytical connection that we derive between the MSE-optimal denoiser for removing white Gaussian noise and the cross-entropy-optimal classifier for predicting the noise level. Our method is the first DRE-based technique that can successfully generate images beyond the MNIST dataset. Furthermore, it can output the likelihood of any input in a single forward pass, achieving state-of-the-art negative log likelihood (NLL) among methods with this property. Code is available on the project's webpage in https://shaharYadin.github.io/CDM/ .
中摘: 一种广泛使用的方法家族依赖于密度比率估计（DRE），通过训练一个模型来对数据样本和某些参考分布的样本进行分类。基于DRE的模型可以直接输出任何给定输入的概率，这是大多数生成技术所缺乏的高度 desirable 的特性。尽管如此，迄今为止，DRE方法在准确捕捉复杂高维数据（如图像）的分布方面仍存在困难，并因此近年来吸引了较少的研究关注。在本文中，我们提出了分类扩散模型（CDMs），这是一种采用去噪扩散模型（DDMs）形式主义的生成方法，同时利用一个预测干净信号中添加噪声水平的分类器。我们的方法基于我们推导出的一个分析连接，该连接将去除白高斯噪声的MSE最优去噪器与预测噪声水平的交叉熵最优分类器联系起来。我们的方法是第一个能够在MNIST数据集之外成功生成图像的基于DRE的技术。此外，它可以在单次前向传递中输出任何输入的概率，实现了具有此类特性的方法中负对数似然（NLL）的最佳状态。项目代码可在网页https://shaharYadin.github.io/CDM/ 中获取。

[No.271] How many classifiers do we need?
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: classif
标题: 我们需要多少个分类器？
类别: learning theory
作者: Hyunsuk Kim;Liam Hodgkinson;Ryan Theisen;Michael W. Mahoney;
机构: University of California, Berkeley;University of Melbourne;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/93775
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.00328
英摘: As performance gains through scaling data and/or model size experience diminishing returns, it is becoming increasingly popular to turn to ensembling, where the predictions of multiple models are combined to improve accuracy. In this paper, we provide a detailed analysis of how the disagreement and the polarization (a notion we introduce and define in this paper) among classifiers relate to the performance gain achieved by aggregating individual classifiers, for majority vote strategies in classification tasks. We address these questions in the following ways. (1) An upper bound for polarization is derived, and we propose what we call a neural polarization law: most interpolating neural network models are 4/3-polarized. Our empirical results not only support this conjecture but also show that polarization is nearly constant for a dataset, regardless of hyperparameters or architectures of classifiers. (2) The error of the majority vote classifier is considered under restricted entropy conditions, and we present a tight upper bound that indicates that the disagreement is linearly correlated with the target, and that the slope is linear in the polarization. (3) We prove results for the asymptotic behavior of the disagreement in terms of the number of classifiers, which we show can help in predicting the performance for a larger number of classifiers from that of a smaller number. Our theories and claims are supported by empirical results on several image classification tasks with various types of neural networks.
中摘: 随着通过扩展数据和/或模型规模所获得的性能提升逐渐呈现递减效应，将多个模型的预测结果进行组合以提高准确性的集成方法越来越受欢迎。在本文中，我们详细分析了分类器之间分歧和极化（我们在本文中引入并定义的一个概念）与通过聚合个体分类器所实现的性能提升之间的关系，特别是在分类任务中的多数投票策略。我们从以下几个方面来回答这些问题：(1) 我们推导出了极化的上界，并提出了所谓的神经极化定律：大多数插值神经网络模型是4/3极化的。我们的实证结果不仅支持这一猜想，还表明极化对于一个数据集而言几乎是不变的，无论分类器的超参数或架构如何。(2) 在受限熵条件下考虑多数投票分类器的错误率，并给出了一个紧致的上界，表明分歧与目标之间呈线性相关，且斜率与极化呈线性关系。(3) 我们证明了关于分类器数量对分歧渐近行为的结果，这表明极化可以有助于预测大量分类器的表现，从少量分类器的表现中进行预测。我们的理论和主张得到了多种类型神经网络在若干图像分类任务上的实证结果的支持。

[No.272] Advancing Fine-Grained Classification by Structure and Subject Preserving Augmentation
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: classif
标题: 基于结构和主题保真的细粒度分类增强方法
类别: machine vision
作者: Eyal Michaeli;Ohad Fried;
机构: Reichman University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/95527
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.14551
英摘: Fine-grained visual classification (FGVC) involves classifying closely related sub-classes. This task is difficult due to the subtle differences between classes and the high intra-class variance. Moreover, FGVC datasets are typically small and challenging to gather, thus highlighting a significant need for effective data augmentation. Recent advancements in text-to-image diffusion models offer new possibilities for augmenting classification datasets. While these models have been used to generate training data for classification tasks, their effectiveness in full-dataset training of FGVC models remains under-explored. Recent techniques that rely on Text2Image generation or Img2Img methods, often struggle to generate images that accurately represent the class while modifying them to a degree that significantly increases the dataset's diversity. To address these challenges, we present SaSPA: Structure and Subject Preserving Augmentation. Contrary to recent methods, our method does not use real images as guidance, thereby increasing generation flexibility and promoting greater diversity. To ensure accurate class representation, we employ conditioning mechanisms, specifically by conditioning on image edges and subject representation. We conduct extensive experiments and benchmark SaSPA against both traditional and recent generative data augmentation methods. SaSPA consistently outperforms all established baselines across multiple settings, including full dataset training, contextual bias, and few-shot classification. Additionally, our results reveal interesting patterns in using synthetic data for FGVC models; for instance, we find a relationship between the amount of real data used and the optimal proportion of synthetic data. Code is available at https://github.com/EyalMichaeli/SaSPA-Aug.
中摘: 细粒度视觉分类（FGVC）涉及对密切相关的子类进行分类。由于类之间的微妙差异和高类别内方差，这一任务非常困难。此外，FGVC数据集通常规模较小且难以收集，因此突显了有效数据增强的重要需求。近期在文本到图像扩散模型方面的进展为增强分类数据集提供了新的可能性。虽然这些模型已被用于生成分类任务的训练数据，但它们在FGVC模型全数据集训练中的有效性仍然未被充分探索。依赖于Text2Image生成或Img2Img方法的近期技术往往难以同时准确地代表类别并显著增加数据集的多样性。为应对这些挑战，我们提出了SaSPA：结构和主题保持增强。与近期的方法不同，我们的方法不使用真实图像作为指导，从而增加了生成的灵活性，并促进了更大的多样性。为了确保准确的类别表示，我们采用了条件机制，具体来说，通过基于图像边缘和主题表示进行条件化。我们在广泛的实验中将SaSPA与传统的以及近期的生成数据增强方法进行了基准测试。SaSPA在多个场景下，包括全数据集训练、上下文偏差和少样本分类中，始终优于所有已建立的基础模型。此外，我们的结果揭示了合成数据在FGVC模型中使用的有趣模式；例如，我们发现真实数据量与合成数据的最佳比例之间存在关系。相关代码可在https://github.com/EyalMichaeli/SaSPA-Aug获取。

[No.273] FAST: A Dual-tier Few-Shot Learning Paradigm for Whole Slide Image Classification
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: classif
标题: 快速分类：一种双层少样本学习框架用于全切片图像分类
类别: machine vision
作者: Kexue Fu;xiaoyuan Luo;Linhao Qu;Shuo Wang;Ying Xiong;Ilias Maglogiannis;Longxiang Gao;Manning Wang;
机构: Qilu University of Technology;Fudan University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/96289
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.19720
英摘: The expensive fine-grained annotation and data scarcity have become the primary obstacles for the widespread adoption of deep learning-based Whole Slide Images (WSI) classification algorithms in clinical practice. Unlike few-shot learning methods in natural images that can leverage the labels of each image, existing few-shot WSI classification methods only utilize a small number of fine-grained labels or weakly supervised slide labels for training in order to avoid expensive fine-grained annotation. They lack sufficient mining of available WSIs, severely limiting WSI classification performance. To address the above issues, we propose a novel and efficient dual-tier few-shot learning paradigm for WSI classification, named FAST. FAST consists of a dual-level annotation strategy and a dual-branch classification framework. Firstly, to avoid expensive fine-grained annotation, we collect a very small number of WSIs at the slide level, and annotate an extremely small number of patches. Then, to fully mining the available WSIs, we use all the patches and available patch labels to build a cache branch, which utilizes the labeled patches to learn the labels of unlabeled patches and through knowledge retrieval for patch classification. In addition to the cache branch, we also construct a prior branch that includes learnable prompt vectors, using the text encoder of visual-language models for patch classification. Finally, we integrate the results from both branches to achieve WSI classification. Extensive experiments on binary and multi-class datasets demonstrate that our proposed method significantly surpasses existing few-shot classification methods and approaches the accuracy of fully supervised methods with only 0.22$\%$ annotation costs. All codes and models will be publicly available on https://github.com/fukexue/FAST.
中摘: 基于深度学习的全切片图像（Whole Slide Images, WSI）分类算法在临床实践中的广泛应用受到了精细标注成本高昂和数据稀缺性的主要制约。与自然图像中的少样本学习方法可以利用每张图像的标签不同，现有的少样本WSI分类方法仅利用少量精细标签或弱监督切片标签进行训练，以避免昂贵的精细标注。这些方法缺乏对可用WSI的充分挖掘，严重限制了WSI分类性能。为了解决上述问题，我们提出了一种新的高效双层少样本学习范式，名为FAST。FAST由双层标注策略和双分支分类框架组成。首先，为了避开昂贵的精细标注，我们在切片级别收集非常少量的WSI，并标注极少量的patches。然后，为了充分挖掘可用的WSI，我们使用所有patches和可用的patch标签构建缓存分支，该分支利用已标注的patches来学习未标注patches的标签并通过知识检索来进行patch分类。除了缓存分支外，我们还构建了一个先验分支，其中包括可学习的提示向量，使用视觉语言模型的文本编码器来进行patch分类。最后，我们将两个分支的结果整合起来实现WSI分类。在二分类和多分类数据集上的广泛实验表明，我们提出的方法显著超越了现有的少样本分类方法，并且仅需0.22%的标注成本就能接近完全监督方法的准确性。所有代码和模型将在https://github.com/fukexue/FAST公开发布。

[No.274] Towards Multi-dimensional Explanation Alignment for Medical Classification
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: classif
标题: 面向多维度解释对齐的医疗分类
类别: machine learning for healthcare
作者: Lijie Hu;Songning Lai;Wenshuo Chen;Hongru Xiao;Hongbin Lin;Lu Yu;Jingfeng Zhang;Di Wang;
机构: King Abdullah University of Science and Technology;Shandong University;Tongji University;Hong Kong University of Science and Technology;Ant Group;University of Auckland;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/96768
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.21494
英摘: The lack of interpretability in the field of medical image analysis has significant ethical and legal implications. Existing interpretable methods in this domain encounter several challenges, including dependency on specific models, difficulties in understanding and visualization, as well as issues related to efficiency. To address these limitations, we propose a novel framework called Med-MICN (Medical Multi-dimensional Interpretable Concept Network). Med-MICN provides interpretability alignment for various angles, including neural symbolic reasoning, concept semantics, and saliency maps, which are superior to current interpretable methods. Its advantages include high prediction accuracy, interpretability across multiple dimensions, and automation through an end-to-end concept labeling process that reduces the need for extensive human training effort when working with new datasets. To demonstrate the effectiveness and interpretability of Med-MICN, we apply it to four benchmark datasets and compare it with baselines. The results clearly demonstrate the superior performance and interpretability of our Med-MICN.
中摘: 医学图像分析领域缺乏可解释性具有重大的伦理和法律意义。现有的一些可解释方法在这个领域遇到了几个挑战，包括对特定模型的依赖性、理解和可视化困难，以及效率问题。为了解决这些限制，我们提出了一种名为Med-MICN（医学多维度可解释概念网络）的新框架。Med-MICN在神经符号推理、概念语义和显著图等多个角度提供了可解释性对齐，其性能优于当前的可解释方法。它的优点包括高预测准确性、多维度的可解释性以及通过端到端的概念标签化过程实现自动化，从而减少处理新数据集时需要进行大量的人工训练工作。为了展示Med-MICN的有效性和可解释性，我们将其应用于四个基准数据集，并与基线方法进行了比较。结果显示，我们的Med-MICN在性能和可解释性方面明显更优。

[No.275] Strategic Littlestone Dimension: Improved Bounds on Online Strategic Classification
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: classif
标题: 战略Littlestone维数：改进的在线战略分类界值

英摘: We study the problem of online binary classification in settings where strategic agents can modify their observable features to receive a positive classification. [No.279] Detecting Brittle Decisions for Free: Leveraging Margin Consistency in Deep Robust Classifiers
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: classif
标题: 免费检测脆弱决策：利用深度鲁棒分类器中的边际一致性
类别: machine vision
作者: Jonas Ngnawe;Sabyasachi Sahoo;Yann Batiste Pequignot;Frederic Precioso;Christian Gagné;
机构: Mila-Quebec AI Institute;Mila;Université Laval;Université de Nice-Sophia Antipolis;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/94783
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.18451
英摘: Despite extensive research on adversarial training strategies to improve robustness, the decisions of even the most robust deep learning models can still be quite sensitive to imperceptible perturbations, creating serious risks when deploying them for high-stakes real-world applications. While detecting such cases may be critical, evaluating a model's vulnerability at a per-instance level using adversarial attacks is computationally too intensive and unsuitable for real-time deployment scenarios. The input space margin is the exact score to detect non-robust samples and is intractable for deep neural networks. This paper introduces the concept of margin consistency -- a property that links the input space margins and the logit margins in robust models -- for efficient detection of vulnerable samples. First, we establish that margin consistency is a necessary and sufficient condition to use a model's logit margin as a score for identifying non-robust samples. Next, through comprehensive empirical analysis of various robustly trained models on CIFAR10 and CIFAR100 datasets, we show that they indicate high margin consistency with a strong correlation between their input space margins and the logit margins. Then, we show that we can effectively and confidently use the logit margin to detect brittle decisions with such models. Finally, we address cases where the model is not sufficiently margin-consistent by learning a pseudo-margin from the feature representation. Our findings highlight the potential of leveraging deep representations to assess adversarial vulnerability in deployment scenarios efficiently.
中摘: 尽管在提高鲁棒性方面进行了广泛的研究，即使是最为鲁棒的深度学习模型在面对微小不可感知的扰动时，其决策仍然可能非常敏感，这在将其部署到高风险的实际应用中时会带来严重风险。虽然检测这类情况可能是至关重要的，但在实际应用场景中使用对抗攻击来评估模型在单个实例级别的脆弱性会过于耗费计算资源，不适合实时部署的需求。输入空间边缘是检测非鲁棒样本的确切得分，但对深度神经网络来说是难以处理的。本文引入了鲁棒模型中的边缘一致性这一概念——一种将输入空间边缘和逻辑边缘联系起来的性质——以实现对脆弱样本的有效检测。首先，我们证明了边缘一致性是使用模型的逻辑边缘作为识别非鲁棒样本得分的必要且充分条件。接着，通过在CIFAR10和CIFAR100数据集上对各种鲁棒训练模型进行全面的经验分析，我们展示了这些模型具有高度的边缘一致性，并且它们的输入空间边缘与逻辑边缘之间存在强烈的关联性。然后，我们表明可以有效地且自信地使用逻辑边缘来检测这些模型中的脆弱决策。最后，对于那些不足够边缘一致性的模型，我们通过从特征表示中学习伪边缘来应对这种情况。我们的研究结果突显了利用深层表示高效评估部署场景中的对抗脆弱性的潜力。

[No.283] Bandit-Feedback Online Multiclass Classification: Variants and Tradeoffs
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: classif
标题:  bandit反馈在线多类分类：变体与权衡
类别: online learning
作者: Yuval Filmus;Steve Hanneke;Idan Mehalel;Shay Moran;
机构: Technion - Israel Institute of Technology;Purdue University;Technion-Israel Institute of Technology;Google;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/96335
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.07453
英摘: Consider the domain of multiclass classification within the adversarial online setting. What is the price of relying on bandit feedback as opposed to full information? To what extent can an adaptive adversary amplify the loss compared to an oblivious one? To what extent can a randomized learner reduce the loss compared to a deterministic one? We study these questions in the mistake bound model and provide nearly tight answers.
  We demonstrate that the optimal mistake bound under bandit feedback is at most $O(k)$ times higher than the optimal mistake bound in the full information case, where $k$ represents the number of labels. This bound is tight and provides an answer to an open question previously posed and studied by Daniely and Helbertal ['13] and by Long ['17, '20], who focused on deterministic learners.
  Moreover, we present nearly optimal bounds of $\tildeΘ(k)$ on the gap between randomized and deterministic learners, as well as between adaptive and oblivious adversaries in the bandit feedback setting. This stands in contrast to the full information scenario, where adaptive and oblivious adversaries are equivalent, and the gap in mistake bounds between randomized and deterministic learners is a constant multiplicative factor of $2$.
  In addition, our results imply that in some cases the optimal randomized mistake bound is approximately the square-root of its deterministic parallel. Previous results show that this is essentially the smallest it can get.
中摘: 在对抗在线设置下的多类分类领域，依赖于bandit反馈相比于full information反馈会付出什么样的代价？适应性对手能够将损失放大到非适应性对手的程度的什么程度？随机化学习者能够将损失减少到确定性学习者的什么程度？我们在这类问题的错误界模型中进行了研究，并提供了几乎精确的答案。

我们证明，在bandit反馈情况下，最优的错误界最多是full information情况下最优错误界的$O(k)$倍，这里的$k$表示标签的数量。这个界限是紧致的，并且回答了Daniely和Helbertal（2013年）以及Long（2017年、2020年）之前提出并研究的问题，他们主要关注的是确定性学习者。

此外，我们在bandit反馈设置中给出了随机化学习者与确定性学习者之间，以及适应性对手与非适应性对手之间几乎最优的界限$\tildeΘ(k)$。这与full information场景形成对比，在full information场景中，适应性和非适应性对手是等价的，而随机化学习者与确定性学习者之间的错误界差距是一个常数倍因子2。

另外，我们的结果还表明，在某些情况下，最优的随机化错误界大约是其确定性平行版本的平方根。之前的成果显示，这几乎是可能达到的最小值。

[No.284] Building a stable classifier with the inflated argmax
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: classif
标题: 使用膨胀argmax构建一个稳定的分类器
类别: learning theory
作者: Jake A Soloff;Rina Foygel Barber;Rebecca Willett;
机构: University of Chicago;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/95544
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.14064
英摘: We propose a new framework for algorithmic stability in the context of multiclass classification. In practice, classification algorithms often operate by first assigning a continuous score (for instance, an estimated probability) to each possible label, then taking the maximizer -- i.e., selecting the class that has the highest score. A drawback of this type of approach is that it is inherently unstable, meaning that it is very sensitive to slight perturbations of the training data, since taking the maximizer is discontinuous. Motivated by this challenge, we propose a pipeline for constructing stable classifiers from data, using bagging (i.e., resampling and averaging) to produce stable continuous scores, and then using a stable relaxation of argmax, which we call the "inflated argmax," to convert these scores to a set of candidate labels. The resulting stability guarantee places no distributional assumptions on the data, does not depend on the number of classes or dimensionality of the covariates, and holds for any base classifier. Using a common benchmark data set, we demonstrate that the inflated argmax provides necessary protection against unstable classifiers, without loss of accuracy.
中摘: 我们提出了一种在多类分类背景下算法稳定性的新框架。实际上，分类算法通常首先为每个可能的标签分配一个连续得分（例如，估计的概率），然后选择得分最高的类别。这种类型的方法的一个缺点是它本质上是不稳定的，意味着它对训练数据的微小扰动非常敏感，因为选择得分最高的类别是一个不连续的过程。受这一挑战的启发，我们提出了一种从数据构建稳定分类器的管道，使用袋装抽样和平均值生成稳定的连续得分，并随后使用一种稳定的argmax的松弛版本，即“膨胀argmax”，将这些得分转换为候选标签集。这种稳定性保证不对数据分布做出假设，不依赖于类别的数量或协变量的维度，并适用于任何基础分类器。通过一个常用的基准数据集，我们证明了膨胀argmax能够提供必要的保护，防止不稳定分类器的影响，同时不会损失准确性。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.285] Interpretable Image Classification with Adaptive Prototype-based Vision Transformers
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: classif
标题: 自解释图像分类的自适应原型视觉变换器
类别: interpretability and explainability
作者: Chiyu Ma;Jon Donnelly;Wenjun Liu;Soroush Vosoughi;Cynthia Rudin;Chaofan Chen;

英摘: We present ProtoViT, a method for interpretable image classification combining deep learning and case-based reasoning. In our model, a prototype consists of \textit{parts}, which can deform over irregular geometries to create a better comparison between images. 中摘: 我们提出了一种名为ProtoViT的方法，将深度学习和案例推理相结合进行可解释的图像分类。该方法通过将图像与一组学习原型进行比较来进行分类，并提供类似于“这看起来像那”的解释。在我们的模型中，一个原型由多个部分组成，这些部分可以适应不规则的几何形状，从而更好地进行图像比较。与现有的依赖于卷积神经网络（CNN）骨干和空间刚性原型的模型不同，我们的模型将视觉变换器（ViT）骨干整合到基于原型的模型中，同时提供了空间变形的原型，不仅能够适应物体的几何变化，还能够以自适应的原型部分数量提供一致且清晰的原型特征表示。我们的实验表明，我们的模型通常可以比现有的基于原型的模型实现更高的性能。全面的分析确保了原型的一致性和解释的忠实性。

[No.286] Con4m: Context-aware Consistency Learning Framework for Segmented Time Series Classification
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: classif
标题: 基于上下文感知一致性的分割时间序列分类框架
类别: machine learning for other sciences and fields
作者: Junru Chen;Tianyu Cao;Jing Xu;Jiahe Li;Zhilong Chen;Tao Xiao;Yang Yang;
机构: Zhejiang University;State Grid Corporation of China;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/93973

英摘: Time Series Classification (TSC) encompasses two settings: classifying entire sequences or classifying segmented subsequences. The raw time series for segmented TSC usually contain Multiple classes with Varying Duration of each class (MVD). Therefore, the characteristics of MVD pose unique challenges for segmented TSC, yet have been largely overlooked by existing works. Specifically, there exists a natural temporal dependency between consecutive instances (segments) to be classified within MVD. However, mainstream TSC models rely on the assumption of independent and identically distributed (i.i.d.), focusing on independently modeling each segment. Additionally, annotators with varying expertise may provide inconsistent boundary labels, leading to unstable performance of noise-free TSC models. To address these challenges, we first formally demonstrate that valuable contextual information enhances the discriminative power of classification instances. Leveraging the contextual priors of MVD at both the data and label levels, we propose a novel consistency learning framework Con4m, which effectively utilizes contextual information more conducive to discriminating consecutive segments in segmented TSC tasks, while harmonizing inconsistent boundary labels for training. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets validate the effectiveness of Con4m in handling segmented TSC tasks on MVD. The source code is available at https://github.com/MrNobodyCali/Con4m.
中摘: 时间序列分类（TSC）包括两种设置：对整个序列进行分类或对分割子序列进行分类。对于分割时间序列分类（MVD），原始时间序列通常包含多个具有不同持续时间的类。因此，MVD的特点为分割TSC带来了独特的挑战，而这些挑战在现有研究中大多被忽视了。具体来说，在MVD中，需要分类的连续实例（片段）之间存在自然的时间依赖性。然而，主流的TSC模型依赖于独立且同分布（i.i.d.）的假设，专注于独立建模每个片段。此外，不同专业水平的注释员可能会提供不一致的边界标签，导致无噪声TSC模型的性能不稳定。为了应对这些挑战，我们首先正式证明有价值的上下文信息能够增强分类实例的辨别能力。利用MVD在数据和标签两个层面的上下文先验，我们提出了一种新颖的一致性学习框架Con4m，该框架有效利用了更有利于区分连续片段的上下文信息，并且通过协调不一致的边界标签来改进训练效果。多项实验在多个数据集上验证了Con4m在处理MVD的分割TSC任务方面的有效性。源代码可在https://github.com/MrNobodyCali/Con4m获取。

[No.288] Classifier Clustering and Feature Alignment for Federated Learning under Distributed Concept Drift
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: classif
标题: 联邦学习下的分布式概念漂移分类器聚类与特征对齐
类别: optimization for deep networks
作者: Junbao Chen;Jingfeng Xue;Yong Wang;Zhenyan Liu;Lu Huang;
机构: Beijing Institute of Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/96509
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.18478
英摘: Data heterogeneity is one of the key challenges in federated learning, and many efforts have been devoted to tackling this problem. However, distributed concept drift with data heterogeneity, where clients may additionally experience different concept drifts, is a largely unexplored area. In this work, we focus on real drift, where the conditional distribution $P(Y|X)$ changes. We first study how distributed concept drift affects the model training and find that local classifier plays a critical role in drift adaptation. Moreover, to address data heterogeneity, we study the feature alignment under distributed concept drift, and find two factors that are crucial for feature alignment: the conditional distribution $P(Y|X)$ and the degree of data heterogeneity. Motivated by the above findings, we propose FedCCFA, a federated learning framework with classifier clustering and feature alignment. To enhance collaboration under distributed concept drift, FedCCFA clusters local classifiers at class-level and generates clustered feature anchors according to the clustering results. Assisted by these anchors, FedCCFA adaptively aligns clients' feature spaces based on the entropy of label distribution $P(Y)$, alleviating the inconsistency in feature space. Our results demonstrate that FedCCFA significantly outperforms existing methods under various concept drift settings. Code is available at https://github.com/Chen-Junbao/FedCCFA.
中摘: 数据异质性是联邦学习中的一个关键挑战，已经投入了大量努力来解决这一问题。然而，在具有数据异质性的分布式概念漂移场景中，客户端可能还会经历不同的概念漂移，这是一个尚未充分探索的领域。在本文中，我们专注于实际概念漂移，即条件分布 $P(Y|X)$ 的变化。首先，我们研究了分布式概念漂移如何影响模型训练，并发现局部分类器在漂移适应中起着关键作用。此外，为了应对数据异质性，我们研究了分布式概念漂移下的特征对齐，并发现两个关键因素：条件分布 $P(Y|X)$ 和数据异质性的程度。受上述发现的启发，我们提出了FedCCFA，这是一种带有分类器聚类和特征对齐的联邦学习框架。为了在分布式概念漂移下增强合作，FedCCFA 在类级别聚类局部分类器，并根据聚类结果生成聚类特征锚点。借助这些锚点，FedCCFA 根据标签分布的熵 $P(Y)$ 自适应地对齐客户端的特征空间，缓解了特征空间的一致性问题。我们的实验结果表明，FedCCFA 在各种概念漂移设置下显著优于现有方法。代码可在 https://github.com/Chen-Junbao/FedCCFA 获取。

英摘: Convolutional residual neural networks (ConvResNets), though overparameterized, can achieve remarkable prediction performance in practice, which cannot be well explained by conventional wisdom. To bridge this gap, we study the performance of ConvResNeXts, which cover ConvResNets as a special case, trained with weight decay from the perspective of nonparametric classification. 英摘: Customizing diffusion models to generate identity-preserving images from user-provided reference images is an intriguing new problem. To address this issue, we exploit classifier guidance, a training-free technique that steers diffusion models using an existing classifier, for personalized image generation. [No.291] DiffAug: A Diffuse-and-Denoise Augmentation for Training Robust Classifiers
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: classif
标题: DiffAug：一种扩散和去噪增强方法用于训练鲁棒分类器
类别: diffusion based models
作者: Chandramouli Shama Sastry;Sri Harsha Dumpala;Sageev Oore;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.09192
英摘: We introduce DiffAug, a simple and efficient diffusion-based augmentation technique to train image classifiers for the crucial yet challenging goal of improved classifier robustness. Applying DiffAug to a given example consists of one forward-diffusion step followed by one reverse-diffusion step. Using both ResNet-50 and Vision Transformer architectures, we comprehensively evaluate classifiers trained with DiffAug and demonstrate the surprising effectiveness of single-step reverse diffusion in improving robustness to covariate shifts, certified adversarial accuracy and out of distribution detection. [No.294] Fast Graph Sharpness-Aware Minimization for Enhancing and Accelerating Few-Shot Node Classification
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: classif
标题: 快速图形锐化感知最小化方法用于增强和加速少-shot节点分类
类别: graph neural networks
作者: Yihong Luo;Yuhan Chen;Siya Qiu;Yiwei Wang;Chen Zhang;Yan Zhou;Xiaochun Cao;Jing Tang;
机构: Hong Kong University of Science and Technology;Sun Yat-sen University;University of California, Los Angeles;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/96271
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.16845
英摘: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have shown superior performance in node classification. However, GNNs perform poorly in the Few-Shot Node Classification (FSNC) task that requires robust generalization to make accurate predictions for unseen classes with limited labels. To tackle the challenge, we propose the integration of Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM)--a technique designed to enhance model generalization by finding a flat minimum of the loss landscape--into GNN training. The standard SAM approach, however, consists of two forward-backward steps in each training iteration, doubling the computational cost compared to the base optimizer (e.g., Adam). To mitigate this drawback, we introduce a novel algorithm, Fast Graph Sharpness-Aware Minimization (FGSAM), that integrates the rapid training of Multi-Layer Perceptrons (MLPs) with the superior performance of GNNs. Specifically, we utilize GNNs for parameter perturbation while employing MLPs to minimize the perturbed loss so that we can find a flat minimum with good generalization more efficiently. Moreover, our method reutilizes the gradient from the perturbation phase to incorporate graph topology into the minimization process at almost zero additional cost. To further enhance training efficiency, we develop FGSAM+ that executes exact perturbations periodically. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our proposed algorithm outperforms the standard SAM with lower computational costs in FSNC tasks. In particular, our FGSAM+ as a SAM variant offers a faster optimization than the base optimizer in most cases. In addition to FSNC, our proposed methods also demonstrate competitive performance in the standard node classification task for heterophilic graphs, highlighting the broad applicability. The code is available at https://github.com/draym28/FGSAM_NeurIPS24.
中摘: 图神经网络（GNNs）在节点分类任务中表现出优越的性能。然而，在需要在有限标签的情况下对未见过的类别进行准确预测以实现鲁棒泛化的少量样本节点分类（FSNC）任务中，GNNs表现不佳。为了解决这一挑战，我们提出将Sharpness-Aware Minimization（SAM）——一种旨在通过找到损失景观的平坦最小值来增强模型泛化能力的技术——集成到GNN训练中。标准的SAM方法每次训练迭代包含两个前向-后向步骤，与基础优化器（例如Adam）相比，计算成本翻倍。为了缓解这一缺点，我们引入了一种新型算法——快速图Sharpness-Aware Minimization（FGSAM），该算法结合了多层感知机（MLPs）的快速训练能力和GNNs的卓越性能。具体而言，我们利用GNNs进行参数扰动，同时使用MLPs最小化扰动后的损失，以便更高效地找到具有良好泛化能力的平坦最小值。此外，我们的方法在扰动阶段重新利用梯度，几乎不增加额外的成本，将图拓扑结构纳入最小化过程。为进一步提高训练效率，我们开发了FGSAM+，该算法周期性地执行精确扰动。大量实验表明，我们在FSNC任务中提出的算法在计算成本较低的情况下优于标准的SAM方法。特别是，我们的FGSAM+作为一种SAM变体，在大多数情况下比基础优化器提供了更快的优化。除了FSNC任务，我们提出的方法也在异质图的标准节点分类任务中表现出竞争力，突显了其广泛的应用性。代码可在https://github.com/draym28/FGSAM_NeurIPS24获取。
