[No.295] Bayesian Strategic Classification
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: classif
标题: 贝叶斯战略分类
类别: algorithmic game theory
作者: Lee Cohen;Saeed Sharifi -Malvajerdi;Kevin Stangl;Ali Vakilian;Juba Ziani;
机构: Stanford University;Toyota Technological Institute at Chicago;Georgia Institute of Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/95094
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.08758
英摘: In strategic classification, agents modify their features, at a cost, to ideally obtain a positive classification from the learner's classifier. The typical response of the learner is to carefully modify their classifier to be robust to such strategic behavior. When reasoning about agent manipulations, most papers that study strategic classification rely on the following strong assumption: agents fully know the exact parameters of the deployed classifier by the learner. This often is an unrealistic assumption when using complex or proprietary machine learning techniques in real-world prediction tasks.
  We initiate the study of partial information release by the learner in strategic classification. We move away from the traditional assumption that agents have full knowledge of the classifier. Instead, we consider agents that have a common distributional prior on which classifier the learner is using. The learner in our model can reveal truthful, yet not necessarily complete, information about the deployed classifier to the agents. The learner's goal is to release just enough information about the classifier to maximize accuracy. We show how such partial information release can, counter-intuitively, benefit the learner's accuracy, despite increasing agents' abilities to manipulate.
  We show that while it is intractable to compute the best response of an agent in the general case, there exist oracle-efficient algorithms that can solve the best response of the agents when the learner's hypothesis class is the class of linear classifiers, or when the agents' cost function satisfies a natural notion of submodularity as we define. We then turn our attention to the learner's optimization problem and provide both positive and negative results on the algorithmic problem of how much information the learner should release about the classifier to maximize their expected accuracy.
中摘: 在战略分类中，代理会以一定的成本修改其特征，以便从学习者的分类器中获得正面分类。通常，学习者会对这种战略行为进行仔细调整，使自己的分类器更加稳健。在考虑代理的操作时，大多数研究战略分类的论文都依赖于以下强烈假设：代理完全了解学习者部署的分类器的确切参数。然而，在使用复杂或专有机器学习技术进行实际预测任务时，这一假设往往是不现实的。

我们开始了关于学习者在战略分类中部分信息释放的研究。我们不再假设代理完全了解分类器的情况，而是考虑代理拥有学习者所使用的分类器的共同分布先验。在我们的模型中，学习者可以向代理透露有关部署分类器的真实但未必完整的信息。学习者的目的是恰当地透露关于分类器的信息以最大化准确性。我们展示了在这种情况下，即使增加了代理操纵的能力，部分信息释放反而可能意外地提高学习者的准确性。

我们证明，在一般情况下计算代理的最佳响应是不可解的，但在特定条件下，例如当学习者的假设类为线性分类器类，或者当代理的成本函数满足我们定义的自然子模性时，存在高效的算法可以解决代理的最佳响应问题。然后我们转向学习者优化问题，并提供了关于学习者应透露多少关于分类器的信息以最大化其预期准确性的算法问题的正反面结果。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

[No.299] Abstracted Shapes as Tokens - A Generalizable and Interpretable Model for Time-series Classification
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: classif
标题: 抽象形状作为标记——一种适用于时间序列分类的一般化和可解释模型
类别: interpretability and explainability
作者: Yunshi Wen;Tengfei Ma;Tsui-Wei Weng;Lam M. Nguyen;Anak Agung Julius;
机构: Rensselaer Polytechnic Institute;State University of New York at Stony Brook;University of California, San Diego;IBM;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.01006
英摘: In time-series analysis, many recent works seek to provide a unified view and representation for time-series across multiple domains, leading to the development of foundation models for time-series data. Despite diverse modeling techniques, existing models are black boxes and fail to provide insights and explanations about their representations. In this paper, we present VQShape, a pre-trained, generalizable, and interpretable model for time-series representation learning and classification. By introducing a novel representation for time-series data, we forge a connection between the latent space of VQShape and shape-level features. Using vector quantization, we show that time-series from different domains can be described using a unified set of low-dimensional codes, where each code can be represented as an abstracted shape in the time domain. On classification tasks, we show that the representations of VQShape can be utilized to build interpretable classifiers, achieving comparable performance to specialist models. Additionally, in zero-shot learning, VQShape and its codebook can generalize to previously unseen datasets and domains that are not included in the pre-training process. The code and pre-trained weights are available at https://github.com/YunshiWen/VQShape.

[No.300] Curriculum Fine-tuning of Vision Foundation Model for Medical Image Classification Under Label Noise
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: classif
标题: 基于标签噪声下的视觉基础模型在医学图像分类中的课程微调
类别: machine learning for healthcare
作者: Yeonguk Yu;Minhwan Ko;Sungho Shin;Kangmin Kim;Kyoobin Lee;
机构: Gwangju Institute of Science and Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/93198
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.00150
英摘: Deep neural networks have demonstrated remarkable performance in various vision tasks, but their success heavily depends on the quality of the training data. Noisy labels are a critical issue in medical datasets and can significantly degrade model performance. Previous clean sample selection methods have not utilized the well pre-trained features of vision foundation models (VFMs) and assumed that training begins from scratch. In this paper, we propose CUFIT, a curriculum fine-tuning paradigm of VFMs for medical image classification under label noise. Our method is motivated by the fact that linear probing of VFMs is relatively unaffected by noisy samples, as it does not update the feature extractor of the VFM, thus robustly classifying the training samples. Subsequently, curriculum fine-tuning of two adapters is conducted, starting with clean sample selection from the linear probing phase. Our experimental results demonstrate that CUFIT outperforms previous methods across various medical image benchmarks. Specifically, our method surpasses previous baselines by 5.0%, 2.1%, 4.6%, and 5.8% at a 40% noise rate on the HAM10000, APTOS-2019, BloodMnist, and OrgancMnist datasets, respectively. Furthermore, we provide extensive analyses to demonstrate the impact of our method on noisy label detection. For instance, our method shows higher label precision and recall compared to previous approaches. Our work highlights the potential of leveraging VFMs in medical image classification under challenging conditions of noisy labels.
中摘: 深度神经网络在各种视觉任务中展现了卓越的表现，但它们的成功很大程度上依赖于训练数据的质量。标签噪声是医学数据集中的一个关键问题，会显著降低模型性能。之前的清洁样本选择方法并未利用视觉基础模型（VFMs）的良好预训练特征，并且假设训练是从头开始的。在本文中，我们提出了一种名为CUFIT的方法，这是一种在标签噪声条件下用于医学图像分类的VFMs Curriculum Fine-Tuning范式。我们的方法受到启发的事实是，在线性探针阶段，VFMs相对不受噪声样本的影响，因为它不会更新VFMs的特征提取器，从而能够稳健地对训练样本进行分类。随后，我们从线性探针阶段开始进行两个适配器的Curriculum Fine-Tuning，首先选择清洁样本。我们的实验结果表明，CUFIT在各种医学图像基准测试中优于先前的方法。具体而言，与HAM10000、APTOS-2019、BloodMnist和OrgancMnist数据集上的先前基线相比，我们的方法分别在40%的噪声率下提高了5.0%、2.1%、4.6%和5.8%的性能。此外，我们进行了广泛的分析以展示我们的方法对标签噪声检测的影响。例如，我们的方法在标签精度和召回率方面优于之前的方案。我们的工作突显了在标签噪声条件下的挑战性环境中利用VFMs进行医学图像分类的潜力。

[No.302] Enhancing Consistency-Based Image Generation via Adversarialy-Trained Classification and Energy-Based Discrimination
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: classif
标题: 基于一致性增强的图像生成通过对抗训练的分类和能量基鉴别
类别: generative models
作者: Shelly Golan;Roy Ganz;Michael Elad;
机构: Technion - Israel Institute of Technology;Verily;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/93288
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.16260
英摘: The recently introduced Consistency models pose an efficient alternative to diffusion algorithms, enabling rapid and good quality image synthesis. These methods overcome the slowness of diffusion models by directly mapping noise to data, while maintaining a (relatively) simpler training. Consistency models enable a fast one- or few-step generation, but they typically fall somewhat short in sample quality when compared to their diffusion origins. In this work we propose a novel and highly effective technique for post-processing Consistency-based generated images, enhancing their perceptual quality. Our approach utilizes a joint classifier-discriminator model, in which both portions are trained adversarially. While the classifier aims to grade an image based on its assignment to a designated class, the discriminator portion of the very same network leverages the softmax values to assess the proximity of the input image to the targeted data manifold, thereby serving as an Energy-based Model. By employing example-specific projected gradient iterations under the guidance of this joint machine, we refine synthesized images and achieve an improved FID scores on the ImageNet 64x64 dataset for both Consistency-Training and Consistency-Distillation techniques.
中摘: 最近引入的一致性模型为替代扩散算法提供了一种高效的选择，能够快速且高质量地生成图像。这些方法通过直接将噪声映射到数据上克服了扩散模型的缓慢问题，同时保持相对简单的训练过程。一致性模型允许快速的一步或多步生成，但与它们的扩散起源相比，通常在样本质量方面略逊一筹。在这项工作中，我们提出了一种新颖而有效的后处理技术，用于提升基于一致性生成的图像的感知质量。我们的方法利用了一个联合分类器-判别器模型，在该模型中，两部分都进行了对抗训练。分类器旨在根据图像所属的指定类别对其进行评分，而网络的判别器部分则利用softmax值评估输入图像与目标数据流形的接近程度，从而充当能量模型。通过在这种联合机器的指导下进行示例特定的投影梯度迭代，我们对合成图像进行了细化，并在ImageNet 64x64数据集上实现了改进的FID分数，无论是一致性训练还是一致性蒸馏技术。

[No.303] Hierarchical Selective Classification
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: classif
标题: 层次选择性分类

英摘: Deploying deep neural networks for risk-sensitive tasks necessitates an uncertainty estimation mechanism. This paper introduces hierarchical selective classification, extending selective classification to a hierarchical setting. [No.304] Smoothed Online Classification can be Harder than Batch Classification
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: classif
标题: 平滑在线分类可能比批量分类更复杂
类别: online learning
作者: Vinod Raman;Unique Subedi;Ambuj Tewari;
机构: Apple;University of Michigan;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/95442
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.15424
英摘: We study online classification under smoothed adversaries. In this setting, at each time point, the adversary draws an example from a distribution that has a bounded density with respect to a fixed base measure, which is known apriori to the learner. For binary classification and scalar-valued regression, previous works \citep{haghtalab2020smoothed, block2022smoothed} have shown that smoothed online learning is as easy as learning in the iid batch setting under PAC model. However, we show that smoothed online classification can be harder than the iid batch classification when the label space is unbounded. In particular, we construct a hypothesis class that is learnable in the iid batch setting under the PAC model but is not learnable under the smoothed online model. Finally, we identify a condition that ensures that the PAC learnability of a hypothesis class is sufficient for its smoothed online learnability.
中摘: 我们在平滑对手的情况下研究在线分类问题。在这种设置下，每个时间点，对手从一个相对于固定基测度具有有界密度的分布中抽取一个样本，而学习者事先知道这个基测度。对于二元分类和标量回归，以往的研究[1]表明，平滑在线学习与在PAC模型下的独立同分布批次学习一样容易。然而，我们证明，在标签空间未被限定的情况下，平滑在线分类可能比独立同分布批次分类更难。特别是，我们构造了一个在PAC模型下可以在独立同分布批次设置中学习的假设类，但在平滑在线模型中却无法学习。最后，我们确定了一个条件，该条件确保假设类的PAC可学习性足以保证其平滑在线可学习性。

[No.305] Graph Classification via Reference Distribution Learning: Theory and Practice
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: classif
标题: 通过参考分布学习进行图分类：理论与实践
类别: graph neural networks
作者: Zixiao Wang;Jicong Fan;
机构: Chinese University of Hong Kong;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/96839
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.11370
英摘: Graph classification is a challenging problem owing to the difficulty in quantifying the similarity between graphs or representing graphs as vectors, though there have been a few methods using graph kernels or graph neural networks (GNNs). Graph kernels often suffer from computational costs and manual feature engineering, while GNNs commonly utilize global pooling operations, risking the loss of structural or semantic information. This work introduces Graph Reference Distribution Learning (GRDL), an efficient and accurate graph classification method. GRDL treats each graph's latent node embeddings given by GNN layers as a discrete distribution, enabling direct classification without global pooling, based on maximum mean discrepancy to adaptively learned reference distributions. To fully understand this new model (the existing theories do not apply) and guide its configuration (e.g., network architecture, references' sizes, number, and regularization) for practical use, we derive generalization error bounds for GRDL and verify them numerically. More importantly, our theoretical and numerical results both show that GRDL has a stronger generalization ability than GNNs with global pooling operations. Experiments on moderate-scale and large-scale graph datasets show the superiority of GRDL over the state-of-the-art, emphasizing its remarkable efficiency, being at least 10 times faster than leading competitors in both training and inference stages.
中摘: 图分类是一个具有挑战性的问题，原因在于量化图之间的相似性或将图表示为向量的难度较大，尽管已经有一些方法使用图核或图神经网络（GNNs）。图核通常会面临计算成本高和手动特征工程的问题，而GNNs则常用全局聚合操作，这可能会导致结构或语义信息的丢失。本研究引入了图参考分布学习（GRDL），这是一种高效且准确的图分类方法。GRDL 将每个图由GNN层给出的潜在节点嵌入视为一个离散分布，从而可以直接进行分类，无需全局聚合操作，并基于最大均值偏差来适应性地学习参考分布。为了完全理解这一新模型（现有理论不适用）并指导其配置（例如，网络架构、参考大小、数量以及正则化等）以实际应用，我们推导了GRDL 的泛化误差界，并通过数值验证了这些边界。更重要的是，我们的理论和数值结果都表明，GRDL 的泛化能力比带有全局聚合操作的GNNs 更强。在中等规模和大规模图数据集上的实验显示，GRDL 在训练和推理阶段的速度至少比领先竞争对手快10倍，突显了其显著的效率优势。

[No.306] Listenable Maps for Zero-Shot Audio Classifiers
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: classif
标题: 零样本音频分类器的可监听映射
类别: interpretability and explainability
作者: Francesco Paissan;Luca Della Libera;Mirco Ravanelli;Cem Subakan;
机构: Fondazione Bruno Kessler;Meta;Concordia University;University of Montreal;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/93828
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.17615
英摘: Interpreting the decisions of deep learning models, including audio classifiers, is crucial for ensuring the transparency and trustworthiness of this technology. In this paper, we introduce LMAC-ZS (Listenable Maps for Audio Classifiers in the Zero-Shot context), which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first decoder-based post-hoc interpretation method for explaining the decisions of zero-shot audio classifiers. The proposed method utilizes a novel loss function that maximizes the faithfulness to the original similarity between a given text-and-audio pair. We provide an extensive evaluation using the Contrastive Language-Audio Pretraining (CLAP) model to showcase that our interpreter remains faithful to the decisions in a zero-shot classification context. Moreover, we qualitatively show that our method produces meaningful explanations that correlate well with different text prompts.
中摘: 理解深度学习模型（包括音频分类器）的决策对于确保这项技术的透明性和可信度至关重要。在本文中，我们介绍了LMAC-ZS（Listenable Maps for Audio Classifiers in the Zero-Shot context），据我们所知，这是首个基于解码器的后验解释方法，用于解释零样本音频分类器的决策。所提出的方法利用了一个新颖的损失函数，该函数最大化了给定文本-音频对原始相似性的忠实度。我们通过使用对比语言-音频预训练（CLAP）模型进行了广泛评估，展示了我们的解释器在零样本分类上下文中仍然忠实于决策。此外，我们从定性的角度证明，我们的方法生成了有意义的解释，这些解释与不同的文本提示相关性良好。

[No.308] Classification Done Right for Vision-Language Pre-Training

英摘: We introduce SuperClass, a super simple classification method for vision-language pre-training on image-text data. [No.309] Neuronal Competition Groups with Supervised STDP for Spike-Based Classification
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: classif
标题: 基于神经元竞争的监督STDP突触可塑性用于 spike 基础分类
类别: other
作者: Gaspard Goupy;Pierre Tirilly;Ioan Marius Bilasco;
机构: Université de Lille;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/95890
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.17066
英摘: Spike Timing-Dependent Plasticity (STDP) is a promising substitute to backpropagation for local training of Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) on neuromorphic hardware. STDP allows SNNs to address classification tasks by combining unsupervised STDP for feature extraction and supervised STDP for classification. Unsupervised STDP is usually employed with Winner-Takes-All (WTA) competition to learn distinct patterns. However, WTA for supervised STDP classification faces unbalanced competition challenges. In this paper, we propose a method to effectively implement WTA competition in a spiking classification layer employing first-spike coding and supervised STDP training. We introduce the Neuronal Competition Group (NCG), an architecture that improves classification capabilities by promoting the learning of various patterns per class. An NCG is a group of neurons mapped to a specific class, implementing intra-class WTA and a novel competition regulation mechanism based on two-compartment thresholds. We incorporate our proposed architecture into spiking classification layers trained with state-of-the-art supervised STDP rules. On top of two different unsupervised feature extractors, we obtain significant accuracy improvements on image recognition datasets such as CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100. We show that our competition regulation mechanism is crucial for ensuring balanced competition and improved class separation.
中摘:  spike时间依赖可塑性（STDP）是神经形态硬件上局部训练脉冲神经网络（SNNs）的一个有前景的替代方法，用于反向传播。STDP通过结合无监督STDP进行特征提取和监督STDP进行分类，允许SNNs解决分类任务。无监督STDP通常与Winner-Takes-All（WTA）竞争结合使用，以学习不同的模式。然而，监督STDP分类中的WTA竞争面临不平衡的竞争挑战。在本文中，我们提出了一种方法，在使用第一尖峰编码和监督STDP训练的脉冲分类层中有效实现WTA竞争。我们引入了神经元竞争组（NCG），这是一种通过促进每类各种模式的学习来提高分类能力的架构。一个NCG是一组映射到特定类别的神经元，实现了同类别WTA和基于两室阈值的新颖竞争调节机制。我们将提出的架构整合到使用最先进的监督STDP规则训练的脉冲分类层中。在两种不同类型的无监督特征提取器之上，我们在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100等图像识别数据集上获得了显著的准确率提升。我们展示了我们的竞争调节机制对于确保竞争平衡和提高类间区分度至关重要。

[No.310] RankUp: Boosting Semi-Supervised Regression with an Auxiliary Ranking Classifier
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: classif
标题: RankUp：利用辅助排名分类器提升半监督回归性能
类别: other

英摘: State-of-the-art (SOTA) semi-supervised learning techniques, such as FixMatch and it's variants, have demonstrated impressive performance in classification tasks. However, these methods are not directly applicable to regression tasks. In this paper, we present RankUp, a simple yet effective approach that adapts existing semi-supervised classification techniques to enhance the performance of regression tasks. RankUp achieves this by converting the original regression task into a ranking problem and training it concurrently with the original regression objective. This auxiliary ranking classifier outputs a classification result, thus enabling integration with existing semi-supervised classification methods. Moreover, we introduce regression distribution alignment (RDA), a complementary technique that further enhances RankUp's performance by refining pseudo-labels through distribution alignment. Despite its simplicity, RankUp, with or without RDA, achieves SOTA results in across a range of regression benchmarks, including computer vision, audio, and natural language processing tasks. 中摘: 最先进的半监督学习技术（如FixMatch及其变种）在分类任务中展示了令人印象深刻的性能。然而，这些方法并不直接适用于回归任务。在本文中，我们提出了一种名为RankUp的简单而有效的方法，该方法将现有的半监督分类技术适应于增强回归任务的表现。RankUp通过将原始回归任务转换为排名问题，并与原始回归目标同时进行训练来实现这一点。该辅助排名分类器输出分类结果，从而能够与现有的半监督分类方法集成。此外，我们引入了回归分布对齐（RDA），这是一种补充技术，通过分布对齐进一步优化伪标签，从而进一步提升RankUp的性能。尽管结构简单，RankUp（无论是有还是没有RDA）在包括计算机视觉、音频和自然语言处理在内的多种回归基准测试中均取得了最先进的结果。我们的代码和日志数据可在https://github.com/pm25/semi-supervised-regression开源获取。

[No.312] Sm: enhanced localization in Multiple Instance Learning for medical imaging classification
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: classif
标题: Sm：在医学影像分类中的多实例学习增强局部化
类别: machine learning for healthcare
作者: Francisco M Castro-Macías;Pablo Morales-Alvarez;Yunan Wu;Rafael Molina;Aggelos Katsaggelos;
机构: Northwestern University;Universidad de Granada;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/94011
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.03276
英摘: Multiple Instance Learning (MIL) is widely used in medical imaging classification to reduce the labeling effort. While only bag labels are available for training, one typically seeks predictions at both bag and instance levels (classification and localization tasks, respectively). Early MIL methods treated the instances in a bag independently. Recent methods account for global and local dependencies among instances. Although they have yielded excellent results in classification, their performance in terms of localization is comparatively limited. We argue that these models have been designed to target the classification task, while implications at the instance level have not been deeply investigated. Motivated by a simple observation -- that neighboring instances are likely to have the same label -- we propose a novel, principled, and flexible mechanism to model local dependencies. It can be used alone or combined with any mechanism to model global dependencies (e.g., transformers). A thorough empirical validation shows that our module leads to state-of-the-art performance in localization while being competitive or superior in classification. Our code is at https://github.com/Franblueee/SmMIL.
中摘: 多实例学习（MIL）在医学影像分类中广泛应用，以减少标注工作量。虽然在训练时只有袋子标签可用，但通常需要在袋子和实例两个级别进行预测（分别对应分类任务和定位任务）。早期的MIL方法将袋子中的实例独立处理。近期的方法则考虑了实例之间的全局和局部依赖关系。尽管它们在分类任务上取得了出色的结果，但在定位方面的表现相对有限。我们认为这些模型主要是为了解决分类任务而设计的，而实例级别的影响尚未被深入研究。受一个简单的观察——相邻实例很可能具有相同的标签——的启发，我们提出了一种新颖、原则性和灵活的机制来建模局部依赖关系。该机制可以单独使用，也可以与任何建模全局依赖关系的机制（例如变压器）结合使用。详尽的经验验证表明，我们的模块在定位方面达到了最先进的性能，同时在分类方面也具有竞争力或更优的表现。我们的代码位于https://github.com/Franblueee/SmMIL。

[No.313] Amnesia as a Catalyst for Enhancing Black Box Pixel Attacks in Image Classification and Object Detection
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: classif
标题: 记忆丧失作为增强黑盒像素攻击在图像分类和目标检测中的催化剂
类别: machine vision
作者: Dongsu Song;Daehwa Ko;Jay Hoon Jung;
机构: Korea Aerospace University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/95435
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.07821
英摘: It is well known that query-based attacks tend to have relatively higher success rates in adversarial black-box attacks. While research on black-box attacks is actively being conducted, relatively few studies have focused on pixel attacks that target only a limited number of pixels. In image classification, query-based pixel attacks often rely on patches, which heavily depend on randomness and neglect the fact that scattered pixels are more suitable for adversarial attacks. Moreover, to the best of our knowledge, query-based pixel attacks have not been explored in the field of object detection. To address these issues, we propose a novel pixel-based black-box attack called Remember and Forget Pixel Attack using Reinforcement Learning(RFPAR), consisting of two main components: the Remember and Forget processes. RFPAR mitigates randomness and avoids patch dependency by leveraging rewards generated through a one-step RL algorithm to perturb pixels. RFPAR effectively creates perturbed images that minimize the confidence scores while adhering to limited pixel constraints. Furthermore, we advance our proposed attack beyond image classification to object detection, where RFPAR reduces the confidence scores of detected objects to avoid detection. Experiments on the ImageNet-1K dataset for classification show that RFPAR outperformed state-of-the-art query-based pixel attacks. For object detection, using the MSCOCO dataset with YOLOv8 and DDQ, RFPAR demonstrates comparable mAP reduction to state-of-the-art query-based attack while requiring fewer query. Further experiments on the Argoverse dataset using YOLOv8 confirm that RFPAR effectively removed objects on a larger scale dataset. Our code is available at https://github.com/KAU-QuantumAILab/RFPAR.
中摘: 众所周知，基于查询的攻击在对抗性黑盒攻击中通常具有相对较高的成功率。尽管黑盒攻击的研究正在积极进行，但相对较少的研究集中在仅针对少量像素的像素攻击上。在图像分类中，基于查询的像素攻击往往依赖于补丁，这严重依赖于随机性，并忽略了分散的像素对于对抗攻击更为合适的事实。此外，据我们所知，基于查询的像素攻击尚未在目标检测领域得到探索。为了解决这些问题，我们提出了一种名为Remember and Forget Pixel Attack using Reinforcement Learning (RFPAR) 的新型基于像素的黑盒攻击方法，该方法由两个主要组件组成：Remember和Forget过程。RFPAR通过利用一步强化学习算法生成的奖励来扰动像素，从而减轻随机性并避免补丁依赖。RFPAR有效地创建了最小化置信分数同时遵守有限像素约束的扰动图像。此外，我们将提出的攻击从图像分类推进到了目标检测领域，在目标检测中，RFPAR通过减少检测对象的置信分数来避免检测。实验表明，RFPAR在ImageNet-1K数据集上的分类性能优于最先进的基于查询的像素攻击。使用MSCOCO数据集和YOLOv8及DDQ进行目标检测实验时，RFPAR显示出与最先进的基于查询的攻击相当的mAP降低效果，但所需的查询次数更少。进一步使用Argoverse数据集和YOLOv8进行的实验也证实了RFPAR在更大规模的数据集上有效删除了对象。我们的代码可在https://github.com/KAU-QuantumAILab/RFPAR获取。

[No.314] Optimal Classification under Performative Distribution Shift
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: classif
标题: 最优分类下的表现性分布偏移分类
类别: optimization
作者: Edwige Cyffers;Muni Sreenivas Pydi;Jamal Atif;Olivier Cappé;
机构: INRIA;Université Paris Dauphine;Université Paris-Dauphine;Centre National de la Recherche Scientifique;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/96756
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.02023
英摘: Performative learning addresses the increasingly pervasive situations in which algorithmic decisions may induce changes in the data distribution as a consequence of their public deployment. We propose a novel view in which these performative effects are modelled as push-forward measures. This general framework encompasses existing models and enables novel performative gradient estimation methods, leading to more efficient and scalable learning strategies. For distribution shifts, unlike previous models which require full specification of the data distribution, we only assume knowledge of the shift operator that represents the performative changes. This approach can also be integrated into various change-of-variablebased models, such as VAEs or normalizing flows. Focusing on classification with a linear-in-parameters performative effect, we prove the convexity of the performative risk under a new set of assumptions. Notably, we do not limit the strength of performative effects but rather their direction, requiring only that classification becomes harder when deploying more accurate models. In this case, we also establish a connection with adversarially robust classification by reformulating the minimization of the performative risk as a min-max variational problem. Finally, we illustrate our approach on synthetic and real datasets.
中摘: 表演性学习关注的是随着算法决策在公共部署中的逐渐普及，这些决策可能会引起数据分布的变化。我们提出了一种新的观点，将这些表演性效果建模为推进测度。这种通用框架涵盖了现有的模型，并能够实现新颖的表演性梯度估计方法，从而导致更高效和可扩展的学习策略。对于数据分布变化的情况，不同于之前需要完全指定数据分布的模型，我们仅假设了解表示征表演性变化的转移算子。这种方法也可以整合到各种基于变量变换的模型中，例如VAEs或规范流中。专注于参数线性化的分类问题，我们在一组新假设下证明了表演性风险的凸性。值得注意的是，我们不限制表演性效果的强度，而是限制其方向，只需确保部署更准确的模型会使分类变得更困难。在这种情况下，我们还通过重新定义性能风险的最小化问题为一个极小极大变分问题，建立了与对抗鲁棒分类之间的联系。最后，我们在合成和真实数据集上展示了我们的方法。

[No.315] Classifier-guided Gradient Modulation for Enhanced Multimodal Learning
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: classif
标题: 基于分类器引导的梯度调制以增强多模态学习
类别: optimization for deep networks
作者: Zirun Guo;Tao Jin;Jingyuan Chen;Zhou Zhao;
机构: Zhejiang University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/93612
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.01409
英摘: Multimodal learning has developed very fast in recent years. However, during the multimodal training process, the model tends to rely on only one modality based on which it could learn faster, thus leading to inadequate use of other modalities. Existing methods to balance the training process always have some limitations on the loss functions, optimizers and the number of modalities and only consider modulating the magnitude of the gradients while ignoring the directions of the gradients. To solve these problems, in this paper, we present a novel method to balance multimodal learning with Classifier-Guided Gradient Modulation (CGGM), considering both the magnitude and directions of the gradients. We conduct extensive experiments on four multimodal datasets: UPMC-Food 101, CMU-MOSI, IEMOCAP and BraTS 2021, covering classification, regression and segmentation tasks. The results show that CGGM outperforms all the baselines and other state-of-the-art methods consistently, demonstrating its effectiveness and versatility. Our code is available at https://github.com/zrguo/CGGM.
中摘: 多模态学习在近年来发展非常迅速。然而，在多模态训练过程中，模型往往会依赖于一种能够更快学习的模态，从而导致其他模态的不足利用。现有的平衡训练过程的方法在损失函数、优化器和模态数量方面都存在一定局限性，并且仅考虑调节梯度的大小，而忽视了梯度的方向。为了解决这些问题，本文提出了一种名为Classifier-Guided Gradient Modulation (CGGM)的新方法，该方法同时考虑梯度的大小和方向来平衡多模态学习。我们在四个多模态数据集（UPMC-Food 101、CMU-MOSI、IEMOCAP 和 BraTS 2021）上进行了广泛的实验，涵盖了分类、回归和分割任务。实验结果显示，CGGM方法在所有基线和最先进的方法中表现始终优于它们，证明了其有效性和适用性。我们的代码可在https://github.com/zrguo/CGGM获取。

[No.316] Learning Low-Rank Feature for Thorax Disease Classification
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: classif
标题: 胸部疾病分类的低秩特征学习
类别: machine learning for healthcare
作者: Yancheng Wang;Rajeev Goel;Utkarsh Nath;Alvin C Silva;Teresa Wu;Yingzhen Yang;
机构: Arizona State University;Mayo Clinic;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/95883
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.18933
英摘: Deep neural networks, including Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) and Visual Transformers (ViT), have achieved stunning success in medical image domain. We study thorax disease classification in this paper. Effective extraction of features for the disease areas is crucial for disease classification on radiographic images. While various neural architectures and training techniques, such as self-supervised learning with contrastive/restorative learning, have been employed for disease classification on radiographic images, there are no principled methods which can effectively reduce the adverse effect of noise and background, or non-disease areas, on the radiographic images for disease classification. To address this challenge, we propose a novel Low-Rank Feature Learning (LRFL) method in this paper, which is universally applicable to the training of all neural networks. The LRFL method is both empirically motivated by the low frequency property observed on all the medical datasets in this paper, and theoretically motivated by our sharp generalization bound for neural networks with low-rank features. In the empirical study, using a neural network such as a ViT or a CNN pre-trained on unlabeled chest X-rays by Masked Autoencoders (MAE), our novel LRFL method is applied on the pre-trained neural network and demonstrate better classification results in terms of both multiclass area under the receiver operating curve (mAUC) and classification accuracy.
中摘: 深度神经网络，包括卷积神经网络（CNNs）和视觉变换器（ViT），在医学图像领域取得了令人瞩目的成功。本文研究了胸部疾病分类问题。在胸部疾病的放射影像分类中，有效提取病灶区域的特征至关重要。尽管各种神经架构和训练技术，如自监督学习中的对比学习/修复学习等，已被应用于放射影像的疾病分类，但目前尚无有效的原理方法能够减少噪声和背景或非病灶区域对放射影像分类的负面影响。为解决这一挑战，本文提出了一种新颖的低秩特征学习（LRFL）方法，该方法适用于所有神经网络的训练。LRFL方法既受到本文所研究的所有医学数据集中低频特性的启发，也基于我们对具有低秩特征的神经网络泛化边界的严格理论分析。在实证研究中，通过使用掩码自动编码器（MAE）预训练的ViT或CNN神经网络，我们的新型LRFL方法被应用于预训练的神经网络，并在多类接收操作特性曲线下的面积（mAUC）和分类准确性方面展示了更好的分类效果。

[No.317] Are nuclear masks all you need for improved out-of-domain generalisation? A closer look at cancer classification in histopathology
来源: NeurIPS 2024

作者: Dhananjay Tomar;Alexander Binder;Andreas Kleppe;

链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/96177
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.09373
英摘: Domain generalisation in computational histopathology is challenging because the images are substantially affected by differences among hospitals due to factors like fixation and staining of tissue and imaging equipment. We hypothesise that focusing on nuclei can improve the out-of-domain (OOD) generalisation in cancer detection. We propose a simple approach to improve OOD generalisation for cancer detection by focusing on nuclear morphology and organisation, as these are domain-invariant features critical in cancer detection. Our approach integrates original images with nuclear segmentation masks during training, encouraging the model to prioritise nuclei and their spatial arrangement. Going beyond mere data augmentation, we introduce a regularisation technique that aligns the representations of masks and original images. We show, using multiple datasets, that our method improves OOD generalisation and also leads to increased robustness to image corruptions and adversarial attacks. The source code is available at https://github.com/undercutspiky/SFL/
中摘: 在计算病理学中的领域泛化具有挑战性，因为图像会受到医院之间差异的影响，这些差异可能是由于组织固定和染色以及成像设备等因素造成的。我们假设关注核细胞可以提高癌症检测的跨域泛化能力。我们提出了一种简单的方法来通过关注核形态和组织来改进癌症检测的跨域泛化能力，因为这些特征是癌症检测中关键的领域不变特征。我们的方法在训练过程中将原始图像与核分割掩码结合起来，鼓励模型优先考虑核细胞及其空间排列。超越简单的数据增强，我们引入了一种正则化技术，使掩码和原始图像的表示相互对齐。我们使用多个数据集展示了该方法可以提高跨域泛化能力，并且还能增强模型对图像损坏和对抗攻击的鲁棒性。源代码可在https://github.com/undercutspiky/SFL/获取。

[No.318] Why are Visually-Grounded Language Models Bad at Image Classification?
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: classif
标题: 为什么视觉接地语言模型不擅长图像分类？
类别: interpretability and explainability
作者: Yuhui Zhang;Alyssa Unell;Xiaohan Wang;Dhruba Ghosh;Yuchang Su;Ludwig Schmidt;Serena Yeung-Levy;
机构: Stanford University;University of Washington;Tsinghua University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/95478
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.18415
英摘: Image classification is one of the most fundamental capabilities of machine vision intelligence. In this work, we revisit the image classification task using visually-grounded language models (VLMs) such as GPT-4V and LLaVA. We find that existing proprietary and public VLMs, despite often using CLIP as a vision encoder and having many more parameters, significantly underperform CLIP on standard image classification benchmarks like ImageNet. To understand the reason, we explore several hypotheses concerning the inference algorithms, training objectives, and data processing in VLMs. Our analysis reveals that the primary cause is data-related: critical information for image classification is encoded in the VLM's latent space but can only be effectively decoded with enough training data. Specifically, there is a strong correlation between the frequency of class exposure during VLM training and instruction-tuning and the VLM's performance in those classes; when trained with sufficient data, VLMs can match the accuracy of state-of-the-art classification models. Based on these findings, we enhance a VLM by integrating classification-focused datasets into its training, and demonstrate that the enhanced classification performance of the VLM transfers to its general capabilities, resulting in an improvement of 11.8% on the newly collected ImageWikiQA dataset.
中摘: 图像分类是机器视觉智能的一项最基本的能力。在这项工作中，我们重新审视了使用基于视觉的语言模型（VLMs）如GPT-4V和LLaVA进行图像分类任务的方法。我们发现，现有的专有和公开的VLMs，尽管通常使用CLIP作为视觉编码器，并且参数数量更多，但在标准图像分类基准测试（如ImageNet）上显著低于CLIP的表现。为了理解其中的原因，我们探索了几种关于VLMs推理算法、训练目标以及数据处理的假设。我们的分析揭示了主要原因是数据相关的：对于图像分类至关重要的信息被编码在VLM的潜在空间中，但只有通过足够的训练数据才能有效地解码这些信息。具体来说，图像分类中的类别曝光频率与VLM在训练和指令调优过程中的表现之间存在强烈的关联；当用足够的数据进行训练时，VLMs可以达到最先进的分类模型的准确性。基于这些发现，我们通过将分类聚焦的数据集整合到VLM的训练中来增强该模型，并证明增强后的图像分类性能也转移到了其通用能力上，在新收集的ImageWikiQA数据集上实现了11.8%的性能提升。

[No.319] Your Diffusion Model is Secretly a Noise Classifier and Benefits from Contrastive Training
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: classif
标题: 你的扩散模型实际上是噪声分类器，并且可以从对比训练中受益。
类别: diffusion based models
作者: Yunshu Wu;Yingtao Luo;Xianghao Kong;Evangelos E. Papalexakis;Greg Ver Steeg;
机构: University of California, Riverside;Carnegie Mellon University;University of Southern California;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/95188
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.08946
英摘: Diffusion models learn to denoise data and the trained denoiser is then used to generate new samples from the data distribution. In this paper, we revisit the diffusion sampling process and identify a fundamental cause of sample quality degradation: the denoiser is poorly estimated in regions that are far Outside Of the training Distribution (OOD), and the sampling process inevitably evaluates in these OOD regions. This can become problematic for all sampling methods, especially when we move to parallel sampling which requires us to initialize and update the entire sample trajectory of dynamics in parallel, leading to many OOD evaluations. To address this problem, we introduce a new self-supervised training objective that differentiates the levels of noise added to a sample, leading to improved OOD denoising performance. The approach is based on our observation that diffusion models implicitly define a log-likelihood ratio that distinguishes distributions with different amounts of noise, and this expression depends on denoiser performance outside the standard training distribution. We show by diverse experiments that the proposed contrastive diffusion training is effective for both sequential and parallel settings, and it improves the performance and speed of parallel samplers significantly.
中摘: 扩散模型学习去噪数据，并且训练好的去噪器随后用于从数据分布生成新的样本。在本文中，我们重新审视了扩散采样过程，并识别出样本质量下降的根本原因：去噪器在远离训练分布（OOD）的区域被不良估计，而采样过程不可避免地会在这些OOD区域进行评估。这可能会对所有采样方法产生问题，尤其是在转向并行采样时，需要并行初始化和更新整个动态样本轨迹，导致大量OOD评估。为了解决这个问题，我们引入了一个新的自监督训练目标，该目标区分添加到样本中的噪声水平，从而提高了OOD去噪性能。这种方法基于我们的观察，即扩散模型隐式定义了一个对数似然比，可以区分不同噪声量的分布，而这个表达式依赖于去噪器在标准训练分布之外的表现。通过多样化的实验，我们展示了提出的对比扩散训练对于顺序和并行设置都是有效的，并且显著提高了并行采样器的性能和速度。

[No.320] Confidence Calibration of Classifiers with Many Classes
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: classif
标题: 多类分类器的置信度校准
类别: safety in machine learning
作者: Adrien Le Coz;Stéphane Herbin;Faouzi Adjed;
机构: IRT SystemX;ONERA;SystemX;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/94258
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.02988
英摘: For classification models based on neural networks, the maximum predicted class probability is often used as a confidence score. This score rarely predicts well the probability of making a correct prediction and requires a post-processing calibration step. However, many confidence calibration methods fail for problems with many classes. To address this issue, we transform the problem of calibrating a multiclass classifier into calibrating a single surrogate binary classifier. This approach allows for more efficient use of standard calibration methods. We evaluate our approach on numerous neural networks used for image or text classification and show that it significantly enhances existing calibration methods.
中摘: 对于基于神经网络的分类模型，最大预测类别概率通常被用作置信度评分。这种评分很少能准确预测做出正确预测的概率，并且需要后续处理校准步骤。然而，许多置信度校准方法在多类问题上效果不佳。为了解决这个问题，我们将多类分类器的校准问题转化为校准单一的代理二元分类器的问题。这种方法允许更高效地使用标准校准方法。我们评估了该方法在用于图像或文本分类的众多神经网络上的表现，并展示了它能够显著提升现有的校准方法的效果。

[No.321] SSA-Seg: Semantic and Spatial Adaptive Pixel-level Classifier for Semantic Segmentation
来源: NeurIPS 2024
命中: classif
标题: SSA-Seg: 语义和空间自适应像素级分类器用于语义分割
类别: machine vision
作者: Xiaowen Ma;Zhen-Liang Ni;Xinghao Chen;
机构: Zhejiang University;Huawei;
类型: Poster
链接: https://neurips.cc/virtual/2024/poster/95163
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.06525
英摘: Vanilla pixel-level classifiers for semantic segmentation are based on a certain paradigm, involving the inner product of fixed prototypes obtained from the training set and pixel features in the test image. This approach, however, encounters significant limitations, \ie, feature deviation in the semantic domain and information loss in the spatial domain. The former struggles with large intra-class variance among pixel features from different images, while the latter fails to utilize the structured information of semantic objects effectively. This leads to blurred mask boundaries as well as a deficiency of fine-grained recognition capability. In this paper, we propose a novel Semantic and Spatial Adaptive Classifier (SSA-Seg) to address the above challenges. Specifically, we employ the coarse masks obtained from the fixed prototypes as a guide to adjust the fixed prototype towards the center of the semantic and spatial domains in the test image. The adapted prototypes in semantic and spatial domains are then simultaneously considered to accomplish classification decisions. In addition, we propose an online multi-domain distillation learning strategy to improve the adaption process. Experimental results on three publicly available benchmarks show that the proposed SSA-Seg significantly improves the segmentation performance of the baseline models with only a minimal increase in computational cost. Code is available at https://github.com/xwmaxwma/SSA-Seg.
中摘: 基于像素级的 vanilla 分类器在语义分割任务中遵循一定的范式，涉及将训练集中获得的固定原型与测试图像中的像素特征进行内积操作。然而，这种方法遇到了显著的局限性，即语义域中的特征偏差和空间域中的信息损失。前者难以处理来自不同图像的像素特征之间的大类内方差，后者则无法有效地利用语义对象的空间结构信息。这导致了分割掩码边界模糊以及细粒度识别能力不足的问题。在本文中，我们提出了一种新的语义和空间自适应分类器（SSA-Seg）来解决上述挑战。具体而言，我们利用固定原型从测试图像中获得的粗略掩码作为指导，调整固定原型以靠近语义和空间域的中心。然后，在语义和空间域中同时考虑自适应原型以完成分类决策。此外，我们还提出了一种在线多域蒸馏学习策略来提高自适应过程的效果。在三个公开可用的标准基准上的实验结果显示，所提出的 SSA-Seg 显著提高了基线模型的分割性能，并且仅增加了极小的计算成本。代码可在 https://github.com/xwmaxwma/SSA-Seg 获取。

[No.334] Near-Optimal Bounds for Learning Gaussian Halfspaces with Random Classification Noise
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: classif
标题: 近最优边界：学习具有随机分类噪声的高斯半空间
类别: 
作者: Ilias Diakonikolas;Jelena Diakonikolas;Daniel Kane;Puqian Wang;Nikos Zarifis;
机构: University of Wisconsin;University of California, San Diego;
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/71279
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2307.08438
英摘: We study the problem of learning general (i.e., not necessarily homogeneous) halfspaces with Random Classification Noise under the Gaussian distribution. We establish nearly-matching algorithmic and Statistical Query (SQ) lower bound results revealing a surprising information-computation gap for this basic problem. Specifically, the sample complexity of this learning problem is $\widetildeΘ(d/ε)$, where $d$ is the dimension and $ε$ is the excess error. Our positive result is a computationally efficient learning algorithm with sample complexity $\tilde{O}(d/ε+ d/(\max\{p, ε\})^2)$, where $p$ quantifies the bias of the target halfspace. On the lower bound side, we show that any efficient SQ algorithm (or low-degree test) for the problem requires sample complexity at least $Ω(d^{1/2}/(\max\{p, ε\})^2)$. Our lower bound suggests that this quadratic dependence on $1/ε$ is inherent for efficient algorithms.
中摘: 我们研究了在高斯分布下学习一般（即不一定同质）半空间问题，并在随机分类噪声条件下建立了几乎匹配的算法和统计查询（SQ）下界结果，揭示了这个基本问题中的一个令人惊讶的信息-计算差距。具体来说，这个问题的学习样本复杂度为$\widetildeΘ(d/ε)$，其中$d$是维度，$ε$是超额误差。我们的正面结果是一个计算效率高的学习算法，其样本复杂度为$\tilde{O}(d/ε + d/(\max\{p, ε\})^2)$，其中$p$量化了目标半空间的偏差。在下界方面，我们证明了任何高效的问题SQ算法（或低次多项式测试）都需要至少$Ω(d^{1/2}/(\max\{p, ε\})^2)$的样本复杂度。我们的下界表明，这种对$1/ε$的二次依赖性对于高效算法来说是固有的。

[No.335] RS-Del: Edit Distance Robustness Certificates for Sequence Classifiers via Randomized Deletion
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: classif
标题: RS-Del: 基于随机删除的序列分类器鲁棒性编辑距离证书
类别: 
作者: Zhuoqun Huang;Neil G Marchant;Keane Lucas;Lujo Bauer;Olga Ohrimenko;Benjamin I. P. Rubinstein;
机构: University of Melbourne;Carnegie Mellon University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/70896
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2302.01757
英摘: Randomized smoothing is a leading approach for constructing classifiers that are certifiably robust against adversarial examples. Existing work on randomized smoothing has focused on classifiers with continuous inputs, such as images, where $\ell_p$-norm bounded adversaries are commonly studied. However, there has been limited work for classifiers with discrete or variable-size inputs, such as for source code, which require different threat models and smoothing mechanisms. In this work, we adapt randomized smoothing for discrete sequence classifiers to provide certified robustness against edit distance-bounded adversaries. Our proposed smoothing mechanism randomized deletion (RS-Del) applies random deletion edits, which are (perhaps surprisingly) sufficient to confer robustness against adversarial deletion, insertion and substitution edits. Our proof of certification deviates from the established Neyman-Pearson approach, which is intractable in our setting, and is instead organized around longest common subsequences. We present a case study on malware detection--a binary classification problem on byte sequences where classifier evasion is a well-established threat model. When applied to the popular MalConv malware detection model, our smoothing mechanism RS-Del achieves a certified accuracy of 91% at an edit distance radius of 128 bytes.
中摘: 随机化平滑是一种构建对抗样本具有可验证鲁棒性的分类器的主要方法。现有的随机化平滑工作主要集中在连续输入的分类器上，例如图像，其中通常研究范数受限的对手。然而，对于离散或变长输入的分类器，如源代码，已有较少的研究，这些分类器需要不同的威胁模型和平滑机制。在本工作中，我们针对编辑距离受限的对手，将随机化平滑适应于离散序列分类器，以提供可验证的鲁棒性。我们提出的平滑机制随机删除（RS-Del）应用随机删除编辑，这些编辑（或许令人惊讶的是）足以赋予对抗删除、插入和替换编辑的鲁棒性。

我们的验证证明偏离了传统的Neyman-Pearson方法，在我们的设置中该方法难以实现，而是围绕最长公共子序列组织。我们对恶意软件检测进行了案例研究——一个基于字节序列的二元分类问题，其中分类器逃避是一个已建立的威胁模型。当应用于流行的MalConv恶意软件检测模型时，我们的平滑机制RS-Del在编辑距离半径为128字节的情况下实现了91%的可验证准确性。

[No.393] Does VLM Classification Benefit from LLM Description Semantics?
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: classif
标题: VLG分类是否受益于LLG描述语义？
类别: computer vision v
作者: Pingchuan Ma;Lennart Rietdorf;Dmytro Kotovenko;Vincent Tao Hu;Björn Ommer;
机构: Ludwig Maximilian University of Munich;Munich Center for Machine Learning;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/32638
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.11917
英摘: Accurately describing images with text is a foundation of explainable AI. Vision-Language Models (VLMs) like CLIP have recently addressed this by aligning images and texts in a shared embedding space, expressing semantic similarities between vision and language embeddings. VLM classification can be improved with descriptions generated by Large Language Models (LLMs). However, it is difficult to determine the contribution of actual description semantics, as the performance gain may also stem from a semantic-agnostic ensembling effect, where multiple modified text prompts act as a noisy test-time augmentation for the original one. We propose an alternative evaluation scenario to decide if a performance boost of LLM-generated descriptions is caused by such a noise augmentation effect or rather by genuine description semantics. The proposed scenario avoids noisy test-time augmentation and ensures that genuine, distinctive descriptions cause the performance boost. Furthermore, we propose a training-free method for selecting discriminative descriptions that work independently of classname-ensembling effects. Our approach identifies descriptions that effectively differentiate classes within a local CLIP label neighborhood, improving classification accuracy across seven datasets. Additionally, we provide insights into the explainability of description-based image classification with VLMs.
中摘: 用文本准确描述图像是一些可解释的人工智能的基础。视觉-语言模型（VLMs）如CLIP最近通过在共享嵌入空间中对齐图像和文本来解决这一问题，表达了视觉和语言嵌入之间的语义相似性。使用大型语言模型（LLMs）生成的描述可以提高VLM分类性能。然而，很难确定实际描述语义的贡献，因为性能提升也可能来自一种语义无关的集成效应，即多个修改后的文本提示作为原始提示的嘈杂测试时间增强。我们提出了一种替代评估场景，以决定LLM生成描述的性能提升是由这种噪声增强效应引起的，还是由真正的描述语义引起的。该提出的场景避免了嘈杂的测试时间增强，并确保真正、独特的描述能够引起性能提升。此外，我们还提出了一种无需训练的方法来选择具有区分性的描述，这些描述独立于类名集成效应。我们的方法识别出能够在局部CLIP标签邻域内有效区分类别的描述，从而在七个数据集中提高了分类准确性。另外，我们还探讨了使用VLM进行基于描述的图像分类的可解释性。

[No.394] Efficient Image-to-Image Diffusion Classifier for Adversarial Robustness
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: classif
标题: 高效图像到图像扩散分类器以提高对抗鲁棒性
类别: computer vision v
作者: Hefei Mei;Minjing Dong;Chang Xu;
机构: City University of Hong Kong;University of Sydney;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/32650
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.08502
英摘: Diffusion models (DMs) have demonstrated great potential in the field of adversarial robustness, where DM-based defense methods can achieve superior defense capability without adversarial training. However, they all require huge computational costs due to the usage of large-scale pre-trained DMs, making it difficult to conduct full evaluation under strong attacks and compare with traditional CNN-based methods. Simply reducing the network size and timesteps in DMs could significantly harm the image generation quality, which invalidates previous frameworks. To alleviate this issue, we redesign the diffusion framework from generating high-quality images to predicting distinguishable image labels. Specifically, we employ an image translation framework to learn many-to-one mapping from input samples to designed orthogonal image labels. Based on this framework, we introduce an efficient Image-to-Image diffusion classifier with a pruned U-Net structure and reduced diffusion timesteps. Besides the framework, we redesign the optimization objective of DMs to fit the target of image classification, where a new classification loss is incorporated in the DM-based image translation framework to distinguish the generated label from those of other classes. We conduct sufficient evaluations of the proposed classifier under various attacks on popular benchmarks. Extensive experiments show that our method achieves better adversarial robustness with fewer computational costs than DM-based and CNN-based methods. The code is available at https://github.com/hfmei/IDC
中摘: 扩散模型（DMs）在对抗鲁棒性领域展现了巨大的潜力，基于DM的方法可以在无需对抗训练的情况下实现卓越的防御能力。然而，由于使用了大规模预训练的DM，这些方法都面临着巨大的计算成本问题，使得在强攻击下进行全面评估变得困难，并且难以与传统的基于CNN的方法进行比较。简单地减少DM的网络规模和时间步数会显著损害图像生成质量，这使得之前的框架无效。为了解决这一问题，我们重新设计了扩散框架，从生成高质量图像转变为预测可区分的图像标签。具体来说，我们采用了一种图像转换框架，从输入样本到设计的正交图像标签学习多对一映射。在此基础上，我们引入了一种高效的图像到图像扩散分类器，其结构包括剪枝的U-Net和减少的时间步数。除了框架之外，我们还重新设计了DM的优化目标以适应图像分类的目标，在基于DM的图像转换框架中引入了一个新的分类损失函数，用于区分生成的标签与其他类的标签。我们在流行基准上的各种攻击下充分评估了所提出的分类器。广泛实验表明，我们的方法在较少的计算成本下实现了更好的对抗鲁棒性，优于基于DM和基于CNN的方法。代码可在https://github.com/hfmei/IDC获取。

英摘: Few-shot defect multi-classification (FSDMC) is an emerging trend in quality control within industrial manufacturing. However, current FSDMC research often lacks generalizability due to its focus on specific datasets. Additionally, defect classification heavily relies on contextual information within images, and existing methods fall short of effectively extracting this information. To address these challenges, we propose a general FSDMC framework called MVREC, which offers two primary advantages: (1) MVREC extracts general features for defect instances by incorporating the pre-trained AlphaCLIP model. (2) It utilizes a region-context framework to enhance defect features by leveraging mask region input and multi-view context augmentation. Furthermore, Few-shot Zip-Adapter(-F) classifiers within the model are introduced to cache the visual features of the support set and perform few-shot classification. We also introduce MVTec-FS, a new FSDMC benchmark based on MVTec AD, which includes 1228 defect images with instance-level mask annotations and 46 defect types. 中摘: 少样本缺陷多分类（Few-shot defect multi-classification, FSDMC）是工业制造领域质量控制中的一个新兴趋势。然而，当前的FSDMC研究往往缺乏普适性，因为它们主要集中在特定的数据集上。此外，缺陷分类高度依赖于图像内的上下文信息，而现有的方法在有效提取这些信息方面存在不足。为了解决这些问题，我们提出了一种通用的FSDMC框架，称为MVREC，该框架具有两个主要优势：(1) MVREC通过结合预训练的AlphaCLIP模型来提取缺陷实例的一般特征。(2) 它利用区域上下文框架通过利用掩码区域输入和多视图上下文增强来提升缺陷特征。此外，模型中引入了Few-shot Zip-Adapter(-F)分类器，用于缓存支持集的视觉特征并执行少样本分类。我们还基于MVTec AD引入了一个新的FSDMC基准MVTec-FS，其中包括1228张带有实例级掩码注释的缺陷图像和46种缺陷类型。在MVTec-FS和四个额外数据集上进行的广泛实验表明，MVREC在通用缺陷分类方面表现出有效性，并且能够通过整合上下文信息来提高分类性能。代码：https://github.com/ShuaiLYU/MVREC

[No.397] Common Sense Bias Modeling for Classification Tasks
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: classif
标题: 常识偏差建模在分类任务中的应用
类别: computer vision viii
作者: Miao Zhang;Zee Fryer;Ben Colman;Ali Shahriyari;Gaurav Bharaj;
机构: New York University;Reality Defender Inc.;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/33084
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.13213
英摘: Machine learning model bias can arise from dataset composition: correlated sensitive features can distort the downstream classification model's decision boundary and lead to performance differences along these features. Existing de-biasing works tackle the most prominent bias features, such as colors of digits or background of animals. However, real-world datasets often include a large number of feature correlations that intrinsically manifest in the data as common sense information. Such spurious visual cues can further reduce model robustness. Thus, domain practitioners desire a comprehensive understanding of correlations and the flexibility to address relevant biases. To this end, we propose a novel framework to extract comprehensive biases in image datasets based on textual descriptions, a common sense-rich modality. Specifically, features are constructed by clustering noun phrase embeddings with similar semantics. The presence of each feature across the dataset is inferred, and their co-occurrence statistics are measured, with spurious correlations optionally examined by a human-in-the-loop module. Downstream experiments show that our method uncovers novel model biases in multiple image benchmark datasets. Furthermore, the discovered bias can be mitigated by simple data re-weighting to de-correlate the features, outperforming state-of-the-art unsupervised bias mitigation methods.
中摘: 机器学习模型偏差可能源自数据集组成：相关敏感特征可以扭曲下游分类模型的决策边界，并在这些特征上导致性能差异。现有的去偏见工作主要针对显而易见的偏差特征，如数字的颜色或动物的背景。然而，现实世界的数据集通常包含大量的特征关联，这些关联在数据中以常识信息的形式内在地表现出来。这些虚假的视觉线索进一步降低了模型的鲁棒性。因此，领域专家希望全面理解这些关联并具备解决相关偏差的灵活性。为此，我们提出了一种新的框架，基于文本描述从图像数据集中提取全面的偏差，这是一种富含常识信息的模态。具体来说，特征通过聚类具有相似语义的名词短语嵌入来构建。每个特征在整个数据集中的存在情况被推断出来，并测量它们的共现统计信息，必要时可以通过人工介入模块来检查虚假的关联。下游实验表明，我们的方法在多个图像基准数据集中发现了新的模型偏差。此外，发现的偏差可以通过简单的数据重新加权来减轻，从而解关联特征，这种方法优于当前最先进的无监督偏差缓解方法。

[No.398] PointDGMamba: Domain Generalization of Point Cloud Classification via Generalized State Space Model
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: classif
标题: 点云分类的一般域泛化：通过广义状态空间模型实现领域一般化
类别: computer vision viii
作者: Hao Yang;Qianyu Zhou;Haijia Sun;Xiangtai Li;Fengqi Liu;Xuequan Lu;Lizhuang Ma;Shuicheng Yan;
机构: Shanghai Jiao Tong University;Nanjing University;Skywork AI;Nanyang Technological University;University of Western Australia;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/32995
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.13574
英摘: Domain Generalization (DG) has been recently explored to improve the generalizability of point cloud classification (PCC) models toward unseen domains. However, they often suffer from limited receptive fields or quadratic complexity due to using convolution neural networks or vision Transformers. In this paper, we present the first work that studies the generalizability of state space models (SSMs) in DG PCC and find that directly applying SSMs into DG PCC will encounter several challenges: the inherent topology of the point cloud tends to be disrupted and leads to noise accumulation during the serialization stage. Besides, the lack of designs in domain-agnostic feature learning and data scanning will introduce unanticipated domain-specific information into the 3D sequence data. To this end, we propose a novel framework, PointDGMamba, that excels in strong generalizability toward unseen domains and has the advantages of global receptive fields and efficient linear complexity. PointDGMamba consists of three innovative components: Masked Sequence Denoising (MSD), Sequence-wise Cross-domain Feature Aggregation (SCFA), and Dual-level Domain Scanning (DDS). In particular, MSD selectively masks out the noised point tokens of the point cloud sequences, SCFA introduces cross-domain but same-class point cloud features to encourage the model to learn how to extract more generalized features. DDS includes intra-domain scanning and cross-domain scanning to facilitate information exchange between features. In addition, we propose a new and more challenging benchmark PointDG-3to1 for multi-domain generalization. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness and state-of-the-art performance of PointDGMamba.
中摘: 域泛化（Domain Generalization, DG）最近被探索以提高点云分类（Point Cloud Classification, PCC）模型在未见域上的普适性。然而，这些方法往往由于使用卷积神经网络或视觉Transformer而导致有限的感受野或二次复杂度问题。在本文中，我们首次研究了状态空间模型（State Space Models, SSMs）在DG PCC中的普适性，并发现直接将SSMs应用于DG PCC会遇到几个挑战：点云固有的拓扑结构容易被破坏，在序列化阶段导致噪声积累。此外，缺乏无域特定特征学习和数据扫描的设计会导致意外的域特定信息进入三维序列数据。为此，我们提出了一种新颖的框架——PointDGMamba，它在未见域上的强普适性和全局感受野及高效的一次性复杂度方面具有优势。PointDGMamba由三个创新组件组成：掩码序列去噪（Masked Sequence Denoising, MSD）、序列级跨域特征聚合（Sequence-wise Cross-domain Feature Aggregation, SCFA）以及双层域扫描（Dual-level Domain Scanning, DDS）。特别是，MSD有选择地屏蔽点云序列中的噪声点，SCFA引入跨域但同类别点云特征，以鼓励模型学习提取更通用的特征。DDS包括域内扫描和跨域扫描，以促进特征之间的信息交流。此外，我们还提出了一个新的更具挑战性的多域泛化基准——PointDG-3to1。大量实验验证了PointDGMamba的有效性和最先进的性能。

[No.399] Optimal Classification Trees for Continuous Feature Data Using Dynamic Programming with Branch-and-Bound
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: classif
标题: 使用动态规划与分支定界法进行连续特征数据的最佳分类树构建
类别: constraint satisfaction and optimization
作者: Cătălin E. Brița;Jacobus G. M. van der Linden;Emir Demirović;
机构: University of Amsterdam;Delft University of Technology;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/33210
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.07903
英摘: Computing an optimal classification tree that provably maximizes training performance within a given size limit, is NP-hard, and in practice, most state-of-the-art methods do not scale beyond computing optimal trees of depth three. Therefore, most methods rely on a coarse binarization of continuous features to maintain scalability. We propose a novel algorithm that optimizes trees directly on the continuous feature data using dynamic programming with branch-and-bound. We develop new pruning techniques that eliminate many sub-optimal splits in the search when similar to previously computed splits and we provide an efficient subroutine for computing optimal depth-two trees. Our experiments demonstrate that these techniques improve runtime by one or more orders of magnitude over state-of-the-art optimal methods and improve test accuracy by 5% over greedy heuristics.
中摘: 在给定大小限制内计算一个能证明最大化训练性能的最优分类树是NP难问题，在实践中，大多数最先进的方法无法扩展到计算深度超过三的最优树。因此，大多数方法依赖于对连续特征进行粗略二元化以保持可扩展性。我们提出了一种新的算法，直接在连续特征数据上优化树结构，使用动态规划和分支定界技术。我们开发了新的剪枝技术，在搜索过程中可以消除许多与之前计算的最优分割相似的次优分割，并提供了一个高效的子程序来计算最优的深度为两的树结构。我们的实验表明，这些技术比最先进的最优方法提高了运行时间一个或多个数量级，并且在测试准确率上比贪婪启发式方法提高了5%。

[No.400] Cluster-guided Contrastive Class-imbalanced Graph Classification
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: classif
标题: 基于聚类引导对比的类别不平衡图分类
类别: data mining knowledge management i
作者: Wei Ju;Zhengyang Mao;Siyu Yi;Yifang Qin;Yiyang Gu;Zhiping Xiao;Jianhao Shen;Ziyue Qiao;Ming Zhang;
机构: Sichuan University;Peking University;University of Washington;Huawei;Great Bay University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/33298
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.12984
英摘: This paper studies the problem of class-imbalanced graph classification, which aims at effectively classifying the graph categories in scenarios with imbalanced class distributions. While graph neural networks (GNNs) have achieved remarkable success, their modeling ability on imbalanced graph-structured data remains suboptimal, which typically leads to predictions biased towards the majority classes. On the other hand, existing class-imbalanced learning methods in vision may overlook the rich graph semantic substructures of the majority classes and excessively emphasize learning from the minority classes. To address these challenges, we propose a simple yet powerful approach called C$^3$GNN that integrates the idea of clustering into contrastive learning to enhance class-imbalanced graph classification. Technically, C$^3$GNN clusters graphs from each majority class into multiple subclasses, with sizes comparable to the minority class, mitigating class imbalance. It also employs the Mixup technique to generate synthetic samples, enriching the semantic diversity of each subclass. Furthermore, supervised contrastive learning is used to hierarchically learn effective graph representations, enabling the model to thoroughly explore semantic substructures in majority classes while avoiding excessive focus on minority classes. Extensive experiments on real-world graph benchmark datasets verify the superior performance of our proposed method against competitive baselines.
中摘: 这篇论文研究了类别不平衡图分类的问题，旨在有效分类类别分布不平衡场景中的图类别。虽然图神经网络（GNNs）已经取得了显著的成功，但它们在处理类别不平衡的图结构数据时的建模能力仍然不够理想，这通常会导致预测偏向多数类。另一方面，现有的视觉领域类别不平衡学习方法可能会忽略多数类丰富的图语义子结构，并过度强调从少数类中学习。为了解决这些挑战，我们提出了一种简单而强大的方法——C$^3$GNN，将聚类的思想融入对比学习中以增强类别不平衡图分类的效果。技术上，C$^3$GNN 将每个多数类的图聚类成多个子类，子类的数量和大小与少数类相当，从而减轻类别不平衡问题。此外，它还采用了Mixup 技术生成合成样本，丰富了每个子类的语义多样性。进一步地，使用监督对比学习来层次化学习有效的图表示，使模型能够充分探索多数类中的语义子结构，同时避免过度关注少数类。在真实世界的图基准数据集上的广泛实验验证了我们所提出的方法在竞争性基线方法中的优越性能。

[No.404] Normalize Then Propagate: Efficient Homophilous Regularization for Few-Shot Semi-Supervised Node Classification
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: classif
标题: 归一化后再传播：高效的同质化正则化方法用于少样本半监督节点分类
类别: data mining knowledge management ii
作者: Baoming Zhang;MingCai Chen;Jianqing Song;Shuangjie Li;Jie Zhang;Chongjun Wang;
机构: Nanjing University;Nanjing University of Posts and Telecommunications;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/33437
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.08581
英摘: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have demonstrated remarkable ability in semi-supervised node classification. However, most existing GNNs rely heavily on a large amount of labeled data for training, which is labor-intensive and requires extensive domain knowledge. In this paper, we first analyze the restrictions of GNNs generalization from the perspective of supervision signals in the context of few-shot semi-supervised node classification. To address these challenges, we propose a novel algorithm named NormProp, which utilizes the homophily assumption of unlabeled nodes to generate additional supervision signals, thereby enhancing the generalization against label scarcity. The key idea is to efficiently capture both the class information and the consistency of aggregation during message passing, via decoupling the direction and Euclidean norm of node representations. Moreover, we conduct a theoretical analysis to determine the upper bound of Euclidean norm, and then propose homophilous regularization to constraint the consistency of unlabeled nodes. Extensive experiments demonstrate that NormProp achieve state-of-the-art performance under low-label rate scenarios with low computational complexity.
中摘: 图神经网络（GNNs）在半监督节点分类方面展现了显著的能力。然而，大多数现有的GNNs在训练时依赖大量的标注数据，这需要大量的人力投入和深厚的专业知识。在本文中，我们首先从少量标注数据的半监督节点分类的角度出发，分析了GNNs泛化的限制。为了解决这些挑战，我们提出了一种名为NormProp的新算法，该算法利用未标注节点的同质性假设生成额外的监督信号，从而增强面对标签稀缺情况下的泛化能力。核心思想是通过解耦节点表示的方向和欧几里得范数，在消息传递过程中高效地捕捉类信息和聚合的一致性。此外，我们进行了理论分析以确定欧几里得范数的上界，并提出了同质性正则化来约束未标注节点的一致性。广泛的实验表明，在低标签率场景下，NormProp具有较低的计算复杂度并能达到最先进的性能。

[No.406] Tokenphormer: Structure-aware Multi-token Graph Transformer for Node Classification
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: classif
标题: Tokenphormer：一种考虑结构的多令牌图变换器，用于节点分类
类别: data mining knowledge management ii
作者: Zijie Zhou;Zhaoqi Lu;Xuekai Wei;Rongqin Chen;Shenghui Zhang;Pak Lon Ip;Leong Hou U;
机构: University of Macau;Hong Kong Polytechnic University;Guangdong Institute of Intelligent Science and Technology;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/33466
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.15302
英摘: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) are widely used in graph data mining tasks. Traditional GNNs follow a message passing scheme that can effectively utilize local and structural information. However, the phenomena of over-smoothing and over-squashing limit the receptive field in message passing processes. Graph Transformers were introduced to address these issues, achieving a global receptive field but suffering from the noise of irrelevant nodes and loss of structural information. Therefore, drawing inspiration from fine-grained token-based representation learning in Natural Language Processing (NLP), we propose the Structure-aware Multi-token Graph Transformer (Tokenphormer), which generates multiple tokens to effectively capture local and structural information and explore global information at different levels of granularity. Specifically, we first introduce the walk-token generated by mixed walks consisting of four walk types to explore the graph and capture structure and contextual information flexibly. To ensure local and global information coverage, we also introduce the SGPM-token (obtained through the Self-supervised Graph Pre-train Model, SGPM) and the hop-token, extending the length and density limit of the walk-token, respectively. Finally, these expressive tokens are fed into the Transformer model to learn node representations collaboratively. Experimental results demonstrate that the capability of the proposed Tokenphormer can achieve state-of-the-art performance on node classification tasks.
中摘: 图神经网络（GNNs）广泛应用于图数据挖掘任务。传统的GNNs遵循消息传递方案，能够有效利用局部和结构信息。然而，过拟合并导致的信息压缩限制了消息传递过程中的感受野。为了应对这些问题，引入了图变换器（Graph Transformers），实现了全局的感受野，但同时也面临着无关节点噪声干扰和结构信息丢失的问题。因此，借鉴自然语言处理（NLP）中细粒度的基于令牌的表示学习方法，我们提出了结构感知多令牌图变换器（Tokenphormer），它通过生成多个令牌来有效地捕捉局部和结构信息，并在不同粒度级别上探索全局信息。具体而言，我们首先引入了一种由四种走法混合而成的走令牌（walk-token），以灵活地探索图并捕获结构和上下文信息。为了确保局部和全局信息的覆盖，我们还引入了SGPM-令牌（通过自我监督的图预训练模型SGPM获得）和跳令牌（hop-token），分别扩展了走令牌的长度和密度限制。最后，这些具有表现力的令牌被输入到Transformer模型中，以协作学习节点表示。实验结果表明，所提出的Tokenphormer的能力可以在节点分类任务中实现最先进的性能。

[No.407] Human and AI Perceptual Differences in Image Classification Errors
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: classif
标题: 人类与AI在图像分类错误上的感知差异
类别: humans and ai
作者: Minghao Liu;Jiaheng Wei;Yang Liu;James Davis;
机构: University of California, Santa Cruz;Hong Kong University of Science and Technology;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/33568
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2304.08733
英摘: Artificial intelligence (AI) models for computer vision trained with supervised machine learning are assumed to solve classification tasks by imitating human behavior learned from training labels. Most efforts in recent vision research focus on measuring the model task performance using standardized benchmarks such as accuracy. However, limited work has sought to understand the perceptual difference between humans and machines. To fill this gap, this study first analyzes the statistical distributions of mistakes from the two sources and then explores how task difficulty level affects these distributions. We find that even when AI learns an excellent model from the training data, one that outperforms humans in overall accuracy, these AI models have significant and consistent differences from human perception. We demonstrate the importance of studying these differences with a simple human-AI teaming algorithm that outperforms humans alone, AI alone, or AI-AI teaming.
中摘: 基于监督机器学习训练的计算机视觉人工智能（AI）模型被认为通过模仿从训练标签中学到的人类行为来解决分类任务。近年来，大多数视觉研究的努力集中在使用标准化基准（如准确性）来衡量模型的任务性能上。然而，很少有工作试图理解人类与机器在感知上的差异。为了解决这一问题，本研究首先分析了来自两种来源的错误统计分布，然后探讨了任务难度级别如何影响这些分布。我们发现，即使AI从训练数据中学习到了一个优秀的模型，整体准确性优于人类的模型，在感知上依然与人类存在显著且一致的差异。我们通过一个简单的以人为中心的AI团队算法展示了研究这些差异的重要性，该算法在单独的人类、单独的AI或AI-AI团队中均表现出更优的性能。

[No.410] Synthetic Tabular Data Generation for Imbalanced Classification: The Surprising Effectiveness of an Overlap Class
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: classif
标题: 合成表格数据生成在不平衡分类中的效果：重叠类的意外有效性
类别: machine learning i
作者: Annie D'souza;Swetha M;Sunita Sarawagi;
机构: Indian Institute of Technology Bombay;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/33771
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.15657
英摘: Handling imbalance in class distribution when building a classifier over tabular data has been a problem of long-standing interest. One popular approach is augmenting the training dataset with synthetically generated data. While classical augmentation techniques were limited to linear interpolation of existing minority class examples, recently higher capacity deep generative models are providing greater promise.
  However, handling of imbalance in class distribution when building a deep generative model is also a challenging problem, that has not been studied as extensively as imbalanced classifier model training. We show that state-of-the-art deep generative models yield significantly lower-quality minority examples than majority examples. %In this paper, we start with the observation that imbalanced data training of generative models trained imbalanced dataset which under-represent the minority class. We propose a novel technique of converting the binary class labels to ternary class labels by introducing a class for the region where minority and majority distributions overlap. We show that just this pre-processing of the training set, significantly improves the quality of data generated spanning several state-of-the-art diffusion and GAN-based models. While training the classifier using synthetic data, we remove the overlap class from the training data and justify the reasons behind the enhanced accuracy. We perform extensive experiments on four real-life datasets, five different classifiers, and five generative models demonstrating that our method enhances not only the synthesizer performance of state-of-the-art models but also the classifier performance.
中摘: 在构建分类器时处理表格数据中类别分布不平衡的问题一直是长期关注的问题。一种流行的方法是通过生成合成数据来扩充训练数据集。虽然经典的扩充技术仅限于对现有少数类示例进行线性插值，但最近高容量的深度生成模型提供了更大的希望。

然而，在构建深度生成模型时处理类别分布不平衡也是一个具有挑战性的问题，这个问题的研究不如不平衡分类器模型训练广泛。我们展示了最先进的深度生成模型生成的少数类示例显著劣于多数类示例的质量。在这篇论文中，我们从观察不平衡数据训练生成模型开始，这些模型在训练过程中少数类被严重低估。我们提出了一种新颖的技术，即将二元分类标签转换为三元分类标签，通过引入一个类别来表示少数类和多数类分布重叠的区域。我们证明了仅仅这种预处理显著提高了多种最先进的扩散模型和GAN基模型生成的数据质量。在使用合成数据训练分类器时，我们从训练数据中移除重叠类别，并解释了这种方法如何提高准确性。我们在四个真实数据集、五种不同的分类器和五种生成模型上进行了广泛的实验，证明我们的方法不仅提升了最先进的生成模型的合成性能，也提升了分类器的性能。
